# Appoint Cardinal Interaction

designate_theocratic_heir_interaction = {
	icon = heir
	category = interaction_category_diplomacy
	interface_priority = 1000

	desc = designate_theocratic_heir_interaction_desc

	is_available = {
		has_pam_dlc_trigger = yes
	}

	is_shown = {
		scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
			has_realm_law = clerical_appointment_law
			NOT = { has_realm_law = theocratic_elective_succession_law }
			government_has_flag = government_is_theocracy
		}
		NOT = { scope:recipient = scope:puppet_or_actor }
		scope:recipient = {
			OR = { # Relevant people only
				is_councillor_of = scope:puppet_or_actor
				is_courtier_of = scope:puppet_or_actor
				scope:puppet_or_actor.house ?= house
				is_close_or_extended_family_of = scope:puppet_or_actor
			}
		}
	}

	is_valid_showing_failures_only = {
		scope:puppet_or_actor = {
			is_adult = yes
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = designate_theocratic_heir_interaction_dynasty_tt
				exists = dynasty
			}
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			is_adult = yes # Children are not valid for Theocratic succession
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = designate_theocratic_heir_interaction_older_tt
				# The adoption below compares birth dates, not whole years, so equal ages are not enough
				birth_date >= scope:puppet_or_actor.birth_date
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = designate_theocratic_heir_interaction_unlanded_tt
				is_ruler = no
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = designate_theocratic_heir_interaction_faith_tt
				scope:puppet_or_actor.faith ?= faith
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = pam_interaction_clerical_gender_tt
				trigger_if = {
					limit = { exists = scope:puppet_or_actor }
					can_be_granted_theocratic_titles_by = { RULER = scope:puppet_or_actor }
				}
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = designate_theocratic_heir_interaction_already_heir_tt
			NOR = {
				scope:puppet_or_actor.designated_heir ?= scope:recipient
				scope:puppet_or_actor.player_heir ?= scope:recipient
			}
		}
		scope:puppet_or_actor = {
			is_valid_designated_heir = scope:recipient
		}
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { scope:puppet_or_actor != scope:actor }
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				dynasty ?= { dynasty_prestige >= pam_designate_theocratic_heir_renown_cost_value }
			}
		}
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { scope:puppet_or_actor != scope:actor }
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				prestige >= pam_designate_theocratic_heir_prestige_cost_value
			}
		}
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { scope:puppet_or_actor != scope:actor }
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				piety >= major_piety_value
			}
		}
	}

	on_auto_accept = {
		scope:recipient = {
			trigger_event = char_interaction.0121
		}
	}

	on_accept = {
		if = {
			limit = { scope:puppet_or_actor != scope:actor }
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				if = {
					limit = { pam_designate_theocratic_heir_renown_cost_value > 0 }
					dynasty ?= {
						add_dynasty_prestige = {
							value = {
								add = pam_designate_theocratic_heir_renown_cost_value
								multiply = -1
							}
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { pam_designate_theocratic_heir_prestige_cost_value > 0 }
					add_prestige = {
						value = {
							add = pam_designate_theocratic_heir_prestige_cost_value
							max = prestige
							multiply = -1
						}
					}
				}
				add_piety = { value = { add = major_piety_value max = piety multiply = -1 } }
			}
		}
		scope:puppet_or_actor = {
			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_neutral
				title = designate_heir_interaction_notification

				left_icon = scope:puppet_or_actor
				right_icon = scope:recipient

				# The order of operations on this one matters
				hidden_effect = { # To not clout the toast even more
					if = {
						limit = {
							scope:recipient = {
								NOT = { is_courtier_of = scope:puppet_or_actor }
							}
						}
						add_courtier = scope:recipient
					}
				}

				if = { # set_designated_heir only accepts direct descendants, so everyone else is adopted in first
					limit = {
						scope:recipient = {
							NOT = { is_child_of = scope:puppet_or_actor }
						}
					}
					adopt_effect = {
						CHILD = scope:recipient
						ADOPTER = scope:puppet_or_actor
					}
				}

				primary_title = { # Enough to put them first
					change_appointment_investment = {
						target = scope:recipient
						investor = scope:puppet_or_actor
						value = {
							if = { # An appointment title without eligible candidates has no current heir to outbid
								limit = { exists = scope:puppet_or_actor.primary_title.current_heir }
								add = {
									value = "scope:puppet_or_actor.primary_title.current_heir.appointment_candidate_score(scope:puppet_or_actor.primary_title)"
									multiply = 1.1
								}
								subtract = "scope:recipient.appointment_candidate_score(scope:puppet_or_actor.primary_title)"
							}
							min = 4 # We do this to prevent cases when the default score of the candidate is already much higher than that the heir
						}
					}
				}

				set_designated_heir = scope:recipient
			}
		}
		notify_puppeteer_of_outcome_effect = {
			TYPE = event_toast_effect_neutral
			TITLE = designate_heir_interaction_notification_puppeteer
		}
	}

	auto_accept = yes

	cost = {
		renown = {
			value = pam_designate_theocratic_heir_renown_cost_value
			if = {
				limit = { scope:puppet_or_actor != scope:actor }
				multiply = 0
			}
		}
		prestige = {
			value = pam_designate_theocratic_heir_prestige_cost_value
			if = {
				limit = { scope:puppet_or_actor != scope:actor }
				multiply = 0
			}
		}
		piety = {
			value = {
				add = major_piety_value
			}
			if = {
				limit = { scope:puppet_or_actor != scope:actor }
				multiply = 0
			}
		}
	}

	ai_targets = {
		ai_recipients = family
		ai_recipients = courtiers
		ai_recipients = vassals
		max = 5
	}

	ai_frequency_by_tier = {
		barony = 0
		county = 120
		duchy = 60
		kingdom = 60
		empire = 60
		hegemony = 60
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		base = 50

		modifier = { # Traits in common
			add = 25
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				number_of_personality_traits_in_common = {
					target = scope:recipient
					value = 1
				}
			}
		}

		modifier = { # Traits in common
			add = 25
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				number_of_personality_traits_in_common = {
					target = scope:recipient
					value = 2
				}
			}
		}

		modifier = { # Traits in common
			add = 25
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				number_of_personality_traits_in_common = {
					target = scope:recipient
					value = 3
				}
			}
		}

		modifier = { # I like them
			add = 25
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				opinion = {
					value >= medium_positive_opinion
					target = scope:recipient
				}
			}
		}

		modifier = { # Same rite
			factor = 2
			scope:puppet_or_actor.rite = scope:recipient.rite
		}

		modifier = {
			factor = 0
			scope:puppet_or_actor.designated_heir ?= { is_alive = yes }
		}

		modifier = {
			factor = 0
			scope:recipient = {
				OR = {
					any_heir_title = {
						exists = holder
						holder != scope:puppet_or_actor
					}
					employer ?= {
						is_ai = no
						this != scope:puppet_or_actor
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

filial_piety_request_child_gold_interaction = {
	category = interaction_category_friendly
	icon = icon_gold
	interface_priority = 30
	common_interaction = yes
	ai_maybe = no
	popup_on_receive = yes
	use_diplomatic_range = yes

	desc = filial_piety_request_child_gold_interaction_desc
	notification_text = filial_piety_request_child_gold_notification

	cooldown_against_recipient = { years = 5 }

	is_shown = {
		scope:actor = {
			has_personal_tenet_flag = tenet_filial_piety_child_gold_request
			is_available_adult = yes
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			is_child_of = scope:actor
			is_available_adult = yes
		}
	}

	is_valid_showing_failures_only = {
		scope:actor = {
			is_available_adult = yes
			NOT = { is_at_war_with = scope:recipient }
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			is_available_adult = yes
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = filial_piety_request_child_gold_not_enough_gold
				gold > 20
			}
		}
	}

	auto_accept = yes

	on_accept = {
		save_scope_value_as = {
			name = requested_gold_value
			value = {
				value = scope:recipient.gold
				divide = 4
				multiply = {
					value = scope:recipient.ai_greed
					divide = -300
					add = 1
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						scope:recipient = {
							has_any_best_good_relationship_with_character_trigger = {
								CHARACTER = scope:actor
							}
						}
					}
					multiply = 1.5
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						scope:recipient = {
							has_any_moderate_good_relationship_with_character_trigger = {
								CHARACTER = scope:actor
							}
						}
					}
					multiply = 1.25
				}
				max = {
					scope:recipient.culture = {
						if = {
							limit = { has_cultural_era_or_later = culture_era_late_medieval }
							value = 250
						}
						else_if = {
							limit = { has_cultural_era_or_later = culture_era_high_medieval }
							value = 200
						}
						else_if = {
							limit = { has_cultural_era_or_later = culture_era_early_medieval }
							value = 150
						}
						else_if = {
							limit = { has_cultural_era_or_later = culture_era_tribal }
							value = 100
						}
						else = {
							value = 50
						}
					}
				}
				floor = yes
				min = 5
			}
		}
		scope:actor = {
			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_good
				title = filial_piety_request_child_gold_actor_toast
				left_icon = scope:actor
				right_icon = scope:recipient
				show_as_tooltip = {
					add_gold = scope:requested_gold_value
				}
			}
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_neutral
				title = filial_piety_request_child_gold_recipient_toast
				left_icon = scope:recipient
				right_icon = scope:actor
				pay_short_term_gold = {
					target = scope:actor
					gold = scope:requested_gold_value
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

increase_theocratic_heir_candidacy = {
	icon = icon_increase_theocratic_heir_candidacy
	category = interaction_category_diplomacy
	interface_priority = 800

	desc = increase_theocratic_heir_candidacy_desc

	target_type = title
	target_source = puppet_or_actor
	target_filter = domain_titles # TODO_PAM_VP Ecc. Titles filter

	is_shown = {
		scope:puppet_or_actor ?= { # You need to have a valid title no matter what
			any_held_title = {
				OR = {
					holder ?= { has_realm_law = clerical_appointment_law }
					has_title_law = clerical_appointment_law
				}
			}
		}
		has_pam_dlc_trigger = yes
		trigger_if = { # For Theocracies, boost your Heir/courtiers/house members
			limit = {
				scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
					has_realm_law = clerical_appointment_law
					government_has_flag = government_is_theocracy
				}
			}
			OR = {
				scope:puppet_or_actor.player_heir ?= scope:secondary_recipient
				scope:secondary_recipient ?= {
					is_ruler = no
					is_available = yes
					faith ?= scope:puppet_or_actor.faith
					OR = {
						is_close_or_extended_family_of = scope:puppet_or_actor
						is_courtier_of = scope:puppet_or_actor
					}
					can_be_granted_theocratic_titles_by = { RULER = scope:puppet_or_actor }
				}
			}
		}
		trigger_else = { # For normal rulers, boost members of your house if you're the House Head
			scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
				exists = house
				is_house_head = yes
				NOT = { government_has_flag = government_is_theocracy }
				faith ?= scope:secondary_recipient.faith
				house ?= scope:secondary_recipient.house
			}
			scope:secondary_recipient ?= {
				is_ruler = no
				is_available = yes
				trigger_if = {
					limit = { exists = scope:puppet_or_actor }
					can_be_granted_theocratic_titles_by = { RULER = scope:puppet_or_actor }
				}
			}
		}
	}

	is_valid_showing_failures_only = {
		scope:recipient = {
			is_adult = yes # Children are not valid for Theocratic succession
		}
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes }
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				piety >= pam_increase_theocratic_heir_candidacy_cost_value
			}
		}
	}

	custom_character_sort = { candidate_score }

	populate_recipient_list = {
		scope:puppet_or_actor = {
			house ?= {
				every_house_member = {
					limit = {
						top_liege != this
						faith = scope:puppet_or_actor.faith
						is_ruler = no
						can_be_granted_theocratic_titles_by = { RULER = scope:puppet_or_actor }
					}
					add_to_list = characters
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { government_has_flag = government_is_theocracy }
				designated_heir ?= {
					add_to_list = characters
				}
				every_courtier = {
					limit = {
						is_available = yes
						faith = scope:puppet_or_actor.faith
						can_be_granted_theocratic_titles_by = { RULER = scope:puppet_or_actor }
					}
					add_to_list = characters
				}
				every_close_or_extended_family_member = {
					limit = {
						is_available = yes
						faith = scope:puppet_or_actor.faith
						can_be_granted_theocratic_titles_by = { RULER = scope:puppet_or_actor }
					}
					add_to_list = characters
				}
			}
		}
	}

	can_be_picked_title = {
		scope:target = {
			# domain_titles offers baronies too, and appointment investment is county tier and up
			tier >= tier_county
			is_head_of_faith = no
			OR = {
				this = scope:puppet_or_actor.primary_title
				trigger_if = {
					limit = { exists = scope:secondary_recipient }
					is_clerical_appointment_valid_trigger = {
						TITLE = scope:target
						CANDIDATE = scope:secondary_recipient
						TOP_LIEGE = scope:puppet_or_actor
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	has_valid_target_showing_failures_only = {
		exists = scope:target
		exists = scope:recipient
		scope:target = {
			exists = holder
			OR = {
				has_title_law = clerical_appointment_law
				holder = { has_realm_law = clerical_appointment_law }
			}
		}
	}

	redirect = {
		scope:recipient = { save_scope_as = secondary_recipient }
		scope:puppet_or_actor = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = no_head_of_faith }
					}
				}
				faith.religious_head ?= { save_scope_as = recipient }
			}
		}
	}

	auto_accept = yes

	send_option = {
		flag = as_much_as_needed_candidacy_option
		localization = "as_much_as_needed_candidacy_option_desc"
		is_valid = {
			trigger_if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:target
					exists = scope:secondary_recipient
				}
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = already_first_in_line_candidacy_option_desc
					NOT = { scope:target.current_heir = scope:secondary_recipient }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	send_option = {
		flag = major_candidacy_option
		localization = "major_candidacy_option_desc"
	}
	send_option = {
		flag = medium_candidacy_option
		localization = "medium_candidacy_option_desc"
		starts_enabled = { always = yes }
	}

	localization_values = {
		AS_MUCH_AS_NEEDED_CANDIDACY_OPTION = scope:puppet_or_actor.clerical_appointment_score_max_tt_value
		MAJOR_CANDIDACY_OPTION = scope:puppet_or_actor.clerical_appointment_score_major_tt_value
		MEDIUM_CANDIDACY_OPTION = scope:puppet_or_actor.clerical_appointment_score_medium_tt_value
	}

	on_accept = {
		if = {
			limit = { scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes }
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				if = {
					limit = { pam_increase_theocratic_heir_candidacy_cost_value > 0 }
					add_piety = {
						value = {
							add = pam_increase_theocratic_heir_candidacy_cost_value
							max = piety
							multiply = -1
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		switch = {
			trigger = yes
			scope:as_much_as_needed_candidacy_option = {
				# Save the score value
				save_scope_value_as = {
					name = support_candidate_score
					value = {
						value = clerical_appointment_score_max_value
						multiply = scope:puppet_or_actor.appointment_score_interaction_multiplier_modifiers_value
						multiply = scope:puppet_or_actor.pam_theocratic_candidacy_situation_multiplier_value
					}
				}
			}
			scope:major_candidacy_option = {
				save_scope_value_as = {
					name = support_candidate_score
					value = {
						value = appointment_score_major_value
						multiply = scope:puppet_or_actor.appointment_score_interaction_multiplier_modifiers_value
						multiply = scope:puppet_or_actor.pam_theocratic_candidacy_situation_multiplier_value
					}
				}
			}
			scope:medium_candidacy_option = {
				# Save the score value
				save_scope_value_as = {
					name = support_candidate_score
					value = {
						value = appointment_score_medium_value
						multiply = scope:puppet_or_actor.appointment_score_interaction_multiplier_modifiers_value
						multiply = scope:puppet_or_actor.pam_theocratic_candidacy_situation_multiplier_value
					}
				}
			}
		}
		scope:puppet_or_actor = {
			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_neutral
				title = increase_theocratic_heir_candidacy_notification

				left_icon = scope:puppet_or_actor
				right_icon = scope:secondary_recipient

				scope:target = {
					change_appointment_investment = {
						target = scope:secondary_recipient
						investor = scope:puppet_or_actor
						value = scope:support_candidate_score
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes
				scope:actor != scope:recipient
			}
			scope:actor = {
				send_interface_toast = {
					type = event_toast_effect_neutral
					title = increase_theocratic_heir_candidacy_notification_puppeteer
					left_icon = scope:puppet_or_actor
					right_icon = scope:secondary_recipient
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			scope:secondary_recipient = {
				send_interface_message = {
					type = msg_candidacy_improved
					title = supported_candidacy_toast
					left_icon = scope:puppet_or_actor
					right_icon = scope:target
					show_as_tooltip = {
						scope:target = {
							change_appointment_investment = {
								target = scope:secondary_recipient
								investor = scope:puppet_or_actor
								value = scope:support_candidate_score
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	auto_accept = yes

	cost = {
		piety = {
			value = pam_increase_theocratic_heir_candidacy_cost_value
			if = {
				limit = { scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes }
				multiply = 0
			}
		}
	}

	ai_targets = {
		ai_recipients = family
		ai_recipients = scripted_relations
		ai_recipients = courtiers
		max = 20
	}

	ai_frequency_by_tier = {
		barony = 0
		county = 240
		duchy = 120
		kingdom = 120
		empire = 120
		hegemony = 120
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		base = 50

		modifier = { # Traits in common
			add = 25
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				number_of_personality_traits_in_common = {
					target = scope:secondary_recipient
					value = 1
				}
			}
		}

		modifier = { # Traits in common
			add = 25
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				number_of_personality_traits_in_common = {
					target = scope:secondary_recipient
					value = 2
				}
			}
		}

		modifier = { # Traits in common
			add = 25
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				number_of_personality_traits_in_common = {
					target = scope:secondary_recipient
					value = 3
				}
			}
		}

		modifier = { # I like this guy
			add = 25
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				opinion = {
					value >= medium_positive_opinion
					target = scope:secondary_recipient
				}
			}
		}

		modifier = { # This guy is my friend
			add = 25
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				has_relation_friend = scope:secondary_recipient
			}
		}

		modifier = { # Same rite
			factor = 2
			scope:puppet_or_actor.rite = scope:secondary_recipient.rite
		}
	}
}
