# PLAYER ONLY - Head of Faith and Head of Rite titles are not clerical regions, so they need their own picker
influence_head_of_faith_candidacy_interaction = {
	icon = support_candidacy
	category = interaction_category_religion
	interface_priority = 50

	desc = influence_head_of_faith_candidacy_interaction_desc

	target_type = title
	target_source = actor
	target_filter = religious_head_titles

	custom_character_sort = { candidate_score }

	is_available = {
		has_pam_dlc_trigger = yes
		# Mirrors the religious_head_titles filter, so a faith whose head titles are all filled by conclave or inheritance hides the interaction instead of opening an empty picker
		faith ?= {
			OR = {
				religious_head_title ?= { pam_head_of_faith_candidacy_valid_title_trigger = yes }
				any_faith_rite = {
					head_of_rite ?= {
						is_ruler = yes
						primary_title ?= { pam_head_of_faith_candidacy_valid_title_trigger = yes }
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	is_shown = {
		scope:secondary_recipient ?= {
			this != faith.main_rite.head_of_rite
			faith ?= scope:actor.faith
			is_clerical_appointment_valid_base_trigger = {
				CANDIDATE = scope:secondary_recipient
				TOP_LIEGE = scope:actor
			}
			NOR = {
				scope:actor.religious_head_or_challenger ?= this
				scope:secondary_recipient.religious_head_or_challenger ?= this
			}
		}
	}

	is_valid_showing_failures_only = {
		scope:recipient = {
			is_adult = yes
		}
		trigger_if = {
			limit = {
				exists = scope:target
			}
			influence_head_of_faith_candidacy_target_trigger = yes
			scope:secondary_recipient = {
				is_clerical_appointment_valid_trigger = {
					TITLE = scope:target
					CANDIDATE = scope:secondary_recipient
					TOP_LIEGE = scope:actor
				}
			}
		}
		pam_clerical_self_candidacy_restriction_trigger = yes
	}

	can_be_picked_title = {
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { exists = scope:secondary_recipient }
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = influence_clerical_candidacy_interaction_already_holds_title_tt
				scope:target = {
					holder != scope:secondary_recipient
				}
			}
		}
		influence_head_of_faith_candidacy_target_trigger = yes
		pam_clerical_self_candidacy_restriction_trigger = yes
	}

	has_valid_target_showing_failures_only = {
		exists = scope:target
		exists = scope:recipient
		# Dropping a title here instead of greying it out also keeps it out of the window's automatic selection, so the candidacy score values are never asked to price a title that does not use appointment succession
		scope:target = { pam_head_of_faith_candidacy_valid_title_trigger = yes }
	}

	redirect = {
		scope:recipient = { save_scope_as = secondary_recipient }
		scope:actor.faith.main_rite.head_of_rite ?= { save_scope_as = recipient }
	}

	can_send = {
		trigger_if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					scope:as_much_as_needed_candidacy_option ?= yes
					scope:major_candidacy_option ?= yes
					scope:medium_candidacy_option ?= yes
				}
			}
			scope:actor = {
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = support_candidacy_cap_reached_tt
					succession_appointment_score_invested = {
						title = scope:target
						candidate = scope:secondary_recipient
						value < scope:actor.appointment_investment_cap
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	auto_accept = yes

	send_option = {
		flag = as_much_as_needed_candidacy_option
		localization = as_much_as_needed_candidacy_option_desc
		is_valid = {
			trigger_if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:target
					exists = scope:secondary_recipient
				}
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = already_first_in_line_candidacy_option_desc
					NOT = { scope:target.current_heir = scope:secondary_recipient }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	send_option = {
		flag = major_candidacy_option
		localization = major_candidacy_option_desc
	}
	send_option = {
		flag = medium_candidacy_option
		localization = medium_candidacy_option_desc
		starts_enabled = { always = yes }
	}
	send_option = {
		flag = medium_candidacy_loss_option
		localization = medium_candidacy_loss_option_desc
	}
	send_option = {
		flag = major_candidacy_loss_option
		localization = major_candidacy_loss_option_desc
	}

	localization_values = {
		AS_MUCH_AS_NEEDED_CANDIDACY_OPTION = scope:actor.clerical_appointment_score_max_tt_value
		MAJOR_CANDIDACY_OPTION = scope:actor.clerical_appointment_score_major_tt_value
		MEDIUM_CANDIDACY_OPTION = scope:actor.clerical_appointment_score_medium_tt_value
		MEDIUM_CANDIDACY_LOSS_OPTION = scope:actor.appointment_score_medium_loss_tt_value
		MAJOR_CANDIDACY_LOSS_OPTION = scope:actor.appointment_score_major_loss_tt_value
	}

	cost = {
		piety = {
			value = {
				add = 0
				switch = {
					trigger = yes
					scope:as_much_as_needed_candidacy_option = {
						add = {
							value = pam_clerical_candidacy_outbid_piety_base_cost_value
						}
					}
					scope:major_candidacy_option = {
						add = { value = massive_piety_value }
					}
					scope:medium_candidacy_option = {
						add = { value = major_piety_value }
					}
					scope:medium_candidacy_loss_option = {
						add = { value = major_piety_value }
					}
					scope:major_candidacy_loss_option = {
						add = { value = massive_piety_value }
					}
				}
				multiply = scope:actor.pam_increase_theocratic_heir_candidacy_piety_cost_mult_value
			}
		}
	}

	on_accept = {
		switch = {
			trigger = yes
			scope:as_much_as_needed_candidacy_option = {
				save_scope_value_as = {
					name = support_candidate_score
					value = {
						value = clerical_appointment_score_max_value
						multiply = scope:actor.appointment_score_interaction_multiplier_modifiers_value
						multiply = scope:actor.pam_theocratic_candidacy_situation_multiplier_value
					}
				}
				save_scope_value_as = {
					name = opinion_change_value
					value = 15
				}
			}
			scope:major_candidacy_option = {
				save_scope_value_as = {
					name = support_candidate_score
					value = {
						value = appointment_score_major_value
						multiply = scope:actor.appointment_score_interaction_multiplier_modifiers_value
						multiply = scope:actor.pam_theocratic_candidacy_situation_multiplier_value
					}
				}
				save_scope_value_as = {
					name = opinion_change_value
					value = 10
				}
			}
			scope:medium_candidacy_option = {
				save_scope_value_as = {
					name = support_candidate_score
					value = {
						value = appointment_score_medium_value
						multiply = scope:actor.appointment_score_interaction_multiplier_modifiers_value
						multiply = scope:actor.pam_theocratic_candidacy_situation_multiplier_value
					}
				}
				save_scope_value_as = {
					name = opinion_change_value
					value = 5
				}
			}
			scope:medium_candidacy_loss_option = {
				save_scope_value_as = {
					name = support_candidate_score
					value = {
						value = appointment_score_medium_loss_value
						multiply = scope:actor.appointment_score_interaction_multiplier_modifiers_value
						multiply = scope:actor.pam_theocratic_candidacy_situation_multiplier_value
					}
				}
				save_scope_value_as = {
					name = opinion_change_value
					value = -10
				}
			}
			scope:major_candidacy_loss_option = {
				save_scope_value_as = {
					name = support_candidate_score
					value = {
						value = appointment_score_major_loss_value
						multiply = scope:actor.appointment_score_interaction_multiplier_modifiers_value
						multiply = scope:actor.pam_theocratic_candidacy_situation_multiplier_value
					}
				}
				save_scope_value_as = {
					name = opinion_change_value
					value = -20
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					scope:as_much_as_needed_candidacy_option = yes
					scope:major_candidacy_option = yes
					scope:medium_candidacy_option = yes
				}
			}
			scope:secondary_recipient ?= {
				if = {
					limit = {
						this = scope:actor
						is_landless_adventurer = yes
						is_clergy = no
					}
					add_trait = cleric
				}
				if = {
					limit = { this != scope:actor }
					hidden_effect = {
						send_interface_message = {
							type = msg_candidacy_improved
							title = supported_candidacy_toast
							left_icon = scope:actor
							right_icon = scope:target
							show_as_tooltip = {
								scope:target = {
									change_appointment_investment = {
										target = scope:secondary_recipient
										investor = scope:actor
										value = scope:support_candidate_score
									}
								}
							}
						}
					}
					add_opinion = {
						modifier = supported_candidacy_opinion
						target = scope:actor
						opinion = scope:opinion_change_value
					}
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			scope:secondary_recipient ?= {
				if = {
					limit = { this != scope:actor }
					hidden_effect = {
						send_interface_message = {
							type = msg_candidacy_harmed
							title = harmed_candidacy_toast
							left_icon = scope:actor
							right_icon = scope:target
							show_as_tooltip = {
								scope:target = {
									change_appointment_investment = {
										target = scope:secondary_recipient
										investor = scope:actor
										value = scope:support_candidate_score
									}
								}
							}
						}
					}
					add_opinion = {
						modifier = harmed_candidacy_opinion
						target = scope:actor
						opinion = scope:opinion_change_value
					}
				}
			}
		}

		scope:actor = {
			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_neutral
				title = influence_head_of_faith_candidacy_interaction_notification
				left_icon = scope:actor
				right_icon = scope:secondary_recipient
				scope:target = {
					change_appointment_investment = {
						target = scope:secondary_recipient
						investor = scope:actor
						value = scope:support_candidate_score
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# AI ONLY
support_clerical_candidacy_interaction = {
	icon = support_candidacy
	common_interaction = yes
	hidden = yes

	desc = support_clerical_candidacy_interaction_desc

	target_type = title
	target_source = actor
	target_filter = domain_clerical_regions

	can_be_picked_title = {
		# Only the capital see, so the search stays one title wide
		scope:actor.capital_county.clerical_region_title ?= scope:target
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { exists = scope:target.current_heir }
			scope:target.current_heir = {
				NOR = {
					this = scope:actor
					puppeteer ?= scope:actor
					has_relation_friend = scope:actor
					has_relation_lover = scope:actor
				}
			}
		}
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { exists = scope:secondary_recipient }
			is_clerical_appointment_valid_trigger = {
				TITLE = scope:target
				CANDIDATE = scope:secondary_recipient
				TOP_LIEGE = scope:actor
			}
		}
		scope:secondary_recipient = {
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = is_candidate_for_title_tt
				scope:target = {
					holder != scope:secondary_recipient
				}
				"appointment_candidate_accumulated_score(scope:target)" > 0
			}
		}
	}

	is_available = {
		can_influence_clerical_appointment_trigger = yes
		has_pam_dlc_trigger = yes
		piety >= major_piety_value
	}

	is_shown = {
		scope:secondary_recipient ?= {
			# Without this a Head of Rite backing themselves would send the interaction to themselves
			this != faith.main_rite.head_of_rite
			is_clerical_appointment_valid_base_trigger = {
				CANDIDATE = scope:secondary_recipient
				TOP_LIEGE = scope:actor
			}
			NOR = {
				scope:actor.religious_head_or_challenger ?= this
				scope:secondary_recipient.religious_head_or_challenger ?= this
			}
		}
	}

	redirect = {
		scope:recipient = { save_scope_as = secondary_recipient }
		scope:actor.faith.main_rite.head_of_rite ?= { save_scope_as = recipient }
	}

	populate_recipient_list = {
		scope:actor = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_clerical_appointment_valid_base_trigger = {
						CANDIDATE = this
						TOP_LIEGE = scope:actor
					}
				}
				add_to_list = characters
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					puppet:theological_agent_puppet ?= {
						is_clerical_appointment_valid_base_trigger = {
							CANDIDATE = this
							TOP_LIEGE = scope:actor
						}
					}
				}
				puppet:theological_agent_puppet = { add_to_list = characters }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					puppet:external_theological_agent_puppet ?= {
						is_clerical_appointment_valid_base_trigger = {
							CANDIDATE = this
							TOP_LIEGE = scope:actor
						}
					}
				}
				puppet:external_theological_agent_puppet = { add_to_list = characters }
			}
			every_relation = {
				type = friend
				limit = {
					is_clerical_appointment_valid_base_trigger = {
						CANDIDATE = this
						TOP_LIEGE = scope:actor
					}
				}
				add_to_list = characters
			}
			every_relation = {
				type = lover
				limit = {
					is_clerical_appointment_valid_base_trigger = {
						CANDIDATE = this
						TOP_LIEGE = scope:actor
					}
				}
				add_to_list = characters
			}
		}
	}

	auto_accept = yes

	send_option = {
		flag = as_much_as_needed_candidacy_option
		localization = as_much_as_needed_candidacy_option_desc
	}
	send_option = {
		flag = major_candidacy_option
		localization = major_candidacy_option_desc
	}
	send_option = {
		flag = medium_candidacy_option
		localization = medium_candidacy_option_desc
	}

	cost = {
		piety = {
			value = {
				add = 0
				switch = {
					trigger = yes
					scope:as_much_as_needed_candidacy_option = {
						add = {
							value = pam_clerical_candidacy_outbid_piety_cost_value
							desc = BASE
						}
					}
					scope:major_candidacy_option = {
						add = {
							value = pam_clerical_candidacy_major_piety_cost_value
							desc = BASE
						}
					}
					scope:medium_candidacy_option = {
						add = {
							value = pam_clerical_candidacy_medium_piety_cost_value
							desc = BASE
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	on_accept = {
		switch = {
			trigger = yes
			scope:as_much_as_needed_candidacy_option = {
				save_scope_value_as = {
					name = support_candidate_score
					value = {
						value = clerical_appointment_score_max_value
						multiply = scope:actor.appointment_score_interaction_multiplier_modifiers_value
						multiply = scope:actor.pam_theocratic_candidacy_situation_multiplier_value
					}
				}
			}
			scope:major_candidacy_option = {
				save_scope_value_as = {
					name = support_candidate_score
					value = {
						value = appointment_score_major_value
						multiply = scope:actor.appointment_score_interaction_multiplier_modifiers_value
						multiply = scope:actor.pam_theocratic_candidacy_situation_multiplier_value
					}
				}
			}
			scope:medium_candidacy_option = {
				save_scope_value_as = {
					name = support_candidate_score
					value = {
						value = appointment_score_medium_value
						multiply = scope:actor.appointment_score_interaction_multiplier_modifiers_value
						multiply = scope:actor.pam_theocratic_candidacy_situation_multiplier_value
					}
				}
			}
		}

		scope:target = {
			change_appointment_investment = {
				target = scope:secondary_recipient
				investor = scope:actor
				value = scope:support_candidate_score
			}
		}

		scope:secondary_recipient ?= {
			if = {
				limit = {
					this != scope:actor
					is_ai = no
				}
				hidden_effect = {
					send_interface_message = {
						type = msg_candidacy_improved
						title = supported_candidacy_toast
						left_icon = scope:actor
						right_icon = scope:target
						show_as_tooltip = {
							scope:target = {
								change_appointment_investment = {
									target = scope:secondary_recipient
									investor = scope:actor
									value = scope:support_candidate_score
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}

		# Christian Situation Catalysts
		# if = {
		# 	limit = {
		# 		scope:actor = {
		# 			NOR = {
		# 				government_has_flag = government_is_ecclesiastical
		# 				this = faith.main_rite.head_of_rite
		# 			}
		# 		}
		# 		situation:the_christian_church ?= {
		# 			situation_top_has_catalyst = catalyst_secular_influenced_clerical_candidacy
		# 		}
		# 	}
		# 	situation:the_christian_church = {
		# 		trigger_situation_catalyst = catalyst_secular_influenced_clerical_candidacy
		# 	}
		# }
		# if = {
		# 	limit = {
		# 		scope:actor ?= scope:secondary_recipient
		# 		scope:actor = {
		# 			government_has_flag = government_is_ecclesiastical
		# 		}
		# 		situation:the_christian_church ?= {
		# 			situation_top_has_catalyst = catalyst_clergy_influenced_own_candidacy
		# 		}
		# 	}
		# 	situation:the_christian_church = {
		# 		trigger_situation_catalyst = catalyst_clergy_influenced_own_candidacy
		# 	}
		# }
	}

	ai_targets = {
		ai_recipients = self
	}
	ai_targets = {
		ai_recipients = puppets
	}
	ai_targets = {
		ai_recipients = scripted_relations
		max = 20
	}

	# A crown returns to its capital see readily; a count has one see and rarely the piety to contest it
	ai_frequency_by_tier = {
		barony = 0
		county = 240
		duchy = 60
		kingdom = 12
		empire = 12
		hegemony = 12
	}

	# Score the piety tiers explicitly so the AI does not fall back on send_option declaration order
	ai_will_do = {
		base = 0

		modifier = {
			scope:medium_candidacy_option = yes
			piety >= pam_clerical_candidacy_medium_piety_cost_value
			add = 100
		}
		modifier = {
			scope:major_candidacy_option = yes
			piety >= pam_clerical_candidacy_major_piety_cost_value
			add = 200
		}
		modifier = {
			scope:as_much_as_needed_candidacy_option = yes
			piety >= pam_clerical_candidacy_outbid_piety_cost_value
			add = 300
		}

		modifier = {
			exists = scope:secondary_recipient
			has_relation_rival = scope:secondary_recipient
			factor = 0
		}
	}
}

# AI ONLY - smear campaign against a clerical appointment candidate
harm_clerical_candidacy_interaction = {
	icon = harm_candidacy
	common_interaction = yes
	hidden = yes

	desc = harm_clerical_candidacy_interaction_desc

	target_type = title
	target_source = actor
	target_filter = domain_clerical_regions

	can_be_picked_title = {
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { exists = scope:secondary_recipient }
			is_clerical_appointment_valid_trigger = {
				TITLE = scope:target
				CANDIDATE = scope:secondary_recipient
				TOP_LIEGE = scope:actor
			}
		}
		scope:secondary_recipient = {
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = is_candidate_for_title_tt
				scope:target = {
					holder != scope:secondary_recipient
				}
				"appointment_candidate_accumulated_score(scope:target)" > 0
			}
		}
	}

	is_available = {
		ai_honor > 25
		OR = {
			has_trait = arbitrary
			has_trait = sadistic
			has_trait = deceitful
			has_trait = callous
			has_trait = vengeful
		}
		can_influence_clerical_appointment_trigger = yes
		#is_ai = yes
		has_pam_dlc_trigger = yes
	}

	is_shown = {
		scope:secondary_recipient ?= {
			is_clerical_appointment_valid_base_trigger = {
				CANDIDATE = scope:secondary_recipient
				TOP_LIEGE = scope:actor
			}
		}
	}

	is_valid_showing_failures_only = {
		# When realms get really large, the AI needs a fixed pool of characters to promote
		# Otherwise performance tanks significantly in the form of 'stutters' or smaller freezes
		scope:secondary_recipient ?= {
			trigger_if = {
				limit = {
					scope:actor.realm_size >= 250
				}
				static_group_filter = {
					group = ai_filter
					match = {
						value = 1
						subtract = {
							add = scope:actor.realm_size
							multiply = 0.001
							multiply = 3
							min = 0.1
							max = 0.9
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		scope:actor = {
			piety >= major_piety_value
		}
	}

	redirect = {
		scope:recipient = { save_scope_as = secondary_recipient }
		scope:actor.faith.main_rite.head_of_rite ?= { save_scope_as = recipient }
	}

	populate_recipient_list = {
		scope:actor = {
			every_relation = {
				type = rival
				type = nemesis
				limit = {
					is_clerical_appointment_valid_base_trigger = {
						CANDIDATE = this
						TOP_LIEGE = scope:actor
					}
					NOT = { is_close_or_extended_family_of = scope:actor }
				}
				add_to_list = characters
			}
			house ?= {
				every_house_relation = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							has_house_relation_level = quarrel
							has_house_relation_level = rivalry
							has_house_relation_level = feud
						}
					}
					every_relation_house = {
						house_head = {
							every_close_family_member = {
								limit = {
									is_clerical_appointment_valid_base_trigger = {
										CANDIDATE = this
										TOP_LIEGE = scope:actor
									}
									NOT = { is_close_or_extended_family_of = scope:actor }
								}
								add_to_list = characters
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	auto_accept = yes

	send_option = {
		flag = minor_candidacy_harm_option
		localization = minor_candidacy_harm_option_desc
		starts_enabled = { always = yes }
	}
	send_option = {
		flag = medium_candidacy_harm_option
		localization = medium_candidacy_harm_option_desc
	}
	send_option = {
		flag = major_candidacy_harm_option
		localization = major_candidacy_harm_option_desc
	}

	localization_values = {
		MINOR_CANDIDACY_HARM_OPTION = scope:actor.clerical_appointment_score_minor_loss_tt_value
		MEDIUM_CANDIDACY_HARM_OPTION = scope:actor.clerical_appointment_score_medium_loss_tt_value
		MAJOR_CANDIDACY_HARM_OPTION = scope:actor.clerical_appointment_score_major_loss_tt_value
	}

	cost = {
		piety = {
			value = {
				add = 0
				switch = {
					trigger = yes
					scope:minor_candidacy_harm_option = {
						add = {
							value = minor_piety_value
							desc = BASE
						}
					}
					scope:medium_candidacy_harm_option = {
						add = {
							value = major_piety_value
							desc = BASE
						}
					}
					scope:major_candidacy_harm_option = {
						add = {
							value = massive_piety_value
							desc = BASE
						}
					}
				}
				multiply = scope:actor.pam_increase_theocratic_heir_candidacy_piety_cost_mult_value
			}
		}
	}

	on_accept = {
		switch = {
			trigger = yes
			scope:minor_candidacy_harm_option = {
				save_scope_value_as = {
					name = harm_candidate_score
					value = {
						value = appointment_score_minor_loss_value
						multiply = scope:actor.appointment_score_interaction_multiplier_modifiers_value
						multiply = scope:actor.pam_theocratic_candidacy_situation_multiplier_value
					}
				}
				save_scope_value_as = {
					name = opinion_change_value
					value = -5
				}
			}
			scope:medium_candidacy_harm_option = {
				save_scope_value_as = {
					name = harm_candidate_score
					value = {
						value = appointment_score_medium_loss_value
						multiply = scope:actor.appointment_score_interaction_multiplier_modifiers_value
						multiply = scope:actor.pam_theocratic_candidacy_situation_multiplier_value
					}
				}
				save_scope_value_as = {
					name = opinion_change_value
					value = -15
				}
			}
			scope:major_candidacy_harm_option = {
				save_scope_value_as = {
					name = harm_candidate_score
					value = {
						value = appointment_score_major_loss_value
						multiply = scope:actor.appointment_score_interaction_multiplier_modifiers_value
						multiply = scope:actor.pam_theocratic_candidacy_situation_multiplier_value
					}
				}
				save_scope_value_as = {
					name = opinion_change_value
					value = -30
				}
			}
		}

		scope:target = {
			change_appointment_investment = {
				target = scope:secondary_recipient
				investor = scope:actor
				value = scope:harm_candidate_score
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:secondary_recipient = { this != scope:actor }
			}
			scope:secondary_recipient = {
				add_opinion = {
					modifier = harmed_candidacy_opinion
					target = scope:actor
					opinion = scope:opinion_change_value
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_ai = no
					}
					hidden_effect = {
						send_interface_message = {
							type = msg_candidacy_harmed
							title = harmed_candidacy_toast
							left_icon = scope:actor
							right_icon = scope:target
							show_as_tooltip = {
								scope:target = {
									change_appointment_investment = {
										target = scope:secondary_recipient
										investor = scope:actor
										value = scope:harm_candidate_score
									}
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:actor = { is_ai = no }
				}
				scope:actor = {
					send_interface_toast = {
						type = event_toast_effect_bad
						title = harm_candidate_rival_progress_toast
						left_icon = scope:secondary_recipient
						scope:secondary_recipient = {
							progress_towards_rival_effect = {
								REASON = rival_harmed_candidacy
								CHARACTER = scope:actor
								OPINION = 0
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}

		# Christian Situation Catalysts
		# if = {
		# 	limit = {
		# 		scope:actor = {
		# 			NOR = {
		# 				government_has_flag = government_is_ecclesiastical
		# 				this = faith.main_rite.head_of_rite
		# 			}
		# 		}
		# 		situation:the_christian_church ?= {
		# 			situation_top_has_catalyst = catalyst_secular_influenced_clerical_candidacy
		# 		}
		# 	}
		# 	situation:the_christian_church = {
		# 		trigger_situation_catalyst = catalyst_secular_influenced_clerical_candidacy
		# 	}
		# }
	}

	ai_targets = {
		ai_recipients = scripted_relations
	}
	ai_targets = {
		ai_recipients = family
		ai_recipients = courtiers
		ai_recipients = vassals
		max = 20
	}

	ai_frequency_by_tier = {
		barony = 0
		county = 360
		duchy = 180
		kingdom = 180
		empire = 180
		hegemony = 180
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		base = 0
		modifier = {
			scope:actor = {
				has_relation_rival = scope:secondary_recipient
			}
			add = 100
		}
		modifier = {
			scope:actor = {
				has_relation_nemesis = scope:secondary_recipient
			}
			add = 150
		}
		modifier = {
			scope:actor = {
				opinion = {
					target = scope:secondary_recipient
					value < medium_positive_opinion
				}
			}
			add = 20
		}
		modifier = {
			scope:secondary_recipient ?= {
				is_close_family_of = scope:actor
			}
			add = -50
		}
		modifier = {
			scope:secondary_recipient ?= {
				is_extended_family_of = scope:actor
			}
			add = -25
		}
		modifier = {
			scope:actor = {
				OR = {
					has_trait = ambitious
					has_trait = callous
					has_trait = deceitful
				}
			}
			add = 40
		}
		modifier = {
			scope:actor = {
				has_usable_hook = scope:secondary_recipient
			}
			add = -10
		}
		modifier = {
			scope:actor = {
				OR = {
					has_relation_friend = scope:secondary_recipient
					has_relation_lover = scope:secondary_recipient
				}
			}
			factor = 0
		}
	}
}

request_realm_priest_replacement_interaction = {
	category = interaction_category_religion
	icon = icon_request_priest
	interface_priority = 100
	common_interaction = yes
	notification_text = REQUEST_REALM_PRIEST_REPLACEMENT_NOTIFICATION

	desc = request_realm_priest_replacement_interaction_desc

	redirect = {
		# Prioritize direct superior of chaplain, over e.g. head of faith
		# But still allow right clicking on anyone in religious hierachy
		scope:actor = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					# Standard recipient triggers are true...
					can_request_priest_from_recipient_trigger = { RECIPIENT = scope:recipient }
				}
				# A realm under an antipope has only them to ask, so anyone else clicked is left to fail validity
				if = {
					limit = { is_following_religious_head_challenger = yes }
					if = {
						limit = { cp:councillor_court_chaplain ?= scope:recipient }
						religious_head_or_challenger ?= { save_scope_as = recipient }
					}
				}
				# Ensure direct superior of chaplain is always prioritized...
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						# Selected recipient is not direct superior of chaplain
						exists = cp:councillor_court_chaplain.superior
						cp:councillor_court_chaplain.superior != scope:recipient
					}
					cp:councillor_court_chaplain.superior = { save_scope_as = recipient }
				}
				# ...otherwise ensure the recipient is not the current chaplain
				else_if = {
					limit = { cp:councillor_court_chaplain ?= scope:recipient }
					if = {
						limit = { exists = faith.religious_head }
						faith.religious_head = { save_scope_as = recipient }
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = { exists = faith.main_rite.head_of_rite }
						faith.main_rite.head_of_rite = { save_scope_as = recipient }
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	populate_recipient_list = {
		scope:actor = {
			every_vassal_or_below = {
				every_courtier = { # All non-councillor realm courtiers of the actor's vassals
					limit = {
						can_be_court_chaplain_trigger = { COURT_OWNER = scope:actor }
						is_councillor = no
					}
					add_to_list = characters
				}
			}
			every_courtier = { # All non-councillor realm courtiers of the actor
				limit = {
					can_be_court_chaplain_trigger = { COURT_OWNER = scope:actor }
					is_councillor = no
				}
				add_to_list = characters
			}
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			every_vassal_or_below = {
				every_courtier = { # All non-councillor realm courtiers of the recipient's vassals
					limit = {
						can_be_court_chaplain_trigger = { COURT_OWNER = scope:actor }
						is_councillor = no
					}
					add_to_list = characters
				}
			}
			every_courtier = { # All non-councillor realm courtiers of the recipient
				limit = {
					can_be_court_chaplain_trigger = { COURT_OWNER = scope:actor }
					is_councillor = no
				}
				add_to_list = characters
			}
		}
		scope:actor = {
			every_vassal_or_below = { # All non-councillor theocratic vassals of the actor
				limit = {
					government_has_flag = government_is_theocracy
					can_be_court_chaplain_trigger = { COURT_OWNER = scope:actor }
					is_councillor = no
				}
				add_to_list = characters
			}
			#Monk house members too!
			house ?= {
				every_house_member = {
					limit = {
						has_trait = devoted
						in_diplomatic_range = scope:actor
						is_adult = yes
						is_ruler = no
						can_be_court_chaplain_trigger = { COURT_OWNER = scope:actor }
						is_councillor = no
						trigger_if = {
							limit = {
								exists = liege
								liege = {
									NOT = {
										this = scope:actor
									}
								}
							}
							liege = {
								is_ai = yes
							}
						}
						NOT = {
							has_relation_rival = scope:actor
						}
					}
					add_to_list = characters
				}
			}
			every_child = {
				limit = {
					has_trait = devoted	
				}
			}
		}
	}

	is_available = {
		# Temporal/Conditional use council Appoint, which fires on_fired_from_position instead
		NOT = { faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = clerical_appointment_ruler_can_replace_ecc } }
		government_allows = council
		# Nomads have no Court Chaplain position to replace, so they cannot request a new Realm Priest
		NOT = { government_has_flag = government_is_nomadic }
		# The Minister of Rites is appointed through the ministry, not by petitioning the clergy
		tgp_has_access_to_ministry_trigger = no
		has_pam_dlc_trigger = yes
	}

	is_shown = {
		# The Head of Faith appoints their own chaplain freely via the council instead of petitioning themselves
		scope:recipient != scope:actor
		# Allow right clicking on anyone in religious hierachy
		scope:actor = {
			can_request_priest_from_recipient_trigger = { RECIPIENT = scope:recipient }
		}
	}

	cooldown_against_recipient = { years = 5 }

	is_valid_showing_failures_only = {
		scope:actor = {
			trigger_if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						government_has_flag = government_is_theocracy
					}
				}
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = piety_level_or_external_puppet_req_tt
					OR = {
						exists = puppet:external_theological_agent_puppet
						piety_level >= 3
					}
				}
			}
			trigger_else = {
				piety_level >= 1
			}
			pam_antipope_clerical_request_trigger = { RECIPIENT = scope:recipient }
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = pam_blocked_by_promised_pilgrimage_tt
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = promised_pilgrimage_to_clergy_modifier }
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = recipient_will_not_replace_self_tt
			scope:recipient != scope:actor.cp:councillor_court_chaplain
		}
	}

	can_send = {
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = recipient_will_not_replace_self_tt
			scope:recipient != scope:actor.cp:councillor_court_chaplain
		}
	}

	cost = {
		piety = {
			value = 0
			# Change the piety loss amount based on the candidate's education level
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:secondary_recipient ?= { has_education_rank_trigger = { RANK = 1 OPERATOR = equals } }
				}
				add = minor_piety_value
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:secondary_recipient ?= { has_education_rank_trigger = { RANK = 2 OPERATOR = equals } }
				}
				add = medium_piety_value
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:secondary_recipient ?= { has_education_rank_trigger = { RANK = 3 OPERATOR = equals } }
				}
				add = major_piety_value
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:secondary_recipient ?= { has_education_rank_trigger = { RANK = 4 OPERATOR = equals } }
				}
				add = massive_piety_value
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:secondary_recipient ?= { has_education_rank_trigger = { RANK = 5 OPERATOR = equals } }
				}
				add = monumental_piety_value
			}
			#Costs more to send for your monk relatives
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:secondary_recipient = {
						NOR = {
							is_vassal_or_below_of = scope:actor
							is_courtier_of = scope:actor
						}
					}
				}
				multiply = 1.5
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:actor = {
						domicile ?= { has_domicile_parameter = estate_cheaper_realm_priest }
					}
				}
				multiply = ecclesiastical_domicile_cheaper_realm_priest_mult
			}
		}
	}

	on_accept = {
		scope:actor = {
			# Remove the current realm priest and make the candidate the realm priest
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:secondary_recipient = {
						NOT = {
							is_courtier_of = scope:actor
						}
						is_ruler = no
					}
				}
				add_courtier = scope:secondary_recipient # Move the character to the court first if he's from another court and not a ruler.
			}
			cp:councillor_court_chaplain = {
				save_scope_as = old_chaplain
			}
			# Flag so on_fired_from_position does not treat this as the crown acting
			add_character_flag = { flag = pam_church_replacing_realm_priest days = 1 }
			assign_councillor_type = {
				type = councillor_court_chaplain
				remove_existing_councillor = yes
				target = scope:secondary_recipient
			}
			remove_character_flag = pam_church_replacing_realm_priest
			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_good
				title = new_realm_priest_toast_title
				left_icon = scope:actor
				right_icon = scope:secondary_recipient
				# Make sure the candidate is grateful
				scope:actor = {
					reverse_add_opinion = {
						target = scope:secondary_recipient
						modifier = grateful_opinion
						opinion = 40
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						scope:use_hook = yes
						has_usable_hook = scope:recipient
					}
					use_hook = scope:recipient
				}
			}
			scope:old_chaplain = {
				add_opinion = { # Make sure your kicked realm priest is pissed
					target = scope:actor
					modifier = fp3_resentful_opinion
					opinion = -40
				}
			}
		}
		pam_offer_pilgrimage_effect = {
			ACTOR = scope:actor
			CLERIC = scope:recipient
		}
		# Catalysts
		# if = {
		# 	limit = {
		# 		scope:actor.religion = { like_christianity_religion_trigger = yes }
		# 		# Only rulers actually taking part in the situation can move it
		# 		scope:actor = { exists = top_participant_group:the_christian_church }
		# 		situation:the_christian_church ?= { situation_top_has_catalyst = catalyst_the_christian_church_requested_realm_priest_replacement }
		# 	}
		# 	situation:the_christian_church = { trigger_situation_catalyst = catalyst_the_christian_church_requested_realm_priest_replacement }
		# }
	}

	on_decline = {

	}

	auto_accept = {
		# Using a hook should make him auto accept
		custom_description = {
			text = "spending_hook"
			subject = scope:actor
			object = scope:recipient
			scope:use_hook = yes
			scope:actor = { has_strong_hook = scope:recipient }
		}
	}

	ai_accept = {
		base = 0

		# Warcraft
		master_thrall_modifier = yes
		# End Warcraft

		celestial_hierarchy_acceptance_modifier = {
			ACTOR = scope:puppet_or_actor
			RECIPIENT = scope:recipient
		}

		# Weak hook: extra points
		modifier = {
			scope:use_hook = yes
			add = 200
			scope:actor = { NOT = { has_strong_hook = scope:recipient } }
			desc = SCHEME_WEAK_HOOK_USED
		}

		modifier = {
			add = pam_offer_pilgrimage_acceptance_value
			scope:offer_pilgrimage = yes
			desc = OFFER_PILGRIMAGE_INTERACTION_OPTION_REASON
		}

		# Respect the liege
		modifier = {
			add = 100
			scope:recipient = { target_is_liege_or_above = scope:actor }
			desc = LIEGE_REASON
		}

		modifier = {
			add = ecclesiastical_domicile_acceptance_minor_value_1
			desc = ESTATE_REALM_PRIEST_REASON
			scope:actor = {
				domicile ?= {
					has_domicile_parameter = estate_easier_realm_priest_replacement_1
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = ecclesiastical_domicile_acceptance_minor_value_2
			desc = ESTATE_REALM_PRIEST_REASON
			scope:actor = {
				domicile ?= {
					has_domicile_parameter = estate_easier_realm_priest_replacement_2
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = ecclesiastical_domicile_acceptance_minor_value_3
			desc = ESTATE_REALM_PRIEST_REASON
			scope:actor = {
				domicile ?= {
					has_domicile_parameter = estate_easier_realm_priest_replacement_3
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = ecclesiastical_domicile_acceptance_micro_value_1
			desc = ESTATE_SEMINARY_REASON
			scope:actor = {
				domicile ?= {
					has_domicile_parameter = estate_confessor_seminary_1
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = ecclesiastical_domicile_acceptance_micro_value_2
			desc = ESTATE_SEMINARY_REASON
			scope:actor = {
				domicile ?= {
					has_domicile_parameter = estate_confessor_seminary_2
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = ecclesiastical_domicile_acceptance_micro_value_3
			desc = ESTATE_SEMINARY_REASON
			scope:actor = {
				domicile ?= {
					has_domicile_parameter = estate_confessor_seminary_3
				}
			}
		}
		ecclesiastical_domicile_cathedral_awe_modifier = yes

		# Actor is from the recipient's house
		modifier = {
			add = 10
			exists = scope:actor.house
			scope:recipient = {
				OR = {
					is_close_family_of = scope:actor
					is_extended_family_of = scope:actor
				}
			}
			desc = YOU_ARE_MY_HOUSE_MEMBER
		}

		# Candidate is from the recipient's house
		modifier = {
			add = 10
			exists = scope:secondary_recipient
			exists = scope:secondary_recipient.house
			scope:recipient = {
				OR = {
					is_close_family_of = scope:secondary_recipient
					is_extended_family_of = scope:secondary_recipient
				}
			}
			desc = THEY_ARE_MY_HOUSE_MEMBER
		}

		# I have sinful traits
		modifier = {
			add = {
				add = -10
				multiply = scope:actor.num_sinful_traits
			}
			scope:actor.num_sinful_traits > 0
			desc = I_AM_SINFUL
		}

		# Candidate has sinful traits
		modifier = {
			exists = scope:secondary_recipient
			add = {
				add = -10
				multiply = scope:secondary_recipient.num_sinful_traits
			}
			scope:secondary_recipient.num_sinful_traits > 0
			desc = THEY_ARE_SINFUL
		}

		# I have virtuous traits
		modifier = {
			add = {
				add = 10
				multiply = scope:actor.num_virtuous_traits
			}
			scope:actor.num_virtuous_traits > 0
			desc = I_AM_VIRTUOUS
		}

		# Candidate has virtuous traits
		modifier = {
			exists = scope:secondary_recipient
			add = {
				add = 10
				multiply = scope:secondary_recipient.num_virtuous_traits
			}
			scope:secondary_recipient.num_virtuous_traits > 0
			desc = THEY_ARE_VIRTUOUS
		}

		# Religious superior's opinion of the actor matters!
		opinion_modifier = {
			opinion_target = scope:actor
			who = scope:recipient
			multiplier = 1
			desc = AI_OPINION_REASON
		}

		# Religious Superior likes candidate more than the realm priest
		modifier = {
			add = 20
			exists = scope:secondary_recipient
			scope:recipient = {
				save_temporary_opinion_value_as = {
					name = secondary_recipient_opinion
					target = scope:secondary_recipient
				}
				save_temporary_opinion_value_as = {
					name = realm_priest_opinion
					target = scope:actor.cp:councillor_court_chaplain
				}
			}
			scope:secondary_recipient_opinion > {
			   add = scope:realm_priest_opinion
			   add = 20
			}
			desc = AI_OPINION_REASON_CANDIDATE_OVER_REALM_PRIEST
		}

		# Religious Superior likes realm priest more than the candidate
		modifier = {
			add = -20
			exists = scope:secondary_recipient
			scope:recipient = {
				save_temporary_opinion_value_as = {
					name = secondary_recipient_opinion
					target = scope:secondary_recipient
				}
				save_temporary_opinion_value_as = {
					name = realm_priest_opinion
					target = scope:actor.cp:councillor_court_chaplain
				}
			}
			scope:realm_priest_opinion > {
			   add = scope:secondary_recipient_opinion
			   add = 20
			}
			desc = AI_OPINION_REASON_REALM_PRIEST_OVER_CANDIDATE
		}

		modifier = { # A trait in common with the candidate
			add = 5
			exists = scope:secondary_recipient
			scope:recipient = {
				number_of_personality_traits_in_common = {
					target = scope:secondary_recipient
					value = 1
				}
			}
			desc = AI_COMMON_TRAITS
		}

		modifier = { # Two trait in common
			add = 10
			exists = scope:secondary_recipient
			scope:recipient = {
				number_of_personality_traits_in_common = {
					target = scope:secondary_recipient
					value = 2
				}
			}
			desc = AI_COMMON_TRAITS
		}

		modifier = { # Three traits in common
			add = 15
			exists = scope:secondary_recipient
			scope:recipient = {
				number_of_personality_traits_in_common = {
					target = scope:secondary_recipient
					value = 3
				}
			}
			desc = AI_COMMON_TRAITS
		}

		# Modifier based on Piety Level of the actor
		modifier = {
			add = {
				add = 5
				multiply = scope:actor.piety_level
			}
			scope:actor.piety_level > 1
			desc = I_AM_PIOUS
		}

		# Modifier based on Piety Level of the candidate
		modifier = {
			exists = scope:secondary_recipient
			add = {
				add = 5
				multiply = scope:secondary_recipient.piety_level
			}
			scope:secondary_recipient.piety_level > 1
			desc = THEY_ARE_PIOUS
		}

		# More likely to accept if you have an alliance
		modifier = {
			add = 40
			scope:actor = { is_allied_to = scope:recipient }
			desc = AI_HAS_ALLIANCE
		}

		# Fs
		modifier = {
			add = 25
			scope:recipient = {
				has_relation_friend = scope:actor
			}
			desc = WE_ARE_FRIENDS
		}

		# BFFs
		modifier = {
			add = 50
			scope:recipient = {
				has_relation_best_friend = scope:actor
			}
			desc = WE_ARE_BEST_FRIENDS
		}

		# Take dread into account
		modifier = {
			add = 50
			scope:recipient = {
				target_is_liege_or_above = scope:actor
				has_dread_level_towards = {
					target = scope:actor
					level = 1
				}
			}
			desc = INTIMIDATED_REASON
		}

		modifier = {
			add = 100
			scope:recipient = {
				target_is_liege_or_above = scope:actor
				has_dread_level_towards = {
					target = scope:actor
					level = 2
				}
			}
			desc = COWED_REASON
		}

		# Below 10 Learning for negative, Above 10 Learning for positive
		modifier = {
			add = {
				value = scope:secondary_recipient.learning
				subtract = 10
				multiply = 2
			}
			trigger = {
				scope:secondary_recipient ?= {
					learning != 10
				}
			}
			desc = THEY_HAVE_LEARNING_SKILL_REASON
		}

		# Is a monk
		modifier = {
			add = 25
			scope:secondary_recipient ?= {
				has_trait = erudite
			}
			desc = THEY_HAVE_ERUDITE_TRAIT
		}
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:recipient != scope:actor
		}
		is_valid = {
			scope:actor = {
				has_usable_hook = scope:recipient
			}
			trigger_if = {
				limit = {
					scope:recipient = {
						is_ai = no
					}
				}
				scope:actor = { is_ai = no }
			}
		}
		flag = use_hook
		localization = EDUCATION_HOOK
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			pam_offer_pilgrimage_option_shown_trigger = { ACTOR = scope:actor }
		}
		is_valid = {
			pam_offer_pilgrimage_option_valid_trigger = { ACTOR = scope:actor }
		}
		flag = offer_pilgrimage
		localization = OFFER_PILGRIMAGE_INTERACTION_OPTION
	}

	should_use_extra_icon = {
		scope:actor = { has_usable_hook = scope:recipient }
	}

	extra_icon = "gfx/interface/icons/character_interactions/hook_icon.dds"

	send_options_exclusive = no

	ai_targets = {
		ai_recipients = realm_priest_superior
	}

	ai_frequency_by_tier = {
		barony = 0
		county = 60
		duchy = 60
		kingdom = 60
		empire = 60
		hegemony = 60
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		base = 0
		opinion_modifier = { # I dislike my realm priest
			opinion_target = scope:actor.cp:councillor_court_chaplain
			who = scope:actor
			multiplier = -1
		}
		opinion_modifier = { # My realm priest dislikes me
			opinion_target = scope:actor
			who = scope:actor.cp:councillor_court_chaplain
			multiplier = -1
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 50
			desc = request_realm_priest_replacement_ai_different_rite
			exists = scope:actor.cp:councillor_court_chaplain
			scope:actor.cp:councillor_court_chaplain.rite != scope:actor.rite
		}
	}
}

# Sway to Rite is split three ways for performance, like the Demand Conversion family
# Persuade one of your own courtiers to adopt your rite
sway_courtier_to_my_rite_interaction = {
	category = interaction_category_religion
	special_interaction = rite_conversion
	icon = icon_rite
	interface_priority = 45
	common_interaction = yes

	ai_maybe = yes
	ai_min_reply_days = 1
	ai_max_reply_days = 5
	popup_on_receive = yes
	notification_text = sway_ruler_to_my_rite_interaction_notification

	desc = sway_ruler_to_my_rite_interaction_desc

	is_available = {
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { is_ai = yes }
			is_adult = yes
			is_incapable = no
		}
	}

	is_shown = {
		scope:recipient = {
			is_courtier_of = scope:puppet_or_actor
		}
		scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
			faith = scope:recipient.faith
			rite != scope:recipient.rite
		}
	}

	cooldown_against_recipient = { years = 5 }

	is_valid_showing_failures_only = {
		scope:puppet_or_actor = {
			NOR = {
				has_trait = incapable
				is_adult = no
			}
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			NOR = {
				is_adult = no
				has_trait = incapable
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = sway_ruler_to_my_rite_interaction_hor_tt
					rite.head_of_rite ?= this
					NOT = { pam_sway_to_rite_head_of_faith_reclaims_trigger = { ACTOR = scope:puppet_or_actor } }
				}
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = sway_ruler_to_my_rite_interaction_hof_tt
					OR = {
						scope:puppet_or_actor.religious_head_or_challenger ?= this
						primary_title ?= { is_head_of_faith = yes }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes }
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				piety >= pam_sway_ruler_to_my_rite_cost_value
			}
		}
	}

	cost = {
		piety = {
			value = pam_sway_ruler_to_my_rite_cost_value
			if = {
				limit = { scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes }
				multiply = 0
			}
			#Free for holy order leaders spreading beliefs
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:holy_order_leader ?= this
					exists = scope:order_tenet
				}
				multiply = 0
			}
		}
	}

	#Use hook
	send_option = {
		is_valid = {
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				has_usable_hook = scope:recipient
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				NOT = { is_imprisoned_by = scope:puppet_or_actor }
			}
		}
		flag = hook
		localization = SCHEME_HOOK
	}
	should_use_extra_icon = {
		scope:puppet_or_actor = { has_usable_hook = scope:recipient }
	}
	extra_icon = "gfx/interface/icons/character_interactions/hook_icon.dds"

	send_options_exclusive = no

	auto_accept = {
		custom_description = {
			text = "spending_hook"
			subject = scope:puppet_or_actor
			object = scope:recipient
			scope:hook = yes
			scope:puppet_or_actor = { has_strong_hook = scope:recipient }
			NOT = { scope:recipient = { has_character_flag = ai_will_not_convert } }
		}
	}

	on_accept = {
		pam_sway_to_rite_on_accept_effect = yes
	}

	on_decline = {
		pam_sway_to_rite_on_decline_effect = yes
	}

	ai_accept = {
		base = -15

		celestial_hierarchy_acceptance_modifier = {
			ACTOR = scope:puppet_or_actor
			RECIPIENT = scope:recipient
		}

		religion_sway_to_rite_default_modifier = yes
		modifier = { # The Head of Faith calling them back to the mainline rite eases the Head of Rite refusal
			add = 100
			scope:recipient.rite.head_of_rite ?= scope:recipient
			pam_sway_to_rite_head_of_faith_reclaims_trigger = { ACTOR = scope:puppet_or_actor }
			desc = PAM_SWAY_HEAD_OF_FAITH_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			desc = ASK_FOR_CONVERSION_RECIPIENT_WILL_NOT_ACCEPT
			add = -1500
			scope:recipient = {
				has_character_flag = ai_will_not_convert
			}
		}
	}

	ai_targets = {
		ai_recipients = courtiers
		max = 5
	}

	ai_target_quick_trigger = {
		adult = yes
	}

	ai_frequency_by_tier = {
		barony = 0
		county = 120
		duchy = 36
		kingdom = 24
		empire = 24
		hegemony = 24
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		base = 25
		modifier = {
			scope:hook = yes
			add = 50
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 50
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				has_trait = zealous
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -50
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				has_trait = cynical
			}
		}
		modifier = { # Try to rigorously convert your heir so all efforts won't go to waste on succession
			add = 100
			scope:recipient = {
				is_primary_heir_of = scope:puppet_or_actor
			}
		}
		modifier = { # More likely against close family members
			add = 50
			scope:recipient = {
				is_close_family_of = scope:puppet_or_actor
			}
		}
		modifier = { # HoRs actually hustle
			add = 50
			scope:puppet_or_actor.rite.head_of_rite ?= scope:puppet_or_actor
		}
		modifier = { # More likely if the government is religious
			add = 50
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				government_is_religious_trigger = yes
			}
		}
		modifier = { # Heresiarchs are able to spread their rites easily
			add = 1000
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				OR = {
					has_trait = heresiarch
					has_trait = herald
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Persuade a subject, tributary, or landed member of your house to adopt your rite
sway_vassal_to_my_rite_interaction = {
	category = interaction_category_religion
	special_interaction = rite_conversion
	icon = icon_rite
	interface_priority = 45
	common_interaction = yes

	use_diplomatic_range = yes
	ai_maybe = yes
	ai_min_reply_days = 1
	ai_max_reply_days = 5
	popup_on_receive = yes
	notification_text = sway_ruler_to_my_rite_interaction_notification

	desc = sway_ruler_to_my_rite_interaction_desc

	is_available = {
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { is_ai = yes }
			is_adult = yes
			is_incapable = no
		}
	}

	is_shown = {
		scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
			faith = scope:recipient.faith
			rite != scope:recipient.rite
		}
		scope:puppet_or_actor != scope:recipient
		scope:recipient = {
			NOT = { is_courtier_of = scope:puppet_or_actor }
			OR = {
				target_is_liege_or_above = scope:puppet_or_actor
				is_tributary_of = scope:puppet_or_actor
				pam_rite_sway_house_lane_trigger = {
					ACTOR = scope:puppet_or_actor
					RECIPIENT = this
				}
			}
		}
	}

	cooldown_against_recipient = { years = 5 }

	is_valid_showing_failures_only = {
		scope:puppet_or_actor = {
			NOR = {
				is_at_war_with = scope:recipient
				has_trait = incapable
				is_adult = no
			}
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			NOR = {
				is_adult = no
				has_trait = incapable
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = sway_ruler_to_my_rite_interaction_hor_tt
					rite.head_of_rite ?= this
					NOT = { pam_sway_to_rite_head_of_faith_reclaims_trigger = { ACTOR = scope:puppet_or_actor } }
				}
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = sway_ruler_to_my_rite_interaction_hof_tt
					OR = {
						scope:puppet_or_actor.religious_head_or_challenger ?= this
						primary_title ?= { is_head_of_faith = yes }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes }
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				piety >= pam_sway_ruler_to_my_rite_cost_value
			}
		}
	}

	cost = {
		piety = {
			value = pam_sway_ruler_to_my_rite_cost_value
			if = {
				limit = { scope:pam_sway_disputation ?= yes }
				add = medium_piety_value
			}
			if = {
				limit = { scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes }
				multiply = 0
			}
		}
	}

	#Use hook
	send_option = {
		is_valid = {
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				has_usable_hook = scope:recipient
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				NOT = { is_imprisoned_by = scope:puppet_or_actor }
			}
		}
		flag = hook
		localization = SCHEME_HOOK
	}
	# Clergy inducements are shared with the outreach variant so vassal sway is never a worse toolkit
	# Endow their church - paid to the recipient on acceptance
	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
				pam_is_clergy_ruler_trigger = yes
				has_treasury = yes
			}
			scope:puppet_or_actor = scope:actor
			trigger_if = {
				limit = { scope:actor = { is_ai = yes } }
				scope:recipient = {
					OR = {
						is_ai = no
						any_held_title = { has_clerical_region = yes }
						highest_held_title_tier >= tier_kingdom
					}
				}
			}
		}
		is_valid = {
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				treasury >= pam_sway_to_rite_endowment_cost_value
			}
		}
		flag = pam_sway_endowment
		localization = PAM_SWAY_TO_RITE_ENDOWMENT_OPTION
	}
	# Theological disputation - unlocked by scholarly renown, costs extra piety
	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
				pam_is_clergy_ruler_trigger = yes
			}
			scope:puppet_or_actor = scope:actor
		}
		is_valid = {
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = PAM_SWAY_TO_RITE_DISPUTATION_REQUIRES_SCHOLAR
				scope:puppet_or_actor = {
					has_trait = lifestyle_scholar
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_scholar
						value >= 60
					}
				}
			}
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				piety >= pam_sway_to_rite_disputation_piety_total_value
			}
		}
		flag = pam_sway_disputation
		localization = PAM_SWAY_TO_RITE_DISPUTATION_OPTION
	}
	# Pledge a favor - only worth anything to a prelate who can call it in against our treasury
	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
				pam_is_clergy_ruler_trigger = yes
				has_treasury = yes
			}
			scope:puppet_or_actor = scope:actor
			scope:recipient = {
				government_has_flag = government_is_ecclesiastical
			}
			trigger_if = {
				limit = { scope:actor = { is_ai = yes } }
				scope:actor = { has_trait = trusting }
			}
		}
		is_valid = {
			scope:recipient = {
				can_add_hook = {
					target = scope:puppet_or_actor
					type = favor_hook
				}
			}
		}
		flag = pam_sway_pledge_favor
		localization = PAM_SWAY_TO_RITE_PLEDGE_FAVOR_OPTION
	}
	should_use_extra_icon = {
		scope:puppet_or_actor = { has_usable_hook = scope:recipient }
	}
	extra_icon = "gfx/interface/icons/character_interactions/hook_icon.dds"

	send_options_exclusive = no

	auto_accept = {
		custom_description = {
			text = "spending_hook"
			subject = scope:puppet_or_actor
			object = scope:recipient
			scope:hook = yes
			scope:puppet_or_actor = { has_strong_hook = scope:recipient }
			NOT = { scope:recipient = { has_character_flag = ai_will_not_convert } }
		}
	}

	on_accept = {
		pam_sway_to_rite_on_accept_effect = yes
	}

	on_decline = {
		pam_sway_to_rite_on_decline_effect = yes
	}

	ai_accept = {
		base = -15

		celestial_hierarchy_acceptance_modifier = {
			ACTOR = scope:puppet_or_actor
			RECIPIENT = scope:recipient
		}

		religion_sway_to_rite_default_modifier = yes
		modifier = { # The Head of Faith calling them back to the mainline rite eases the Head of Rite refusal
			add = 100
			scope:recipient.rite.head_of_rite ?= scope:recipient
			pam_sway_to_rite_head_of_faith_reclaims_trigger = { ACTOR = scope:puppet_or_actor }
			desc = PAM_SWAY_HEAD_OF_FAITH_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			desc = ASK_FOR_CONVERSION_RECIPIENT_WILL_NOT_ACCEPT
			add = -1500
			scope:recipient = {
				has_character_flag = ai_will_not_convert
			}
		}
		modifier = { # Endowing their church sweetens the petition
			add = 25
			scope:pam_sway_endowment ?= yes
			desc = PAM_SWAY_ENDOWMENT_REASON
		}
		modifier = { # Doubly so when our rite blesses such transactions
			add = 20
			scope:pam_sway_endowment ?= yes
			pam_actor_has_simony_trigger = yes
			desc = PAM_SWAY_ENDOWMENT_SIMONY_REASON
		}
		modifier = { # Hard to gainsay a doctor of scripture in open disputation
			add = {
				value = scope:puppet_or_actor.learning
				subtract = scope:recipient.learning
				multiply = 2
				min = 0
				add = 20
			}
			scope:pam_sway_disputation ?= yes
			desc = PAM_SWAY_DISPUTATION_REASON
		}
		modifier = { # They would hold a favor over us
			add = 30
			scope:pam_sway_pledge_favor ?= yes
			desc = PAM_SWAY_PLEDGE_FAVOR_REASON
		}
		modifier = { # Prelate recipients resist; eases as their diocese converts to our rite
			add = {
				value = -75
				add = pam_sway_to_rite_diocese_momentum_value
			}
			scope:puppet_or_actor ?= { pam_is_clergy_ruler_trigger = yes }
			scope:recipient = {
				any_held_title = { has_clerical_region = yes }
			}
			desc = PAM_SWAY_RECIPIENT_PRELATE_REASON
		}
		modifier = { # Their own diocese is already turning to our rite
			add = pam_sway_to_rite_diocese_momentum_value
			scope:puppet_or_actor ?= { pam_is_clergy_ruler_trigger = yes }
			scope:recipient = { is_ruler = yes }
			pam_sway_to_rite_diocese_momentum_value > 0
			desc = PAM_SWAY_DIOCESE_MOMENTUM_REASON
		}
	}

	ai_targets = {
		ai_recipients = vassals
		ai_recipients = tributaries
		max = 10
	}

	ai_target_quick_trigger = {
		adult = yes
	}

	ai_frequency_by_tier = {
		barony = 0
		county = 120
		duchy = 24
		kingdom = 12
		empire = 12
		hegemony = 12
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		base = 25
		modifier = {
			scope:recipient.capital_county.clerical_region_title.holder ?= scope:puppet_or_actor
			add = 100
		}
		modifier = {
			scope:hook = yes
			add = 50
		}
		# Prefer option combinations that include a valid inducement
		modifier = {
			scope:pam_sway_endowment ?= yes
			add = 25
		}
		modifier = {
			scope:pam_sway_disputation ?= yes
			add = 25
		}
		modifier = {
			scope:pam_sway_pledge_favor ?= yes
			add = 25
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 50
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				has_trait = zealous
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -50
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				has_trait = cynical
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 10
			scope:recipient = {
				opinion = {
					target = scope:puppet_or_actor
					value >= 65
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = { # More likely to convert rulers that own a Holy Site
			add = 20
			pam_sway_recipient_holds_holy_site_trigger = yes
		}
		# modifier = { # More likely to happen during Instability
		# 	add = 15
		# 	scope:puppet_or_actor.religion = { like_christianity_religion_trigger = yes }
		# 	situation:the_christian_church ?= {
		# 		has_situation_top_phase_parameter = the_christian_church_easier_sway_to_rite
		# 	}
		# }
		modifier = { # HoRs actually hustle
			add = 50
			scope:puppet_or_actor.rite.head_of_rite ?= scope:puppet_or_actor
		}
		modifier = { # More likely if the government is religious
			add = 50
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				government_is_religious_trigger = yes
			}
		}
		modifier = { # Heresiarchs are able to spread their rites easily
			add = 1000
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				OR = {
					has_trait = heresiarch
					has_trait = herald
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = { # Try to rigorously convert your heir so all efforts won't go to waste on succession
			add = 100
			scope:recipient = {
				is_primary_heir_of = scope:puppet_or_actor
			}
		}
		modifier = { # Much more likely to target vassals with neighboring counties with the swayed rite, instead of random ones
			add = 200
			scope:recipient = {
				is_vassal_of = scope:puppet_or_actor
				any_sub_realm_county = {
					any_neighboring_county = {
						this.rite = scope:puppet_or_actor.rite
					}
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = { # More likely against close family members
			add = 50
			scope:recipient = {
				is_close_family_of = scope:puppet_or_actor
			}
		}
		modifier = { # Prefer higher tiers
			add = scope:recipient.highest_held_title_tier
		}
		modifier = { # Much less likely If no counties in my realm have my rite, unless I'm zealous
			add = -150
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				any_realm_county = {
					this.rite = scope:puppet_or_actor.rite
					count = 0
				}
				NOT = { has_trait = zealous }
			}
		}
		# modifier = { # Less likely for Mozarabic priests to try and convert all of Iberia's nobility
		# 	add = -40
		# 	scope:puppet_or_actor = {
		# 		is_clergy = yes
		# 		rite = rite:mozarabic_church
		# 	}
		# 	scope:recipient.rite = scope:recipient.faith.main_rite
		# }
	}
}

# Clergy outreach: persuade rulers of your diocese, bordering realms, fellow prelates, or your liege to adopt your rite
sway_ruler_to_my_rite_interaction = {
	category = interaction_category_religion
	special_interaction = rite_conversion
	icon = icon_rite
	interface_priority = 45
	common_interaction = yes

	use_diplomatic_range = yes
	ai_maybe = yes
	ai_min_reply_days = 1
	ai_max_reply_days = 5
	popup_on_receive = yes
	notification_text = sway_ruler_to_my_rite_interaction_notification

	desc = sway_ruler_to_my_rite_interaction_desc

	is_shown = {
		scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
			faith = scope:recipient.faith
			rite != scope:recipient.rite
			OR = {
				pam_is_clergy_ruler_trigger = yes
				faith.religious_head ?= this
			}
			trigger_if = {
				limit = {
					is_ai = yes
					NOT = { puppeteer ?= { is_ai = no } }
				}
				is_adult = yes
				is_incapable = no
			}
		}
		scope:puppet_or_actor != scope:recipient
		# Courtiers, subjects, and kin use the other sway variants, so we never show two against the same target
		scope:recipient = {
			NOR = {
				is_courtier_of = scope:puppet_or_actor
				target_is_liege_or_above = scope:puppet_or_actor
				is_tributary_of = scope:puppet_or_actor
			}
		}
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { scope:puppet_or_actor ?= { is_house_head = yes } }
			NOT = {
				pam_rite_sway_house_lane_trigger = {
					ACTOR = scope:puppet_or_actor
					RECIPIENT = scope:recipient
				}
			}
		}
	}

	cooldown_against_recipient = { years = 5 }

	is_valid_showing_failures_only = {
		scope:puppet_or_actor = {
			NOR = {
				is_at_war_with = scope:recipient
				has_trait = incapable
				is_adult = no
			}
			trigger_if = { # Only clergy are told why a target is out of reach; a Head of Faith reaches their whole faith
				limit = {
					pam_is_clergy_ruler_trigger = yes
					NOT = { faith.religious_head ?= this }
					#Also, Holy order leaders are not blocked by this when spreading their beliefs
					NAND = {
						scope:holy_order_leader ?= this
						exists = scope:order_tenet
					}
				}
				# One custom_tooltip lane per route inside the trigger, so the failure tooltip lists them all
				pam_rite_sway_clergy_reach_trigger = {
					ACTOR = scope:puppet_or_actor
					RECIPIENT = scope:recipient
				}
			}
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			NOR = {
				is_adult = no
				has_trait = incapable
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = sway_ruler_to_my_rite_interaction_hor_tt
					rite.head_of_rite ?= this
					NOT = { pam_sway_to_rite_head_of_faith_reclaims_trigger = { ACTOR = scope:puppet_or_actor } }
				}
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = sway_ruler_to_my_rite_interaction_hof_tt
					OR = {
						scope:puppet_or_actor.religious_head_or_challenger ?= this
						primary_title ?= { is_head_of_faith = yes }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes }
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				piety >= pam_sway_ruler_to_my_rite_cost_value
			}
		}
	}

	cost = {
		piety = {
			value = pam_sway_ruler_to_my_rite_cost_value
			if = {
				limit = { scope:pam_sway_disputation ?= yes }
				add = medium_piety_value
			}
			if = {
				limit = { scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes }
				multiply = 0
			}
		}
	}

	#Use hook
	send_option = {
		is_valid = {
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				has_usable_hook = scope:recipient
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				NOT = { is_imprisoned_by = scope:puppet_or_actor }
			}
		}
		flag = hook
		localization = SCHEME_HOOK
	}
	# Endow their church - paid to the recipient on acceptance
	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
				pam_is_clergy_ruler_trigger = yes
				has_treasury = yes
			}
			scope:puppet_or_actor = scope:actor
			trigger_if = {
				limit = { scope:actor = { is_ai = yes } }
				scope:recipient = {
					OR = {
						is_ai = no
						any_held_title = { has_clerical_region = yes }
						highest_held_title_tier >= tier_kingdom
					}
				}
			}
		}
		is_valid = {
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				treasury >= pam_sway_to_rite_endowment_cost_value
			}
		}
		flag = pam_sway_endowment
		localization = PAM_SWAY_TO_RITE_ENDOWMENT_OPTION
	}
	# Theological disputation - unlocked by scholarly renown, costs extra piety
	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
				pam_is_clergy_ruler_trigger = yes
			}
			scope:puppet_or_actor = scope:actor
		}
		is_valid = {
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = PAM_SWAY_TO_RITE_DISPUTATION_REQUIRES_SCHOLAR
				scope:puppet_or_actor = {
					has_trait = lifestyle_scholar
					has_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_scholar
						value >= 60
					}
				}
			}
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				piety >= pam_sway_to_rite_disputation_piety_total_value
			}
		}
		flag = pam_sway_disputation
		localization = PAM_SWAY_TO_RITE_DISPUTATION_OPTION
	}
	# Pledge a favor - only worth anything to a prelate who can call it in against our treasury
	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
				pam_is_clergy_ruler_trigger = yes
				has_treasury = yes
			}
			scope:puppet_or_actor = scope:actor
			trigger_if = {
				limit = { scope:actor = { is_ai = yes } }
				scope:actor = { has_trait = trusting }
			}
		}
		is_valid = {
			scope:recipient = {
				can_add_hook = {
					target = scope:puppet_or_actor
					type = favor_hook
				}
			}
		}
		flag = pam_sway_pledge_favor
		localization = PAM_SWAY_TO_RITE_PLEDGE_FAVOR_OPTION
	}
	should_use_extra_icon = {
		scope:puppet_or_actor = { has_usable_hook = scope:recipient }
	}
	extra_icon = "gfx/interface/icons/character_interactions/hook_icon.dds"

	send_options_exclusive = no

	auto_accept = {
		custom_description = {
			text = "spending_hook"
			subject = scope:puppet_or_actor
			object = scope:recipient
			scope:hook = yes
			scope:puppet_or_actor = { has_strong_hook = scope:recipient }
			NOT = { scope:recipient = { has_character_flag = ai_will_not_convert } }
		}
	}

	on_accept = {
		pam_sway_to_rite_on_accept_effect = yes
	}

	on_decline = {
		pam_sway_to_rite_on_decline_effect = yes
	}

	ai_accept = {
		base = -15

		celestial_hierarchy_acceptance_modifier = {
			ACTOR = scope:puppet_or_actor
			RECIPIENT = scope:recipient
		}

		religion_sway_to_rite_default_modifier = yes
		modifier = { # The Head of Faith calling them back to the mainline rite eases the Head of Rite refusal
			add = 100
			scope:recipient.rite.head_of_rite ?= scope:recipient
			pam_sway_to_rite_head_of_faith_reclaims_trigger = { ACTOR = scope:puppet_or_actor }
			desc = PAM_SWAY_HEAD_OF_FAITH_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			desc = ASK_FOR_CONVERSION_RECIPIENT_WILL_NOT_ACCEPT
			add = -1500
			scope:recipient = {
				has_character_flag = ai_will_not_convert
			}
		}
		modifier = { # Endowing their church sweetens the petition
			add = 25
			scope:pam_sway_endowment ?= yes
			desc = PAM_SWAY_ENDOWMENT_REASON
		}
		modifier = { # Doubly so when our rite blesses such transactions
			add = 20
			scope:pam_sway_endowment ?= yes
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				OR = {
					rite = { rite_has_parameter = tenet_simony_ecclesiastical_purchase }
					has_personal_tenet_flag = tenet_simony_ecclesiastical_purchase
				}
			}
			desc = PAM_SWAY_ENDOWMENT_SIMONY_REASON
		}
		modifier = { # Hard to gainsay a doctor of scripture in open disputation
			add = {
				value = scope:puppet_or_actor.learning
				subtract = scope:recipient.learning
				multiply = 2
				min = 0
				add = 20
			}
			scope:pam_sway_disputation ?= yes
			desc = PAM_SWAY_DISPUTATION_REASON
		}
		modifier = { # They would hold a favor over us
			add = 30
			scope:pam_sway_pledge_favor ?= yes
			desc = PAM_SWAY_PLEDGE_FAVOR_REASON
		}
		modifier = { # Asking our own liege or above
			add = -80
			scope:puppet_or_actor ?= { pam_is_clergy_ruler_trigger = yes }
			scope:recipient = {
				is_liege_or_above_of = scope:puppet_or_actor
			}
			desc = PAM_SWAY_LIEGE_REASON
		}
		modifier = { # Prelate recipients resist; eases as their diocese converts to our rite
			add = {
				value = -75
				add = pam_sway_to_rite_diocese_momentum_value
			}
			scope:puppet_or_actor ?= { pam_is_clergy_ruler_trigger = yes }
			scope:recipient = {
				any_held_title = { has_clerical_region = yes }
			}
			desc = PAM_SWAY_RECIPIENT_PRELATE_REASON
		}
		modifier = { # Mirrors the diocese-adjacency rule the AI targeting always follows
			add = -60
			scope:puppet_or_actor ?= { pam_is_clergy_ruler_trigger = yes }
			scope:recipient = {
				any_held_title = { has_clerical_region = yes }
				NOR = {
					superior ?= scope:puppet_or_actor
					is_liege_or_above_of = scope:puppet_or_actor
					capital_county.clerical_region_title.holder ?= scope:puppet_or_actor
				}
			}
			NOT = {
				pam_rite_sway_adjacent_diocese_trigger = {
					ACTOR = scope:puppet_or_actor
					RECIPIENT = scope:recipient
				}
			}
			desc = PAM_SWAY_DISTANT_PRELATE_REASON
		}
		modifier = { # Independent recipient of higher rank
			add = -60
			scope:puppet_or_actor ?= { pam_is_clergy_ruler_trigger = yes }
			scope:recipient = {
				is_independent_ruler = yes
				government_is_religious_trigger = no
				highest_held_title_tier > scope:puppet_or_actor.highest_held_title_tier
				NOT = { is_liege_or_above_of = scope:puppet_or_actor }
			}
			desc = PAM_SWAY_INDEPENDENT_SECULAR_REASON
		}
		modifier = { # Actor's piety level
			add = {
				value = scope:puppet_or_actor.piety_level
				multiply = 5
			}
			scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
				pam_is_clergy_ruler_trigger = yes
				piety_level >= 1
			}
			desc = PAM_SWAY_MY_PIETY_REASON
		}
		modifier = { # Their own diocese is already turning to our rite
			add = pam_sway_to_rite_diocese_momentum_value
			scope:puppet_or_actor ?= { pam_is_clergy_ruler_trigger = yes }
			scope:recipient = { is_ruler = yes }
			pam_sway_to_rite_diocese_momentum_value > 0
			desc = PAM_SWAY_DIOCESE_MOMENTUM_REASON
		}
		# modifier = { # More likely for secular rulers to accept during instability
		# 	add = 20
		# 	situation:the_christian_church ?= {
		# 		OR = {
		# 			situation_participant_group:christian_regular_main_power_rulers = {
		# 				participant_group_has_character = scope:recipient
		# 				has_participant_group_parameter = the_christian_church_easier_sway_to_rite
		# 			}
		# 			situation_participant_group:christian_regular_secondary_powers_rulers = {
		# 				participant_group_has_character = scope:recipient
		# 				has_participant_group_parameter = the_christian_church_easier_sway_to_rite
		# 			}
		# 		}
		# 	}
		# 	desc = CHRISTIAN_CHURCH_PHASE_REASON
		# }
		modifier = {
			add = 10
			desc = SWAY_TO_RITE_CATHEDRAL_STARTED_REASON
			scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
				has_character_modifier = pam_grand_cathedral_started_modifier
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 20
			desc = SWAY_TO_RITE_CATHEDRAL_COMPLETED_REASON
			scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
				has_character_modifier = pam_grand_cathedral_completed_modifier
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = ecclesiastical_domicile_estate_easier_sway_to_rite_1
			desc = SWAY_TO_RITE_ESTATE_REASON
			scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
				domicile ?= {
					has_domicile_parameter = estate_easier_sway_to_rite_1
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = ecclesiastical_domicile_estate_easier_sway_to_rite_2
			desc = SWAY_TO_RITE_ESTATE_REASON
			scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
				domicile ?= {
					has_domicile_parameter = estate_easier_sway_to_rite_2
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = ecclesiastical_domicile_acceptance_minor_value_1
			desc = ESTATE_SWAY_CHAPTER_REASON
			scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
				domicile ?= {
					has_domicile_parameter = estate_sway_chapter_1
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = ecclesiastical_domicile_acceptance_minor_value_2
			desc = ESTATE_SWAY_CHAPTER_REASON
			scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
				domicile ?= {
					has_domicile_parameter = estate_sway_chapter_2
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = ecclesiastical_domicile_acceptance_minor_value_3
			desc = ESTATE_SWAY_CHAPTER_REASON
			scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
				domicile ?= {
					has_domicile_parameter = estate_sway_chapter_3
				}
			}
		}
	}

	ai_targets = {
		ai_recipients = puppets
	}

	ai_targets = {
		ai_recipients = rulers_in_clerical_region
		ai_recipients = subordinates
		max = 10
	}

		adult = yes
	}

	ai_frequency_by_tier = {
		barony = 0
		county = 48
		duchy = 24
		kingdom = 12
		empire = 12
		hegemony = 12
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		base = 25
		modifier = {
			scope:recipient.capital_county.clerical_region_title.holder ?= scope:puppet_or_actor
			add = 100
		}
		modifier = {
			scope:hook = yes
			add = 50
		}
		# Prefer option combinations that include a valid inducement
		modifier = {
			scope:pam_sway_endowment ?= yes
			add = 25
		}
		modifier = {
			scope:pam_sway_disputation ?= yes
			add = 25
		}
		modifier = {
			scope:pam_sway_pledge_favor ?= yes
			add = 25
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 50
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				has_trait = zealous
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -50
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				has_trait = cynical
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 20
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				is_allied_to = scope:recipient
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 10
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				has_relation_best_friend = scope:recipient
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 10
			scope:recipient = {
				opinion = {
					target = scope:puppet_or_actor
					value >= 65
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = { # More likely to convert rulers that own a Holy Site
			add = 20
			pam_sway_recipient_holds_holy_site_trigger = yes
		}
		# modifier = { # More likely to happen during Instability
		# 	add = 15
		# 	scope:puppet_or_actor = { like_christianity_religion_trigger = yes } # Warcraft
		# 	situation:the_christian_church ?= {
		# 		has_situation_top_phase_parameter = the_christian_church_easier_sway_to_rite
		# 	}
		# }
		modifier = { # HoRs actually hustle
			add = 50
			scope:puppet_or_actor.rite.head_of_rite ?= scope:puppet_or_actor
		}
		modifier = { # AI restraint: stranger prelates are only valid targets while our dioceses touch
			add = -1000
			scope:recipient = {
				is_ruler = yes
				any_held_title = { has_clerical_region = yes }
				NOR = {
					superior ?= scope:puppet_or_actor
					is_liege_or_above_of = scope:puppet_or_actor
					capital_county.clerical_region_title.holder ?= scope:puppet_or_actor
				}
			}
			NAND = {
				scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
					pam_is_archbishop_or_higher_trigger = yes
					piety_level >= high_piety_level
					# A landless recipient has no capital county to measure the distance to
					exists = scope:recipient.capital_county
					capital_county ?= {
						squared_distance = {
							target = scope:recipient.capital_county
							value <= squared_distance_large
						}
					}
				}
				pam_rite_sway_adjacent_diocese_trigger = {
					ACTOR = scope:puppet_or_actor
					RECIPIENT = scope:recipient
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = { # Push the rite into the neighboring diocese once our own is turning
			add = 150
			scope:recipient = {
				any_held_title = { has_clerical_region = yes }
			}
			scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
				any_held_title = { has_clerical_region = yes }
			}
			pam_rite_sway_adjacent_diocese_trigger = {
				ACTOR = scope:puppet_or_actor
				RECIPIENT = scope:recipient
			}
		}
		modifier = { # More likely if the government is religious
			add = 50
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				government_is_religious_trigger = yes
			}
		}
		modifier = { # Heresiarchs are able to spread their rites easily
			add = 1000
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				OR = {
					has_trait = heresiarch
					has_trait = herald
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = { # More likely against close family members
			add = 50
			scope:recipient = {
				is_close_family_of = scope:puppet_or_actor
			}
		}
		modifier = { # Prefer higher tiers
			add = scope:recipient.highest_held_title_tier
		}
		modifier = { # Much less likely If no counties in my realm have my rite, unless I'm zealous
			add = -150
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				any_realm_county = {
					this.rite = scope:puppet_or_actor.rite
					count = 0
				}
				NOT = { has_trait = zealous }
			}
		}
		# modifier = { # Less likely for Mozarabic priests to try and convert all of Iberia's nobility
		# 	add = -40
		# 	scope:puppet_or_actor = {
		# 		is_clergy = yes
		# 		rite = rite:mozarabic_church
		# 	}
		# 	scope:recipient.rite = scope:recipient.faith.main_rite
		# }
	}
}

# interaction sent when reforming a faith
convert_on_reform_interaction = {
	category = interaction_category_religion
	special_interaction = reform_faith
	icon = icon_rite
	hidden = yes

	ai_maybe = no
	popup_on_receive = yes
	notification_text = sway_ruler_to_my_rite_interaction_notification

	desc = sway_ruler_to_my_rite_interaction_desc

	# Every adherent of the old faith receives this on reform, and anyone failing is_shown is skipped
	is_shown = {
		scope:actor.faith != scope:recipient.faith
	}

	cooldown_against_recipient = { years = 5 }

	on_accept = {
		scope:recipient = {
			set_character_rite = scope:actor.rite
		}
	}

	on_decline = {
		pam_sway_to_rite_on_decline_effect = yes
	}

	ai_accept = {
		base = 50

		celestial_hierarchy_acceptance_modifier = {
			ACTOR = scope:puppet_or_actor
			RECIPIENT = scope:recipient
		}

		religion_sway_to_rite_default_modifier = yes

		modifier = {
			desc = ASK_FOR_CONVERSION_REALM_REFORMATION_BONUS
			add = 500
			scope:recipient = {
				OR = {
					target_is_liege_or_above = scope:puppet_or_actor
					AND = {
						exists = superior
						superior = scope:puppet_or_actor
					}
				}
			}
		}

		modifier = {
			desc = ASK_FOR_CONVERSION_RECIPIENT_WILL_NOT_ACCEPT
			add = -1500
			scope:recipient = {
				has_character_flag = ai_will_not_convert
			}
		}
	}
}
