# Appoint Cardinal Interaction
appoint_cardinal_interaction = {
	category = interaction_category_religion
	icon = cardinal
	interface_priority = 45
	common_interaction = yes

	# The Head of Faith names cardinals across all of Christendom
	use_diplomatic_range = no
	ai_maybe = yes
	ai_min_reply_days = 1
	ai_max_reply_days = 5
	popup_on_receive = yes
	notification_text = appoint_cardinal_interaction_notification

	desc = appoint_cardinal_interaction_desc

	is_available = {
		has_pam_dlc_trigger = yes
	}

	is_shown = {
		scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
			this != scope:recipient
			faith ?= { has_doctrine_parameter = has_clerical_electors }
			faith = scope:recipient.faith
			pam_leads_college_of_cardinals_trigger = yes
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			# is_clergy alone misses PAM's landed archbishops, whose organization does not match the faith's
			pam_is_investiture_clergy_trigger = yes
			pam_can_hold_cardinalate_trigger = yes
			pam_can_receive_cardinalate_trigger = yes
			pam_pope_is_head_of_faith_or_rite_trigger = yes
			NOT = { exists = clerical_elector_title } # already a cardinal
		}
	}

	cooldown_against_recipient = { years = 10 }

	is_valid_showing_failures_only = {
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes }
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				piety >= medium_piety_value
			}
		}
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes }
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				treasury >= minor_treasury_value
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = appoint_cardinal_interaction_max_cardinals_tt
			scope:puppet_or_actor.faith ?= {
				NOT = { any_clerical_elector_title = { count >= 70 } }
			}
		}
		scope:puppet_or_actor = {
			is_available_quick = {
				adult = yes
				incapable = no
				imprisoned = no
				hostage = no
			}
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			is_available_quick = {
				adult = yes
				incapable = no
				imprisoned = no
				hostage = no
			}
			NOR = {
				is_at_war_with = scope:puppet_or_actor
				has_trait = excommunicated
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = pam_interaction_clerical_gender_tt
			scope:puppet_or_actor.rite = { rite_has_allowed_gender_for_clergy = scope:recipient }
		}
	}

	cost = {
		treasury = {
			value = scope:actor.minor_treasury_value
			if = {
				limit = { scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes }
				multiply = 0
			}
		}
	}

	auto_accept = {
		trigger_if = {
			limit = {
				scope:recipient = {
					is_ai = yes
				}
			}
			always =  yes
		}
	}

	on_accept = {
		if = {
			limit = { scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes }
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				if = {
					limit = { minor_treasury_value > 0 }
					remove_treasury = {
						value = minor_treasury_value
					}
				}
			}
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = appoint_cardinal_interaction_accept_tt
				hidden_effect = {
					grant_cardinal_title_effect = { CARDINAL_COUNTY = capital_county }
				}
			}
		}
		scope:puppet_or_actor = {
			show_as_tooltip = { add_piety = medium_piety_gain }
			hidden_effect = {
				send_interface_toast = {
					type = event_toast_effect_good
					title = appoint_cardinal_interaction_toast
					left_icon = scope:recipient
					right_icon = scope:cardinalate
					custom_tooltip = appoint_cardinal_interaction_accept_title_tt
					add_piety = medium_piety_value
				}
			}
			# if = {
			# 	limit = {
			# 		scope:recipient = { pam_is_monastic_order_member_trigger = yes }
			# 	}
			# 	if = {
			# 		limit = {
			# 			can_add_hook = {
			# 				target = scope:recipient
			# 				type = favor_hook
			# 			}
			# 		}
			# 		add_hook = {
			# 			type = favor_hook
			# 			target = scope:recipient
			# 		}
			# 	}
			# 	pam_the_christian_church_appointed_cardinal_catalyst_effect = {
			# 		POPE = scope:puppet_or_actor
			# 		CARDINAL = scope:recipient
			# 		OTHERWISE = catalyst_the_christian_church_appointed_monastic_cardinal
			# 	}
			# }
			# else = {
			# 	pam_the_christian_church_appointed_cardinal_catalyst_effect = {
			# 		POPE = scope:puppet_or_actor
			# 		CARDINAL = scope:recipient
			# 		OTHERWISE = catalyst_the_christian_church_appointed_cardinal
			# 	}
			# }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes
				scope:actor != scope:recipient
			}
			scope:actor = {
				send_interface_toast = {
					type = event_toast_effect_good
					title = appoint_cardinal_interaction_toast_puppeteer
					left_icon = scope:puppet_or_actor
					right_icon = scope:recipient
					custom_tooltip = appoint_cardinal_interaction_accept_title_tt
				}
			}
		}
	}

	on_decline = {
		scope:puppet_or_actor = {
			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_bad
				title = appoint_cardinal_interaction_decline_toast
				right_icon = scope:recipient
				scope:recipient = {
					if = {
						limit = { is_ai = yes }
						add_opinion = {
							target = scope:puppet_or_actor
							modifier = annoyed_opinion
							opinion = -10
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes
				scope:actor != scope:recipient
			}
			scope:actor = {
				send_interface_toast = {
					type = event_toast_effect_bad
					title = appoint_cardinal_interaction_decline_toast_puppeteer
					left_icon = scope:puppet_or_actor
					right_icon = scope:recipient
				}
			}
		}
	}

	ai_accept = {
		base = 100
	}
	
	ai_targets = {
		ai_recipients = family
		max = 5
	}
	
	ai_targets = {
		ai_recipients = vassals
		ai_recipients = clerical_region_rulers_in_realm
		ai_recipients = rulers_in_clerical_region
		ai_recipients = subordinates
		ai_recipients = scripted_relations
		ai_recipients = faith_holy_order_leaders
	}
	
	ai_targets = {
		ai_recipients = courtiers
		max = 5
	}
	
	ai_target_quick_trigger = {
		adult = yes
	}

	ai_frequency_by_tier = {
		barony = 0
		county = 0
		duchy = 24
		kingdom = 6
		empire = 6
		hegemony = 6
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		base = 20
		modifier = { #The pope definitely wants to keep family close
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				is_any_family_relation_or_spouse_trigger = { CHARACTER = scope:recipient }
			}
			add = 70
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 10
			scope:recipient = {
				has_trait = overseer
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 10
			scope:recipient = {
				has_trait = administrator
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			scope:recipient = {
				has_trait = zealous
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				has_trait = zealous
			}
		}
		modifier = { # A cynical Pope still builds a College, just a venal one
			add = -20
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				has_trait = cynical
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 20
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				is_allied_to = scope:recipient
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 1000
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				has_relation_friend = scope:recipient
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 1000
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				has_relation_lover = scope:recipient
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 10
			scope:recipient = {
				opinion = {
					target = scope:puppet_or_actor
					value >= 65
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = { # More likely to seek out bishops who own a Holy Site
			add = 20
			scope:puppet_or_actor.faith = {
				any_holy_site = {
					holy_site_county ?= {
						holder ?= {
							OR = {
								this = scope:recipient
								is_vassal_or_below_of = scope:recipient
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		# modifier = {
		# 	add = 150
		# 	desc = pam_the_christian_church_desire_more_cardinals_desc
		# 	scope:puppet_or_actor = { like_christianity_religion_trigger = yes } # Warcraft
		# 	situation:the_christian_church ?= {
		# 		has_situation_top_phase_parameter = the_christian_church_desire_more_cardinals
		# 	}
		# 	scope:puppet_or_actor.faith ?= {
		# 		any_clerical_elector_title = { count < 40 }
		# 	}
		# }
		modifier = { # HoR is a good candidate so that they're more controllable by the Pope.
			add = 50
			scope:recipient.rite.head_of_rite ?= scope:recipient
		}
		modifier = { # Traits in common
			add = 25
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				number_of_personality_traits_in_common = {
					target = scope:recipient
					value = 1
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = { # Traits in common
			add = 25
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				number_of_personality_traits_in_common = {
					target = scope:recipient
					value = 2
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = { # Traits in common
			add = 25
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				number_of_personality_traits_in_common = {
					target = scope:recipient
					value = 3
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = { # Same culture
			add = 40
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				has_same_culture_as = scope:recipient
			}
		}
		modifier = { # More likely to take this if we're running low on cardinals
			add = 100
			scope:puppet_or_actor.faith ?= {
				any_clerical_elector_title = { count < 20 }
			}
		}
		modifier = { # Much more likely to take this if we're running _very_ low
			add = 200
			scope:puppet_or_actor.faith ?= {
				any_clerical_elector_title = { count < 10 }
			}
		}
		modifier = { # Mitre and Crosier dynasty legacy
			add = 40
			scope:recipient = {
				dynasty ?= { has_dynasty_perk = pam_legacy_4 }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0
			scope:recipient = {
				has_relation_rival = scope:puppet_or_actor
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			scope:puppet_or_actor.treasury < 500
			scope:puppet_or_actor.faith ?= {
				any_clerical_elector_title = { count >= 30 }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			scope:puppet_or_actor.faith ?= {
				any_clerical_elector_title = { count >= 30 }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			scope:puppet_or_actor.faith ?= {
				any_clerical_elector_title = { count >= 40 }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0
			scope:recipient = {
				pam_ai_cardinal_candidate_is_immersive_trigger = no
			}
		}
		# Players request their courtiers' cardinalates themselves
		modifier = {
			factor = 0
			scope:recipient = {
				is_ruler = no
				employer ?= {
					is_ai = no
					this != scope:puppet_or_actor
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
# Request Court Chaplain Cardinalate Interaction
request_court_chaplain_cardinalate_interaction = {
	category = interaction_category_religion
	icon = cardinal
	interface_priority = 45
	common_interaction = yes

	ai_maybe = yes
	ai_min_reply_days = 1
	ai_max_reply_days = 5
	popup_on_receive = yes
	notification_text = request_court_chaplain_cardinalate_interaction_notification

	desc = request_court_chaplain_cardinalate_interaction_desc

	cooldown_against_recipient = { years = 10 }

	# Actor-only tests belong here, since is_available runs once per actor and is_shown per recipient
	is_available = {
		pam_can_hold_cardinalate_trigger = yes
		NOT = { religious_head_or_challenger ?= this }
		exists = cp:councillor_court_chaplain
		cp:councillor_court_chaplain = { pam_can_receive_cardinalate_trigger = yes }
		has_pam_dlc_trigger = yes
	}

	is_shown = {
		scope:actor != scope:recipient
		OR = {
			scope:actor.religious_head_or_challenger ?= scope:recipient
			AND = {
				NOT = { exists = scope:actor.faith.religious_head }
				scope:actor.faith = scope:recipient.faith
				scope:recipient = { pam_leads_college_of_cardinals_trigger = yes }
			}
		}
	}

	is_valid_showing_failures_only = {
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = appoint_cardinal_interaction_max_cardinals_tt
			scope:actor.faith ?= {
				NOT = { any_clerical_elector_title = { count >= 70 } }
			}
		}
		pam_not_antipope_when_recipient_is_hof_tt = { CHARACTER = scope:actor }
		scope:actor = {
			NOT = { has_trait = excommunicated }
			NOT = { is_at_war_with = scope:recipient }
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = actor_promised_pilgrimage_tt
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = promised_pilgrimage_to_clergy_modifier }
			}
			clergy_interaction_generic_piety_level_or_puppet_req_trigger = yes
			cp:councillor_court_chaplain ?= {
				is_available_quick = {
					adult = yes
					incapable = no
					imprisoned = no
					hostage = no
				}
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = request_court_chaplain_cardinalate_chaplain_not_excommunicated_tt
					NOT = { has_trait = excommunicated }
				}
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = request_court_chaplain_cardinalate_chaplain_not_cardinal_tt
					NOT = { exists = clerical_elector_title }
				}
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = request_court_chaplain_cardinalate_chaplain_follows_pope_tt
					pam_pope_is_head_of_faith_or_rite_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			is_available_quick = {
				adult = yes
				incapable = no
				imprisoned = no
				hostage = no
			}
		}
	}

	is_highlighted = {
		scope:actor.piety_level > scope:recipient.piety_level
	}

	cost = {
		piety = pam_request_court_chaplain_cardinalate_cost_value
	}

	#Use hook
	send_option = {
		is_valid = {
			scope:actor = {
				has_usable_hook = scope:recipient
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				NOT = { is_imprisoned_by = scope:actor }
			}
		}
		flag = hook
		localization = SCHEME_HOOK
	}
	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			pam_offer_pilgrimage_option_shown_trigger = { ACTOR = scope:actor }
		}
		is_valid = {
			pam_offer_pilgrimage_option_valid_trigger = { ACTOR = scope:actor }
		}
		flag = offer_pilgrimage
		localization = OFFER_PILGRIMAGE_INTERACTION_OPTION
	}
	should_use_extra_icon = {
		scope:actor = { has_usable_hook = scope:recipient }
	}
	extra_icon = "gfx/interface/icons/character_interactions/hook_icon.dds"

	send_options_exclusive = no

	auto_accept = {
		custom_description = {
			text = "spending_hook"
			subject = scope:actor
			object = scope:recipient
			scope:hook = yes
			scope:actor = { has_strong_hook = scope:recipient }
		}
	}

	on_accept = {
		scope:actor.cp:councillor_court_chaplain ?= {
			save_scope_as = new_cardinal
		}
		if = {
			limit = { exists = scope:new_cardinal }
			scope:actor = {
				send_interface_toast = {
					type = event_toast_effect_good
					title = request_court_chaplain_cardinalate_interaction_toast
					left_icon = scope:recipient
					right_icon = scope:new_cardinal
					custom_tooltip = {
						text = request_court_chaplain_cardinalate_interaction_accept_tt
						scope:new_cardinal = {
							grant_cardinal_title_effect = { CARDINAL_COUNTY = capital_county }
						}
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { scope:new_cardinal = { is_ai = no } }
				scope:recipient = { save_scope_as = head_of_faith }
				scope:new_cardinal = { pam_notify_player_became_cardinal_effect = yes }
			}
			# on_lose_position covers other title gains
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:actor = { NOT = { exists = puppet:external_theological_agent_puppet } }
				}
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = request_court_chaplain_cardinalate_becomes_clerical_puppet_tt
					scope:actor = { fire_councillor = scope:new_cardinal }
					scope:new_cardinal = {
						pam_make_clerical_ruler_puppet_effect = { PUPPETEER = scope:actor }
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = request_court_chaplain_cardinalate_leaves_council_tt
					scope:actor = { fire_councillor = scope:new_cardinal }
				}
			}
			scope:new_cardinal = {
				# The hat came from his liege's petition, and he knows whose it was
				add_opinion = {
					target = scope:actor
					modifier = grateful_opinion
					opinion = 50
				}
				# This is a promotion not a dismissal, so we hide the fired from council opinion or it cancels at once
				hidden_effect = {
					remove_opinion = {
						modifier = fired_from_council_opinion
						target = scope:actor
					}
				}
			}
		}
		pam_offer_pilgrimage_effect = {
			ACTOR = scope:actor
			CLERIC = scope:recipient
		}
	}

	on_decline = {
		scope:actor = {
			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_bad
				title = request_court_chaplain_cardinalate_interaction_decline_toast
				right_icon = scope:recipient
				if = {
					limit = { is_ai = yes }
					add_opinion = {
						target = scope:recipient
						modifier = annoyed_opinion
						opinion = -10
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	ai_targets = {
		ai_recipients = head_of_faith
	}

	# Only kings and above bother the Pope with this
	ai_frequency_by_tier = {
		barony = 0
		county = 0
		duchy = 0
		kingdom = 24
		empire = 24
		hegemony = 24
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		base = 30

		modifier = { # A kinsman in the College is a kinsman on the papal throne
			add = 60
			scope:actor.cp:councillor_court_chaplain ?= {
				is_any_family_relation_or_spouse_trigger = { CHARACTER = scope:actor }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 40
			scope:actor.cp:councillor_court_chaplain ?= {
				dynasty ?= scope:actor.dynasty
			}
		}
		modifier = { # No point elevating a chaplain we cannot steer
			add = 40
			scope:actor = {
				puppet:theological_agent_puppet ?= cp:councillor_court_chaplain
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			scope:actor = { has_trait = zealous }
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -60
			scope:actor = { has_trait = cynical }
		}
		modifier = { # A chaplain nobody respects will not be made a Cardinal
			add = -40
			scope:actor.cp:councillor_court_chaplain ?= {
				piety_level < 2
			}
		}
		modifier = { # No use spending the favour on a chaplain we are about to replace
			add = -50
			scope:actor = {
				exists = cp:councillor_court_chaplain
				opinion = {
					target = cp:councillor_court_chaplain
					value < 0
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0
			scope:actor.cp:councillor_court_chaplain ?= {
				pam_ai_cardinal_candidate_is_immersive_trigger = no
			}
		}
	}

	ai_accept = {
		base = 0

		# The petitioner's own standing with the Pope, shared with the other clerical title petitions
		clerical_petition_to_authority_acceptance_modifier = yes

		# Weak hook: a strong one already auto-accepts
		modifier = {
			scope:hook = yes
			add = 100
			scope:actor = { NOT = { has_strong_hook = scope:recipient } }
			desc = SCHEME_WEAK_HOOK_USED
		}

		modifier = {
			add = pam_offer_pilgrimage_acceptance_value
			scope:offer_pilgrimage = yes
			desc = OFFER_PILGRIMAGE_INTERACTION_OPTION_REASON
		}

		# The rest weighs the candidate rather than the petitioner
		opinion_modifier = {
			trigger = { exists = scope:actor.cp:councillor_court_chaplain }
			who = scope:recipient
			opinion_target = scope:actor.cp:councillor_court_chaplain
			desc = AI_OPINION_REASON
		}

		modifier = {
			add = -25
			exists = scope:actor.cp:councillor_court_chaplain
			scope:actor.cp:councillor_court_chaplain.rite != scope:recipient.rite
			desc = COURT_CHAPLAIN_DIFFERENT_RITE
		}

		modifier = {
			add = -50
			exists = scope:actor.cp:councillor_court_chaplain
			scope:recipient.rite = {
				NOT = { rite_has_allowed_gender_for_clergy = scope:actor.cp:councillor_court_chaplain }
			}
			desc = COURT_CHAPLAIN_WRONG_CLERICAL_GENDER
		}

		modifier = {
			add = {
				add = 10
				multiply = scope:actor.cp:councillor_court_chaplain.num_virtuous_traits
			}
			exists = scope:actor.cp:councillor_court_chaplain
			scope:actor.cp:councillor_court_chaplain.num_virtuous_traits > 0
			desc = COURT_CHAPLAIN_VIRTUOUS
		}

		modifier = {
			add = {
				add = -10
				multiply = scope:actor.cp:councillor_court_chaplain.num_sinful_traits
			}
			exists = scope:actor.cp:councillor_court_chaplain
			scope:actor.cp:councillor_court_chaplain.num_sinful_traits > 0
			desc = COURT_CHAPLAIN_SINFUL
		}

		modifier = {
			add = 10
			exists = scope:actor.cp:councillor_court_chaplain
			scope:actor.cp:councillor_court_chaplain = { has_education_learning_trigger = yes }
			desc = COURT_CHAPLAIN_LEARNING_EDUCATION_REASON
		}

		modifier = {
			add = 15
			exists = scope:actor.cp:councillor_court_chaplain
			scope:actor.cp:councillor_court_chaplain = { has_trait = celibate }
			desc = COURT_CHAPLAIN_CELIBATE
		}

		modifier = {
			add = 15
			exists = scope:actor.cp:councillor_court_chaplain
			scope:actor.cp:councillor_court_chaplain = { has_trait = devoted }
			desc = COURT_CHAPLAIN_DEVOTED
		}

		# A chaplain of no standing is not Cardinal material
		modifier = {
			add = -25
			exists = scope:actor.cp:councillor_court_chaplain
			scope:actor.cp:councillor_court_chaplain = { piety_level = 0 }
			desc = COURT_CHAPLAIN_LOW_PIETY
		}

		# A College short on Cardinals needs bodies more than it needs leverage
		modifier = {
			add = 60
			scope:recipient.faith ?= {
				any_clerical_elector_title = { count < 20 }
			}
			desc = COLLEGE_OF_CARDINALS_UNDERMANNED_REASON
		}

		modifier = {
			add = 100
			scope:recipient.faith ?= {
				any_clerical_elector_title = { count < 10 }
			}
			desc = COLLEGE_OF_CARDINALS_SEVERELY_UNDERMANNED_REASON
		}
	}
}
# Destroy Cardinalate Interaction
destroy_cardinalate_interaction = {
	category = interaction_category_religion
	icon = cardinal
	interface_priority = 45

	desc = destroy_cardinalate_interaction_desc
	filter_tags = { papabile }

	auto_accept = { always = yes }
	cooldown_against_recipient = { years = 10 }

	is_available = {
		has_pam_dlc_trigger = yes
	}

	is_shown = {
		scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
			this != scope:recipient
			rite ?= { rite_has_parameter = has_clerical_electors }
			pam_leads_college_of_cardinals_trigger = yes
			trigger_if = { # A shrinking College is a problem the AI should not create
				limit = {
					is_ai = yes
					NOT = { puppeteer ?= { is_ai = no } }
				}
				faith ?= {
					any_clerical_elector_title = { count > 30 }
				}
			}
		}
		exists = scope:recipient.clerical_elector_title
		scope:recipient = {
			is_ai = yes
			faith = scope:actor.faith
		}
	}

	is_valid_showing_failures_only = {
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = destroy_cardinalate_interaction_min_cardinals_tt
			scope:puppet_or_actor.faith ?= {
				any_clerical_elector_title = { count > 10 }
			}
		}
		scope:puppet_or_actor = {
			is_available_quick = {
				adult = yes
				incapable = no
				imprisoned = no
				hostage = no
			}
		}
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes }
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				piety >= medium_piety_value
			}
		}
	}

	cost = {
		piety = major_piety_value
	}

	on_accept = {
		scope:recipient.clerical_elector_title ?= { save_scope_as = cardinalate }
		scope:actor = {
			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_bad
				title = destroy_cardinalate_interaction_toast
				left_icon = scope:puppet_or_actor
				right_icon = scope:recipient
				custom_tooltip = destroy_cardinalate_interaction_accept_tt
				scope:recipient = {
					add_opinion = {
						target = scope:puppet_or_actor
						modifier = revoked_title
					}
					pam_destroy_cardinal_title_effect = { CARDINALATE = scope:cardinalate }
					faith = {
						every_clerical_elector_title = {
							custom = custom.every_clerical_elector_title
							limit = {
								holder != scope:recipient
							}
							holder = {
								add_opinion = {
									target = scope:puppet_or_actor
									modifier = removed_cardinalate
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				# situation:the_christian_church ?= {
				# 	situation_participant_group:christian_clerical_main_power_rulers = {
				# 		every_situation_group_participant = {
				# 			custom = custom.every_main_power_clerical_ruler
				# 			limit = {
				# 				NOR = {
				# 					this = scope:actor
				# 					this = scope:puppet_or_actor
				# 					this = scope:recipient
				# 					exists = clerical_elector_title
				# 				}
				# 			}
				# 			add_opinion = {
				# 				target = scope:puppet_or_actor
				# 				modifier = removed_cardinalate
				# 				opinion = -20
				# 			}
				# 		}
				# 	}
				# }
				# # Catalysts
				# if = {
				# 	limit = {
				# 		scope:puppet_or_actor = { like_christianity_religion_trigger = yes } # Warcraft
				# 		situation:the_christian_church ?= { situation_top_has_catalyst = catalyst_the_christian_church_stripped_cardinalate }
				# 	}
				# 	situation:the_christian_church = { trigger_situation_catalyst = catalyst_the_christian_church_stripped_cardinalate }
				# }
			}
		}
	}

	ai_targets = {
		ai_recipients = subordinates
	}

	ai_target_quick_trigger = {
		adult = yes
	}

	ai_frequency_by_tier = {
		barony = 0
		county = 0
		duchy = 0
		kingdom = 12
		empire = 12
		hegemony = 12
	}

	# Stripping a hat is a last resort, so nothing but a real grievance moves this off zero
	ai_will_do = {
		base = 0

		modifier = { # An excommunicate has no business choosing the next Head of Faith
			add = 100
			scope:recipient = { has_trait = excommunicated }
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				OR = {
					has_relation_rival = scope:recipient
					has_relation_nemesis = scope:recipient
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = { # Never against our own
			factor = 0
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				OR = {
					has_relation_friend = scope:recipient
					has_relation_best_friend = scope:recipient
					is_any_family_relation_or_spouse_trigger = { CHARACTER = scope:recipient }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
# Give Cardinal Order Interaction
give_cardinal_order_interaction = {
	category = interaction_category_religion
	icon = icon_scheme_challenge_status
	interface_priority = 60
	desc = give_cardinal_order_interaction_desc

	common_interaction = yes
	filter_tags = { papabile }

	use_diplomatic_range = {
		scope:puppet_or_actor = { pam_leads_college_of_cardinals_trigger = no }
	}
	ai_maybe = yes
	ai_min_reply_days = 1
	ai_max_reply_days = 5
	popup_on_receive = yes

	auto_accept = {
		scope:recipient = { is_ai = no }
	}
	cooldown_against_recipient = { years = 10 }
	
	is_available = {
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { is_ai = yes }
			is_adult = yes
			is_incapable = no

		}
	}

	is_shown = {
		has_pam_dlc_trigger = yes
		scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
			trigger_if = {
				limit = {
					scope:recipient.liege.puppet:theological_agent_puppet ?= scope:recipient
				}
			}
			trigger_else = {
				pam_leads_college_of_cardinals_trigger = yes
				#If they are a puppet of their liege the HoF cannot use this interaction
				NOT = {
					scope:recipient.liege.puppet:theological_agent_puppet ?= scope:recipient
				}
			}
			faith ?= scope:recipient.faith
		}
		#Check if they are a Cardinal
		exists = scope:recipient.clerical_elector_title
		scope:puppet_or_actor != scope:recipient
	}


	is_valid_showing_failures_only = {
		scope:puppet_or_actor = {
			NOR = {
				is_at_war_with = scope:recipient
				has_trait = incapable
				is_adult = no
			}
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			NOR = {
				is_adult = no
				has_trait = incapable
				has_trait = excommunicated
			}
		}
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes }
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				piety >= minor_piety_value
			}
		}
	}

	cost = {
		piety = {
			value = {
				add = minor_piety_value
			}
			if = {
				limit = { scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes }
				multiply = 0
			}
		}
	}

	send_option = { #Improve Development
		flag = cardinal_improve_development
		is_valid = {
			scope:recipient = {
				capital_county ?= {
					clerical_region_title ?= {
						custom_tooltip = {
							text = cardinal_order_improve_development_tt
							any_county_in_clerical_region = {
								faith = scope:recipient.faith
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	send_option = { #Convert Faith
		flag = cardinal_convert_faith
		is_valid = {
			scope:recipient = {
				capital_county ?= {
					clerical_region_title ?= {
						custom_tooltip = {
							text = cardinal_order_convert_faith_tt
							OR = {
								any_county_in_clerical_region = {
									pam_cardinal_order_convert_faith_in_region_county_trigger = { CARDINAL = scope:recipient }
								}
								any_county_in_clerical_region = {
									any_neighboring_county = {
										pam_cardinal_order_convert_faith_neighboring_county_trigger = { CARDINAL = scope:recipient }
									}
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	send_option = { #Increase Control
		flag = cardinal_increase_control
		is_valid = {
			scope:recipient = {
				capital_county ?= {
					clerical_region_title ?= {
						custom_tooltip = {
							text = cardinal_order_increase_control_tt
							any_county_in_clerical_region = {
								faith = scope:recipient.faith
								county_control < 100
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	send_option = { #Accelerate Cultural Fascination
		flag = cardinal_accelerate_cultural_fascination
		is_valid = {
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = cardinal_order_accelerate_cultural_fascination_tt
				scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
					culture.culture_head ?= {
						NOT = {
							has_character_modifier = pam_cardinal_order_accelerate_cultural_fascination_modifier
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	send_option = { #Improve Plague Resistance
		flag = cardinal_improve_epidemic_resistance
		is_valid = {
			scope:recipient = {
				capital_county ?= {
					clerical_region_title ?= {
						custom_tooltip = {
							text = cardinal_order_improve_epidemic_resistance_tt
							any_county_in_clerical_region = {
								faith = scope:recipient.faith
								NOT = {
									has_county_modifier = pam_cardinal_order_improve_epidemic_resistance_modifier
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	send_option = { #Improve Legitimacy
		flag = cardinal_improve_legitimacy
		is_valid = {
			scope:recipient = {
				capital_county ?= {
					clerical_region_title ?= {
						custom_tooltip = {
							text = cardinal_order_improve_legitimacy_tt
							any_county_in_clerical_region = {
								holder.top_liege = {
									has_legitimacy = yes
									NOT = {
										has_character_modifier = pam_cardinal_order_improve_legitimacy_modifier
									}
									faith = scope:recipient.faith
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	send_option = { #Undermine Legitimacy
		flag = cardinal_undermine_legitimacy
		is_valid = {
			scope:recipient = {
				capital_county ?= {
					clerical_region_title ?= {
						custom_tooltip = {
							text = cardinal_order_undermine_legitimacy_tt
							any_county_in_clerical_region = {
								holder.top_liege = {
									has_legitimacy = yes
									NOT = {
										has_character_modifier = pam_cardinal_order_undermine_legitimacy_modifier
									}
									faith = scope:recipient.faith
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	on_accept = {
		if = {
			limit = { scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes }
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				add_piety = { value = { add = minor_piety_value max = piety multiply = -1 } }
			}
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			if = {
				limit = { is_ai = yes }
				pam_choose_cardinal_order_switch_scripted_effect = yes
			}
			else = {
				trigger_event = { 
					id = pam_clergy_events.0010
					days = 10
				}
				show_as_tooltip = {
					pam_choose_cardinal_order_switch_scripted_effect = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:recipient = { is_ai = yes }
				scope:actor = { is_ai = no }
			}
			pam_cardinal_order_accept_notify_players_effect = {
				HOF = scope:puppet_or_actor
			}
		}
	}

	on_decline = {
		scope:puppet_or_actor = {
			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_bad
				title = give_cardinal_order_interaction_decline_toast
				left_icon = scope:puppet_or_actor
				right_icon = scope:recipient
				scope:recipient = {
					pam_cardinal_order_decline_effect = {
						HOF = scope:puppet_or_actor
						CARDINAL = scope:recipient
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes
				scope:actor != scope:recipient
			}
			scope:actor = {
				send_interface_toast = {
					type = event_toast_effect_bad
					title = give_cardinal_order_interaction_decline_toast_puppeteer
					left_icon = scope:puppet_or_actor
					right_icon = scope:recipient
				}
			}
		}
	}

	ai_accept = {
		base = 25
		opinion_modifier = {
			opinion_target = scope:puppet_or_actor
			who = scope:recipient
			multiplier = 1
			desc = AI_OPINION_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 50
			scope:puppet_or_actor.rite = scope:recipient.rite
			desc = I_HAVE_THE_SAME_RITE
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 200
			scope:recipient.liege.puppet:theological_agent_puppet ?= scope:recipient
			desc = IS_YOUR_PUPPET
		}
		# is_shown already has the College relationship, so this skips the any_subordinate walk
		modifier = {
			add = 20
			scope:puppet_or_actor = { has_realm_law_flag = puppet_acceptance_1 }
			desc = CHURCH_AUTHORITY_1_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			scope:puppet_or_actor = { has_realm_law_flag = puppet_acceptance_2 }
			desc = CHURCH_AUTHORITY_2_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 50
			scope:puppet_or_actor = { has_realm_law_flag = puppet_acceptance_3 }
			desc = CHURCH_AUTHORITY_3_REASON
		}
		# modifier = {
		# 	add = -20
		# 	situation:the_christian_church ?= {
		# 		has_situation_top_phase_parameter = era_type_crisis
		# 	}
		# 	desc = CHRISTIAN_CHURCH_PHASE_REASON
		# }
		# modifier = {
		# 	add = 20
		# 	situation:the_christian_church ?= {
		# 		has_situation_top_phase_parameter = era_type_stable
		# 	}
		# 	desc = CHRISTIAN_CHURCH_PHASE_REASON
		# }
	}
	ai_targets = {
		ai_recipients = subordinates
	}
	ai_frequency_by_tier = {
		barony = 0
		county = 0
		duchy = 0
		kingdom = 12
		empire = 1
		hegemony = 1
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		base = 50

		modifier = { # Traits in common
			add = 25
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				number_of_personality_traits_in_common = {
					target = scope:recipient
					value = 1
				}
			}
		}

		modifier = { # Traits in common
			add = 25
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				number_of_personality_traits_in_common = {
					target = scope:recipient
					value = 2
				}
			}
		}

		modifier = { # Traits in common
			add = 25
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				number_of_personality_traits_in_common = {
					target = scope:recipient
					value = 3
				}
			}
		}

		modifier = { # I like them
			add = 25
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				opinion = {
					value >= medium_positive_opinion
					target = scope:recipient
				}
			}
		}

		modifier = { # Same rite
			factor = 2
			scope:puppet_or_actor.rite = scope:recipient.rite
		}
	}
}

#Request Legitimacy from the Church
request_legitimacy_interaction = {
	category = interaction_category_religion
	icon = icon_clergy_request_legitimacy
	interface_priority = 100
	notification_text = request_legitimacy_interaction_notification
	greeting = positive
	common_interaction = yes

	use_diplomatic_range = yes
	ai_min_reply_days = 1
	ai_max_reply_days = 5
	popup_on_receive = yes

	desc = request_legitimacy_interaction_desc

	is_available = {
		has_pam_dlc_trigger = yes
	}

	is_shown = {
		scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
			has_legitimacy = yes
			#Has theocratic head of faith or theocratic clergy
			pam_clergy_interactions_faith_trigger = yes
			NOT = {
				government_has_flag = government_is_ecclesiastical
			}
			is_ruler = yes
		}
		scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
			has_legitimacy = yes
			NOT = {
				is_at_war_with = scope:recipient
			}
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			pam_request_legitimacy_priest_trigger = { CHARACTER = scope:puppet_or_actor }
			#AI should only bother player recipients if they're similar tier
			trigger_if = {
				limit = {
					scope:puppet_or_actor ?= { is_ai = yes }
					scope:recipient = { is_ai = no }
				}
				OR = {
					scope:puppet_or_actor ?= { highest_held_title_tier >= scope:recipient.highest_held_title_tier }
					scope:puppet_or_actor ?= { is_vassal_of = prev }
					is_allied_to = scope:puppet_or_actor
				}
			}
			NOT = { this = scope:actor }
		}
		pam_should_bother_clergy_interaction_trigger = { PRIEST = scope:recipient }
		# Those under an antipope ask them instead of the real Head of Faith
		trigger_if = {
			limit = {
				scope:recipient ?= scope:puppet_or_actor.faith.religious_head
			}
			scope:puppet_or_actor ?= { is_following_religious_head_challenger = no }
		}
	}

	is_highlighted = {
		scope:puppet_or_actor = {
			trigger_if = {
				limit = {
					highest_held_title_tier >= tier_duchy
				}
				legitimacy_level <= 1
			}
			trigger_else = {
				legitimacy_level = 0
			}
			is_character_interaction_potentially_accepted = {
				recipient = scope:recipient
				interaction = request_legitimacy_interaction
			}
		}
	}

	cooldown = { years = 10 }

	is_valid_showing_failures_only = {
		scope:puppet_or_actor = {
			clergy_interaction_generic_piety_level_or_puppet_req_trigger = yes
			pam_antipope_clerical_request_trigger = { RECIPIENT = scope:recipient }
		}
		scope:puppet_or_actor = {
			is_available_quick = {
				incapable = no
				imprisoned = no
				hostage = no
				activity = no
				travel = no
			}
			NOT = {
				has_trait = excommunicated
			}
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			is_available_quick = {
				incapable = no
				imprisoned = no
				hostage = no
				ruler = yes
				adult = yes
			}
			NOT = {
				has_trait = excommunicated
			}
			highest_held_title_tier >= tier_duchy
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = relevant_theocrat_tt
				OR = {
					scope:puppet_or_actor.religious_head_or_challenger ?= scope:recipient
					scope:puppet_or_actor = {
						any_liege_or_above = {
							this = scope:recipient
						}
					}
					any_held_title = {
						has_clerical_region = yes
						any_county_in_clerical_region = {
							OR = {
								this = scope:puppet_or_actor.primary_title
								scope:puppet_or_actor.primary_title = {
									is_de_jure_liege_or_above_target = prev
								}
							}
						}
					}
					AND = {
						is_landed = yes
						any_held_title = {
							OR = {
								#they have a title beneath actor's titles
								any_this_title_or_de_jure_above = {
									holder ?= scope:puppet_or_actor
								}
								#they have a title that presides over actor's titles
								any_in_de_jure_hierarchy = {
									holder ?= scope:puppet_or_actor
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = below_expected_legitimacy_tt
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				OR = {
					any_powerful_vassal = {
						is_ai = yes
						NOT = {
							scope:puppet_or_actor = {
								meets_legitimacy_expectation_of = prev
							}
						}
					}
					any_vassal = {
						is_ai = yes
						NOT = {
							scope:puppet_or_actor = {
								meets_legitimacy_expectation_of = prev
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		pam_not_promised_pilgrimage_trigger = { ACTOR = scope:puppet_or_actor }
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes }
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				piety >= pam_request_legitimacy_cost_value
			}
		}
	}

	on_send = {

	}

	on_accept = {
		if = {
			limit = { scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes }
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				if = {
					limit = { pam_request_legitimacy_cost_value > 0 }
					add_piety = {
						value = {
							add = pam_request_legitimacy_cost_value
							max = piety
							multiply = -1
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		scope:puppet_or_actor = {
			show_as_tooltip = {
				request_legitimacy_interaction_effect = yes
			}
			trigger_event = pam_interactions_events.0001
		}
	}

	on_decline = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:recipient = {
					is_ai = no
				}
			}
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				add_opinion = {
					modifier = refusal_opinion
					target = scope:recipient
					opinion = -25
				}
				show_as_tooltip = {
					add_legitimacy = medium_legitimacy_loss
				}
			}
		}
		scope:puppet_or_actor = {
			trigger_event = pam_interactions_events.0002
		}
	}

	cost = {
		piety = {
			value = pam_request_legitimacy_cost_value
			if = {
				limit = { scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes }
				multiply = 0
			}
		}
	}

	auto_accept = {
		# Using a hook should make him auto accept
		custom_description = {
			text = "spending_hook"
			subject = scope:puppet_or_actor
			object = scope:recipient
			scope:use_hook = yes
			scope:puppet_or_actor = { has_strong_hook = scope:recipient }
		}
	}

	ai_accept = {
		base = 0

		modifier = { # By God Alone - Secular Faith: the petitioner holds the ear of the clergy
			add = 25
			desc = pam_sf_ear_of_clergy_accept_reason
			trigger = {
				scope:puppet_or_actor = { has_character_modifier = pam_sf_ear_of_clergy_modifier }
			}
		}

		#BIG BONUS FROM NEEDING LEGITIMACY
		modifier = {
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				OR = {
					AND = {
						legitimacy_level = 0
						highest_held_title_tier >= tier_county
					}
					AND = {
						legitimacy_level = 1
						highest_held_title_tier >= tier_duchy
					}
					AND = {
						legitimacy_level = 2
						highest_held_title_tier >= tier_kingdom
					}
					AND = {
						legitimacy_level = 3
						highest_held_title_tier >= tier_empire
					}
				}
			}
			add = 20
			desc = LOW_LEGITIMACY_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				legitimacy_level >= 4
			}
			add = -40
			desc = HIGH_LEGITIMACY_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				legitimacy_level = 0
				highest_held_title_tier >= tier_kingdom
			}
			add = 20
			desc = VERY_LOW_LEGITIMACY_REASON
		}
		#Your gender is scorned by their faith
		modifier = {
			OR = {
				AND = {
					scope:puppet_or_actor = {
						is_female = yes
					}
					scope:recipient = {
						is_female = no
						rite = {
							rite_has_parameter = male_dominated_law
						}
					}
				}
				AND = {
					scope:puppet_or_actor = {
						is_male = yes
					}
					scope:recipient = {
						is_male = no
						rite = {
							rite_has_parameter = female_dominated_law
						}
					}
				}
			}
			add = -60
			desc = NON_DOMINANT_GENDER_REASON
		}

		# Weak hook: extra points
		modifier = {
			scope:use_hook = yes
			add = 200
			scope:puppet_or_actor = { NOT = { has_strong_hook = scope:recipient } }
			desc = SCHEME_WEAK_HOOK_USED
		}

		general_secular_to_ecclesiastic_acceptance_modifier = yes

		#Interaction options
		modifier = {
			add = {
				value = 0
				scope:puppet_or_actor = {
					every_patroned_holy_order = {
						limit = {
							leader = {
								government_has_flag = government_is_monastic_holy_order
							}
						}
						add = 15
					}
				}
				max = 100
			}
			scope:monastic_support = yes
			desc = MONASTIC_SUPPORT_INTERACTION_OPTION_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			add = {
				value = 0
				scope:puppet_or_actor = {
					every_held_title = {
						limit = {
							tier = tier_county
							NOT = {
								any_county_province = {
									is_occupied = yes
								}
							}
							development_level >= 1
						}
						add = 10
						max = 60
					}
				}
			}
			scope:tithe = yes
			desc = RAISE_A_TITHE_INTERACTION_OPTION_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			add = {
				value = 20
				multiply = scope:puppet_or_actor.highest_held_title_tier
			}
			scope:offer_hook = yes
			desc = OFFER_HOOK_INTERACTION_OPTION_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			add = {
				value = 25
				if = {
					limit = {
						pam_actor_rite_has_core_simony_trigger = yes
					}
					multiply = 5
				}
			}
			scope:donate_faith = yes
			scope:puppet_or_actor.major_gold_value < scope:recipient.minor_gold_value
			desc = DONATE_FAITH_INTERACTION_OPTION_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			add = {
				value = 50
				if = {
					limit = {
						pam_actor_rite_has_core_simony_trigger = yes
					}
					multiply = 5
				}
			}
			scope:donate_faith = yes
			scope:puppet_or_actor.major_gold_value < scope:recipient.major_gold_value
			scope:puppet_or_actor.major_gold_value >= scope:recipient.minor_gold_value
			desc = DONATE_FAITH_INTERACTION_OPTION_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			add = {
				value = 75
				if = {
					limit = {
						pam_actor_rite_has_core_simony_trigger = yes
					}
					multiply = 5
				}
			}
			scope:donate_faith = yes
			scope:puppet_or_actor.major_gold_value >= scope:recipient.major_gold_value
			scope:puppet_or_actor.major_gold_value < scope:recipient.monumental_gold_value
			desc = DONATE_FAITH_INTERACTION_OPTION_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			add = {
				value = 100
				if = {
					limit = {
						pam_actor_rite_has_core_simony_trigger = yes
					}
					multiply = 5
				}
			}
			scope:donate_faith = yes
			scope:puppet_or_actor.major_gold_value >= scope:recipient.monumental_gold_value
			desc = DONATE_FAITH_INTERACTION_OPTION_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			add = pam_offer_pilgrimage_acceptance_value
			scope:offer_pilgrimage = yes
			desc = OFFER_PILGRIMAGE_INTERACTION_OPTION_REASON
		}

		#Christian situation
		# modifier = {
		# 	situation:the_christian_church ?= {
		# 		any_participant_group = { # Both characters are involved in the situation
		# 			any_situation_group_participant = {
		# 				this = scope:puppet_or_actor
		# 			}
		# 			trigger_if = {
		# 				limit = { scope:recipient = { is_ruler = yes } }
		# 				any_situation_group_participant = {
		# 					this = scope:recipient
		# 				}
		# 			}
		# 		}
		# 		has_situation_top_phase_parameter = preach_legitimacy_acceptance_bonus
		# 	}
		# 	add = 50
		# 	desc = preach_legitimacy_acceptance_bonus_desc
		# }
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:recipient != scope:puppet_or_actor
		}
		is_valid = {
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				has_usable_hook = scope:recipient
			}
			trigger_if = {
				limit = {
					scope:recipient = {
						is_ai = no
					}
				}
				scope:puppet_or_actor = { is_ai = no }
			}
		}
		flag = use_hook
		localization = EDUCATION_HOOK
	}

	send_option = {
		is_valid = {
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = patroned_low_worldliness_holy_order_tt
				scope:puppet_or_actor = {
					any_patroned_holy_order = {
						leader = {
							government_has_flag = government_is_monastic_holy_order
						}
					}
				}
			}
			trigger_if = {
				limit = {
					scope:puppet_or_actor = {
						is_ai = yes
					}
				}
				scope:puppet_or_actor = {
					short_term_gold >= major_gold_value
				}
			}
		}
		flag = monastic_support
		localization = MONASTIC_SUPPORT_INTERACTION_OPTION
	}

	send_option = {
		is_valid = {
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = tithable_counties_tt
				scope:puppet_or_actor = {
					any_held_title = {
						tier = tier_county
						NOT = {
							any_county_province = {
								is_occupied = yes
							}
						}
						development_level >= 1
					}
				}
			}
			trigger_if = {
				limit = {
					scope:puppet_or_actor = {
						is_ai = yes
					}
				}
				scope:puppet_or_actor = {
					NOR = {
						ai_has_economical_boom_personality = yes
						ai_has_pious_builder_personality = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}
		flag = tithe
		localization = RAISE_A_TITHE_INTERACTION_OPTION
	}

	send_option = {
		is_valid = {
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				trigger_if = {
					limit = {
						scope:puppet_or_actor = {
							is_ai = yes
						}
					}
					short_term_gold >= massive_gold_value
				}
				trigger_else = {
					gold >= medium_gold_value
				}
			}
		}
		flag = donate_faith
		localization = DONATE_TO_THE_FAITH_INTERACTION_OPTION
	}

	send_option = {
		is_valid = {
			NOT = {
				scope:recipient = {
					has_usable_hook = scope:puppet_or_actor
				}
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				can_add_hook = {
					target = scope:puppet_or_actor
					type = favor_hook
				}
			}
			trigger_if = {
				limit = {
					scope:puppet_or_actor = { is_ai = yes }
				}
				scope:puppet_or_actor = { legitimacy_level < 1 }
			}
		}
		flag = offer_hook
		localization = OFFER_HOOK_INTERACTION_OPTION
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			pam_offer_pilgrimage_option_shown_trigger = { ACTOR = scope:puppet_or_actor }
		}
		is_valid = {
			pam_offer_pilgrimage_option_valid_trigger = { ACTOR = scope:puppet_or_actor }
		}
		flag = offer_pilgrimage
		localization = OFFER_PILGRIMAGE_INTERACTION_OPTION
	}

	should_use_extra_icon = {
		scope:puppet_or_actor = { has_usable_hook = scope:recipient }
	}

	extra_icon = "gfx/interface/icons/character_interactions/hook_icon.dds"

	send_options_exclusive = no

	ai_targets = {
		ai_recipients = head_of_faith
		ai_recipients = realm_priest_superior
		ai_recipients = realm_priest_top_superior
		ai_recipients = peer_vassals
		ai_recipients = vassals
		ai_recipients = councillors
		ai_recipients = liege
		ai_recipients = top_liege
	}

	ai_frequency_by_tier = {
		barony = 0
		county = 48
		duchy = 24
		kingdom = 12
		empire = 12
		hegemony = 12
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		base = 10
		#Low legitimacy is the only really good reason to do this
		modifier = {
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				legitimacy_level = 0
			}
			add = 100
		}
		modifier = {
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				legitimacy_level = 1
				highest_held_title_tier >= tier_duchy
			}
			add = 75
		}
		modifier = {
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				legitimacy_level = 2
				highest_held_title_tier >= tier_kingdom
			}
			add = 50
		}
		modifier = {
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				legitimacy_level = 3
				highest_held_title_tier >= tier_empire
			}
			add = 50
		}
		modifier = {
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				rite = {
					has_tenet_status = {
						tenet = tenet_simony
						status = prohibited
					}
				}
			}
			add = -50
		}
		#Angering other faith counties matters a tiny bit
		modifier = {
			add = {
				value = 0
				scope:puppet_or_actor = {
					every_held_title = {
						limit = {
							tier = tier_county
							NOT = {
								faith = scope:puppet_or_actor.faith
							}
							rite = {
								rite_hostility_level = {
									target = scope:puppet_or_actor.rite
									value >= faith_astray_level
								}
							}
						}
						add = -5
					}
				}
			}
		}
		#Don't want to anger different faith powerful vassals
		modifier = {
			add = {
				value = 0
				scope:puppet_or_actor = {
					every_powerful_vassal = {
						limit = {
							NOR = {
								faith = scope:puppet_or_actor.faith
								has_dread_level_towards = {
									target = scope:puppet_or_actor
									level >= 1
								}
							}
							rite = {
								rite_hostility_level = {
									target = scope:puppet_or_actor.rite
									value >= faith_astray_level
								}
							}
						}
						add = -20
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

#Strengthen my claim, powerful clergy guy
strengthen_claim_interaction = {
	category = interaction_category_religion
	icon = icon_clergy_request_pressed_claim
	interface_priority = 30
	target_type = title
	target_filter = claimed_titles
	target_source = puppet_or_actor
	notification_text = strengthen_claim_interaction_notification
	greeting = positive
	cooldown_against_recipient = { years = 10 }
	use_diplomatic_range = yes
	ai_min_reply_days = 1
	ai_max_reply_days = 5
	popup_on_receive = yes

	desc = strengthen_claim_interaction_desc

	is_available = {
		has_pam_dlc_trigger = yes
	}

	is_shown = {
		scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
			#Has theocratic head of faith or
			pam_clergy_interactions_faith_trigger = yes
			is_ruler = yes
			NOT = {
				government_has_flag = government_is_ecclesiastical
			}
			any_claim = {
				pressed = no
			}
		}
		scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
			has_legitimacy = yes
			NOT = {
				is_at_war_with = scope:recipient
			}
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			pam_request_legitimacy_priest_trigger = { CHARACTER = scope:puppet_or_actor }
			NOT = { this = scope:actor }
		}
		pam_should_bother_clergy_interaction_trigger = { PRIEST = scope:recipient }
		# Those under an antipope ask them instead of the real Head of Faith
		trigger_if = {
			limit = {
				scope:recipient ?= scope:puppet_or_actor.faith.religious_head
			}
			scope:puppet_or_actor ?= { is_following_religious_head_challenger = no }
		}
	}

	is_valid_showing_failures_only = {
		scope:puppet_or_actor = {
			clergy_interaction_generic_piety_level_or_puppet_req_trigger = yes
			pam_antipope_clerical_request_trigger = { RECIPIENT = scope:recipient }
			trigger_if = {
				limit = {
					highest_held_title_tier >= tier_kingdom
				}
				legitimacy_level >= 4
			}
			trigger_else = {
				legitimacy_level >= 3
			}
			is_available_quick = {
				incapable = no
				imprisoned = no
				hostage = no
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = above_expected_legitimacy_tt
				any_vassal = {
					count = all
					trigger_if = {
						limit = {
							is_ai = yes
						}
						scope:puppet_or_actor = {
							meets_legitimacy_expectation_of = prev
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			is_available_quick = {
				incapable = no
				imprisoned = no
				hostage = no
				ruler = yes
				adult = yes
			}
			NOT = {
				has_trait = excommunicated
			}
			highest_held_title_tier >= tier_duchy
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = relevant_theocrat_tt
				OR = {
					scope:puppet_or_actor.religious_head_or_challenger ?= scope:recipient
					scope:puppet_or_actor = {
						any_liege_or_above = {
							this = scope:recipient
						}
					}
					any_held_title = {
						has_clerical_region = yes
						any_county_in_clerical_region = {
							OR = {
								this = scope:puppet_or_actor.primary_title
								scope:puppet_or_actor.primary_title = {
									is_de_jure_liege_or_above_target = prev
								}
							}
						}
					}
					AND = {
						is_landed = yes
						any_held_title = {
							OR = {
								#they have a title beneath actor's titles
								any_this_title_or_de_jure_above = {
									holder ?= scope:puppet_or_actor
								}
								#they have a title that presides over actor's titles
								any_in_de_jure_hierarchy = {
									holder ?= scope:puppet_or_actor
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		pam_not_promised_pilgrimage_trigger = { ACTOR = scope:puppet_or_actor }
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes }
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				piety >= pam_strengthen_claim_cost_value
			}
		}
	}
	can_be_picked_title = {
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = must_have_weak_claim_tt
			scope:target ?= { save_temporary_scope_as = claimed_temp }
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				any_claim = {
					pressed = no
					this = scope:claimed_temp
				}
			}
		}

		trigger_if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					scope:puppet_or_actor.religious_head_or_challenger ?= scope:recipient
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = hof_high_claim_req_tt
				scope:target ?= {
					tier <= tier_duchy
				}
			}
		}
		#Try to avoid letting you steal the cleric's lands with this interaction... not simple though. I think this works
		trigger_if = {
			limit = {
				scope:puppet_or_actor = {
					NOT = { is_vassal_or_below_of = scope:recipient }
				}
			}
			# Is not held by recipient or a de jure title under recipient
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = claim_not_in_realm_of_recipient_tt
				scope:target = {
					NOR = {
						any_this_title_or_de_jure_above = {
							holder ?=  {
								OR = {
									this = scope:recipient
									any_liege_or_above = {
										this = scope:recipient
									}
								}
							}
						}
						#A title comparable to recipient, not above them
						AND = {
							tier <= scope:recipient.highest_held_title_tier
							any_de_jure_county = {
								holder ?= {
									OR = {
										this = scope:recipient
										any_liege_or_above = {
											this = scope:recipient
										}
									}
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		trigger_else = {
			# Is not held by recipient or a de jure title under recipient
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = claim_not_held_by_recipient_tt
				scope:target = {
					NOR = {
						holder ?= {
							this = scope:recipient
						}
						#A title comparable to recipient, not above them
						AND = {
							tier <= scope:recipient.highest_held_title_tier
							any_de_jure_county = {
								holder ?= {
									this = scope:recipient
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}

		#Is not for a title in recipient's realm

	}

	on_send = {

	}

	on_accept = {
		if = {
			limit = { scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes }
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				if = {
					limit = { pam_strengthen_claim_cost_value > 0 }
					add_piety = {
						value = {
							add = pam_strengthen_claim_cost_value
							max = piety
							multiply = -1
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		scope:puppet_or_actor = {
			show_as_tooltip = {
				strengthen_claim_interaction_effect = yes
			}
			trigger_event = pam_interactions_events.0010
		}
	}

	on_decline = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:recipient = {
					is_ai = no
				}
			}
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				add_opinion = {
					modifier = refusal_opinion
					target = scope:recipient
					opinion = {
						value = -15
						multiply = {
							value = scope:target.tier
							subtract = 1
						}
					}
				}
			}
			scope:target.holder ?= {
				add_opinion = {
					target = scope:recipient
					modifier = grateful_opinion
					opinion = {
						value = 10
						multiply = {
							value = scope:target.tier
							subtract = 1
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		scope:puppet_or_actor = {
			trigger_event = pam_interactions_events.0011
		}
	}

	cost = {
		piety = {
			value = pam_strengthen_claim_cost_value
			if = {
				limit = { scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes }
				multiply = 0
			}
		}
	}

	auto_accept = {
		# Using a hook should make him auto accept
		custom_description = {
			text = "spending_hook"
			subject = scope:puppet_or_actor
			object = scope:recipient
			scope:use_hook = yes
			scope:puppet_or_actor = { has_strong_hook = scope:recipient }
		}
	}

	ai_accept = {
		base = 0

		modifier = { # By God Alone - Secular Faith: the petitioner holds the ear of the clergy
			add = 25
			desc = pam_sf_ear_of_clergy_accept_reason
			trigger = {
				scope:puppet_or_actor = { has_character_modifier = pam_sf_ear_of_clergy_modifier }
			}
		}

		modifier = {
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				legitimacy_level >= 5
			}
			add = 20
			desc = HIGH_LEGITIMACY_REASON
		}
		#Your gender is scorned by their faith
		modifier = {
			OR = {
				AND = {
					scope:puppet_or_actor = {
						is_female = yes
					}
					scope:recipient = {
						is_female = no
						rite = {
							rite_has_parameter = male_dominated_law
						}
					}
				}
				AND = {
					scope:puppet_or_actor = {
						is_male = yes
					}
					scope:recipient = {
						is_male = no
						rite = {
							rite_has_parameter = female_dominated_law
						}
					}
				}
			}
			add = -20
			desc = NON_DOMINANT_GENDER_REASON
		}
		# Weak hook: extra points
		modifier = {
			scope:use_hook = yes
			add = 200
			scope:puppet_or_actor = { NOT = { has_strong_hook = scope:recipient } }
			desc = SCHEME_WEAK_HOOK_USED
		}

		#Acceptance modifiers for all these ask the clergy for stuff interactions
		general_secular_to_ecclesiastic_acceptance_modifier = yes

		#Acceptance modifiers related to clergy's relation to target title and its possible holders
		title_acquisition_clerical_acceptance_modifier = yes

		#Interaction options
		modifier = {
			add = {
				value = 20
				multiply = scope:puppet_or_actor.highest_held_title_tier
			}
			scope:offer_hook = yes
			desc = OFFER_HOOK_INTERACTION_OPTION_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			add = {
				value = 25
				if = {
					limit = {
						pam_actor_rite_has_core_simony_trigger = yes
					}
					multiply = 5
				}
			}
			scope:donate_faith = yes
			scope:puppet_or_actor.medium_gold_value < scope:recipient.miniscule_gold_value
			desc = DONATE_FAITH_INTERACTION_OPTION_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			add = {
				value = 50
				if = {
					limit = {
						pam_actor_rite_has_core_simony_trigger = yes
					}
					multiply = 5
				}
			}
			scope:donate_faith = yes
			scope:puppet_or_actor.medium_gold_value < scope:recipient.medium_gold_value
			scope:puppet_or_actor.medium_gold_value >= scope:recipient.miniscule_gold_value
			desc = DONATE_FAITH_INTERACTION_OPTION_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			add = {
				value = 75
				if = {
					limit = {
						pam_actor_rite_has_core_simony_trigger = yes
					}
					multiply = 5
				}
			}
			scope:donate_faith = yes
			scope:puppet_or_actor.medium_gold_value >= scope:recipient.medium_gold_value
			scope:puppet_or_actor.medium_gold_value < scope:recipient.massive_gold_value
			desc = DONATE_FAITH_INTERACTION_OPTION_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			add = {
				value = 100
				if = {
					limit = {
						pam_actor_rite_has_core_simony_trigger = yes
					}
					multiply = 5
				}
			}
			scope:donate_faith = yes
			scope:puppet_or_actor.medium_gold_value >= scope:recipient.massive_gold_value
			desc = DONATE_FAITH_INTERACTION_OPTION_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			add = {
				value = 10
				if = {
					limit = {
						scope:puppet_or_actor.highest_held_title_tier > scope:recipient.highest_held_title_tier
					}
					multiply = {
						value = scope:puppet_or_actor.highest_held_title_tier
						subtract = scope:recipient.highest_held_title_tier
						add = 1
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						scope:puppet_or_actor.highest_held_title_tier < scope:recipient.highest_held_title_tier_minus_one
					}
					multiply = 0.25
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						scope:puppet_or_actor.highest_held_title_tier < scope:recipient.highest_held_title_tier
					}
					multiply = 0.5
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							is_liege_or_above_of = scope:recipient
							liege ?= scope:recipient.liege
							capital_county.holder ?= {
								OR = {
									this = scope:puppet_or_actor
									any_liege_or_above = {
										this = scope:puppet_or_actor
									}
								}
							}
						}
					}
					multiply = 3
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						scope:recipient = {
							OR = {
								any_neighboring_top_liege_realm_owner = {
									this = scope:puppet_or_actor
								}
								capital_county = {
									any_neighboring_county = {
										OR = {
											holder = scope:puppet_or_actor
											holder = {
												any_liege_or_above = {
													this = scope:puppet_or_actor
												}
											}
										}
									}
								}
							}
						}
					}
					multiply = 2
				}
			}
			scope:threaten_priest = yes
			desc = THREATEN_PRIEST_INTERACTION_OPTION_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			add = pam_offer_pilgrimage_acceptance_value
			scope:offer_pilgrimage = yes
			desc = OFFER_PILGRIMAGE_INTERACTION_OPTION_REASON
		}


		#Cleric hesitating because of comparison to target's holder
		modifier = {
			exists = scope:target.holder
			scope:recipient = { save_temporary_scope_as = cleric }
			scope:target.holder = { save_temporary_scope_as = owner }
			clerical_hesitation_from_target_holder_value != 0
			desc = RECIPIENTS_ESTIMATION_OF_TARGET_HOLDER_PIETY_REASON
			add = {
				value = clerical_hesitation_from_target_holder_value
				multiply = -20
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			scope:recipient = { save_temporary_scope_as = cleric }
			exists = scope:target.holder
			scope:target.holder = { save_temporary_scope_as = owner }
			scope:target.holder = { is_independent_ruler = no }
			scope:puppet_or_actor = { is_independent_ruler = no }
			scope:target.holder.top_liege ?= scope:puppet_or_actor.top_liege
			NOR = {
				scope:puppet_or_actor.liege = scope:target.holder.liege
				scope:target.holder.liege = scope:puppet_or_actor
				scope:puppet_or_actor.top_liege = scope:target.holder
			}
			clerical_hesitation_from_potential_defender_value != 0
			desc = RECIPIENTS_ESTIMATION_OF_TARGET_LIEGE_PIETY_REASON
			add = {
				value = clerical_hesitation_from_potential_defender_value
				multiply = -20
			}

		}
		modifier = {
			exists = scope:target.holder
			scope:recipient = { save_temporary_scope_as = cleric }
			scope:target.holder = { save_temporary_scope_as = owner }
			scope:target.holder = {
				is_independent_ruler = no
				NOT = {
					top_liege = scope:puppet_or_actor.top_liege
				}
			}
			clerical_hesitation_from_potential_defender_value != 0
			desc = RECIPIENTS_ESTIMATION_OF_TARGET_TOP_LIEGE_PIETY_REASON
			add = {
				value = clerical_hesitation_from_potential_defender_value
				multiply = -20
			}

		}
		modifier = {
			scope:recipient = { save_temporary_scope_as = cleric }
			trigger_if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:target.holder
				}
				scope:target.holder = {
					save_temporary_scope_as = owner
				}
			}
			scope:target = {
				de_jure_liege ?= {
					exists = holder
					NOR = {
						holder = scope:recipient
						holder = scope:puppet_or_actor
						holder = scope:target.holder
					}
				}
			}
			clerical_hesitation_from_potential_defender_value != 0
			desc = RECIPIENTS_ESTIMATION_OF_TARGET_DE_JURE_LIEGE_PIETY_REASON
			add = {
				value = clerical_hesitation_from_potential_defender_value
				multiply = -20
			}
		}

		# #Christian situation
		# modifier = {
		# 	situation:the_christian_church ?= {
		# 		any_participant_group = { # Both characters are involved in the situation
		# 			any_situation_group_participant = {
		# 				this = scope:puppet_or_actor
		# 			}
		# 			trigger_if = {
		# 				limit = { scope:recipient = { is_ruler = yes } }
		# 				any_situation_group_participant = {
		# 					this = scope:recipient
		# 				}
		# 			}
		# 		}
		# 		has_situation_top_phase_parameter = claim_seeking_acceptance_bonus
		# 	}
		# 	add = 50
		# 	desc = claim_seeking_acceptance_bonus_desc
		# }
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:recipient != scope:puppet_or_actor
		}
		is_valid = {
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				has_usable_hook = scope:recipient
			}
			trigger_if = {
				limit = {
					scope:secondary_recipient ?= {
						is_ai = no
					}
				}
				scope:actor = { is_ai = no }
			}
		}
		flag = use_hook
		localization = EDUCATION_HOOK
	}

	send_option = {
		is_valid = {
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				trigger_if = {
					limit = {
						scope:actor = {
							is_ai = yes
						}
					}
					short_term_gold >= massive_gold_value
				}
				trigger_else = {
					gold >= major_gold_value
				}
			}
		}
		flag = donate_faith
		localization = DONATE_TO_THE_FAITH_INTERACTION_OPTION
	}

	send_option = {
		is_valid = {
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				dread >= medium_dread_value
			}
			scope:actor = {
				is_ai = no
			}
		}
		flag = threaten_priest
		localization = THREATEN_PRIEST_INTERACTION_OPTION
	}

	send_option = {
		is_valid = {
			NOT = {
				scope:recipient = {
					has_usable_hook = scope:puppet_or_actor
				}
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				can_add_hook = {
					target = scope:puppet_or_actor
					type = favor_hook
				}
			}
			trigger_if = {
				limit = {
					scope:actor = { is_ai = yes }
				}
				always = no
			}
		}
		flag = offer_hook
		localization = OFFER_HOOK_INTERACTION_OPTION
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			pam_offer_pilgrimage_option_shown_trigger = { ACTOR = scope:puppet_or_actor }
		}
		is_valid = {
			pam_offer_pilgrimage_option_valid_trigger = { ACTOR = scope:puppet_or_actor }
		}
		flag = offer_pilgrimage
		localization = OFFER_PILGRIMAGE_INTERACTION_OPTION
	}

	should_use_extra_icon = {
		scope:puppet_or_actor = { has_usable_hook = scope:recipient }
	}

	extra_icon = "gfx/interface/icons/character_interactions/hook_icon.dds"

	send_options_exclusive = no

	ai_targets = {
		ai_recipients = head_of_faith
		ai_recipients = realm_priest_superior
		ai_recipients = realm_priest_top_superior
		ai_recipients = peer_vassals
		ai_recipients = vassals
		ai_recipients = councillors
		ai_recipients = liege
		ai_recipients = top_liege
	}

	ai_frequency_by_tier = {
		barony = 0
		county = 48
		duchy = 36
		kingdom = 36
		empire = 36
		hegemony = 36
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		base = 10
		#Wants titles that should rightfully be yours
		modifier = {
			scope:target ?= {
				de_jure_liege ?= {
					holder ?= scope:puppet_or_actor
				}
			}
			add = 30
		}
		modifier = {
			scope:target ?= {
				tier >= scope:puppet_or_actor.highest_held_title_tier
			}
			add = 50
		}
		modifier = {
			scope:recipient = { save_temporary_scope_as = cleric }
			scope:target.holder ?= { save_temporary_scope_as = owner }
			clerical_hesitation_from_target_holder_value != 0
			add = {
				value = clerical_hesitation_from_target_holder_value
				multiply = 10
			}
		}
		#low tier titles are less tantalizing
		modifier = {
			scope:target ?= {
				tier < scope:puppet_or_actor.highest_held_title_tier_minus_one
			}
			factor = 0.5
		}
		modifier = {
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				highest_held_title_tier = tier_county
			}
			scope:target ?= {
				title_capital_county = {
					squared_distance = {
						target = scope:puppet_or_actor.capital_province
						value >= squared_distance_major
					}
				}
			}
			factor = 0.25
		}
		modifier = {
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				highest_held_title_tier = tier_duchy
			}
			scope:target ?= {
				title_capital_county = {
					squared_distance = {
						target = scope:puppet_or_actor.capital_province
						value >= squared_distance_large
					}
				}
			}
			factor = 0.25
		}
		modifier = {
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				highest_held_title_tier = tier_kingdom
			}
			scope:target ?= {
				title_capital_county = {
					squared_distance = {
						target = scope:puppet_or_actor.capital_province
						value >= squared_distance_huge
					}
				}
			}
			factor = 0.25
		}
		modifier = {
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				highest_held_title_tier >= tier_empire
			}
			scope:target ?= {
				title_capital_county = {
					squared_distance = {
						target = scope:puppet_or_actor.capital_province
						value >= squared_distance_massive
					}
				}
			}
			factor = 0.25
		}
	}
}

#Sell a fabricated diocese claim to a secular ruler
sell_claim_interaction = {
	category = interaction_category_diplomacy
	icon = sell_claim_interaction
	interface_priority = 35
	target_type = title
	target_filter = counties_of_faith
	target_source = puppet_or_actor
	show_all_targets = no
	notification_text = sell_claim_interaction_notification
	greeting = positive
	
	cooldown_against_recipient = { years = 3 }

	use_diplomatic_range = yes
	ai_min_reply_days = 1
	ai_max_reply_days = 5
	popup_on_receive = yes

	desc = sell_claim_interaction_desc

	cost = {
		piety = {
			value = pam_sell_claim_piety_cost_value
			if = {
				limit = { scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes }
				multiply = 0
			}
		}
	}

	ai_frequency_by_tier = {
		barony = 0
		county = 0
		duchy = 144
		kingdom = 24
		empire = 24
		hegemony = 24
	}

	is_available = {
		has_pam_dlc_trigger = yes
	}

	is_shown = {
		scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
			government_has_flag = government_is_theocracy
			#faith ?= { has_doctrine_parameter = has_clerical_regions }
			OR = {
				any_held_title = { has_clerical_region = yes }
				faith.religious_head ?= this
			}
		}
		scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
			NOT = { is_at_war_with = scope:recipient }
			this != scope:recipient
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			is_ruler = yes
			NOT = { government_has_flag = government_is_theocracy }
			faith ?= scope:puppet_or_actor.faith
			highest_held_title_tier >= tier_county
		}
	}

	is_valid_showing_failures_only = {
		# cost = { piety } is only charged to scope:actor, so a puppeteer's cleric is checked here instead
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes }
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				piety >= pam_sell_claim_piety_cost_value
			}
		}
		scope:puppet_or_actor = {
			is_available_quick = {
				incapable = no
				imprisoned = no
				hostage = no
				ruler = yes
				adult = yes
			}
			NOT = { has_trait = excommunicated }
			trigger_if = {
				limit = {
					can_add_hook = {
						target = scope:recipient
						type = favor_hook
					}
				}
				scope:recipient = {
					gold >= minor_gold_value
				}
			}
			trigger_else = {
				scope:recipient = {
					gold >= pam_sell_claim_gold_cost_value
				}
			}
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			is_available_quick = {
				incapable = no
				imprisoned = no
				hostage = no
				ruler = yes
				adult = yes
			}
			NOT = { has_trait = excommunicated }
		}
	}

	can_be_picked_title = {
		pam_can_sell_claim_on_target_trigger = yes

		trigger_if = {
			limit = { scope:sell_claim_gold_and_hook = no }
			scope:recipient = {
				gold >= pam_sell_claim_gold_cost_value
			}
		}
	}

	# can_be_picked_title is picker-only and is not rechecked on send
	has_valid_target_showing_failures_only = {
		pam_can_sell_claim_on_target_trigger = yes
	}

	on_accept = {
		scope:puppet_or_actor = {
			sell_claim_interaction_effect = yes
		}
	}

	on_decline = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:recipient = {
					is_ai = no
				}
			}
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				add_opinion = {
					modifier = refusal_opinion
					target = scope:recipient
					opinion = -10
				}
			}
		}
	}

	options_heading = sell_claim_payment_heading
	send_options_exclusive = yes

	send_option = {
		flag = sell_claim_gold
		localization = SELL_CLAIM_GOLD_OPTION
		is_valid = {
			scope:recipient = {
				gold >= pam_sell_claim_gold_cost_value
			}
		}
	}

	send_option = {
		flag = sell_claim_gold_and_hook
		localization = SELL_CLAIM_GOLD_AND_HOOK_OPTION
		is_valid = {
			scope:actor = {
				can_add_hook = {
					target = scope:recipient
					type = favor_hook
				}
			}
		}
	}

	ai_accept = {
		base = 10

		opinion_modifier = { # Opinion Factor
			who = scope:recipient
			opinion_target = scope:puppet_or_actor
			multiplier = 0.5
			desc = AI_OPINION_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			scope:recipient = {
				ai_greed >= medium_positive_ai_value
			}
			desc = SELL_CLAIM_GREED_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -50
			scope:sell_claim_gold = yes
			scope:recipient = {
				gold < pam_sell_claim_gold_cost_value
			}
			desc = SELL_CLAIM_CANNOT_AFFORD_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 20
			scope:sell_claim_gold_and_hook = yes
			scope:recipient = {
				ai_greed >= low_positive_ai_value
			}
			desc = SELL_CLAIM_CHEAPER_OPTION_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 15
			scope:recipient = {
				any_neighboring_top_liege_realm_owner = {
					this = scope:puppet_or_actor
				}
			}
			scope:target ?= {
				any_neighboring_county = {
					holder.top_liege ?= scope:recipient
				}
			}
			desc = SELL_CLAIM_NEIGHBORING_TITLE_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 20
			is_in_same_clerical_region_as_trigger = { TARGET = scope:puppet_or_actor }
			desc = SELL_CLAIM_SAME_CLERICAL_REGION_REASON
		}
		modifier = { # Friends
			add = 15
			scope:recipient = {
				has_relation_friend = scope:puppet_or_actor
			}
			desc = WE_ARE_FRIENDS
		}
		modifier = { # BFFs
			add = 25
			scope:recipient = {
				has_relation_best_friend = scope:puppet_or_actor
			}
			desc = WE_ARE_BEST_FRIENDS
		}
		modifier = { # Their rite supports this
			add = 20
			scope:recipient = {
				rite = { rite_has_tenet = tenet_simony }
			}
			desc = SIMONY_RITE_REASON
		}
		modifier = { # Your or their rite condemns it
			add = -50
			OR = {
				scope:actor = {
					rite = {
						has_tenet_status = {
							tenet = tenet_simony
							status = prohibited
						}
					}
				}
				scope:recipient = {
					rite = {
						has_tenet_status = {
							tenet = tenet_simony
							status = prohibited
						}
					}
				}
			}
			desc = SIMONY_IS_PROHIBITED
		}
		modifier = {
			add = {
				add = -5
				multiply = scope:recipient.piety_level
			}
			scope:recipient.piety_level > 1
			desc = RECIPIENT_IS_PIOUS
		}
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		base = 10

		modifier = {
			add = scope:puppet_or_actor.ai_greed
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 25
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				debt_level > 0
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -50
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				has_trait = zealous
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 25
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				has_trait = cynical
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -50
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				rite = {
					has_tenet_status = {
						tenet = tenet_simony
						status = prohibited
					}
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 20
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				OR = {
					has_relation_friend = scope:recipient
					has_relation_best_friend = scope:recipient
					opinion = {
						target = scope:recipient
						value >= high_positive_opinion
					}
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -60
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				OR = {
					has_relation_rival = scope:recipient
					has_relation_nemesis = scope:recipient
					opinion = {
						target = scope:recipient
						value <= medium_negative_opinion
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	ai_targets = {
		ai_recipients = rulers_in_clerical_region
	}

	ai_target_quick_trigger = {
		adult = yes
	}
}

#Hand one of my own claims to a ruler under my spiritual care
gift_claim_interaction = {
	category = interaction_category_diplomacy
	icon = gift_claim_interaction
	common_interaction = no
	interface_priority = 34
	target_type = title
	target_filter = claimed_titles
	target_source = puppet_or_actor
	notification_text = gift_claim_interaction_notification
	greeting = positive

	cooldown_against_recipient = { years = 3 }

	use_diplomatic_range = yes
	ai_min_reply_days = 1
	ai_max_reply_days = 5
	popup_on_receive = yes

	desc = gift_claim_interaction_desc

	cost = {
		piety = medium_piety_value
	}

	ai_frequency_by_tier = {
		barony = 0
		county = 48
		duchy = 48
		kingdom = 24
		empire = 24
		hegemony = 24
	}

	is_available = {
		has_pam_dlc_trigger = yes
	}

	is_shown = {
		scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
			government_has_flag = government_is_theocracy
			#faith ?= { has_doctrine_parameter = has_clerical_regions }
			OR = {
				any_held_title = { has_clerical_region = yes }
				faith.religious_head ?= this
			}
			is_adult = yes
			is_ruler = yes
		}
		scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
			NOT = { is_at_war_with = scope:recipient }
			this != scope:recipient
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			is_ruler = yes
			faith ?= scope:puppet_or_actor.faith
			highest_held_title_tier >= tier_county
		}
	}

	is_valid_showing_failures_only = {
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = gift_claim_must_have_valid_claim_tt
			scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
				any_claim = {
					save_temporary_scope_as = gift_claim_candidate_title
					pam_gift_claim_valid_title_trigger = { TITLE = scope:gift_claim_candidate_title }
				}
			}
		}
		scope:puppet_or_actor = {
			is_available_quick = {
				incapable = no
				imprisoned = no
				hostage = no
				ruler = yes
				adult = yes
			}
			NOT = { has_trait = excommunicated }
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			is_available_quick = {
				incapable = no
				imprisoned = no
				hostage = no
				ruler = yes
				adult = yes
			}
			NOT = { has_trait = excommunicated }
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = gift_claim_recipient_must_be_in_clerical_region_tt
			scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
				OR = {
					pam_gift_claim_recipient_in_own_clerical_region_trigger = { TARGET = scope:recipient }
					faith.religious_head ?= this
				}
			}
		}
	}

	can_be_picked_title = {
		scope:target ?= { tier = tier_county }
	}

	has_valid_target_showing_failures_only = {
		pam_can_gift_claim_on_target_trigger = yes
	}

	on_accept = {
		scope:puppet_or_actor = {
			gift_claim_interaction_effect = yes
		}
	}

	ai_accept = {
		base = 100
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		base = 0

		modifier = {
			add = 25
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				OR = {
					has_relation_friend = scope:recipient
					has_relation_best_friend = scope:recipient
					opinion = {
						target = scope:recipient
						value >= high_positive_opinion
					}
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 25
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				has_trait = generous
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 15
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				has_trait = humble
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -60
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				OR = {
					has_relation_rival = scope:recipient
					has_relation_nemesis = scope:recipient
					opinion = {
						target = scope:recipient
						value <= medium_negative_opinion
					}
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0
			NOT = {
				scope:target = {
					is_neighbor_to_realm = scope:recipient
				}
			}
		}
	}

	ai_targets = {
		ai_recipients = rulers_in_clerical_region
	}

	ai_target_quick_trigger = {
		adult = yes
	}
}
