#Sponsor a same-faith ruler's kingdom holy war against a hostile-faith ruler
ecclesiastic_incite_holy_war_interaction = {
	category = interaction_category_diplomacy
	icon = minor_religious_war
	interface_priority = 75
	common_interaction = yes
	target_type = title
	target_filter = dejure_titles
	target_source = recipient
	notification_text = ecclesiastic_incite_holy_war_interaction_notification
	greeting = negative
	use_diplomatic_range = yes
	ai_min_reply_days = 1
	ai_max_reply_days = 5
	popup_on_receive = no
	cooldown_against_recipient = { years = 5 }
	use_diplomatic_range = no

	is_available = {
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { is_ai = yes }
			piety >= 750
			piety_level >= 3
			OR = {
				government_has_flag = government_is_ecclesiastical
				this = faith.religious_head
			}
			is_at_war = no
			like_christianity_religion_trigger = yes # Warcraft
		}
	}

	is_shown = {
		scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
			actor_incite_holy_war_shown_trigger = yes
			save_temporary_scope_as = attacker
		}
		are_holy_wars_disabled_by_struggle_conciliation_trigger = no # Disabled against other involved faiths in FP2 Conciliation ending
		has_pam_dlc_trigger = yes
		scope:recipient = {
			is_landed = yes
			top_liege ?= this
			NOT = { faith = scope:puppet_or_actor.faith }
			save_temporary_scope_as = defender
			are_holy_wars_disabled_by_struggle_conciliation_trigger = no # Disabled against other involved faiths in FP2 Conciliation ending
		}
		scope:recipient != scope:puppet_or_actor
		# Warcraft
		# trigger_if = {
		# 	limit = {
		# 		scope:attacker ?= {
		# 			any_character_struggle = {
		# 				involvement = involved
		# 				is_struggle_type = iberian_struggle
		# 			}
		# 		}
		# 		scope:defender ?= {
		# 			any_character_struggle = {
		# 				involvement = involved
		# 				is_struggle_type = iberian_struggle
		# 			}
		# 		}
		# 	}
		# 	are_holy_wars_disabled_in_struggle_phase_trigger = yes
		# }
	}

	populate_recipient_list = {
		scope:puppet_or_actor = {
			faith = {
				every_faith_ruler = {
					limit = {
						valid_holy_war_incited_attacker_trigger = { RECIPIENT = scope:recipient }
					}
					add_to_list = characters
				}
			}
		}
	}

	can_be_picked_title = {
		scope:target = {
			incite_holy_war_title_can_be_picked_trigger = { RECIPIENT = scope:recipient SECONDARY = scope:secondary_recipient }
		}
	}

	is_valid_showing_failures_only = {
		scope:puppet_or_actor = {
			is_available_quick = {
				adult = yes
				alive = yes
				imprisoned = no
				incapable = no
			}
			
			trigger_if = {
				limit = {
					scope:target ?= {
						tier >= tier_kingdom
					}
				}
				# trigger_if = {
				# 	limit = {
				# 		top_participant_group:the_christian_church ?= {
				# 			has_participant_group_parameter = the_christian_church_incite_holy_war_interaction
				# 		}
				# 	}
				# 	custom_tooltip = {
				# 		text = piety_too_low_target_kingdom_special_tt
				# 		piety_level >= 3
				# 	}
				# }
				trigger_if = {
					custom_tooltip = {
						text = piety_too_low_target_kingdom_tt
						piety_level >= 4
					}
				}
			}
			trigger_else_if = {
				limit = {
					scope:target ?= {
						tier >= tier_duchy
					}
				}
				# trigger_if = {
				# 	limit = {
				# 		top_participant_group:the_christian_church ?= {
				# 			has_participant_group_parameter = the_christian_church_incite_holy_war_interaction
				# 		}
				# 	}
				# 	custom_tooltip = {
				# 		text = piety_too_low_target_duchy_special_tt
				# 		piety_level >= 2
				# 	}
				# }
				trigger_if = {
					custom_tooltip = {
						text = piety_too_low_target_duchy_tt
						piety_level >= 3
					}
				}
			}
			# trigger_else = {
			# 	trigger_if = {
			# 		limit = {
			# 			top_participant_group:the_christian_church ?= {
			# 				has_participant_group_parameter = the_christian_church_incite_holy_war_interaction
			# 			}
			# 		}
			# 		piety_level >= 1
			# 	}
			# 	trigger_else = {
			# 		piety_level >= 2
			# 	}
			# }
			trigger_else = { # Warcraft - keep the non-church piety requirement
				piety_level >= 2
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = no_valid_sponsored_holy_war_attacker_tt
				faith = {
					any_faith_ruler = {
						valid_holy_war_incited_attacker_trigger = { RECIPIENT = scope:recipient }
					}
				}
			}
			
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = religious_war_hostility_tt
				faith = {
					faith_hostility_level = {
						target = scope:recipient.faith
						value >= religious_cb_enabled_hostility_level
					}
				}
			}
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			is_alive = yes
		}
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes }
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				piety >= pam_ecclesiastic_incite_holy_war_cost_value
			}
		}
	}

	has_valid_target_showing_failures_only = {
		exists = scope:target
		exists = scope:secondary_recipient
		scope:target = {
			tier <= tier_kingdom
			tier >= tier_county
			is_landless_type_title = no
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = sponsored_holy_war_target_must_be_held_by_recipient_tt
				any_in_de_jure_hierarchy = {
					tier = tier_county
					holder ?= {
						target_is_same_character_or_above = scope:recipient
					}
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = sponsored_holy_war_target_must_border_attacker_tt
				any_in_de_jure_hierarchy = {
					tier = tier_county
					holder ?= {
						target_is_same_character_or_above = scope:recipient
					}
					OR = {
						holder.top_liege = scope:secondary_recipient.top_liege
						any_title_to_title_neighboring_and_across_water_county = {
							holder.top_liege = scope:secondary_recipient.top_liege
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	cost = {
		piety = {
			value = pam_ecclesiastic_incite_holy_war_cost_value
			if = {
				limit = { scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes }
				multiply = 0
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:inspire_realm ?= yes
				}
				add = pam_ecclesiastic_incite_holy_war_inspire_realm_value
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
						domicile ?= { has_domicile_parameter = estate_cheaper_incite_war_1 }
					}
				}
				multiply = ecclesiastical_domicile_cheaper_incite_war_mult_1
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
						domicile ?= { has_domicile_parameter = estate_cheaper_incite_war_2 }
					}
				}
				multiply = ecclesiastical_domicile_cheaper_incite_war_mult_2
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
						domicile ?= { has_domicile_parameter = estate_cheaper_incite_war_3 }
					}
				}
				multiply = ecclesiastical_domicile_cheaper_incite_war_mult_3
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:puppet_or_actor ?= { has_perk = chivalric_dominance_perk }
				}
				multiply = 0.75
			}
		}
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			trigger_if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:secondary_recipient
				}
				scope:secondary_recipient = {
					is_ai = yes
				}
			}
		}
		is_valid = {
			trigger_if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:secondary_recipient
				}
				scope:actor = {
					has_usable_hook = scope:secondary_recipient
				}
			}
			trigger_else = {
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = no_attacker_picked_tt
					always = no
				}
			}
		}
		flag = use_hook
		localization = EDUCATION_HOOK
	}

	send_option = {
		is_valid = {
			scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
				trigger_if = {
					limit = {
						scope:actor = {
							is_ai = yes
						}
					}
					prestige_level >= 3
					ai_boldness > low_positive_ai_value
					ai_energy > low_positive_ai_value
					NOT = {
						religious_head_or_challenger ?= this
					}
				}
				NOR = {
					is_at_war_with = scope:recipient
					has_truce = scope:recipient
					is_vassal_or_below_of = scope:recipient
					is_tributary_of = scope:recipient
				}
				prestige_level >= 1
			}
		}
		flag = join_war
		localization = JOIN_WAR_INTERACTION_OPTION
	}

	send_option = {
		is_valid = {
			scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
				trigger_if = {
					limit = {
						scope:actor = {
							is_ai = yes
						}
					}
					short_term_gold >= monumental_gold_value
				}
				trigger_else = {
					gold >= major_gold_value
				}
			}
		}
		flag = fund_war
		localization = FUND_WAR_INTERACTION_OPTION
	}

	send_option = {
		is_valid = {
			scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = rally_crusaders_req_tt
					OR = {
						stewardship >= very_high_skill_rating
						martial >= very_high_skill_rating
						diplomacy >= very_high_skill_rating
					}
				}
				trigger_if = {
					limit = {
						scope:actor = { is_ai = yes }
					}
					prestige >= major_prestige_value
				}
			}
		}
		flag = rally_crusaders
		localization = RALLY_CRUSADERS_INTERACTION_OPTION
	}

	send_option = {
		is_valid = {
			scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
				piety_level >= 3
				piety >= pam_ecclesiastic_incite_holy_war_inspire_realm_value
			}
		}
		flag = inspire_realm
		localization = INSPIRE_REALM_INTERACTION_OPTION
	}

	send_options_exclusive = no

	on_accept = {
		if = {
			limit = { scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes }
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				if = {
					limit = { pam_ecclesiastic_incite_holy_war_cost_value > 0 }
					add_piety = {
						value = {
							add = pam_ecclesiastic_incite_holy_war_cost_value
							max = piety
							multiply = -1
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:puppet_or_actor ?= { has_perk = chivalric_dominance_perk }
			}
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				add_prestige = medium_prestige_value
			}
		}
		ecclesiastic_incite_holy_war_interaction_effect = yes
	}

	auto_accept = {
		OR = {
			#Player recipients auto-accept so they get no say in being attacked
			#Auto-accept skips ai_accept, so ai_will_do is stricter than usual
			scope:recipient = {
				is_ai = no
			}
			AND = {
				exists = scope:secondary_recipient
				custom_description = {
					text = "spending_hook"
					subject = scope:actor
					object = scope:secondary_recipient
					scope:use_hook = yes
					scope:actor = { has_strong_hook = scope:secondary_recipient }
				}
			}
		}
	}

	ai_targets = {

	}
	ai_targets = {
		ai_recipients = neighboring_rulers

	ai_targets = {
		ai_recipients = vassals

					has_trait = herald
					#has_trait = saoshyant_descendant # Warcraft - trait removed
					#has_trait = saoshyant # Warcraft - trait removed
					has_trait = paragon
					has_trait = savior
					has_trait = crusader_king
				}
			}
			add = 30
			desc = VERY_PIOUS_TRAITS_REASON
		}
		#Pious traits
		modifier = {
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				OR = {
					has_trait = zealous
					has_trait = theologian
					has_trait = divine_blood
					has_trait = consecrated_blood
					has_trait = sayyid
					has_trait = order_member
					has_trait = faith_warrior
				}
			}
			add = 10
			desc = PIOUS_TRAITS_REASON
		}
		# Fs
		modifier = { 
			add = 25
			scope:secondary_recipient ?= {
				has_relation_friend = scope:puppet_or_actor
			}
			desc = WE_ARE_FRIENDS
		}
		# BFFs
		modifier = { 
			add = 50
			scope:secondary_recipient ?= {
				has_relation_best_friend = scope:puppet_or_actor
			}
			desc = WE_ARE_BEST_FRIENDS
		}
		# Take dread into account
		modifier = {
			add = {
				value = 15
				if = {
					limit = {
						scope:secondary_recipient ?= {
							target_is_liege_or_above = scope:puppet_or_actor
						}
					}
					multiply = 2
				}
			}
			scope:secondary_recipient ?= {
				has_dread_level_towards = {
					target = scope:puppet_or_actor
					level = 1
				}
			}
			desc = INTIMIDATED_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			add = {
				value = 30
				if = {
					limit = {
						scope:secondary_recipient ?= {
							target_is_liege_or_above = scope:puppet_or_actor
						}
					}
					multiply = 2
				}
			}
			scope:secondary_recipient ?= {
				has_dread_level_towards = {
					target = scope:puppet_or_actor
					level = 2
				}
			}
			desc = COWED_REASON
		}
		# Take dread into account
		modifier = {
			add = -50
			scope:secondary_recipient ?= {
				has_dread_level_towards = {
					target = scope:recipient
					level = 1
				}
			}
			desc = SECONDARY_INTIMIDATED_BY_RECIPIENT_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -150
			scope:secondary_recipient ?= {
				has_dread_level_towards = {
					target = scope:recipient
					level >= 2
				}
			}
			desc = SECONDARY_COWED_BY_RECIPIENT_REASON
		}
	}

	can_send_despite_rejection = no

	ai_targets = {
		ai_recipients = neighboring_rulers_including_tributary_borders
		ai_recipients = war_enemies
		ai_recipients = rulers_in_clerical_region
		max = 10
	}

	ai_frequency_by_tier = {
		barony = 0
		county = 0
		duchy = 60
		kingdom = 24
		empire = 24
		hegemony = 24
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		base = 0

		modifier = {
			add = 25
			scope:recipient.rite = {
				"divergence(faith.main_rite)" >= minor_rite_divergence_value
			}
			desc = grant_pope_blessing_high_divergence_reason
		}

		opinion_modifier = {
			who = scope:recipient
			opinion_target = scope:actor
			multiplier = 0.5
		}
		
		# modifier = {
		# 	scope:puppet_or_actor = {
		# 		top_participant_group:the_christian_church ?= {
		# 			has_participant_group_parameter = the_christian_church_incite_holy_war_interaction
		# 		}
		# 	}
		# 	add = 50
		# }

		modifier = {
			add = 25
			scope:recipient = {
				is_at_war = yes
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 25
			exists = scope:secondary_recipient
			scope:secondary_recipient.current_military_strength > scope:recipient.twice_current_military_strength
		}
		modifier = {
			add = {
				value = scope:actor.ai_zeal
				multiply = 1
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = {
				value = scope:actor.ai_compassion
				multiply = -0.5
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = {
				value = scope:actor.ai_boldness
				multiply = 0.5
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			scope:puppet_or_actor.rite = {
				rite_hostility_level = {
					target = scope:recipient.rite
					value >= faith_evil_level
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				piety < monumental_piety_value
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0
			OR = { 
				AND = {
					exists = scope:secondary_recipient
					scope:secondary_recipient.current_military_strength < scope:recipient.current_military_strength
				}
				#AI will auto-accept when targeting players, so detailed factors needed here
				AND = {
					exists = scope:secondary_recipient
					scope:recipient = { is_ai = no }
					incite_holy_war_ai_invalidation_trigger = { RECIPIENT = scope:recipient SECONDARY = scope:secondary_recipient }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

#Sponsor a same-faith secular ruler's claim war against another secular ruler
ecclesiastic_incite_claim_war_interaction = {
	category = interaction_category_diplomacy
	icon = ecclesiastic_incite_claim_war_interaction
	interface_priority = 74
	common_interaction = yes
	target_type = title
	target_filter = realm_titles
	target_source = recipient
	notification_text = ecclesiastic_incite_claim_war_interaction_notification
	greeting = negative
	ai_min_reply_days = 1
	ai_max_reply_days = 5
	popup_on_receive = no
	cooldown_against_recipient = { years = 5 }
	use_diplomatic_range = no

	is_available = {
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { is_ai = yes }
			piety >= 750
			piety_level >= 2
			government_has_flag = government_is_theocracy
			OR = {
				rite.head_of_rite ?= this
				faith.religious_head ?= this
			}
			is_at_war = no
		}
	}

	is_shown = {
		has_pam_dlc_trigger = yes
		scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
			government_has_flag = government_is_theocracy
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			is_landed = yes
			NOT = { government_has_flag = government_is_theocracy }
			this != scope:puppet_or_actor
			trigger_if = {
				limit = {
					scope:actor = { is_ai = yes }
				}
				is_independent_ruler = yes
			}
		}
	}

	populate_recipient_list = {
		if = {
			limit = { scope:actor = { is_ai = yes } }
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				faith = {
					every_faith_ruler = {
						limit = {
							valid_claim_war_incited_attacker_trigger = { RECIPIENT = scope:recipient }
						}
						add_to_list = characters
					}
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = { exists = scope:target }
				scope:target = {
					every_claimant = {
						limit = {
							is_alive = yes
							this != scope:recipient
							this != scope:actor
							this != scope:puppet_or_actor
						}
						add_to_list = characters
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				scope:recipient = {
					every_held_title = {
						limit = {
							tier >= tier_county
							tier <= tier_kingdom
							is_landless_type_title = no
						}
						every_claimant = {
							limit = {
								is_alive = yes
								this != scope:recipient
								this != scope:actor
								this != scope:puppet_or_actor
							}
							add_to_list = characters
						}
					}
					every_vassal_or_below = {
						limit = { is_landed = yes }
						every_held_title = {
							limit = {
								tier >= tier_county
								tier <= tier_kingdom
								is_landless_type_title = no
							}
							every_claimant = {
								limit = {
									is_alive = yes
									this != scope:recipient
									this != scope:actor
									this != scope:puppet_or_actor
								}
								add_to_list = characters
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	can_be_picked_title = {
		scope:target = {
			incite_claim_war_title_can_be_picked_trigger = { RECIPIENT = scope:recipient SECONDARY = scope:secondary_recipient }
		}
	}

	is_valid_showing_failures_only = {
		scope:puppet_or_actor = {
			is_available_quick = {
				adult = yes
				alive = yes
				imprisoned = no
				incapable = no
			}

			trigger_if = {
				limit = {
					scope:target ?= {
						tier >= tier_kingdom
					}
				}
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = piety_too_low_target_kingdom_tt
					piety_level >= 4
				}
			}
			trigger_else_if = {
				limit = {
					scope:target ?= {
						tier >= tier_duchy
					}
				}
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = piety_too_low_target_duchy_tt
					piety_level >= 3
				}
			}
			trigger_else = {
				piety_level >= 2
			}
			trigger_if = {
				limit = {
					scope:recipient = { is_independent_ruler = yes }
				}
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = no_valid_sponsored_claim_war_attacker_tt
					faith = {
						any_faith_ruler = {
							valid_claim_war_incited_attacker_trigger = { RECIPIENT = scope:recipient }
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			is_alive = yes
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = incite_claim_war_use_on_liege_tt
				is_independent_ruler = yes
			}
		}
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { scope:puppet_or_actor != scope:actor }
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				piety >= pam_ecclesiastic_incite_claim_war_cost_value
			}
		}
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { exists = scope:secondary_recipient }
			scope:secondary_recipient = {
				claim_war_incited_attacker_can_be_picked_trigger = { RECIPIENT = scope:recipient }
			}
		}
	}

	has_valid_target_showing_failures_only = {
		exists = scope:target
		exists = scope:secondary_recipient
		scope:target = {
			tier <= tier_kingdom
			tier >= tier_county
			is_landless_type_title = no
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = sponsored_claim_war_target_must_be_held_by_recipient_tt
				holder ?= {
					target_is_same_character_or_above = scope:recipient
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = sponsored_claim_war_attacker_must_have_claim_tt
				scope:secondary_recipient = { has_claim_on = scope:target }
			}
		}
	}

	cost = {
		piety = {
			value = pam_ecclesiastic_incite_claim_war_cost_value
			if = {
				limit = { scope:puppet_or_actor != scope:actor }
				multiply = 0
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
						domicile ?= { has_domicile_parameter = estate_cheaper_incite_war_1 }
					}
				}
				multiply = ecclesiastical_domicile_cheaper_incite_war_mult_1
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
						domicile ?= { has_domicile_parameter = estate_cheaper_incite_war_2 }
					}
				}
				multiply = ecclesiastical_domicile_cheaper_incite_war_mult_2
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
						domicile ?= { has_domicile_parameter = estate_cheaper_incite_war_3 }
					}
				}
				multiply = ecclesiastical_domicile_cheaper_incite_war_mult_3
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:puppet_or_actor ?= { has_perk = chivalric_dominance_perk }
				}
				multiply = 0.75
			}
			if = {
				limit = { scope:appeal_to_gods_will ?= yes }
				add = major_piety_value
			}
		}
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			trigger_if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:secondary_recipient
				}
				scope:secondary_recipient = {
					is_ai = yes
				}
			}
		}
		is_valid = {
			trigger_if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:secondary_recipient
				}
				scope:actor = {
					has_usable_hook = scope:secondary_recipient
				}
			}
			trigger_else = {
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = no_attacker_picked_tt
					always = no
				}
			}
		}
		flag = use_hook
		localization = EDUCATION_HOOK
	}

	send_option = {
		is_valid = {
			scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
				trigger_if = {
					limit = {
						scope:actor = {
							is_ai = yes
						}
					}
					prestige_level >= 3
					ai_boldness > low_positive_ai_value
					ai_energy > low_positive_ai_value
					NOT = {
						religious_head_or_challenger ?= this
					}
				}
				NOR = {
					is_at_war_with = scope:recipient
					has_truce = scope:recipient
					is_vassal_or_below_of = scope:recipient
					is_tributary_of = scope:recipient
				}
				prestige_level >= 1
			}
		}
		flag = join_war
		localization = JOIN_WAR_INTERACTION_OPTION
	}

	send_option = {
		is_valid = {
			scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
				trigger_if = {
					limit = {
						scope:actor = {
							is_ai = yes
						}
					}
					short_term_gold >= monumental_gold_value
				}
				trigger_else = {
					gold >= major_gold_value
				}
			}
		}
		flag = fund_war
		localization = FUND_WAR_INTERACTION_OPTION
	}

	send_option = {
		is_valid = {
			scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
				piety >= major_piety_value
			}
		}
		flag = appeal_to_gods_will
		localization = APPEAL_TO_GODS_WILL_INTERACTION_OPTION
	}

	send_options_exclusive = no

	on_accept = {
		if = {
			limit = { scope:puppet_or_actor != scope:actor }
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				if = {
					limit = { pam_ecclesiastic_incite_claim_war_cost_value > 0 }
					add_piety = {
						value = {
							add = pam_ecclesiastic_incite_claim_war_cost_value
							max = piety
							multiply = -1
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:puppet_or_actor ?= { has_perk = chivalric_dominance_perk }
			}
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				add_prestige = medium_prestige_value
			}
		}
		ecclesiastic_incite_claim_war_interaction_effect = yes
	}

	auto_accept = {
		OR = {
			scope:recipient = {
				is_ai = no
			}
			AND = {
				exists = scope:secondary_recipient
				custom_description = {
					text = "spending_hook"
					subject = scope:actor
					object = scope:secondary_recipient
					scope:use_hook = yes
					scope:actor = { has_strong_hook = scope:secondary_recipient }
				}
			}
		}
	}

	ai_targets = {
		ai_recipients = rulers_in_clerical_region
	}
	ai_targets = {
		ai_recipients = neighboring_rulers
	}
	ai_targets = {
		ai_recipients = vassals
	}
	ai_targets = {
		ai_recipients = neighboring_rulers_including_tributary_borders
		ai_recipients = war_enemies
		ai_recipients = rulers_in_clerical_region
		max = 10
	}

	ai_accept = {
		base = -25

		modifier = {
			scope:appeal_to_gods_will = yes
			add = 50
			desc = APPEAL_TO_GODS_WILL_INTERACTION_OPTION_REASON
		}

		modifier = {
			add = ecclesiastical_domicile_acceptance_minor_value_1
			desc = ESTATE_WARRIORS_VESTRY_REASON
			scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
				domicile ?= {
					has_domicile_parameter = estate_incite_war_vestry_1
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = ecclesiastical_domicile_acceptance_minor_value_2
			desc = ESTATE_WARRIORS_VESTRY_REASON
			scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
				domicile ?= {
					has_domicile_parameter = estate_incite_war_vestry_2
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = ecclesiastical_domicile_acceptance_minor_value_3
			desc = ESTATE_WARRIORS_VESTRY_REASON
			scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
				domicile ?= {
					has_domicile_parameter = estate_incite_war_vestry_3
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = ecclesiastical_domicile_acceptance_micro_value_1
			desc = ESTATE_RELIQUARY_REASON
			scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
				domicile ?= {
					has_domicile_parameter = estate_incite_war_reliquary_1
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = ecclesiastical_domicile_acceptance_micro_value_2
			desc = ESTATE_RELIQUARY_REASON
			scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
				domicile ?= {
					has_domicile_parameter = estate_incite_war_reliquary_2
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = ecclesiastical_domicile_acceptance_micro_value_3
			desc = ESTATE_RELIQUARY_REASON
			scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
				domicile ?= {
					has_domicile_parameter = estate_incite_war_reliquary_3
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 15
			desc = CHIVALRIC_DOMINANCE_REASON
			scope:puppet_or_actor ?= { has_perk = chivalric_dominance_perk }
		}

		modifier = {
			scope:use_hook = yes
			add = 100
			exists = scope:secondary_recipient
			scope:actor = { NOT = { has_strong_hook = scope:secondary_recipient } }
			desc = SCHEME_WEAK_HOOK_USED
		}

		modifier = {
			add = {
				value = 25
			}
			exists = scope:secondary_recipient
			scope:fund_war = yes
			scope:puppet_or_actor.major_gold_value < scope:secondary_recipient.minor_gold_value
			desc = FUND_HOLY_WAR_INTERACTION_OPTION_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			add = {
				value = 50
			}
			exists = scope:secondary_recipient
			scope:fund_war = yes
			scope:puppet_or_actor.major_gold_value < scope:secondary_recipient.major_gold_value
			scope:puppet_or_actor.major_gold_value >= scope:secondary_recipient.minor_gold_value
			desc = FUND_HOLY_WAR_INTERACTION_OPTION_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			add = {
				value = 75
			}
			exists = scope:secondary_recipient
			scope:fund_war = yes
			scope:puppet_or_actor.major_gold_value >= scope:secondary_recipient.major_gold_value
			scope:puppet_or_actor.major_gold_value < scope:secondary_recipient.monumental_gold_value
			desc = FUND_HOLY_WAR_INTERACTION_OPTION_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			add = {
				value = 100
			}
			exists = scope:secondary_recipient
			scope:fund_war = yes
			scope:puppet_or_actor.major_gold_value >= scope:secondary_recipient.monumental_gold_value
			desc = FUND_HOLY_WAR_INTERACTION_OPTION_REASON
		}

		modifier = {
			exists = scope:secondary_recipient
			scope:secondary_recipient.highest_held_title_tier >= tier_duchy
			add = {
				value = scope:secondary_recipient.highest_held_title_tier
				subtract = 2
				multiply = -25
			}
			desc = INCITE_CLAIM_WAR_ATTACKER_TIER_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			exists = scope:secondary_recipient
			scope:target ?= {
				tier >= tier_kingdom
			}
			scope:secondary_recipient.highest_held_title_tier >= tier_duchy
			add = 25
			desc = KINGDOM_TIER_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			exists = scope:secondary_recipient
			scope:target ?= {
				tier = tier_duchy
			}
			scope:secondary_recipient.highest_held_title_tier = tier_county
			add = 25
			desc = TARGET_TIER_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			exists = scope:secondary_recipient
			scope:target ?= {
				tier = tier_county
			}
			scope:secondary_recipient.highest_held_title_tier >= tier_duchy
			add = {
				value = -25
				multiply = {
					value = scope:secondary_recipient.highest_held_title_tier
					subtract = 2
				}
			}
			desc = TARGET_TIER_REASON
		}

		modifier = {
			exists = scope:secondary_recipient
			scope:join_war ?= yes
			add = {
				value = scope:actor.current_military_strength
				divide = { value = scope:secondary_recipient.current_military_strength min = 1 }
				multiply = 100
				round = yes
				max = 250
			}
			desc = CLERIC_JOIN_HOLY_WAR_REASON
		}

		modifier = {
			exists = scope:secondary_recipient
			scope:secondary_recipient.current_military_strength >= scope:recipient.fifty_percent_more_of_current_military_strength
			add = {
				value = scope:secondary_recipient.current_military_strength
				divide = {
					value = scope:recipient.current_military_strength
					min = 1
				}
				multiply = 15
				round = yes
				max = 75
			}
			desc = SPONSORED_HOLY_WAR_RELATIVE_STRENGTH_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			exists = scope:secondary_recipient
			scope:recipient.current_military_strength > scope:secondary_recipient.current_military_strength
			add = {
				value = scope:recipient.current_military_strength
				divide = { value = scope:secondary_recipient.current_military_strength min = 1 }
				multiply = -50
				round = yes
			}
			desc = SPONSORED_HOLY_WAR_RELATIVE_STRENGTH_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			add = {
				value = scope:actor.piety_level
				multiply = 10
			}
			scope:actor.piety_level > 0
			desc = I_AM_PIOUS
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -100
			scope:secondary_recipient ?= {
				any_personal_tenet = {
					OR = {
						this = tenet:tenet_pacifism
						this = tenet:tenet_dharmic_pacifism
					}
				}
			}
			desc = PERSONAL_TENET_PACIFISM
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -50
			exists = scope:secondary_recipient.capital_province
			exists = scope:recipient.capital_province
			"scope:secondary_recipient.capital_province.squared_distance(scope:recipient.capital_province)" >= squared_distance_huge
			desc = PT_AI_IS_FAR_AWAY
		}
		opinion_modifier = {
			trigger = { exists = scope:secondary_recipient }
			who = scope:secondary_recipient
			opinion_target = scope:puppet_or_actor
			multiplier = 0.5
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -50
			scope:secondary_recipient ?= {
				opinion = {
					target = scope:recipient
					value >= 25
				}
			}
			desc = SECONDARY_RECIPIENT_LIKES_RECIPIENT_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -100
			scope:secondary_recipient ?= {
				has_any_good_relationship_with_character_trigger = { CHARACTER = scope:recipient }
			}
			desc = SECONDARY_RECIPIENT_GOOD_RELATION_WITH_RECIPIENT_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			scope:secondary_recipient ?= {
				OR = {
					has_any_bad_relationship_with_character_trigger = { CHARACTER = scope:recipient }
					opinion = {
						target = scope:recipient
						value <= -95
					}
				}
			}
			desc = SECONDARY_RECIPIENT_LOATHES_RECIPIENT_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			scope:secondary_recipient ?= {
				OR = {
					ai_boldness > 0
					ai_boldness < 0
				}
			}
			add = {
				value = scope:secondary_recipient.ai_boldness
				multiply = 0.5
			}
			desc = SECONDARY_RECIPIENT_BOLDNESS_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			scope:secondary_recipient ?= {
				ai_compassion != 0
			}
			add = {
				value = scope:secondary_recipient.ai_compassion
				multiply = -0.25
			}
			desc = SECONDARY_RECIPIENT_COMPASSION_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			scope:recipient = {
				is_at_war = yes
			}
			add = 40
			desc = RECIPIENT_AT_WAR_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 50
			exists = scope:secondary_recipient
			scope:puppet_or_actor = { is_allied_to = scope:secondary_recipient }
			desc = AI_HAS_ALLIANCE
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 25
			scope:secondary_recipient ?= { target_is_liege_or_above = scope:puppet_or_actor }
			desc = LIEGE_REASON_SECONDARY
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -50
			scope:secondary_recipient ?= {
				has_dread_level_towards = {
					target = scope:recipient
					level = 1
				}
			}
			desc = SECONDARY_INTIMIDATED_BY_RECIPIENT_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -150
			scope:secondary_recipient ?= {
				has_dread_level_towards = {
					target = scope:recipient
					level >= 2
				}
			}
			desc = SECONDARY_COWED_BY_RECIPIENT_REASON
		}
	}

	can_send_despite_rejection = no

	ai_frequency_by_tier = {
		barony = 0
		county = 0
		duchy = 60
		kingdom = 48
		empire = 24
		hegemony = 24
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		base = 0

		opinion_modifier = {
			who = scope:recipient
			opinion_target = scope:actor
			multiplier = 0.5
		}

		modifier = {
			add = 25
			scope:recipient = {
				is_at_war = yes
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 25
			exists = scope:secondary_recipient
			scope:secondary_recipient.current_military_strength > scope:recipient.twice_current_military_strength
		}
		modifier = {
			add = {
				value = scope:actor.ai_zeal
				multiply = 0.5
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = {
				value = scope:actor.ai_compassion
				multiply = -0.5
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = {
				value = scope:actor.ai_boldness
				multiply = 0.5
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				piety < monumental_piety_value
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0
			OR = {
				AND = {
					exists = scope:secondary_recipient
					scope:secondary_recipient.current_military_strength < scope:recipient.current_military_strength
				}
				AND = {
					exists = scope:secondary_recipient
					scope:recipient = { is_ai = no }
					incite_holy_war_ai_invalidation_trigger = { RECIPIENT = scope:recipient SECONDARY = scope:secondary_recipient }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Reduces a rite's divergence score
grant_pope_blessing_interaction = {
	category = interaction_category_religion
	icon = religious

	use_diplomatic_range = no
	auto_accept = yes

	desc = grant_pope_blessing_interaction_desc

	is_available = {
		faith.religious_head ?= this
		has_pam_dlc_trigger = yes
	}

	is_shown = {
		scope:recipient = {
			rite.head_of_rite ?= this
			faith ?= scope:actor.faith
			this != scope:actor
			rite ?= {
				always = no # Warcraft - special_doctrine_pope_blessing does not exist in GoA
			}
			is_ai = yes
		}
	}
	
	cost = {
		piety = massive_piety_value
	}

	is_valid_showing_failures_only = {
		scope:recipient = {
			is_alive = yes
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = grant_pope_blessing_hor_tt
				rite.head_of_rite ?= this
			}
		}
	}

	on_accept = {
		scope:actor = {
			send_interface_toast = {
				title = grant_pope_blessing_toast_title
				desc = grant_pope_blessing_toast_desc
				left_icon = scope:actor
				right_icon = scope:recipient
				faith = {
					change_fervor = {
						value = notable_fervor_loss
						desc = fervor_loss_grant_pope_blessing
					}
				}
				scope:recipient = {
					add_opinion = {
						target = scope:actor
						modifier = grateful_opinion
						opinion = 15
					}
					# rite = { add_rite_doctrine = special_doctrine_pope_blessing } # Warcraft - special_doctrine_pope_blessing does not exist in GoA
				}
			}
		}
	}

	ai_targets = {
		ai_recipients = rulers_in_clerical_region
		ai_recipients = neighboring_rulers
		ai_recipients = vassals
		ai_recipients = subordinates
	}

	ai_frequency_by_tier = {
		barony = 0
		county = 0
		duchy = 144
		kingdom = 36
		empire = 36
		hegemony = 36
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		base = 0

		modifier = {
			add = 50
			scope:recipient.rite = {
				"divergence(faith.main_rite)" >= minor_rite_divergence_value
			}
			desc = grant_pope_blessing_high_divergence_reason
		}

		opinion_modifier = {
			who = scope:recipient
			opinion_target = scope:actor
			multiplier = 0.5
		}
	}
}

# Make a cardinal vote for whoever you want
influence_papal_vote_interaction = {
	category = interaction_category_religion
	icon = influence_papal_vote_interaction
	common_interaction = no
	interface_priority = 74

	filter_tags = { papabile }

	use_diplomatic_range = yes
	ai_maybe = yes
	ai_min_reply_days = 1
	ai_max_reply_days = 5
	popup_on_receive = yes

	cooldown_against_recipient = { years = 10 }

	desc = influence_papal_vote_interaction_desc
	notification_text = influence_papal_vote_interaction_notification

	is_available = {
		
		faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = has_clerical_electors }
		pam_pope_is_head_of_faith_or_rite_trigger = yes
		is_adult = yes
		NOR = {
			government_has_flag = government_is_mercenary
			government_has_flag = government_is_herder
		}
		has_pam_dlc_trigger = yes
	}

	is_shown = {
		scope:recipient = {
			faith ?= scope:actor.faith
			exists = clerical_elector_title
			is_ruler = yes
			is_adult = yes
			this != scope:actor
		}
	}

	populate_recipient_list = {
		scope:actor.faith = {
			every_clerical_elector_title = {
				holder ?= {
					if = {
						limit = {
							this != scope:recipient
							NOT = { scope:actor.faith.religious_head ?= this }
							is_alive = yes
							is_papabile = yes
						}
						add_to_list = characters
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	is_valid_showing_failures_only = {
		scope:actor = {
			is_imprisoned = no
			is_at_war = no
			is_incapable = no
			piety_level >= high_piety_level
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			is_imprisoned = no
			is_at_war = no
			is_incapable = no
			NOT = { has_trait = excommunicated }
		}
	}

	send_option = {
		is_valid = {
			scope:actor = {
				has_usable_hook = scope:recipient
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				NOT = { is_imprisoned_by = scope:actor }
			}
		}
		flag = hook
		localization = SCHEME_HOOK
	}
	should_use_extra_icon = {
		scope:actor = { has_usable_hook = scope:recipient }
	}
	extra_icon = "gfx/interface/icons/character_interactions/hook_icon.dds"

	send_option = {
		flag = pam_papal_vote_bribe
		localization = influence_papal_vote_bribe_option_desc
		is_valid = {
			scope:actor = { gold >= major_gold_value }
		}
	}
	send_options_exclusive = no

	cost = {
		piety = {
			value = major_piety_value
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:actor = {
						domicile ?= { has_domicile_parameter = estate_cheaper_papal_vote }
					}
				}
				multiply = ecclesiastical_domicile_cheaper_papal_vote_mult
			}
		}
	}

	auto_accept = {
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = influence_papal_vote_autoaccept_tt
			OR = {
				custom_description = {
					text = "spending_hook"
					subject = scope:actor
					object = scope:recipient
					scope:hook = yes
					scope:actor = { has_strong_hook = scope:recipient }
				}
				custom_description = {
					text = "influence_papal_vote_is_puppet_tt"
					scope:recipient.puppeteer ?= scope:actor
				}
			}
		}
	}

	on_accept = {
		scope:actor = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:hook = yes
					has_usable_hook = scope:recipient
				}
				use_hook = scope:recipient
			}
			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_good
				title = influence_papal_vote_interaction_toast
				left_icon = scope:actor
				right_icon = scope:recipient
				custom_tooltip = influence_papal_vote_interaction_tt
				scope:recipient = {
					set_variable = {
						name = pam_forced_papal_vote_target
						value = scope:secondary_recipient
						years = 20
					}
					# Rather than waiting for the next AI tick, force update views immediately
					hidden_effect = {
						recalculate_succession_election_vote = yes
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { scope:pam_papal_vote_bribe = yes }
					pay_short_term_gold = {
						target = scope:recipient
						gold = major_gold_value
					}
				}
			}
		}
		# if = {
		# 	limit = {
		# 		exists = scope:actor.top_participant_group:the_christian_church
		# 		scope:actor = {
		# 			NOT = { government_has_flag = government_is_theocracy }
		# 		}
		# 		situation:the_christian_church ?= {
		# 			situation_top_has_catalyst = catalyst_secular_interference_in_papal_election
		# 		}
		# 	}
		# 	situation:the_christian_church = {
		# 		trigger_situation_catalyst = catalyst_secular_interference_in_papal_election
		# 	}
		# }
	}

	on_decline = {
		scope:actor = {
			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_bad
				title = influence_papal_vote_interaction_decline_toast
				right_icon = scope:recipient
				scope:recipient = {
					add_opinion = {
						target = scope:actor
						modifier = annoyed_opinion
						opinion = -10
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	ai_accept = {
		base = -80

		opinion_modifier = {
			who = scope:recipient
			opinion_target = scope:actor
			multiplier = 0.5
			desc = AI_OPINION_REASON
		}

		modifier = { # Your Intrigue
			add = {
				value = scope:actor.intrigue
				multiply = 1.5
			}
			desc = INTERACTION_ACTOR_INTRIGUE
		}

		modifier = { # Court of Shadows
			add = 20
			scope:actor = { has_perk = court_of_shadows_perk }
			desc = COURT_OF_SHADOWS_PAPAL_VOTE_REASON
		}

		modifier = {
			add = ecclesiastical_domicile_acceptance_value_1
			desc = ESTATE_PAPAL_VOTE_REASON
			scope:actor = {
				domicile ?= {
					has_domicile_parameter = estate_easier_papal_vote_1
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = ecclesiastical_domicile_acceptance_value_2
			desc = ESTATE_PAPAL_VOTE_REASON
			scope:actor = {
				domicile ?= {
					has_domicile_parameter = estate_easier_papal_vote_2
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = ecclesiastical_domicile_acceptance_value_3
			desc = ESTATE_PAPAL_VOTE_REASON
			scope:actor = {
				domicile ?= {
					has_domicile_parameter = estate_easier_papal_vote_3
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = ecclesiastical_domicile_acceptance_minor_value_1
			desc = ESTATE_ELECTION_COFFERS_REASON
			scope:actor = {
				domicile ?= {
					has_domicile_parameter = estate_papal_vote_coffers_1
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = ecclesiastical_domicile_acceptance_minor_value_2
			desc = ESTATE_ELECTION_COFFERS_REASON
			scope:actor = {
				domicile ?= {
					has_domicile_parameter = estate_papal_vote_coffers_2
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = ecclesiastical_domicile_acceptance_minor_value_3
			desc = ESTATE_ELECTION_COFFERS_REASON
			scope:actor = {
				domicile ?= {
					has_domicile_parameter = estate_papal_vote_coffers_3
				}
			}
		}
		ecclesiastical_domicile_cathedral_awe_modifier = yes

		modifier = { # Used a hook
			scope:hook = yes
			add = 50
			scope:actor = { NOT = { has_strong_hook = scope:recipient } }
			desc = SCHEME_WEAK_HOOK_USED
		}

		modifier = { # Bribe + they're greedy
			add = 80
			scope:pam_papal_vote_bribe = yes
			scope:recipient = { ai_greed >= 20 }
			desc = AI_GREED_REASON
		}

		modifier = { # Bribe
			add = 40
			scope:pam_papal_vote_bribe = yes
			scope:recipient = {
				ai_greed < 20
			}
			desc = AI_BRIBE_REASON
		}

		modifier = { # Dread
			add = 50
			scope:recipient = {
				has_dread_level_towards = {
					target = scope:actor
					level = 2
				}
			}
			desc = COWED_REASON
		}

		modifier = { # Dread
			add = 25
			scope:recipient = {
				has_dread_level_towards = {
					target = scope:actor
					level = 1
				}
			}
			desc = INTIMIDATED_REASON
		}

		modifier = { # Friends
			add = 80
			scope:recipient = {
				OR = {
					has_relation_friend = scope:actor
					has_relation_lover = scope:actor
				}
			}
			desc = WE_ARE_FRIENDS
		}

		modifier = { # BFFs
			add = 40
			scope:recipient = { has_relation_best_friend = scope:actor }
			desc = WE_ARE_BEST_FRIENDS
		}
		
		modifier = { # Dynasty
			add = 80
			scope:recipient.dynasty ?= scope:actor.dynasty
			desc = AI_SAME_DYNASTY_REASON
		}
		
		modifier = { # Close Family
			add = 100
			scope:recipient = {
				is_close_family_of = scope:actor
			}
			desc = AI_FAMILY_REASON
		}

		modifier = { # They like who you're asking them to vote for
			add = 25
			exists = scope:secondary_recipient
			scope:recipient = {
				opinion = {
					target = scope:secondary_recipient
					value >= 50
				}
			}
			desc = INFLUENCE_PAPAL_VOTE_LIKES_CANDIDATE_REASON
		}

		modifier = { # They're just
			add = -30
			scope:recipient = { has_trait = just }
			desc = INFLUENCE_PAPAL_VOTE_JUST_REASON
		}

		modifier = { # They're pious
			add = {
				value = scope:recipient.piety_level
				multiply = -15
			}
			scope:recipient.piety_level > 0
			desc = RECIPIENT_IS_PIOUS
		}

		modifier = { # They're rivals with the candidate
			add = -50
			exists = scope:secondary_recipient
			scope:recipient = { has_relation_rival = scope:secondary_recipient }
			desc = INFLUENCE_PAPAL_VOTE_RIVAL_CANDIDATE_REASON
		}

		modifier = { # You're sinful
			add = {
				value = scope:actor.num_sinful_traits
				multiply = -10
			}
			scope:actor.num_sinful_traits > 0
			desc = I_AM_SINFUL
		}
	}

	ai_targets = {
		ai_recipients = rulers_in_clerical_region
		max = 5
	}
	
	ai_targets = {
		ai_recipients = clerical_region_rulers_in_realm
		ai_recipients = puppets
	}

	ai_target_quick_trigger = {
		adult = yes
	}

	ai_frequency_by_tier = {
		barony = 0
		county = 0
		duchy = 72
		kingdom = 48
		empire = 36
		hegemony = 36
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		base = 0

		modifier = {
			add = 20
			OR = {
				has_usable_hook = scope:recipient
				scope:recipient.puppeteer ?= this
			}
			OR = {
				has_trait = ambitious
				has_trait = deceitful
				has_trait = cynical
			}
		}

		modifier = {
			factor = 0
			piety_level < high_piety_level
		}

		modifier = {
			factor = 0
			exists = clerical_elector_title
		}

		modifier = {
			factor = 0
			piety < major_piety_value
		}
	}
}

increase_papabile_score_interaction = {
	category = interaction_category_religion
	icon = icon_increase_theocratic_heir_candidacy
	common_interaction = yes
	interface_priority = 75
	
	cooldown_against_recipient = { years = 3 }
	
	filter_tags = { papabile }

	desc = increase_papabile_score_interaction_desc
	filter_tags = { papabile }

	is_available = {
		always = no # Warcraft
		faith ?= { has_doctrine_parameter = has_clerical_electors }
		pam_pope_is_head_of_faith_or_rite_trigger = yes
		# title:k_papal_state ?= { # Warcraft
		# 	any_elector = {}
		# 	is_title_created = yes
		# }
		is_adult = yes
		NOR = {
			government_has_flag = government_is_mercenary
			government_has_flag = government_is_herder
			government_has_flag = government_is_military_holy_order
		}
		has_pam_dlc_trigger = yes
		trigger_if = {
			# The incumbent stands in no conclave, so the AI Head of Faith has no candidacy worth buying
			limit = { is_ai = yes }
			NOT = { faith.religious_head ?= this }
		}
	}

	is_shown = {
		scope:recipient = {
			faith ?= scope:actor.faith
			is_adult = yes
			exists = clerical_elector_title
			NOT = { faith.religious_head ?= this }
		}
	}

	is_valid_showing_failures_only = {
		scope:actor = {
			is_imprisoned = no
			is_at_war = no
			is_incapable = no
			trigger_if = {
				limit = {
					is_ai = yes
				}
				scope:recipient = {
					OR = {
						is_papabile = yes
						is_close_or_extended_family_of = scope:actor
						custom_tooltip = {
							text = increase_papabile_score_interaction_puppet_tt
							OR = {
								scope:actor.puppet:theological_agent_puppet ?= scope:recipient
								scope:actor.puppet:external_theological_agent_puppet ?= scope:recipient
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			is_imprisoned = no
			is_at_war = no
			is_incapable = no
			NOT = { has_trait = excommunicated }
		}
	}

	send_option = {
		flag = pam_papabile_place_first_option
		localization = increase_papabile_score_place_first_option_desc
		is_valid = {
			scope:actor = { is_ai = no }
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = increase_papabile_score_already_powerful_tt
				scope:recipient = {
					pam_papabile_slot_open_to_trigger = { SLOT = theocratic_elective_powerful }
				}
			}
		}
	}

	send_option = {
		flag = pam_papabile_gold_option
		localization = increase_papabile_score_gold_option_desc
		is_valid = {
			scope:actor = { gold >= major_gold_value }
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = increase_papabile_score_gold_option_place_first_tt
				NOT = { scope:pam_papabile_place_first_option = yes }
			}
		}
	}
	send_options_exclusive = no

	localization_values = {
		PAM_PAPABILE_PLACE_FIRST = scope:recipient.pam_papabile_place_first_powerful_value
	}

	cost = {
		piety = {
			value = major_piety_value
			if = {
				limit = { scope:pam_papabile_place_first_option = yes }
				add = {
					value = scope:recipient.pam_papabile_place_first_powerful_value
					multiply = pam_papabile_place_first_piety_per_point_value
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { scope:actor = { has_perk = court_of_shadows_perk } }
				multiply = 0.75
			}
		}
		gold = {
			value = 0
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:pam_papabile_gold_option = yes
					NOT = { scope:pam_papabile_place_first_option = yes }
				}
				add = scope:actor.major_gold_value
			}
		}
	}

	auto_accept = yes

	on_accept = {
		scope:actor = {
			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_good
				title = increase_papabile_score_interaction_toast
				left_icon = scope:actor
				right_icon = scope:recipient
				scope:recipient = {
					if = {
						limit = { scope:pam_papabile_place_first_option = yes }
						pam_place_first_as_powerful_effect = { VALUE = pam_papabile_place_first_powerful_value }
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = { scope:pam_papabile_gold_option = yes }
						pam_increase_papabile_score_effect = { VALUE = pam_papabile_gold_option_score_value }
					}
					else = {
						pam_increase_papabile_score_effect = { VALUE = pam_papabile_base_score_value }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					this != scope:actor
					is_ai = no
				}
				hidden_effect = {
					send_interface_toast = {
						type = event_toast_effect_good
						title = increase_papabile_score_interaction_toast
						left_icon = scope:recipient
						right_icon = scope:actor
						show_as_tooltip = {
							if = {
								limit = { scope:pam_papabile_place_first_option = yes }
								pam_place_first_as_powerful_effect = { VALUE = pam_papabile_place_first_powerful_value }
							}
							else_if = {
								limit = { scope:pam_papabile_gold_option = yes }
								pam_increase_papabile_score_effect = { VALUE = pam_papabile_gold_option_score_value }
							}
							else = {
								pam_increase_papabile_score_effect = { VALUE = pam_papabile_base_score_value }
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	ai_targets = {
		ai_recipients = family
		max = 5
	}

	ai_targets = {
		ai_recipients = dynasty
		max = 5
	}

	ai_targets = {
		ai_recipients = self
		ai_recipients = puppets
		ai_recipients = spouses
		ai_recipients = papabile
	}

	ai_target_quick_trigger = {
		adult = yes
	}

	ai_frequency_by_tier = {
		barony = 0
		county = 72
		duchy = 36
		kingdom = 12
		empire = 12
		hegemony = 12
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		base = 0

		modifier = {
			add = 1000
			scope:recipient = scope:actor
		}

		modifier = {
			add = 100
			OR = {
				is_close_or_extended_family_of = scope:recipient
				scope:recipient.puppeteer ?= this
				has_relation_friend = scope:recipient
				has_relation_lover = scope:recipient
			}
		}

		modifier = {
			add = 50
			is_spouse_of = scope:recipient
		}

		modifier = {
			add = 25
			exists = scope:recipient.dynasty
			scope:actor.dynasty ?= scope:recipient.dynasty
		}

		modifier = {
			add = -25
			scope:actor = { has_trait = cynical }
		}

		modifier = {
			add = -25
			scope:actor = { has_trait = greedy }
		}

		modifier = { # A candidate of another rite is a rival faction's champion, not ours to elevate
			add = -60
			scope:recipient != scope:actor
			scope:actor = { rite != scope:recipient.rite }
		}
		
		modifier = {
			factor = 0
			scope:actor = { has_relation_rival = scope:recipient }
		}
	}
}

# Reform a patroned monastic order's tenet or rite
pam_reform_order_interaction = {
	category = interaction_category_religion
	icon = reform_order
	interface_priority = 41
	common_interaction = yes
	
	cooldown_against_recipient = { years = 5 }

	use_diplomatic_range = no
	ai_maybe = no
	popup_on_receive = yes
	greeting = negative
	notification_text = pam_reform_order_interaction_notification
	desc = pam_reform_order_interaction_desc
	
	is_available = {
		has_pam_dlc_trigger = yes
		is_adult = yes
	}

	is_shown = {
		scope:actor = {
			any_patroned_holy_order = {
				leader = scope:recipient
			}
		}
	}

	is_valid_showing_failures_only = {
		scope:actor = {
			is_imprisoned = no
			is_incapable = no
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			is_imprisoned = no
			is_incapable = no
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = pam_reform_order_is_valid_showing_failures_only_tt
			scope:actor = {
				OR = {
					rite != scope:recipient.rite
					any_personal_tenet = {
						NOT = { scope:recipient.holy_order.order_tenet ?= this }
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	send_options_exclusive = yes

	auto_accept = {
		custom_description = {
			text = pam_reform_order_puppet_acceptance_reason
			scope:recipient.puppeteer ?= scope:actor
		}
	}

	ai_accept = {
		base = -25
		opinion_modifier = {
			opinion_target = scope:actor
			multiplier = 0.5
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 25
			scope:recipient = { has_trait = humble }
			desc = pam_reform_order_humble_reason
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -25
			scope:recipient = { has_trait = ambitious }
			desc = pam_reform_order_ambitious_reason
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -25
			scope:recipient = { has_trait = stubborn }
			desc = pam_reform_order_stubborn_reason
		}
	}

	can_send = {
		OR = {
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = pam_reform_order_select_tenet_tt
				pam_evangelize_personal_tenet_send_option_selected_trigger = yes
			}
			scope:recipient.rite != scope:actor.rite
		}
	}

# Evangelize Personal Tenet Interaction
# The actor spreads their own Personal Tenet to a subject or courtier, increases that tenet's popularity
pam_evangelize_personal_tenet_interaction = {
	category = interaction_category_religion
	icon = evangelize_personal_tenet
	interface_priority = 40
	common_interaction = yes

	use_diplomatic_range = yes
	ai_maybe = yes
	ai_min_reply_days = 1
	ai_max_reply_days = 5
	popup_on_receive = yes
	can_send_despite_rejection = yes
	greeting = positive
	notification_text = pam_evangelize_personal_tenet_interaction_notification

	is_available = {
		is_available_quick = {
			ai = no
			adult = yes
			advanced_ruler = yes
		}
		OR = {
			is_clergy = yes
			has_theological_agent_puppet_trigger = yes
		}
	}
	#Use hook
	send_option = {
		is_valid = {
			scope:actor = {
				has_usable_hook = scope:recipient
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				NOT = { is_imprisoned_by = scope:actor }
			}
		}
		flag = hook
		localization = SCHEME_HOOK
		starts_enabled = { always = yes }
	}
	should_use_extra_icon = {
		scope:actor = { has_usable_hook = scope:recipient }
	}
	extra_icon = "gfx/interface/icons/character_interactions/hook_icon.dds"

	send_options_exclusive = no

	auto_accept = {
		custom_description = {
			text = "spending_hook"
			subject = scope:actor
			object = scope:recipient
			scope:hook = yes
			scope:actor = { has_strong_hook = scope:recipient }
		}
	}

	ai_accept = {
		base = -20
		religion_sway_to_rite_default_modifier = yes
		# modifier = { # More likely for secular rulers to accept during instability
		# 	add = 20
		# 	situation:the_christian_church ?= {
		# 		OR = {
		# 			situation_participant_group:christian_regular_main_power_rulers = {
		# 				participant_group_has_character = scope:recipient
		# 				has_participant_group_parameter = the_christian_church_easier_sway_to_rite
		# 			}
		# 			situation_participant_group:christian_regular_secondary_powers_rulers = {
		# 				participant_group_has_character = scope:recipient
		# 				has_participant_group_parameter = the_christian_church_easier_sway_to_rite
		# 			}
		# 		}
		# 	}
		# 	desc = CHRISTIAN_CHURCH_PHASE_REASON
		# }
		modifier = {
			add = -50
			pam_evangelize_recipient_desires_selected_tenet_trigger = no
			desc = pam_evangelize_recipient_undesired_selected_tenet_reason
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -50
			pam_evangelize_selected_tenet_prohibited_trigger = yes
			desc = pam_evangelize_recipient_prohibited_selected_tenet_reason
		}
		modifier = {
			add = ecclesiastical_domicile_acceptance_value_1
			desc = ESTATE_EVANGELIZE_REASON
			scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
				domicile ?= {
					has_domicile_parameter = estate_easier_evangelize_1
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = ecclesiastical_domicile_acceptance_value_2
			desc = ESTATE_EVANGELIZE_REASON
			scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
				domicile ?= {
					has_domicile_parameter = estate_easier_evangelize_2
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = ecclesiastical_domicile_acceptance_value_3
			desc = ESTATE_EVANGELIZE_REASON
			scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
				domicile ?= {
					has_domicile_parameter = estate_easier_evangelize_3
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = ecclesiastical_domicile_acceptance_minor_value_1
			desc = ESTATE_EVANGELIZE_HOMILIARY_REASON
			scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
				domicile ?= {
					has_domicile_parameter = estate_evangelize_homiliary_1
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = ecclesiastical_domicile_acceptance_minor_value_2
			desc = ESTATE_EVANGELIZE_HOMILIARY_REASON
			scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
				domicile ?= {
					has_domicile_parameter = estate_evangelize_homiliary_2
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = ecclesiastical_domicile_acceptance_minor_value_3
			desc = ESTATE_EVANGELIZE_HOMILIARY_REASON
			scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
				domicile ?= {
					has_domicile_parameter = estate_evangelize_homiliary_3
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = ecclesiastical_domicile_acceptance_micro_value_1
			desc = ESTATE_EVANGELIZE_ARCHIVES_REASON
			scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
				domicile ?= {
					has_domicile_parameter = estate_evangelize_archives_1
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = ecclesiastical_domicile_acceptance_micro_value_2
			desc = ESTATE_EVANGELIZE_ARCHIVES_REASON
			scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
				domicile ?= {
					has_domicile_parameter = estate_evangelize_archives_2
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = ecclesiastical_domicile_acceptance_micro_value_3
			desc = ESTATE_EVANGELIZE_ARCHIVES_REASON
			scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
				domicile ?= {
					has_domicile_parameter = estate_evangelize_archives_3
				}
			}
		}
		ecclesiastical_domicile_cathedral_awe_modifier = yes
	}

	desc = pam_evangelize_personal_tenet_interaction_desc

	is_shown = {
		scope:recipient = {
			this != scope:actor
			faith ?= scope:actor.faith
			is_available_quick = {
				adult = yes
				advanced_ruler = yes
			}
		}
		scope:actor = {
			any_personal_tenet = {
				scope:recipient = {
					NOT = { has_personal_tenet = prev }
				}
			}
		}
	}

	cooldown_against_recipient = { years = 5 }

	cost = {
		piety = {
			value = minor_piety_value
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:actor = { has_character_modifier = pam_doctrinal_clarity_modifier }
				}
				multiply = 0.5
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
						domicile ?= { has_domicile_parameter = estate_cheaper_evangelize }
					}
				}
				multiply = ecclesiastical_domicile_cheaper_evangelize_mult
			}
			#no cost when forced from interaction
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:holy_order_leader ?= scope:actor
					exists = scope:order_tenet
				}
				multiply = 0
			}
		}
	}

	can_send = {
		OR = {
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = pam_evangelize_select_personal_tenet_tt
				pam_evangelize_personal_tenet_send_option_selected_trigger = yes
			}
			#holy order interaction uses order tenet
			AND = {
				scope:holy_order_leader ?= scope:actor
				exists = scope:order_tenet
			}
		}
	}

	auto_accept = no

	on_send = {
		#holy order interaction uses order tenet
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:holy_order_leader ?= scope:actor
				exists = scope:order_tenet
			}
			scope:order_tenet = {
				save_scope_as = pam_evangelize_tenet
			}
		}
		else = {
			pam_save_selected_interaction_tenet_effect = yes
			scope:pam_selected_interaction_tenet = { save_scope_as = pam_evangelize_tenet }
		}
	}

	on_accept = {
		# Needed for character interaction interface to show a proper tooltip when send options are not selected
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { exists = scope:pam_evangelize_tenet }
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				custom_tooltip = pam_evangelize_personal_tenet_interaction_tooltip
				custom_tooltip = pam_evangelize_personal_tenet_interaction_popularity_tooltip
			}
			scope:actor = {
				show_as_tooltip = {
					change_spiritual_fulfillment = miniscule_spiritual_fulfillment_value
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						scope:hook = yes
						has_usable_hook = scope:recipient
					}
					show_as_tooltip = { use_hook = scope:recipient }
				}
				custom_tooltip = pam_evangelize_personal_tenet_interaction_prohibited_secret_tooltip
			}
		}
		else = {
			scope:actor = {
				send_interface_toast = {
					type = event_toast_effect_good
					title = pam_evangelize_personal_tenet_interaction_toast
					left_icon = scope:actor
					right_icon = scope:recipient
					pam_evangelize_personal_tenet_effect = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}
	on_decline = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { exists = scope:pam_evangelize_tenet }
			}
			scope:actor = {
				change_spiritual_fulfillment = miniscule_spiritual_fulfillment_loss
				custom_tooltip = pam_evangelize_personal_tenet_interaction_prohibited_secret_tooltip
			}
		}
		else = {
			scope:actor = {
				show_as_tooltip = {
					pam_evangelize_personal_tenet_decline_effect = yes
				}
				send_interface_toast = {
					type = event_toast_effect_bad
					title = pam_evangelize_personal_tenet_interaction_toast_declined
					left_icon = scope:actor
					right_icon = scope:recipient
					pam_evangelize_personal_tenet_decline_effect = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Players pick a tenet through send_options, and the AI only has the hook so on_send picks a random tenet
pam_ai_evangelize_personal_tenet_interaction = {
	category = interaction_category_religion
	icon = evangelize_personal_tenet
	interface_priority = 40
	hidden = yes

	use_diplomatic_range = yes
	ai_maybe = yes
	ai_min_reply_days = 1
	ai_max_reply_days = 5
	popup_on_receive = yes
	can_send_despite_rejection = yes
	greeting = positive
	notification_text = pam_evangelize_personal_tenet_interaction_notification

	#Use hook
	send_option = {
		is_valid = {
			scope:actor = {
				has_usable_hook = scope:recipient
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				NOT = { is_imprisoned_by = scope:actor }
			}
		}
		flag = hook
		localization = SCHEME_HOOK
		starts_enabled = { always = yes }
	}
	should_use_extra_icon = {
		scope:actor = { has_usable_hook = scope:recipient }
	}
	extra_icon = "gfx/interface/icons/character_interactions/hook_icon.dds"

	send_options_exclusive = no

	auto_accept = {
		custom_description = {
			text = "spending_hook"
			subject = scope:actor
			object = scope:recipient
			scope:hook = yes
			scope:actor = { has_strong_hook = scope:recipient }
		}
	}

	ai_accept = {
		base = -20
		religion_sway_to_rite_default_modifier = yes
		# modifier = { # More likely for secular rulers to accept during instability
		# 	add = 20
		# 	situation:the_christian_church ?= {
		# 		OR = {
		# 			situation_participant_group:christian_regular_main_power_rulers = {
		# 				participant_group_has_character = scope:recipient
		# 				has_participant_group_parameter = the_christian_church_easier_sway_to_rite
		# 			}
		# 			situation_participant_group:christian_regular_secondary_powers_rulers = {
		# 				participant_group_has_character = scope:recipient
		# 				has_participant_group_parameter = the_christian_church_easier_sway_to_rite
		# 			}
		# 		}
		# 	}
		# 	desc = CHRISTIAN_CHURCH_PHASE_REASON
		# }
		modifier = {
			add = -50
			pam_evangelize_recipient_desires_selected_tenet_trigger = no
			desc = pam_evangelize_recipient_undesired_selected_tenet_reason
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -50
			pam_evangelize_selected_tenet_prohibited_trigger = yes
			desc = pam_evangelize_recipient_prohibited_selected_tenet_reason
		}
		modifier = {
			add = ecclesiastical_domicile_acceptance_value_1
			desc = ESTATE_EVANGELIZE_REASON
			scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
				domicile ?= {
					has_domicile_parameter = estate_easier_evangelize_1
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = ecclesiastical_domicile_acceptance_value_2
			desc = ESTATE_EVANGELIZE_REASON
			scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
				domicile ?= {
					has_domicile_parameter = estate_easier_evangelize_2
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = ecclesiastical_domicile_acceptance_value_3
			desc = ESTATE_EVANGELIZE_REASON
			scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
				domicile ?= {
					has_domicile_parameter = estate_easier_evangelize_3
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = ecclesiastical_domicile_acceptance_minor_value_1
			desc = ESTATE_EVANGELIZE_HOMILIARY_REASON
			scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
				domicile ?= {
					has_domicile_parameter = estate_evangelize_homiliary_1
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = ecclesiastical_domicile_acceptance_minor_value_2
			desc = ESTATE_EVANGELIZE_HOMILIARY_REASON
			scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
				domicile ?= {
					has_domicile_parameter = estate_evangelize_homiliary_2
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = ecclesiastical_domicile_acceptance_minor_value_3
			desc = ESTATE_EVANGELIZE_HOMILIARY_REASON
			scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
				domicile ?= {
					has_domicile_parameter = estate_evangelize_homiliary_3
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = ecclesiastical_domicile_acceptance_micro_value_1
			desc = ESTATE_EVANGELIZE_ARCHIVES_REASON
			scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
				domicile ?= {
					has_domicile_parameter = estate_evangelize_archives_1
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = ecclesiastical_domicile_acceptance_micro_value_2
			desc = ESTATE_EVANGELIZE_ARCHIVES_REASON
			scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
				domicile ?= {
					has_domicile_parameter = estate_evangelize_archives_2
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = ecclesiastical_domicile_acceptance_micro_value_3
			desc = ESTATE_EVANGELIZE_ARCHIVES_REASON
			scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
				domicile ?= {
					has_domicile_parameter = estate_evangelize_archives_3
				}
			}
		}
		ecclesiastical_domicile_cathedral_awe_modifier = yes
	}

	ai_targets = {
		ai_recipients = family
		max = 5
	}

	ai_targets = {
		ai_recipients = courtiers
		max = 5
	}

	ai_targets = {
		ai_recipients = vassals
		ai_recipients = tributaries
		ai_recipients = clerical_region_rulers_in_realm
		ai_recipients = rulers_in_clerical_region
		ai_recipients = puppets
		ai_recipients = subordinates
		ai_recipients = councillors
		max = 20
	}

	ai_target_quick_trigger = {
		adult = yes
	}

	# Four times slower than usual: each firing pays a one-off-sized popularity gain
	ai_frequency_by_tier = {
		barony = 0
		county = 240
		duchy = 120
		kingdom = 48
		empire = 48
		hegemony = 48
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		base = 25
		modifier = {
			scope:hook = yes
			add = 50
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 50
			scope:actor = {
				has_trait = zealous
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -50
			scope:actor = {
				has_trait = cynical
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 20
			scope:actor = {
				is_allied_to = scope:recipient
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 10
			scope:actor = {
				has_relation_best_friend = scope:recipient
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 10
			scope:recipient = {
				opinion = {
					target = scope:actor
					value >= 65
				}
			}
		}
		# modifier = { # More likely to happen during Instability
		# 	add = 15
		# 	scope:actor = { like_christianity_religion_trigger = yes } # Warcraft
		# 	situation:the_christian_church ?= {
		# 		has_situation_top_phase_parameter = the_christian_church_easier_sway_to_rite
		# 	}
		# }
		modifier = { # More likely if the government is religious
			add = 50
			scope:actor = {
				government_is_religious_trigger = yes
			}
		}
		modifier = { # Heresiarchs are able to spread their rites easily
			add = 1000
			scope:actor = {
				OR = {
					has_trait = heresiarch
					has_trait = herald
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = { # Try to rigorously convert your heir so all efforts won't go to waste on succession
			add = 100
			scope:recipient = {
				is_primary_heir_of = scope:actor
			}
		}
		modifier = { # More likely against close family members
			add = 50
			scope:recipient = {
				is_close_family_of = scope:actor
			}
		}
		modifier = { # Prefer higher tiers
			add = scope:recipient.highest_held_title_tier
		}
		modifier = { # Converts clinging to a Popular belief are the ones worth luring away
			add = -20
			scope:recipient = {
				NOT = {
					any_personal_tenet = {
						pam_tenet_is_at_least_popular_trigger = { CHARACTER = scope:recipient }
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	desc = pam_evangelize_personal_tenet_interaction_desc

	# Actor-only gating (cheap, runs often for AI/menu).
	is_available = {
		is_available_quick = {
			ai = yes
			adult = yes
			advanced_ruler = yes
		}
		is_clergy = yes # The puppet route is reserved for players; the AI preaches through its own clergy
		faith ?= { has_tenet_popularity_trigger = yes }
		has_pam_dlc_trigger = yes
		# Actor scope: drop out in is_available once every tenet is already Very Popular
		save_temporary_scope_as = pam_evangelize_popularity_actor
		any_personal_tenet = {
			save_temporary_scope_as = pam_evangelize_tenet_check
			scope:pam_evangelize_popularity_actor = {
				pam_evangelize_tenet_below_very_popular_trigger = yes
			}
		}
	}

	is_shown = {
		scope:actor = {
			any_personal_tenet = {
				save_temporary_scope_as = pam_evangelize_tenet_check
				scope:recipient = {
					NOT = { has_personal_tenet = scope:pam_evangelize_tenet_check }
				}
				scope:actor = {
					pam_evangelize_tenet_below_very_popular_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			this != scope:actor
			faith ?= scope:actor.faith
			is_available_quick = {
				adult = yes
				advanced_ruler = yes
			}
		}
	}

	cooldown_against_recipient = { years = 5 }

	cost = {
		piety = {
			value = minor_piety_value
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
						domicile ?= { has_domicile_parameter = estate_cheaper_evangelize }
					}
				}
				multiply = ecclesiastical_domicile_cheaper_evangelize_mult
			}
		}
	}

	auto_accept = no

	on_send = {
		# A random tenet uses the same two is_shown checks, so there is always one left to pick
		scope:actor = {
			random_personal_tenet = {
				limit = {
					save_temporary_scope_as = pam_evangelize_tenet_check
					scope:recipient = {
						NOT = { has_personal_tenet = scope:pam_evangelize_tenet_check }
					}
					scope:actor = {
						pam_evangelize_tenet_below_very_popular_trigger = yes
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = pam_evangelize_tenet
			}
		}

	}
	on_accept = {
		scope:actor = {
			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_good
				title = pam_evangelize_personal_tenet_interaction_toast
				left_icon = scope:actor
				right_icon = scope:recipient
				pam_evangelize_personal_tenet_effect = yes
			}
		}
	}
	on_decline = {
		scope:actor = {
			show_as_tooltip = {
				pam_evangelize_personal_tenet_decline_effect = yes
			}
			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_bad
				title = pam_evangelize_personal_tenet_interaction_toast_declined
				left_icon = scope:actor
				right_icon = scope:recipient
				pam_evangelize_personal_tenet_decline_effect = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

# HoF only, on HoRs
# Replace a doctrine of other rite in your faith by your rite's in same group
pam_reaffirm_doctrine_interaction = {
	category = interaction_category_religion
	icon = reaffirm_doctrine
	interface_priority = 40
	common_interaction = yes

	use_diplomatic_range = yes
	ai_maybe = yes
	ai_min_reply_days = 1
	ai_max_reply_days = 5
	popup_on_receive = yes
	can_send_despite_rejection = yes
	greeting = positive
	notification_text = pam_reaffirm_doctrine_interaction_notification
	desc = pam_reaffirm_doctrine_interaction_desc

	is_available = {
		faith.religious_head ?= this
		is_available_adult = yes
	}
	#Use hook
	send_option = {
		is_valid = {
			scope:actor = {
				has_usable_hook = scope:recipient
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				NOT = { is_imprisoned_by = scope:actor }
			}
		}
		flag = hook
		localization = SCHEME_HOOK
		starts_enabled = { always = yes }
	}
	should_use_extra_icon = {
		scope:actor = { has_usable_hook = scope:recipient }
	}
	extra_icon = "gfx/interface/icons/character_interactions/hook_icon.dds"

	send_options_exclusive = no

	auto_accept = {
		custom_description = {
			text = "spending_hook"
			subject = scope:actor
			object = scope:recipient
			scope:hook = yes
			scope:actor = { has_strong_hook = scope:recipient }
		}
	}

	ai_accept = {
		base = -100

		modifier = {
			add = {
				value = scope:actor.piety_level
				subtract = scope:recipient.piety_level
				multiply = 15
				min = 0
			}
			scope:actor.piety_level > scope:recipient.piety_level
			desc = spiritual_counsel_ai_priest_pious
		}
		modifier = { # Heralds are more convincing
			add = 75
			scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
				has_trait = herald
			}
			desc = I_HAVE_HERALD_TRAIT
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -75
			scope:recipient = {
				has_trait = herald
			}
			desc = I_HAVE_HERALD_TRAIT
		}
		modifier = { # Heresiarchs are more convincing
			add = 30
			scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
				has_trait = heresiarch
			}
			desc = I_HAVE_HERESIARCH_TRAIT
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -100
			scope:recipient = {
				has_trait = heresiarch
			}
			desc = ASK_FOR_CONVERSION_RECIPIENT_IS_HERESIARCH
		}
		opinion_modifier = {
			who = scope:recipient
			opinion_target = scope:puppet_or_actor
			multiplier = 0.5
			desc = AI_OPINION_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 20
			scope:recipient = {
				is_close_family_of = scope:puppet_or_actor
			}
			desc = CLOSE_FAMILY_REASON
		}

		modifier = {
			add = 10
			scope:recipient = {
				NOT = { is_close_family_of = scope:puppet_or_actor }
				is_extended_family_of = scope:puppet_or_actor
			}
			desc = EXTENDED_FAMILY_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			scope:recipient = {
				has_relation_lover = scope:puppet_or_actor
			}
			add = {
				value = 25
			}
			desc = WE_ARE_LOVERS
		}
		modifier = {
			scope:recipient = {
				has_relation_friend = scope:puppet_or_actor
			}
			add = {
				value = 25
			}
			desc = WE_ARE_FRIENDS
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -40
			scope:recipient = {
				has_trait = zealous
			}
			desc = INTERACTION_ZEALOUS
		}
		modifier = {
			add = {
				value = scope:puppet_or_actor.learning
				subtract = scope:recipient.learning
			}
			desc = ASK_FOR_CONVERSION_RECIPIENT_LEARNING
		}
		modifier = {
			add = intimidated_halved_reason_value
			scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
				is_landless_adventurer = no
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				has_dread_level_towards = {
					target = scope:puppet_or_actor
					level = 1
				}
			}
			desc = INTIMIDATED_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			add = cowed_halved_reason_value
			scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
				is_landless_adventurer = no
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				has_dread_level_towards = {
					target = scope:puppet_or_actor
					level = 2
				}
			}
			desc = COWED_REASON
		}
		modifier = { # Title tier difference
			add = {
				value = scope:puppet_or_actor.highest_held_title_tier
				subtract = scope:recipient.highest_held_title_tier
				multiply = 20
			}
			scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
				OR = {
					highest_held_title_tier > scope:recipient.highest_held_title_tier
					highest_held_title_tier < scope:recipient.highest_held_title_tier
				}
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				trigger_if = { # If clerical region holder, works against any ruler
					limit = {
						scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
							any_held_title = { has_clerical_region = yes }
						}
					}
					is_ruler = yes
				}
				trigger_else = { # If secular holder, only works against vassals
					limit = {
						scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
							NOT = {
								any_held_title = { has_clerical_region = yes }
							}
						}
					}
					is_vassal_of = scope:puppet_or_actor
				}
			}
			desc = RELATIVE_TITLE_TIER_REASON
		}
		modifier = { # More likely if the recipient's capital is already of our rite
			add = 15
			scope:recipient = {
				is_ruler = yes
				capital_county.rite = scope:puppet_or_actor.rite
			}
			desc = THEY_HAVE_CAPITAL_WITH_SAME_RITE_REASON
		}
		modifier = { # We don't share the same realm (secular players only)
			add = -20
			scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
				is_ai = no
				any_held_title = { has_clerical_region = no }
				this.top_liege != scope:recipient.top_liege
			}
			desc = not_same_realm_value_tt
		}
		# modifier = { # More likely for secular rulers to accept during instability
		# 	add = 10
		# 	situation:the_christian_church ?= {
		# 		OR = {
		# 			situation_participant_group:christian_regular_main_power_rulers = {
		# 				participant_group_has_character = scope:recipient
		# 				has_participant_group_parameter = the_christian_church_easier_sway_to_rite
		# 			}
		# 			situation_participant_group:christian_regular_secondary_powers_rulers = {
		# 				participant_group_has_character = scope:recipient
		# 				has_participant_group_parameter = the_christian_church_easier_sway_to_rite
		# 			}
		# 		}
		# 	}
		# 	desc = CHRISTIAN_CHURCH_PHASE_REASON
		# }
		modifier = {
			add = -1000
			switch = {
				trigger = yes
				scope:doctrine_bastardry_all = {
					scope:recipient = { has_trait = bastard }
				}
				scope:doctrine_homosexuality_crime = {
					scope:recipient = {
						OR = {
							is_attracted_to_gender_of = this
							is_sodomite_trigger = yes
						}
					}
				}
				scope:doctrine_homosexuality_shunned = {
					scope:recipient = {
						OR = {
							is_attracted_to_gender_of = this
							is_sodomite_trigger = yes
						}
						NOT = { rite = { rite_has_doctrine = doctrine_homosexuality_crime } }
					}
				}
				scope:doctrine_deviancy_crime = {
					scope:recipient = { is_deviant_trigger = yes }
				}
				scope:doctrine_deviancy_shunned = {
					scope:recipient = {
						is_deviant_trigger = yes
						NOT = { rite = { rite_has_doctrine = doctrine_deviancy_crime } }
					}
				}
				scope:doctrine_adultery_men_shunned = {
					scope:recipient = {
						is_female = no
						is_adulterer_or_fornicator_trigger = yes
						NOT = { rite = { rite_has_doctrine = doctrine_adultery_men_crime } }
					}
				}
				scope:doctrine_adultery_men_crime = {
					scope:recipient = {
						is_female = no
						is_adulterer_or_fornicator_trigger = yes
					}
				}
				scope:doctrine_adultery_women_shunned = {
					scope:recipient = {
						is_female = yes
						is_adulterer_or_fornicator_trigger = yes
						NOT = { rite = { rite_has_doctrine = doctrine_adultery_women_crime } }
					}
				}
				scope:doctrine_adultery_women_crime = {
					scope:recipient = {
						is_female = yes
						is_adulterer_or_fornicator_trigger = yes
					}
				}
				scope:doctrine_witchcraft_crime = {
					scope:recipient = { is_witch_trigger = yes }
				}
				scope:doctrine_witchcraft_shunned = {
					scope:recipient = {
						is_witch_trigger = yes
						NOT = { rite = { rite_has_doctrine = doctrine_witchcraft_crime } }
					}
				}

				scope:doctrine_kinslaying_any_dynasty_member_crime = {
					scope:recipient = { pam_reaffirm_doctrine_kinslayer_decline_trigger = yes }
				}
				scope:doctrine_kinslaying_extended_family_crime = {
					scope:recipient = { pam_reaffirm_doctrine_kinslayer_decline_trigger = yes }
				}
				scope:doctrine_kinslaying_close_kin_crime = {
					scope:recipient = { pam_reaffirm_doctrine_kinslayer_decline_trigger = yes }
				}
				scope:doctrine_kinslaying_shunned = {
					scope:recipient = {
						rite = { rite_has_doctrine = doctrine_kinslaying_accepted }
						OR = {
							has_trait = kinslayer
							any_secret = {
								type = secret_murder
								secret_target = { save_temporary_scope_as = target }
								scope:recipient = {
									murdering_character_is_kinslaying_in_rite_trigger = {
										CHARACTER = scope:target
										RITE = scope:actor.rite
									}
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}

				scope:doctrine_gender_male_dominated = {
					scope:recipient = { is_female = yes }
				}
				scope:doctrine_gender_female_dominated = {
					scope:recipient = { is_female = no }
				}

				scope:doctrine_consanguinity_dynastic = {
					scope:recipient = { pam_reaffirm_doctrine_consanguinity_decline_trigger = yes }

					scope:recipient = { is_female = no }

					scope:recipient = { is_married = yes }

					scope:recipient = { has_trait = pilgrim }

				fallback = { always = no }
			}
			desc = pam_reaffirm_doctrine_recipient_undesired_reason
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 100
			switch = {
				trigger = yes
				scope:doctrine_homosexuality_accepted = {

	is_shown = {
		scope:recipient = {
			is_adult = yes
			faith ?= scope:actor.faith
			rite != scope:actor.rite
			rite.head_of_rite ?= this
		}
		any_doctrine = {
			scope:actor.rite ?= {
				rite_has_doctrine = prev
			}
			scope:recipient.rite ?= {
				NOT = { rite_has_doctrine = prev }
			}
			pam_doctrine_can_be_reaffirm = yes
		}
	}

	cooldown_against_recipient = { years = 5 }
