	on_accept = {
		# Needed for character interaction interface to show a proper tooltip when send options are not selected
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { exists = scope:pam_reaffirm_doctrine }
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				custom_tooltip = pam_reaffirm_doctrine_interaction_tooltip
			}
		}
		else = {
			scope:actor = {
				send_interface_toast = {
					type = event_toast_effect_good
					title = pam_reaffirm_doctrine_interaction_toast
					left_icon = scope:actor
					right_icon = scope:recipient

					scope:recipient = {
						rite = {
							change_rite_doctrine = scope:pam_reaffirm_doctrine
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							scope:hook = yes
							has_usable_hook = scope:recipient
						}
						use_hook = scope:recipient
					}
					scope:actor = {
						add_opinion = {
							target = scope:recipient
							modifier = pleased_opinion
							opinion = 30
						}
					}
					change_spiritual_fulfillment = miniscule_spiritual_fulfillment_value
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	on_decline = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { exists = scope:pam_reaffirm_doctrine }
			}
			scope:actor = {
				change_spiritual_fulfillment = miniscule_spiritual_fulfillment_loss
			}
		}
		else = {
			scope:actor = {
				send_interface_toast = {
					type = event_toast_effect_bad
					title = pam_reaffirm_doctrine_interaction_toast_declined
					left_icon = scope:actor
					right_icon = scope:recipient

					add_opinion = {
						modifier = refusal_opinion
						target = scope:recipient
						opinion = -15
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	ai_targets = {
		ai_recipients = subordinates
	}

	ai_frequency_by_tier = {
		barony = 0
		county = 0
		duchy = 300
		kingdom = 300
		empire = 300
		hegemony = 300
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		base = 25
		modifier = {
			scope:hook = yes
			add = 50
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 50
			scope:actor = {
				has_trait = zealous
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -50
			scope:actor = {
				has_trait = cynical
			}
		}
		# modifier = { # More likely to happen during Instability
		# 	add = 15
		# 	scope:actor = { like_christianity_religion_trigger = yes } # Warcraft
		# 	situation:the_christian_church ?= {
		# 		has_situation_top_phase_parameter = the_christian_church_easier_sway_to_rite
		# 	}
		# }
		modifier = { # More likely if the government is religious
			add = 50
			scope:actor = {
				government_is_religious_trigger = yes
			}
		}
		modifier = { # Heresiarchs are able to spread their rites easily
			add = 1000
			scope:actor = {
				OR = {
					has_trait = heresiarch
					has_trait = herald
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = { # target rite has high divergence
			add = "scope:actor.rite.divergence(scope:recipient.rite)"
		}
		modifier = { # Not worth leaning on rites that have barely drifted from ours
			factor = 0
			"scope:actor.rite.divergence(scope:recipient.rite)" < massive_rite_divergence_value
		}
	}

			localization = doctrine_monasticism_encouraged_descriptive_name
		}
		send_option = {
			is_shown = {
				scope:actor.rite = {
					has_different_doctrine_with_rite_trigger = {
						RITE = scope:recipient.rite
						DOCTRINE = doctrine_monasticism_accepted
					}
				}
			}
			is_valid = {
				pam_reaffirm_doctrine_send_option_valid_trigger = { DOCTRINE = doctrine_monasticism_accepted }
			}
			flag = doctrine_monasticism_accepted
			localization = doctrine_monasticism_accepted_descriptive_name
		}
		send_option = {
			is_shown = {
				scope:actor.rite = {
					has_different_doctrine_with_rite_trigger = {
						RITE = scope:recipient.rite
						DOCTRINE = doctrine_monasticism_absent
					}
				}
			}
			is_valid = {
				pam_reaffirm_doctrine_send_option_valid_trigger = { DOCTRINE = doctrine_monasticism_absent }
			}
			flag = doctrine_monasticism_absent
			localization = doctrine_monasticism_absent_descriptive_name
		}
		send_option = {
			is_shown = {
				scope:actor.rite = {
					has_different_doctrine_with_rite_trigger = {
						RITE = scope:recipient.rite
						DOCTRINE = doctrine_monasticism_forbidden
					}
				}
			}
			is_valid = {
				pam_reaffirm_doctrine_send_option_valid_trigger = { DOCTRINE = doctrine_monasticism_forbidden }
			}
			flag = doctrine_monasticism_forbidden
			localization = doctrine_monasticism_forbidden_descriptive_name
		}

		send_option = {
			is_shown = {
				scope:actor.rite = {
					has_different_doctrine_with_rite_trigger = {
						RITE = scope:recipient.rite
						DOCTRINE = doctrine_sacraments_central
					}
				}
			}
			is_valid = {
				pam_reaffirm_doctrine_send_option_valid_trigger = { DOCTRINE = doctrine_sacraments_central }
			}
			flag = doctrine_sacraments_central
			localization = doctrine_sacraments_central_name
		}
		send_option = {
			is_shown = {
				scope:actor.rite = {
					has_different_doctrine_with_rite_trigger = {
						RITE = scope:recipient.rite
						DOCTRINE = doctrine_sacraments_absent
					}
				}
			}
			is_valid = {
				pam_reaffirm_doctrine_send_option_valid_trigger = { DOCTRINE = doctrine_sacraments_absent }
			}
			flag = doctrine_sacraments_absent
			localization = doctrine_sacraments_absent_name
		}
	# doctrine options end
}

# Force someone to step down
request_enforce_succession_interaction = {
	category = interaction_category_religion
	icon = icon_clergy_succession
	interface_priority = 38
	common_interaction = no
	notification_text = request_enforce_succession_interaction_notification
	greeting = positive

	target_type = title
	target_filter = claimed_titles
	target_source = actor

	use_diplomatic_range = yes
	ai_min_reply_days = 1
	ai_max_reply_days = 5
	popup_on_receive = yes

	desc = request_enforce_succession_interaction_desc

	cooldown_against_recipient = { years = 5 }

	is_available = {
		has_pam_dlc_trigger = yes
	}

	is_shown = {
		scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
			like_christianity_religion_trigger = yes # Warcraft
			any_claim = { } # You have a claim to force succession for
			NOT = {
				government_has_flag = government_is_theocracy
			}
			pam_clergy_interactions_faith_trigger = yes
			is_ruler = yes
		}
		scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
			NOT = {
				is_at_war_with = scope:recipient
			}
			capital_county.clerical_region_title.holder ?= scope:recipient
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			pam_request_legitimacy_priest_trigger = { CHARACTER = scope:puppet_or_actor }
			any_held_title = {
				has_clerical_region = yes
			}
		}
		pam_should_bother_clergy_interaction_trigger = { PRIEST = scope:recipient }
	}

	is_valid_showing_failures_only = {
		scope:puppet_or_actor = {
			is_available_quick = {
				incapable = no
				imprisoned = no
				hostage = no
				ruler = yes
			}
			age >= 12
			legitimacy_level >= 3
			
			NOR = {
				has_trait = excommunicated
				has_character_modifier = promised_pilgrimage_to_clergy_modifier
			}
			custom_tooltip = { # You cannot request this if you are sponsoring an antipope _unless_ the recipient is the antipope
				text = actor_is_antipope_sponsor_tt
				scope:actor = { pam_is_antipope_sponsor_trigger = no }
				scope:recipient = { pam_is_antipope_trigger = no }
			}
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			is_available_quick = {
				incapable = no
				imprisoned = no
				hostage = no
				ruler = yes
				adult = yes
			}
			NOT = {
				has_trait = excommunicated
			}
			highest_held_title_tier >= tier_duchy
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = request_enforce_succession_must_be_archbishop_tt
				scope:puppet_or_actor.capital_county.clerical_region_title.holder ?= this
			}
		}
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes }
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				piety >= medium_piety_value
			}
		}
	}

	has_valid_target_showing_failures_only = {
		exists = scope:target
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = request_enforce_succession_no_valid_title_tt
			pam_valid_enforce_succession_title_trigger = {
				TITLE = scope:target
				ACTOR = scope:puppet_or_actor
			}
		}
	}

	can_be_picked_title = {
		pam_valid_enforce_succession_title_trigger = {
			TITLE = scope:target
			ACTOR = scope:puppet_or_actor
		}
	}

	on_accept = {
		if = {
			limit = { scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes }
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				add_piety = { value = { add = medium_piety_value max = piety multiply = -1 } }
			}
		}
		scope:puppet_or_actor = {
			pam_enforce_title_succession_effect = yes
			trigger_event = pam_interactions_events.0081
			scope:succession_holder = {
				trigger_event = pam_interactions_events.0082
			}
		}
		pam_offer_pilgrimage_effect = {
			ACTOR = scope:puppet_or_actor
			CLERIC = scope:recipient
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_good
				title = request_enforce_succession_recipient_toast
				left_icon = scope:recipient
				right_icon = scope:puppet_or_actor
				if = {
					limit = {
						can_add_hook = {
							target = scope:puppet_or_actor
							type = favor_hook
						}
					}
					add_hook_no_toast = {
						target = scope:puppet_or_actor
						type = favor_hook
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	on_decline = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:recipient = {
					is_ai = no
				}
			}
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				add_opinion = {
					modifier = refusal_opinion
					target = scope:recipient
					opinion = -15
				}
			}
		}
	}

	send_options_exclusive = no

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			pam_actor_rite_has_core_simony_trigger = yes
		}
		is_valid = {
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				short_term_gold >= major_gold_value
			}
		}
		flag = donate_faith
		localization = DONATE_TO_THE_FAITH_INTERACTION_OPTION
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			pam_offer_pilgrimage_option_shown_trigger = { ACTOR = scope:puppet_or_actor }
		}
		is_valid = {
			pam_offer_pilgrimage_option_valid_trigger = { ACTOR = scope:puppet_or_actor }
		}
		flag = offer_pilgrimage
		localization = OFFER_PILGRIMAGE_INTERACTION_OPTION
	}

	cost = {
		piety = {
			value = medium_piety_value
			multiply = pam_peace_of_god_clergy_peacemaking_cost_mult_value
			if = {
				limit = { scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes }
				multiply = 0
			}
		}
		gold = {
			value = 0
			if = {
				limit = { scope:donate_faith = yes }
				add = major_gold_value
			}
		}
	}

	ai_accept = {
		base = -30

		modifier = { # By God Alone - Secular Faith: the petitioner holds the ear of the clergy
			add = 25
			desc = pam_sf_ear_of_clergy_accept_reason
			trigger = {
				scope:puppet_or_actor = { has_character_modifier = pam_sf_ear_of_clergy_modifier }
			}
		}

		modifier = { # Peace of God: settling succession is preferable to a war over it
			add = 40
			desc = pam_peace_of_god_clergy_peacemaking_accept_reason
			trigger = {
				scope:recipient.rite ?= {
					rite_has_parameter = tenet_peace_of_god_clergy_peacemaking
				}
			}
		}

		opinion_modifier = {
			who = scope:recipient
			opinion_target = scope:actor
			multiplier = 0.5
			desc = AI_OPINION_REASON
		}

		modifier = {
			exists = scope:target.holder
			scope:target.holder = {
				NOT = {
					is_in_same_clerical_region_as_trigger = { TARGET = scope:actor }
				}
			}
			add = -20
			desc = REQUEST_ENFORCE_SUCCESSION_HOLDER_OUTSIDE_DIOCESE_REASON
		}
		
		modifier = {
			exists = scope:actor.liege
			scope:target.holder ?= scope:actor.liege
			add = -50
			desc = REQUEST_ENFORCE_SUCCESSION_LIEGE_TITLE_REASON
		}
		
		modifier = {
			scope:actor = {
				OR = {
					has_trait = saint
					has_trait = herald
					#has_trait = saoshyant_descendant # Warcraft - trait removed
					has_trait = order_member
					has_trait = faith_warrior
					#has_trait = saoshyant # Warcraft - trait removed
					has_trait = paragon
					has_trait = savior
					has_trait = crusader_king
				}
			}
			add = 100
			desc = VERY_PIOUS_TRAITS_REASON
		}
		
		modifier = {
			scope:actor = {
				OR = {
					has_trait = zealous
					has_trait = theologian
					has_trait = divine_blood
					has_trait = consecrated_blood
					has_trait = sayyid
				}
			}
			add = 30
			desc = PIOUS_TRAITS_REASON
		}
	
		# Fs
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			scope:recipient = {
				has_relation_friend = scope:actor
			}
			desc = WE_ARE_FRIENDS
		}
		# BFFs
		modifier = {
			add = 60
			scope:recipient = {
				has_relation_best_friend = scope:actor
			}
			desc = WE_ARE_BEST_FRIENDS
		}
		# Take dread into account
		modifier = {
			add = {
				value = 25
				if = {
					limit = {
						scope:recipient = {
							target_is_liege_or_above = scope:actor
						}
					}
					multiply = 2
				}
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				has_dread_level_towards = {
					target = scope:actor
					level = 1
				}
			}
			desc = INTIMIDATED_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			add = {
				value = 50
				if = {
					limit = {
						scope:recipient = {
							target_is_liege_or_above = scope:actor
						}
					}
					multiply = 2
				}
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				has_dread_level_towards = {
					target = scope:actor
					level = 2
				}
			}
			desc = COWED_REASON
		}
		
		modifier = {
			add = 100
			scope:actor.puppet:theological_agent_puppet ?= scope:recipient
			desc = IS_YOUR_PUPPET
		}
		
		modifier = {
			scope:actor.num_sinful_traits > 0
			add = {
				value = -10
				multiply = scope:actor.num_sinful_traits
			}
			desc = I_AM_SINFUL
		}

		modifier = {
			exists = scope:target.holder
			scope:target.holder = {
				num_sinful_traits > 0
			}
			add = {
				value = 10
				multiply = scope:target.holder.num_sinful_traits
			}
			desc = REQUEST_ENFORCE_SUCCESSION_HOLDER_SINFUL_REASON
		}

		modifier = {
			exists = scope:target.holder
			add = {
				value = scope:actor.piety_level
				subtract = scope:target.holder.piety_level
				multiply = 15
				min = 0
			}
			scope:actor.piety_level > scope:target.holder.piety_level
			desc = REQUEST_ENFORCE_SUCCESSION_ACTOR_MORE_PIOUS_REASON
		}

		modifier = {
			exists = scope:target.holder
			add = {
				value = scope:target.holder.piety_level
				subtract = scope:actor.piety_level
				multiply = -15
				min = 0
			}
			scope:target.holder.piety_level > scope:actor.piety_level
			desc = REQUEST_ENFORCE_SUCCESSION_HOLDER_MORE_PIOUS_REASON
		}

		modifier = {
			exists = scope:target.holder
			scope:target.holder = {
				NOT = {
					any_child = {
						is_alive = yes
					}
				}
			}
			add = 30
			desc = REQUEST_ENFORCE_SUCCESSION_HOLDER_CHILDLESS_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			add = {
				value = 25
				if = {
					limit = {
						pam_actor_rite_has_core_simony_trigger = yes
					}
					multiply = 5
				}
			}
			scope:donate_faith = yes
			scope:puppet_or_actor.major_gold_value < scope:recipient.minor_gold_value
			desc = DONATE_FAITH_INTERACTION_OPTION_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			add = {
				value = 50
				if = {
					limit = {
						pam_actor_rite_has_core_simony_trigger = yes
					}
					multiply = 5
				}
			}
			scope:donate_faith = yes
			scope:puppet_or_actor.major_gold_value < scope:recipient.major_gold_value
			scope:puppet_or_actor.major_gold_value >= scope:recipient.minor_gold_value
			desc = DONATE_FAITH_INTERACTION_OPTION_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			add = {
				value = 75
				if = {
					limit = {
						pam_actor_rite_has_core_simony_trigger = yes
					}
					multiply = 5
				}
			}
			scope:donate_faith = yes
			scope:puppet_or_actor.major_gold_value >= scope:recipient.major_gold_value
			scope:puppet_or_actor.major_gold_value < scope:recipient.monumental_gold_value
			desc = DONATE_FAITH_INTERACTION_OPTION_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			add = {
				value = 100
				if = {
					limit = {
						pam_actor_rite_has_core_simony_trigger = yes
					}
					multiply = 5
				}
			}
			scope:donate_faith = yes
			scope:puppet_or_actor.major_gold_value >= scope:recipient.monumental_gold_value
			desc = DONATE_FAITH_INTERACTION_OPTION_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			add = pam_offer_pilgrimage_acceptance_value
			scope:offer_pilgrimage = yes
			desc = OFFER_PILGRIMAGE_INTERACTION_OPTION_REASON
		}
		#RITE RELATIONS
		modifier = { # Head of Rite is more convincing
			add = 50
			scope:actor.rite = scope:recipient.rite
			scope:actor.rite.head_of_rite ?= scope:actor
			desc = HEAD_OF_RITE_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			scope:actor = {
				NOT = { rite = scope:recipient.rite }
			}
			add = -20
			desc = DIFFERENT_RITE_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			scope:actor = {
				NOT = { rite = scope:recipient.rite }
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				rite_fulfillment_score = {
					target = scope:actor.rite
					value >= 75
				}
			}
			add = 25
			desc = PERSONAL_APPROVAL_OF_RITE_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			scope:actor = {
				NOT = { rite = scope:recipient.rite }
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				rite_fulfillment_score = {
					target = scope:actor.rite
					value < 25
				}
			}
			add = -25
			desc = PERSONAL_DISAPPROVAL_OF_RITE_REASON
		}
		#Your rite is heretical
		modifier = {
			scope:actor = {
				NOT = { rite = scope:recipient.rite }
			}
			any_tenet = {
				scope:actor.rite = {
					has_tenet_status = {
						tenet = prev
						status = core
					}
				}
				scope:recipient.rite = {
					has_tenet_status = {
						tenet = prev
						status = prohibited
					}
				}
			}
			add = -100
			desc = RITE_CORE_TENET_PROHIBITED
		}
		modifier = {
			scope:actor = {
				NOT = { rite = scope:recipient.rite }
			}
			any_tenet = {
				scope:actor.rite = {
					has_tenet_status = {
						tenet = prev
						status = permitted
					}
				}
				scope:recipient.rite = {
					has_tenet_status = {
						tenet = prev
						status = prohibited
					}
				}
			}
			add = -40
			desc = RITE_TENET_PROHIBITED
		}
	}

	ai_targets = {
		ai_recipients = rulers_in_clerical_region
	}

	ai_target_quick_trigger = {
		adult = yes
	}

	ai_frequency_by_tier = {
		barony = 0
		county = 48
		duchy = 36
		kingdom = 24
		empire = 24
		hegemony = 24
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		base = 0

		modifier = {
			exists = scope:target
			scope:actor = {
				has_claim_on = scope:target
			}
			add = 50
		}

		modifier = {
			exists = scope:target.holder
			scope:target.holder = {
				NOT = {
					any_child = {
						is_alive = yes
					}
				}
			}
			add = 25
		}

		modifier = {
			scope:actor = {
				piety_level >= 3
			}
			add = 15
		}

		modifier = {
			scope:actor = {
				num_sinful_traits >= 2
			}
			add = -40
		}
	}
}

# Ecclesiastical rulers petition a religious superior or subordinate for treasury funds
request_ecclesiastical_treasury_interaction = {
	category = interaction_category_religion
	icon = request_ecclesiastical_treasury_interaction
	interface_priority = 45
	common_interaction = yes
	notification_text = request_ecclesiastical_treasury_interaction_notification
	greeting = positive

	use_diplomatic_range = yes
	ai_min_reply_days = 1
	ai_max_reply_days = 5
	popup_on_receive = yes

	desc = request_ecclesiastical_treasury_interaction_desc

	cooldown_against_recipient = { years = 5 }
	
	recipient_recieve_cooldown = {
		years = marriage_recieve_proposal_cooldown # Intentional
	}
	
	ignore_recipient_recieve_cooldown = {
		AND = {
			is_ai = no
			scope:recipient = { is_ai = no }
		}
	}

	is_available = {
		government_has_flag = government_is_ecclesiastical
		has_pam_dlc_trigger = yes
		is_adult = yes
	}

	is_shown = {
		scope:actor = {
			pam_ecclesiastical_superior_or_subordinate_trigger = {
				ACTOR = scope:actor
				RECIPIENT = scope:recipient
			}
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			faith ?= scope:actor.faith
			government_has_flag = government_is_ecclesiastical
			is_ruler = yes
		}
	}

	is_valid_showing_failures_only = {
		scope:actor = {
			is_available_quick = {
				incapable = no
				imprisoned = no
				hostage = no
				ruler = yes
			}
			NOT = {
				has_trait = excommunicated
			}
			NOT = {
				is_at_war_with = scope:recipient
			}
			pam_not_antipope_when_recipient_is_hof_tt = { CHARACTER = scope:actor }
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			is_available_quick = {
				incapable = no
				imprisoned = no
				hostage = no
				ruler = yes
				adult = yes
			}
			NOT = {
				has_trait = excommunicated
			}
			treasury >= medium_treasury_or_gold_value
		}
	}

	send_option = {
		is_valid = {
			scope:actor = {
				has_usable_hook = scope:recipient
			}
		}
		flag = hook
		localization = GENERIC_SPEND_A_HOOK
	}
	should_use_extra_icon = {
		scope:actor = {
			has_usable_hook = scope:recipient
		}
	}
	extra_icon = "gfx/interface/icons/character_interactions/hook_icon.dds"
	send_options_exclusive = no
	
	cost = {
		piety = {
			value = medium_piety_value
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:actor = {
						domicile ?= { has_domicile_parameter = estate_cheaper_treasury_request }
					}
				}
				multiply = ecclesiastical_domicile_cheaper_treasury_request_mult
			}
		}
	}

	auto_accept = {
		custom_description = {
			text = spending_hook
			subject = scope:actor
			object = scope:recipient
			scope:hook = yes
			scope:actor = {
				has_usable_hook = scope:recipient
			}
		}
	}

	on_accept = {
		scope:actor = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:hook = yes
					has_usable_hook = scope:recipient
				}
				use_hook = scope:recipient
			}
			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_good
				title = request_ecclesiastical_treasury_interaction_title
				right_icon = scope:recipient
				scope:recipient = {
					pay_treasury_or_gold = {
						target = scope:actor
						value = medium_treasury_or_gold_value
					}
					add_piety = minor_piety_value
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								AND = {
									has_trait = generous
									rite = { trait_is_virtue_rite = generous }
								}
								rite = {
									has_tenet_status = {
										tenet = tenet_philanthropia
										status = core
									}
								}
								has_personal_tenet = tenet_philanthropia
							}
						}
						change_spiritual_fulfillment = minor_spiritual_fulfillment_value
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	on_decline = {
		scope:recipient = {
			add_piety = miniscule_piety_loss
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:recipient = {
					is_ai = no
				}
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_opinion = {
					modifier = refusal_opinion
					target = scope:recipient
					opinion = -15
				}
			}
		}
	}

	ai_accept = {
		base = -25

		modifier = {
			scope:hook = yes
			add = 200
			desc = SCHEME_WEAK_HOOK_USED
		}

		opinion_modifier = {
			who = scope:recipient
			opinion_target = scope:actor
			multiplier = 0.5
			desc = AI_OPINION_REASON
		}

		modifier = {
			add = 25
			scope:actor = {
				any_sub_realm_county = {
					faith != scope:actor.faith
				}
			}
			desc = REQUEST_ECCLESIASTICAL_TREASURY_NON_FAITH_COUNTIES_REASON
		}

		modifier = {
			add = ecclesiastical_domicile_acceptance_value_1
			desc = ESTATE_TREASURY_REQUEST_REASON
			scope:actor = {
				domicile ?= {
					has_domicile_parameter = estate_easier_treasury_request_1
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = ecclesiastical_domicile_acceptance_value_2
			desc = ESTATE_TREASURY_REQUEST_REASON
			scope:actor = {
				domicile ?= {
					has_domicile_parameter = estate_easier_treasury_request_2
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = ecclesiastical_domicile_acceptance_value_3
			desc = ESTATE_TREASURY_REQUEST_REASON
			scope:actor = {
				domicile ?= {
					has_domicile_parameter = estate_easier_treasury_request_3
				}
			}
		}
		ecclesiastical_domicile_cathedral_awe_modifier = yes

		modifier = {
			add = 25
			scope:actor = {
				any_character_war = {
					using_holy_war_cb_trigger = yes
				}
			}
			desc = REQUEST_ECCLESIASTICAL_TREASURY_HOLY_WAR_REASON
		}

		modifier = {
			add = 20
			scope:recipient = {
				has_trait = generous
			}
			desc = INTERACTION_GENEROUS
		}

		modifier = {
			add = -25
			scope:recipient = {
				has_trait = greedy
			}
			desc = INTERACTION_GREEDY
		}

		modifier = {
			add = -30
			scope:recipient = {
				treasury <= {
					value = medium_treasury_or_gold_value
				}
			}
			desc = REQUEST_ECCLESIASTICAL_TREASURY_RECIPIENT_LOW_TREASURY_REASON
		}

		modifier = { # AI-to-AI only, and undescribed: a reserve between AI characters must never read
			# to a player as an unexplained refusal, in either direction
			add = -1000
			scope:actor = { is_ai = yes }
			scope:recipient = {
				is_ai = yes
				any_held_title = { is_head_of_faith = yes }
				treasury < pam_head_of_faith_treasury_reserve_value
			}
		}

		modifier = {
			add = -50
			NOR = {
				scope:actor.superior ?= scope:recipient
				scope:recipient.superior ?= scope:actor
			}
			desc = REQUEST_ECCLESIASTICAL_TREASURY_NOT_IMMEDIATE_REASON
		}

		modifier = {
			add = 25
			scope:actor = {
				dynasty ?= { has_dynasty_perk = pam_legacy_2 }
			}
			desc = REQUEST_ECCLESIASTICAL_TREASURY_LEGACY_DYNASTY_REASON
		}

		modifier = { # Undescribed: gated on an AI asker, so this line can never reach a player's tooltip
			add = 40
			scope:actor = {
				is_ai = yes
				any_held_title = { is_head_of_faith = yes }
			}
		}
	}

	ai_targets = {
		ai_recipients = subordinates
	}
	ai_targets = {
		ai_recipients = superior
	}

	ai_target_quick_trigger = {
		adult = yes
	}

	ai_frequency_by_tier = {
		barony = 0
		county = 36
		duchy = 24
		kingdom = 6
		empire = 6
		hegemony = 6
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		base = 0
		
		modifier = {
			debt_level >= 0
			add = 100
		}

		modifier = {
			scope:actor.treasury < 0
			add = 40
		}

		modifier = {
			scope:actor.treasury <= minor_treasury_value
			add = 20
		}

		modifier = { # A Head of Faith calling on his own prelates is routine, not a sign of desperation
			add = 40
			scope:actor = {
				any_held_title = { is_head_of_faith = yes }
			}
		}

		modifier = {
			scope:hook = yes
			add = 50
		}

		modifier = {
			scope:actor = {
				any_character_war = {
					using_holy_war_cb_trigger = yes
				}
			}
			add = 30
		}

		modifier = {
			scope:actor.superior.superior ?= scope:recipient
			factor = 0
		}
	}
}

pam_ask_subordinates_for_gold_interaction = {
	category = interaction_category_religion
	icon = icon_gold
	interface_priority = 44
	common_interaction = yes
	notification_text = pam_ask_subordinates_for_gold_interaction_notification
	greeting = positive

	use_diplomatic_range = yes
	ai_min_reply_days = 1
	ai_max_reply_days = 5
	popup_on_receive = yes

	desc = pam_ask_subordinates_for_gold_interaction_desc

	cooldown = { years = 1 }
	cooldown_against_recipient = { years = 5 }

	is_available = {
		has_pam_dlc_trigger = yes
		is_adult = yes
	}

	is_shown = {
		scope:actor = {
			pam_is_clerical_comprehensive_trigger = yes
			any_subordinate = { this = scope:recipient }
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			faith ?= scope:actor.faith
			trigger_if = {
				limit = { faith.religious_head ?= scope:actor }
				pam_is_antipope_trigger = no
			}
		}
	}

	is_valid_showing_failures_only = {
		scope:actor = {
			is_available_quick = {
				incapable = no
				imprisoned = no
				hostage = no
			}
			NOR = {
				has_trait = excommunicated
				is_at_war_with = scope:recipient
			}
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			is_available_quick = {
				incapable = no
				imprisoned = no
				hostage = no
				adult = yes
			}
			NOT = {
				has_trait = excommunicated
			}
			treasury_or_gold >= minor_treasury_or_gold_value
		}
	}

	send_option = {
		is_valid = {
			scope:actor = {
				has_usable_hook = scope:recipient
			}
		}
		flag = hook
		localization = GENERIC_SPEND_A_HOOK
	}
	should_use_extra_icon = {
		scope:actor = {
			has_usable_hook = scope:recipient
		}
	}
	extra_icon = "gfx/interface/icons/character_interactions/hook_icon.dds"
	send_options_exclusive = no

	cost = {
		piety = pam_ask_subordinates_for_gold_piety_cost_value
	}

	auto_accept = {
		custom_description = {
			text = spending_hook
			subject = scope:actor
			object = scope:recipient
			scope:hook = yes
			scope:actor = {
				has_usable_hook = scope:recipient
			}
		}
	}

	on_accept = {
		save_scope_value_as = {
			name = siphoned_amount
			value = scope:recipient.pam_ask_subordinates_for_gold_value
		}
		scope:actor = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:hook = yes
					has_usable_hook = scope:recipient
				}
				use_hook = scope:recipient
			}
			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_good
				title = pam_ask_subordinates_for_gold_interaction_title
				right_icon = scope:recipient
				add_short_term_gold = scope:siphoned_amount
				scope:recipient = {
					remove_treasury_or_gold = scope:siphoned_amount
					add_piety = minor_piety_value
				}
			}
		}
	}

	on_decline = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:recipient = {
					is_ai = no
				}
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_opinion = {
					modifier = refusal_opinion
					target = scope:recipient
					opinion = -15
				}
			}
		}
	}

	ai_accept = {
		base = -10

		ask_for_gold_acceptance_modifier = yes

		modifier = {
			scope:hook = yes
			add = 200
			desc = SCHEME_WEAK_HOOK_USED
		}

		modifier = {
			add = 20
			scope:recipient = {
				has_trait = generous
			}
			desc = INTERACTION_GENEROUS
		}

		modifier = {
			add = -25
			scope:recipient = {
				has_trait = greedy
			}
			desc = INTERACTION_GREEDY
		}

		modifier = {
			add = -30
			scope:recipient = {
				treasury_or_gold <= medium_treasury_or_gold_value
			}
			desc = REQUEST_ECCLESIASTICAL_TREASURY_RECIPIENT_LOW_TREASURY_REASON
		}

		modifier = { # Undescribed: gated on an AI asker, so this line can never reach a player's tooltip
			add = 100
			scope:actor = {
				is_ai = yes
				any_held_title = { is_head_of_faith = yes }
			}
		}
	}

	ai_targets = {
		ai_recipients = subordinates
	}

	ai_target_quick_trigger = {
		adult = yes
	}

	ai_frequency_by_tier = {
		barony = 0
		county = 36
		duchy = 24
		kingdom = 12
		empire = 12
		hegemony = 12
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		base = 0

		modifier = {
			debt_level >= 0
			add = 100
		}

		modifier = {
			scope:actor.treasury_or_gold < 0
			add = 40
		}

		modifier = { # A Head of Faith calling on his own prelates is routine, not a sign of desperation
			add = 100
			scope:actor = {
				any_held_title = { is_head_of_faith = yes }
				treasury <= 750
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				is_ai = yes
			}
		}

		modifier = {
			add = 100
			scope:recipient = {
				is_ruler = no
				treasury_or_gold >= 50
			}
			scope:actor = {
				any_held_title = { has_clerical_region = yes }
				NOT = { faith.religious_head ?= this } # A Head of Faith keeps the restraint of their own clause above
			}
		}

		modifier = {
			scope:hook = yes
			add = 50
		}
	}
}

#Request white peace through the archbishop of your clerical region
request_white_peace_interaction = {
	category = interaction_category_religion
	icon = icon_clergy_enfore_white_peace
	interface_priority = 40
	common_interaction = no
	notification_text = request_white_peace_interaction_notification
	greeting = positive

	use_diplomatic_range = yes
	ai_min_reply_days = 1
	ai_max_reply_days = 5
	popup_on_receive = yes

	desc = request_white_peace_interaction_desc

	cooldown_against_recipient = { years = 5 }

	is_available = {
		has_pam_dlc_trigger = yes
	}

	is_shown = {
		scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
			like_christianity_religion_trigger = yes # Warcraft
			pam_clergy_interactions_faith_trigger = yes
			is_ruler = yes
			NOR = {
				government_has_flag = government_is_theocracy
				is_at_war_with = scope:recipient
			}
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			pam_request_legitimacy_priest_trigger = { CHARACTER = scope:puppet_or_actor }
			any_held_title = {
				has_clerical_region = yes
			}
		}
		pam_should_bother_clergy_interaction_trigger = { PRIEST = scope:recipient }
		# Those under an antipope ask them instead of the real Head of Faith
		trigger_if = {
			limit = {
				scope:recipient ?= scope:puppet_or_actor.faith.religious_head
			}
			scope:puppet_or_actor ?= { is_following_religious_head_challenger = no }
		}
	}

	populate_recipient_list = {
		scope:puppet_or_actor = {
			every_war_enemy = {
				limit = {
					pam_request_white_peace_enemy_trigger = yes
				}
				add_to_list = characters
			}
		}
	}

	is_valid_showing_failures_only = {
		scope:puppet_or_actor = {
			clergy_interaction_generic_piety_level_or_puppet_req_trigger = yes
			is_available_quick = {
				incapable = no
				imprisoned = no
				hostage = no
				ruler = yes
			}
			is_at_war = yes
			NOR = {
				has_trait = excommunicated
				has_character_modifier = promised_pilgrimage_to_clergy_modifier
			}
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			is_available_quick = {
				incapable = no
				imprisoned = no
				hostage = no
				ruler = yes
				adult = yes
			}
			NOT = {
				has_trait = excommunicated
			}
			highest_held_title_tier >= tier_duchy
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = pam_clergy_arbiter_must_be_archbishop_or_hof_tt
				OR = {
					scope:puppet_or_actor.capital_county.clerical_region_title.holder ?= this
					scope:puppet_or_actor.religious_head_or_challenger ?= this
					scope:puppet_or_actor.faith.religious_head ?= this
					scope:puppet_or_actor.rite.head_of_rite ?= this
				}
			}
		}
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes }
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				piety >= pam_request_white_peace_cost_value
			}
		}
	}

	has_valid_target_showing_failures_only = {
		scope:secondary_recipient ?= {
			pam_request_white_peace_enemy_trigger = yes
			# Only a Head of Faith or Rite has authority to impose international peace among kings and above
			trigger_if = {
				limit = {
					top_liege != scope:puppet_or_actor.top_liege
					OR = {
						highest_held_title_tier >= tier_kingdom
						scope:puppet_or_actor.highest_held_title_tier >= tier_kingdom
					}
				}
				custom_tooltip = { # shared Head of Faith
					text = pam_request_white_peace_enemy_invalid_tt
					OR = {
						AND = {
							religious_head_or_challenger ?= scope:recipient
							scope:puppet_or_actor.religious_head_or_challenger ?= scope:recipient
						}
						AND = {
							faith.religious_head ?= scope:recipient
							scope:puppet_or_actor.faith.religious_head ?= scope:recipient
						}
						AND = {
							rite.head_of_rite ?= scope:recipient
							scope:puppet_or_actor.rite.head_of_rite ?= scope:recipient
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		scope:puppet_or_actor = {
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = request_white_peace_no_valid_war_tt
				any_character_war = {
					pam_request_white_peace_war_with_enemy_trigger = { ENEMY = scope:secondary_recipient }
				}
			}
		}
	}

	on_accept = {
		if = {
			limit = { scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes }
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				if = {
					limit = { pam_request_white_peace_cost_value > 0 }
					add_piety = {
						value = {
							add = pam_request_white_peace_cost_value
							max = piety
							multiply = -1
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		scope:puppet_or_actor = {
			request_white_peace_interaction_effect = yes
		}
	}

	on_decline = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:recipient = {
					is_ai = no
				}
			}
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				add_opinion = {
					modifier = refusal_opinion
					target = scope:recipient
					opinion = -15
				}
			}
		}
	}

	cost = {
		piety = {
			value = pam_request_white_peace_cost_value
			if = {
				limit = { scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes }
				multiply = 0
			}
		}
	}

	auto_accept = {
		custom_description = {
			text = "spending_hook"
			subject = scope:puppet_or_actor
			object = scope:recipient
			scope:use_hook = yes
			scope:puppet_or_actor = { has_strong_hook = scope:recipient }
		}
	}

	send_options_exclusive = no

	send_option = {
		is_valid = {
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				trigger_if = {
					limit = {
						scope:actor = {
							is_ai = yes
						}
					}
					short_term_gold >= major_gold_value
				}
				trigger_else = {
					short_term_gold >= major_gold_value
				}
			}
		}
		flag = donate_faith
		localization = DONATE_TO_THE_FAITH_INTERACTION_OPTION
	}

	send_option = {
		is_valid = {
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				piety >= major_piety_value
			}
		}
		flag = offer_piety
		localization = PAM_CLERGY_ARBITRATION_OFFER_PIETY_OPTION
	}

	send_option = {
		is_valid = {
			NOT = {
				scope:recipient = {
					has_usable_hook = scope:puppet_or_actor
				}
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				can_add_hook = {
					target = scope:puppet_or_actor
					type = favor_hook
				}
			}
		}
		flag = offer_hook
		localization = OFFER_HOOK_INTERACTION_OPTION_CLERGY
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:recipient != scope:puppet_or_actor
		}
		is_valid = {
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				has_usable_hook = scope:recipient
			}
			trigger_if = {
				limit = {
					scope:recipient = {
						is_ai = no
					}
				}
				scope:actor = { is_ai = no }
			}
		}
		flag = use_hook
		localization = EDUCATION_HOOK
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			pam_offer_pilgrimage_option_shown_trigger = { ACTOR = scope:puppet_or_actor }
		}
		is_valid = {
			pam_offer_pilgrimage_option_valid_trigger = { ACTOR = scope:puppet_or_actor }
		}
		flag = offer_pilgrimage
		localization = OFFER_PILGRIMAGE_INTERACTION_OPTION
	}

	ai_accept = {
		base = -10

		modifier = {
			add = 25
			desc = pam_sf_ear_of_clergy_accept_reason
			trigger = {
				scope:puppet_or_actor = { has_character_modifier = pam_sf_ear_of_clergy_modifier }
			}
		}

		modifier = {
			scope:use_hook = yes
			add = 200
			scope:puppet_or_actor = { NOT = { has_strong_hook = scope:recipient } }
			desc = SCHEME_WEAK_HOOK_USED
		}

		general_secular_to_ecclesiastic_acceptance_modifier = yes

		modifier = {
			add = {
				value = 25
				if = {
					limit = {
						pam_actor_rite_has_core_simony_trigger = yes
					}
					multiply = 5
				}
			}
			scope:donate_faith = yes
			scope:puppet_or_actor.major_gold_value < scope:recipient.miniscule_gold_value
			desc = DONATE_FAITH_INTERACTION_OPTION_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			add = {
				value = 50
				if = {
					limit = {
						pam_actor_rite_has_core_simony_trigger = yes
					}
					multiply = 5
				}
			}
			scope:donate_faith = yes
			scope:puppet_or_actor.major_gold_value >= scope:recipient.miniscule_gold_value
			scope:puppet_or_actor.major_gold_value < scope:recipient.major_gold_value
			desc = DONATE_FAITH_INTERACTION_OPTION_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			add = {
				value = 75
				if = {
					limit = {
						pam_actor_rite_has_core_simony_trigger = yes
					}
					multiply = 5
				}
			}
			scope:donate_faith = yes
			scope:puppet_or_actor.major_gold_value >= scope:recipient.major_gold_value
			desc = DONATE_FAITH_INTERACTION_OPTION_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 40
			scope:offer_piety = yes
			desc = PAM_CLERGY_ARBITRATION_OFFER_PIETY_OPTION_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			add = {
				value = 20
				multiply = scope:puppet_or_actor.highest_held_title_tier
			}
			scope:offer_hook = yes
			desc = OFFER_HOOK_INTERACTION_OPTION_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			add = pam_offer_pilgrimage_acceptance_value
			scope:offer_pilgrimage = yes
			desc = OFFER_PILGRIMAGE_INTERACTION_OPTION_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				any_character_war = {
					pam_request_white_peace_war_with_enemy_trigger = { ENEMY = scope:secondary_recipient }
					OR = {
						AND = {
							casus_belli = {
								primary_attacker = scope:puppet_or_actor
							}
							defender_war_score >= 20
						}
						AND = {
							casus_belli = {
								primary_defender = scope:puppet_or_actor
							}
							attacker_war_score >= 20
						}
					}
				}
			}
			add = 25
			desc = REQUEST_WHITE_PEACE_ACTOR_LOSING_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				any_character_war = {
					pam_request_white_peace_war_with_enemy_trigger = { ENEMY = scope:secondary_recipient }
					war_days >= 1825
				}
			}
			add = 20
			desc = PAM_CLERGY_ARBITRATION_LONG_WAR_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			pam_character_has_peaceful_tenet_trigger = { CHARACTER = scope:puppet_or_actor }
			add = 35
			desc = PAM_CLERGY_ARBITRATION_ACTOR_PEACEFUL_TENET_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			pam_character_has_peaceful_tenet_trigger = { CHARACTER = scope:recipient }
			add = 45
			desc = PAM_CLERGY_ARBITRATION_RECIPIENT_PEACEFUL_TENET_REASON
		}
		opinion_modifier = {
			who = scope:recipient
			opinion_target = scope:secondary_recipient
			multiplier = -0.5
			desc = AI_OPINION_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			add = {
				value = -5
				multiply = scope:secondary_recipient.piety_level
			}
			scope:secondary_recipient.piety_level > 1
			desc = THEY_ARE_PIOUS
		}
		modifier = {
			add = {
				value = -5
				multiply = scope:secondary_recipient.num_virtuous_traits
			}
			scope:secondary_recipient.num_virtuous_traits > 0
			desc = THEY_ARE_VIRTUOUS
		}
		modifier = {
			add = {
				value = 5
				multiply = scope:secondary_recipient.num_sinful_traits
			}
			scope:secondary_recipient.num_sinful_traits > 0
			desc = THEY_ARE_SINFUL
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -50
			scope:recipient = {
				OR = {
					has_relation_friend = scope:secondary_recipient
					has_relation_lover = scope:secondary_recipient
					has_relation_best_friend = scope:secondary_recipient
				}
			}
			desc = PAM_CLERGY_ARBITRATION_FRIEND_OF_OPPONENT_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 50
			scope:recipient = {
				OR = {
					has_relation_rival = scope:secondary_recipient
					has_relation_nemesis = scope:secondary_recipient
				}
			}
			desc = PAM_CLERGY_ARBITRATION_RIVAL_OF_OPPONENT_REASON
		}
	}

	ai_targets = {
		ai_recipients = realm_priest_top_superior
		ai_recipients = head_of_faith
	}

	ai_target_quick_trigger = {
		adult = yes
	}

	ai_frequency_by_tier = {
		barony = 0
		county = 24
		duchy = 24
		kingdom = 12
		empire = 12
		hegemony = 12
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		base = 0

		modifier = {
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				any_character_war = {
					pam_request_white_peace_war_with_enemy_trigger = { ENEMY = scope:secondary_recipient }
					OR = {
						AND = {
							casus_belli = {
								primary_attacker = scope:puppet_or_actor
							}
							defender_war_score >= 30
						}
						AND = {
							casus_belli = {
								primary_defender = scope:puppet_or_actor
							}
							attacker_war_score >= 30
						}
					}
				}
			}
			add = 40
		}
		modifier = {
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				any_character_war = {
					pam_request_white_peace_war_with_enemy_trigger = { ENEMY = scope:secondary_recipient }
					war_days >= 1825
				}
			}
			add = 25
		}
		modifier = {
			scope:donate_faith = yes
			add = 15
		}
		modifier = {
			scope:offer_piety = yes
			add = 15
		}
		modifier = {
			scope:offer_hook = yes
			add = 15
		}
		# Send options alone must not make a winning or fresh war worth ending
		modifier = {
			factor = 0
			NOT = {
				scope:puppet_or_actor = {
					any_character_war = {
						pam_request_white_peace_war_with_enemy_trigger = { ENEMY = scope:secondary_recipient }
						OR = {
							war_days >= 1825
							AND = {
								casus_belli = {
									primary_attacker = scope:puppet_or_actor
								}
								defender_war_score >= 30
							}
							AND = {
								casus_belli = {
									primary_defender = scope:puppet_or_actor
								}
								attacker_war_score >= 30
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}


#Ask the clergy to rule an ongoing war won at 90% war score instead of 100%, with the Church compensating the loser
request_victory_interaction = {
	category = interaction_category_religion
	icon = icon_clergy_request_victory
	interface_priority = 41
	common_interaction = no
	notification_text = request_victory_interaction_notification
	greeting = positive

	use_diplomatic_range = yes
	ai_min_reply_days = 1
	ai_max_reply_days = 5
	popup_on_receive = yes

	desc = request_victory_interaction_desc

	#A ruling this favourable is a once-a-decade indulgence, not a war-by-war tool
	cooldown = { years = 10 }

	is_available = {
		has_pam_dlc_trigger = yes
	}

	is_shown = {
		scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
			like_christianity_religion_trigger = yes # Warcraft
			NOT = {
				government_has_flag = government_is_theocracy
			}
			pam_clergy_interactions_faith_trigger = yes
			is_ruler = yes
		}
		scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
			NOT = {
				is_at_war_with = scope:recipient
			}
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			pam_request_legitimacy_priest_trigger = { CHARACTER = scope:puppet_or_actor }
			any_held_title = {
				has_clerical_region = yes
			}
			NOT = { this = scope:actor }
		}
		pam_should_bother_clergy_interaction_trigger = { PRIEST = scope:recipient }
		# Those under an antipope ask them instead of the real Head of Faith
		trigger_if = {
			limit = {
				scope:recipient ?= scope:puppet_or_actor.faith.religious_head
			}
			scope:puppet_or_actor ?= { is_following_religious_head_challenger = no }
		}
	}

	populate_recipient_list = {
		scope:puppet_or_actor = {
			every_war_enemy = {
				limit = {
					#The clergy can only press its own flock, so the eligible opponents are the same set as Request White Peace
					pam_request_white_peace_enemy_trigger = yes
				}
				add_to_list = characters
			}
		}
	}

	is_valid_showing_failures_only = {
		scope:puppet_or_actor = {
			clergy_interaction_generic_piety_level_or_puppet_req_trigger = yes
			is_available_quick = {
				incapable = no
				imprisoned = no
				hostage = no
				ruler = yes
			}
			is_at_war = yes
			NOR = {
				has_trait = excommunicated
				has_character_modifier = promised_pilgrimage_to_clergy_modifier
			}
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			is_available_quick = {
				incapable = no
				imprisoned = no
				hostage = no
				ruler = yes
				adult = yes
			}
			NOT = {
				has_trait = excommunicated
			}
			highest_held_title_tier >= tier_duchy
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = pam_clergy_arbiter_must_be_archbishop_or_hof_tt
				OR = {
					scope:puppet_or_actor.capital_county.clerical_region_title.holder ?= this
					scope:puppet_or_actor.faith.religious_head ?= this
					scope:puppet_or_actor.religious_head_or_challenger ?= this
					scope:puppet_or_actor.rite.head_of_rite ?= this
				}
			}
		}
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes }
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				piety >= pam_request_victory_cost_value
			}
		}
	}

	has_valid_target_showing_failures_only = {
		scope:secondary_recipient ?= {
			pam_request_white_peace_enemy_trigger = yes
			# The arbiter needs authority over the opponent too, not just over the petitioner
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = pam_request_victory_enemy_invalid_tt
				OR = {
					AND = {
						religious_head_or_challenger ?= scope:recipient
						scope:puppet_or_actor.religious_head_or_challenger ?= scope:recipient
					}
					AND = {
						faith.religious_head ?= scope:recipient
						scope:puppet_or_actor.faith.religious_head ?= scope:recipient
					}
					AND = {
						rite.head_of_rite ?= scope:recipient
						scope:puppet_or_actor.rite.head_of_rite ?= scope:recipient
					}
					capital_county.clerical_region_title.holder ?= scope:recipient
				}
			}
			# Even a shared archdiocese does not let an archbishop rule on a war between crowns of separate realms
			trigger_if = {
				limit = {
					top_liege != scope:puppet_or_actor.top_liege
					OR = {
						highest_held_title_tier >= tier_kingdom
						scope:puppet_or_actor.highest_held_title_tier >= tier_kingdom
					}
				}
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = pam_request_victory_crowns_need_hof_tt
					OR = {
						religious_head_or_challenger ?= scope:recipient
						faith.religious_head ?= scope:recipient
						rite.head_of_rite ?= scope:recipient
					}
				}
			}
		}
		scope:puppet_or_actor = {
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = request_victory_no_valid_war_tt
				any_character_war = {
					pam_request_victory_war_with_enemy_trigger = { ENEMY = scope:secondary_recipient }
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = request_victory_already_won_tt
				NOT = {
					any_character_war = {
						pam_request_victory_war_already_won_trigger = { ENEMY = scope:secondary_recipient }
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	on_accept = {
		if = {
			limit = { scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes }
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				if = {
					limit = { pam_request_victory_cost_value > 0 }
					add_piety = {
						value = {
							add = pam_request_victory_cost_value
							max = piety
							multiply = -1
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		scope:puppet_or_actor = {
			request_victory_interaction_effect = yes
		}
	}

	on_decline = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:recipient = {
					is_ai = no
				}
			}
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				add_opinion = {
					modifier = refusal_opinion
					target = scope:recipient
					opinion = -15
				}
			}
		}
	}

	cost = {
		piety = {
			value = pam_request_victory_cost_value
			if = {
				limit = { scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes }
				multiply = 0
			}
		}
	}

	auto_accept = {
		custom_description = {
			text = "spending_hook"
			subject = scope:puppet_or_actor
			object = scope:recipient
			scope:use_hook = yes
			scope:puppet_or_actor = { has_strong_hook = scope:recipient }
		}
	}

	send_options_exclusive = no

	send_option = {
		is_valid = {
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				short_term_gold >= major_gold_value
			}
		}
		flag = donate_faith
		localization = DONATE_TO_THE_FAITH_INTERACTION_OPTION
	}

	send_option = {
		is_valid = {
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				piety >= major_piety_value
			}
		}
		flag = offer_piety
		localization = PAM_CLERGY_ARBITRATION_OFFER_PIETY_OPTION
	}

	send_option = {
		is_valid = {
			NOT = {
				scope:recipient = {
					has_usable_hook = scope:puppet_or_actor
				}
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				can_add_hook = {
					target = scope:puppet_or_actor
					type = favor_hook
				}
			}
		}
		flag = offer_hook
		localization = OFFER_HOOK_INTERACTION_OPTION_CLERGY
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:recipient != scope:puppet_or_actor
		}
		is_valid = {
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				has_usable_hook = scope:recipient
			}
			trigger_if = {
				limit = {
					scope:recipient = {
						is_ai = no
					}
				}
				scope:actor = { is_ai = no }
			}
		}
		flag = use_hook
		localization = EDUCATION_HOOK
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			pam_offer_pilgrimage_option_shown_trigger = { ACTOR = scope:puppet_or_actor }
		}
		is_valid = {
			pam_offer_pilgrimage_option_valid_trigger = { ACTOR = scope:puppet_or_actor }
		}
		flag = offer_pilgrimage
		localization = OFFER_PILGRIMAGE_INTERACTION_OPTION
	}

	should_use_extra_icon = {
		scope:puppet_or_actor = { has_usable_hook = scope:recipient }
	}

	extra_icon = "gfx/interface/icons/character_interactions/hook_icon.dds"

	ai_accept = {
		#Ruling on who wins a war is a far greater favour than mediating a truce
		base = -40

		modifier = {
			add = 25
			desc = pam_sf_ear_of_clergy_accept_reason
			trigger = {
				scope:puppet_or_actor = { has_character_modifier = pam_sf_ear_of_clergy_modifier }
			}
		}

		modifier = {
			scope:use_hook = yes
			add = 200
			scope:puppet_or_actor = { NOT = { has_strong_hook = scope:recipient } }
			desc = SCHEME_WEAK_HOOK_USED
		}

		general_secular_to_ecclesiastic_acceptance_modifier = yes

		modifier = {
			add = 25
			scope:donate_faith = yes
			scope:puppet_or_actor.major_gold_value < scope:recipient.miniscule_gold_value
			desc = DONATE_FAITH_INTERACTION_OPTION_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 50
			scope:donate_faith = yes
			scope:puppet_or_actor.major_gold_value >= scope:recipient.miniscule_gold_value
			scope:puppet_or_actor.major_gold_value < scope:recipient.major_gold_value
			desc = DONATE_FAITH_INTERACTION_OPTION_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 75
			scope:donate_faith = yes
			scope:puppet_or_actor.major_gold_value >= scope:recipient.major_gold_value
			desc = DONATE_FAITH_INTERACTION_OPTION_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 40
			scope:offer_piety = yes
			desc = PAM_CLERGY_ARBITRATION_OFFER_PIETY_OPTION_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			add = {
				value = 20
				multiply = scope:puppet_or_actor.highest_held_title_tier
			}
			scope:offer_hook = yes
			desc = OFFER_HOOK_INTERACTION_OPTION_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			add = pam_offer_pilgrimage_acceptance_value
			scope:offer_pilgrimage = yes
			desc = OFFER_PILGRIMAGE_INTERACTION_OPTION_REASON
		}
		#The closer the war is to won, the less the clergy is really deciding anything
		modifier = {
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				any_character_war = {
					pam_request_victory_war_with_enemy_trigger = { ENEMY = scope:secondary_recipient }
					OR = {
						AND = {
							casus_belli = {
								primary_attacker = scope:puppet_or_actor
							}
							attacker_war_score >= 95
						}
						AND = {
							casus_belli = {
								primary_defender = scope:puppet_or_actor
							}
							defender_war_score >= 95
						}
					}
				}
			}
			add = 20
			desc = REQUEST_VICTORY_WAR_ALL_BUT_WON_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				any_character_war = {
					pam_request_victory_war_with_enemy_trigger = { ENEMY = scope:secondary_recipient }
					war_days >= 1825
				}
			}
			add = 20
			desc = PAM_CLERGY_ARBITRATION_LONG_WAR_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			pam_character_has_peaceful_tenet_trigger = { CHARACTER = scope:puppet_or_actor }
			add = 35
			desc = PAM_CLERGY_ARBITRATION_ACTOR_PEACEFUL_TENET_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			pam_character_has_peaceful_tenet_trigger = { CHARACTER = scope:recipient }
			add = 45
			desc = PAM_CLERGY_ARBITRATION_RECIPIENT_PEACEFUL_TENET_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 40
			scope:secondary_recipient = {
				has_trait = excommunicated
			}
			desc = REQUEST_VICTORY_OPPONENT_EXCOMMUNICATED_REASON
		}
		opinion_modifier = {
			who = scope:recipient
			opinion_target = scope:secondary_recipient
			multiplier = -0.5
			desc = AI_OPINION_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			add = {
				value = -5
				multiply = scope:secondary_recipient.piety_level
			}
			scope:secondary_recipient.piety_level > 1
			desc = THEY_ARE_PIOUS
		}
		modifier = {
			add = {
				value = -5
				multiply = scope:secondary_recipient.num_virtuous_traits
			}
			scope:secondary_recipient.num_virtuous_traits > 0
			desc = THEY_ARE_VIRTUOUS
		}
		modifier = {
			add = {
				value = 5
				multiply = scope:secondary_recipient.num_sinful_traits
			}
			scope:secondary_recipient.num_sinful_traits > 0
			desc = THEY_ARE_SINFUL
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -50
			scope:recipient = {
				OR = {
					has_relation_friend = scope:secondary_recipient
					has_relation_lover = scope:secondary_recipient
					has_relation_best_friend = scope:secondary_recipient
				}
			}
			desc = PAM_CLERGY_ARBITRATION_FRIEND_OF_OPPONENT_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 50
			scope:recipient = {
				OR = {
					has_relation_rival = scope:secondary_recipient
					has_relation_nemesis = scope:secondary_recipient
				}
			}
			desc = PAM_CLERGY_ARBITRATION_RIVAL_OF_OPPONENT_REASON
		}
	}

	ai_targets = {
		ai_recipients = realm_priest_top_superior
		ai_recipients = head_of_faith
	}

	ai_target_quick_trigger = {
		adult = yes
	}

	ai_frequency_by_tier = {
		barony = 0
		county = 24
		duchy = 24
		kingdom = 12
		empire = 12
		hegemony = 12
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		base = 0

		modifier = {
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				any_character_war = {
					pam_request_victory_war_with_enemy_trigger = { ENEMY = scope:secondary_recipient }
				}
			}
			add = 50
		}
		modifier = {
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				any_character_war = {
					pam_request_victory_war_with_enemy_trigger = { ENEMY = scope:secondary_recipient }
					war_days >= 1825
				}
			}
			add = 25
		}
		modifier = {
			scope:donate_faith = yes
			add = 15
		}
		modifier = {
			scope:offer_piety = yes
			add = 15
		}
		modifier = {
			scope:offer_hook = yes
			add = 15
		}
	}
}


#Head of faith or head of rite creates a custom kingdom for a same-faith duke
ecclesiastic_consecrate_kingdom_interaction = {
	category = interaction_category_religion
	icon = icon_clergy_claim_unformed_kingdom
	notification_text = ECCLESIASTICAL_CONSECRATE_KINGDOM_NOTIFICATION
	interface_priority = 69
	common_interaction = yes
	popup_on_receive = yes

	desc = ecclesiastic_consecrate_kingdom_interaction_desc

	is_available = {
		has_pam_dlc_trigger = yes
	}

	is_shown = {
		scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
			government_has_flag = government_is_theocracy
			is_available_quick = {
				adult = yes
				alive = yes
				imprisoned = no
				incapable = no
			}
		}
		exists = scope:puppet_or_actor
		scope:puppet_or_actor != scope:recipient
		scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
			OR = {
				scope:recipient.religious_head_or_challenger ?= this
				AND = {
					highest_held_title_tier >= tier_duchy
					government_has_flag = government_is_theocracy
					NOT = { exists = scope:recipient.religious_head_or_challenger }
					scope:recipient.rite.head_of_rite ?= this
				}
			}
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			faith ?= scope:puppet_or_actor.faith
			is_landed = yes
			highest_held_title_tier = tier_duchy
			NOT = { has_game_rule = off_custom_kingdoms }
			is_confederation_member = no
			OR = {
				government_has_flag = government_is_theocracy
				has_legitimacy = yes
			}			
			NOT = {
				any_held_title = {
					tier = tier_duchy
					de_jure_liege ?= {
						tier = tier_kingdom
						NOT = { exists = holder }
						is_landless_type_title = no
					}
				}
			}
			trigger_if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						scope:puppet_or_actor.religious_head_or_challenger ?= scope:puppet_or_actor
					}
				}
				rite = scope:puppet_or_actor.rite
			}
		}
		trigger_if = {
			limit = {
				scope:actor = {
					is_ai = yes
				}
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				piety_level >= 2
			}
			scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
				NOT = {
					has_any_bad_relationship_with_character_trigger = { CHARACTER = scope:recipient }
				}
			}
		}
	}

	is_valid_showing_failures_only = {
		scope:puppet_or_actor = {
			piety_level >= 3
			trigger_if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						religious_head_or_challenger ?= scope:puppet_or_actor
					}
					scope:recipient = {
						is_independent_ruler = no
					}
				}
        		custom_tooltip = {
					text = recipient_head_of_rite_is_not_lieges_head_of_rite_tt
					exists = scope:recipient.liege.rite.head_of_rite
					rite.head_of_rite ?= scope:recipient.liege.rite.head_of_rite
				}
        	}
        	NOT = {
        		has_trait = excommunicated
        	}
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			is_available_quick = {
				alive = yes
				incapable = no
				imprisoned = no
			}
			is_at_war = no
			faith = scope:puppet_or_actor.faith
			NOT = {
				has_trait = excommunicated
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = must_hold_landed_duchy_tt
				any_held_title = {
					tier = tier_duchy
					is_landless_type_title = no
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = recipient_liege_is_emperor_or_none_tt
				OR = {
					is_independent_ruler = yes
					liege.highest_held_title_tier >= tier_empire
				}
			}
			trigger_if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						scope:puppet_or_actor.religious_head_or_challenger ?= scope:puppet_or_actor
					}
				}
				rite = scope:puppet_or_actor.rite
			}
		}
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes }
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				gold >= pam_ecclesiastic_consecrate_kingdom_gold_cost_value
			}
		}
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes }
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				treasury >= pam_ecclesiastic_consecrate_kingdom_treasury_cost_value
			}
		}
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes }
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				piety >= pam_ecclesiastic_consecrate_kingdom_piety_cost_value
			}
		}
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes }
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				prestige >= pam_ecclesiastic_consecrate_kingdom_prestige_cost_value
			}
		}
	}

	cost = {
		gold = {
			value = pam_ecclesiastic_consecrate_kingdom_gold_cost_value
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes
						scope:puppet_or_actor = { has_treasury = yes }
					}
				}
				multiply = 0
			}
		}
		treasury = {
			value = pam_ecclesiastic_consecrate_kingdom_treasury_cost_value
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes
						scope:puppet_or_actor = { has_treasury = no }
					}
				}
				multiply = 0
			}
		}
		piety = {
			value = pam_ecclesiastic_consecrate_kingdom_piety_cost_value
			if = {
				limit = { scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes }
				multiply = 0
			}
		}
		prestige = {
			value = pam_ecclesiastic_consecrate_kingdom_prestige_cost_value
			if = {
				limit = { scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes }
				multiply = 0
			}
		}
	}

			scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {

					has_domicile_parameter = estate_consecrate_nave_3
				}
			}

		modifier = {

						value >= 25
					}
					has_dread_level_towards = {
						target = scope:recipient.liege
						level >= 1
					}
				}
				
			}
			add = {

				if = {
					limit = {

							opinion = {
								target = scope:recipient.liege

							}
						}
					}
					add = -50

				if = {

							}

					add = -20

				if = {

	on_accept = {
		if = {
			limit = { scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes }
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				if = {
					limit = { pam_ecclesiastic_consecrate_kingdom_treasury_cost_value > 0 }
					remove_treasury = pam_ecclesiastic_consecrate_kingdom_treasury_cost_value
				}
				if = {
					limit = { pam_ecclesiastic_consecrate_kingdom_gold_cost_value > 0 }
					remove_short_term_gold = {
						value = pam_ecclesiastic_consecrate_kingdom_gold_cost_value
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { pam_ecclesiastic_consecrate_kingdom_piety_cost_value > 0 }
					add_piety = {
						value = {
							add = pam_ecclesiastic_consecrate_kingdom_piety_cost_value
							max = piety
							multiply = -1
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { pam_ecclesiastic_consecrate_kingdom_prestige_cost_value > 0 }
					add_prestige = {
						value = {
							add = pam_ecclesiastic_consecrate_kingdom_prestige_cost_value
							max = prestige
							multiply = -1
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		scope:puppet_or_actor = {
			save_scope_as = religious_authority
			save_scope_as = interaction
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			save_scope_as = king_aspirant
			show_as_tooltip = {
				consecrate_kingdom_player_cleric_effect = yes
				consecrate_kingdom_effect = yes
			}
		}
		#Opinion effects
		grant_title_populate_concerned_de_jure_list_effect = { TITLE_GAINER = scope:king_aspirant }
		#Show opinion losses to relevant characters
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:actor = {
					is_ai = no
				}
			}
			every_in_list = {
				list = grateful_refusal_chars
				add_opinion = {
					target = scope:puppet_or_actor
					modifier = gave_away_de_jure_lands_opinion
					opinion = -50
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:king_aspirant = {
						is_independent_ruler = no
						liege = {
							NOR = {
								is_in_list = grateful_refusal_chars
								this = scope:puppet_or_actor
							}
						}
					}
				}
				scope:king_aspirant.liege = {
					add_opinion = {
						target = scope:puppet_or_actor
						modifier = circumvented_my_authority_opinion
						opinion = -50
					}
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			hidden_effect = {
				every_in_list = {
					list = grateful_refusal_chars
					add_opinion = {
						target = scope:puppet_or_actor
						modifier = gave_away_de_jure_lands_opinion
						opinion = -50
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						scope:king_aspirant = {
							is_independent_ruler = no
							liege = {
								NOR = {
									is_in_list = grateful_refusal_chars
									this = scope:puppet_or_actor
								}
							}
						}
					}
					scope:king_aspirant.liege = {
						add_opinion = {
							target = scope:puppet_or_actor
							modifier = circumvented_my_authority_opinion
							opinion = -50
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:king_aspirant = {
					is_ai = no
				}
			}
			every_in_list = {
				list = grateful_refusal_chars
				add_opinion = {
					target = scope:king_aspirant
					modifier = absorbed_de_jure_opinion
					opinion = -30
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:king_aspirant = {
						is_independent_ruler = no
						liege = {
							NOR = {
								is_in_list = grateful_refusal_chars
								this = scope:puppet_or_actor
							}
						}
					}
				}
				scope:king_aspirant.liege = {
					add_opinion = {
						target = scope:king_aspirant
						modifier = circumvented_my_authority_opinion
						opinion = -30
					}
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			hidden_effect = {
				every_in_list = {
					list = grateful_refusal_chars
					add_opinion = {
						target = scope:king_aspirant
						modifier = absorbed_de_jure_opinion
						opinion = -20
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						scope:king_aspirant = {
							is_independent_ruler = no
							liege = {
								NOR = {
									is_in_list = grateful_refusal_chars
									this = scope:puppet_or_actor
								}
							}
						}
					}
					scope:king_aspirant.liege = {
						add_opinion = {
							target = scope:king_aspirant
							modifier = circumvented_my_authority_opinion
							opinion = -20
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		
		scope:puppet_or_actor = {
			trigger_event = pam_interactions_events.0090
		}
	}
	on_decline = {
		scope:actor = {
			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_bad
				title = ecclesiastic_title_refusal_toast_tt
				left_icon = scope:recipient
			}
		}
		grant_title_populate_concerned_de_jure_list_effect = { TITLE_GAINER = scope:recipient }
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:recipient = {
					is_ai = no
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:actor = { is_ai = yes }
				}
				scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				add_opinion = {
						modifier = refusal_opinion
						target = scope:recipient
						opinion = -40
					}
				}
			}
			every_in_list = {
				list = grateful_refusal_chars
				add_opinion = {
					target = scope:recipient
					modifier = respected_de_jure_opinion
					opinion = 20
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:recipient = {
						is_independent_ruler = no
						liege = {
							NOR = {
								is_in_list = grateful_refusal_chars
								this = scope:puppet_or_actor
							}
						}
					}
				}
				scope:recipient.liege = {
					add_opinion = {
						target = scope:recipient
						modifier = loyalty_opinion
						opinion = 20
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
