				}
			}
			add = 25
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -80
			scope:recipient = {
				sub_realm_size <= 1
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -60
			scope:recipient = {
				sub_realm_size <= 3
				sub_realm_size > 1
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -40
			scope:recipient = {
				sub_realm_size <= 6
				sub_realm_size > 3
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -20
			scope:recipient = {
				sub_realm_size <= 9
				sub_realm_size > 6
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -10
			scope:recipient = {
				sub_realm_size <= 12
				sub_realm_size > 9
			}
		}

		modifier = {
			add = 25
			scope:puppet_or_actor = { has_any_good_relationship_with_character_trigger = { CHARACTER = scope:recipient } }
		}
	}
}

#Head of faith or head of rite bestows an unheld de jure liege title
ecclesiastical_bestow_title_interaction = {
	category = interaction_category_religion
	icon = icon_clergy_claim_unformed_kingdom
	notification_text = ECCLESIASTICAL_BESTOW_TITLE_NOTIFICATION
	interface_priority = 69
	common_interaction = yes
	target_type = title
	target_filter = dejure_titles
	target_source = recipient
	show_all_targets = yes
	popup_on_receive = yes

	desc = ecclesiastical_bestow_title_interaction_desc

	is_available = {
		has_pam_dlc_trigger = yes
	}
	cost = {
		gold = {
			value = pam_ecclesiastical_bestow_title_gold_cost_value
			if = {
				limit = { scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes }
				multiply = 0
			}
		}
		treasury = {
			value = pam_ecclesiastical_bestow_title_treasury_cost_value
			if = {
				limit = { scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes }
				multiply = 0
			}
		}
		piety = {
			value = pam_ecclesiastical_bestow_title_piety_cost_value
			if = {
				limit = { scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes }
				multiply = 0
			}
		}
	}

	is_shown = {
		scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
			government_has_flag = government_is_theocracy
			is_available_quick = {
				adult = yes
				alive = yes
				imprisoned = no
				incapable = no
			}
		}
		scope:puppet_or_actor != scope:recipient
		scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
			trigger_if = {
				limit = { exists = scope:recipient.religious_head_or_challenger }
				scope:recipient.religious_head_or_challenger = this
			}
			trigger_else = {
				highest_held_title_tier >= tier_duchy
				government_has_flag = government_is_theocracy
				scope:recipient.rite.head_of_rite ?= this
			}
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			is_landed = yes
			highest_held_title_tier >= tier_county
			highest_held_title_tier < tier_empire
			faith ?= scope:puppet_or_actor.faith
			any_held_title = {
				de_jure_liege ?= {
					tier < tier_hegemony
					NOT = { exists = holder }
					is_landless_type_title = no
				}
			}
		}
	}

	can_be_picked_title = {
		scope:target = {
			tier > tier_county
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = no_bestowing_hegemony_tt
				tier < tier_hegemony
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = must_be_same_tier_or_one_higher_tt
				NOR = {
					tier > scope:recipient.highest_held_title_tier_plus_one
					tier < scope:recipient.highest_held_title_tier
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = target_has_holder_tt
				NOT = { exists = holder }
			}
			is_landless_type_title = no
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			any_held_title = {
				de_jure_liege = scope:target
			}
		}
		# Warcraft
		# trigger_if = {
		# 	limit = {
		# 		scope:target ?= {
		# 			tier = tier_empire
		# 		}
		# 		exists = struggle:iberian_struggle
		# 	}
		# 	custom_tooltip = {
		# 		text = bestow_empire_iberian_struggle_tt
		# 		scope:recipient = {
		# 			NOT = {
		# 				any_character_struggle = {
		# 					is_struggle_type = iberian_struggle
		# 				}
		# 			}
		# 		}
		# 	}
		# }
	}

	has_valid_target_showing_failures_only = {
		exists = scope:target
		scope:target = {
			tier >= scope:recipient.highest_held_title_tier
			tier > tier_county
			is_landless_type_title = no
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			any_held_title = {
				de_jure_liege = scope:target
			}
		}
	}

	is_valid_showing_failures_only = {
		scope:puppet_or_actor = {
			piety_level >= 2
			trigger_if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						religious_head_or_challenger ?= scope:puppet_or_actor
					}
					scope:recipient = {
						is_independent_ruler = no
					}
				}
        		custom_tooltip = {
					text = recipient_head_of_rite_is_not_lieges_head_of_rite_tt
					exists = scope:recipient.liege.rite.head_of_rite
					rite.head_of_rite ?= scope:recipient.liege.rite.head_of_rite
				}
        	}
        	NOT = {
        		has_trait = excommunicated
        	}
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			is_available_quick = {
				incapable = no
				imprisoned = no
				ruler = yes
			}
			NOT = {
				has_trait = excommunicated
			}
			faith = scope:puppet_or_actor.faith
			highest_held_title_tier >= tier_county
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = recipient_can_recieve_titles_normally_tt
				NOT = {
					is_vassal_or_below_of = scope:puppet_or_actor
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = recipient_liege_is_two_tiers_higher_or_none_tt
				OR = {
					is_independent_ruler = yes
					highest_held_title_tier < liege.highest_held_title_tier_minus_one
				}
			}
			trigger_if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						scope:puppet_or_actor.religious_head_or_challenger ?= scope:puppet_or_actor
					}
				}
				rite = scope:puppet_or_actor.rite
			}
			trigger_if = {
				limit = {
					exists = struggle:iberian_struggle
					scope:recipient = {
						any_character_struggle = {
							this = struggle:iberian_struggle
						}
					}
				}
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = bestow_empire_iberian_struggle_tt
					scope:recipient = {
						any_held_title = {
							de_jure_liege ?= {
								tier < tier_empire
								tier >= scope:recipient.highest_held_title_tier
								tier >= tier_county
								NOT = { exists = holder }
								is_landless_type_title = no
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = any_available_title_same_tier_or_higher_tt
				scope:recipient = {
					any_held_title = {
						de_jure_liege ?= {
							tier < tier_hegemony
							tier >= scope:recipient.highest_held_title_tier
							NOT = { exists = holder }
							is_landless_type_title = no
						}
					}
				}
			}

		}
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes }
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				gold >= pam_ecclesiastical_bestow_title_gold_cost_value
			}
		}
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes }
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				treasury >= pam_ecclesiastical_bestow_title_treasury_cost_value
			}
		}
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes }
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				piety >= pam_ecclesiastical_bestow_title_piety_cost_value
			}
		}
	}

	# A title handed over with no strings attached is never refused; the hook demands still go through ai_accept.
	auto_accept = {
		custom_description = {
			text = ecclesiastical_bestow_title_asks_nothing_tt
			scope:demand_nothing = yes
		}
	}

	send_options_exclusive = yes
	send_option = {
		is_valid = {
			scope:actor = {
				NOT = {
					has_strong_hook = scope:recipient
				}
				trigger_if = {
					limit = { is_ai = yes }
					ai_greed >= medium_positive_ai_value
				}
			}
		}
		flag = gain_strong_hook
		localization = GAIN_STRONG_HOOK_INTERACTION_OPTION
	}
	send_option = {
		is_valid = {
			scope:actor = {
				NOT = {
					has_hook = scope:recipient
				}
				trigger_if = {
					limit = { is_ai = yes }
					ai_greed > low_negative_ai_value
				}
			}
		}
		flag = gain_weak_hook
		localization = GAIN_WEAK_HOOK_INTERACTION_OPTION
	}
	send_option = {
		flag = demand_nothing
		localization = DEMAND_NOTHING_INTERACTION_OPTION
		starts_enabled = { always = yes }
	}
	ai_accept = {
		base = 100
		
		modifier = { # Peace of God: a title bestowed by the Church is a title not fought over
			scope:recipient.rite ?= {
				rite_has_parameter = tenet_peace_of_god_clergy_peacemaking
			}
			add = 40
			desc = pam_peace_of_god_clergy_peacemaking_accept_reason
		}
		modifier = {
			scope:recipient = {
				OR = {
					ai_energy > 0
					ai_energy < 0
				}
			}
			add = {
				value = scope:recipient.ai_energy
				multiply = 0.25
			} 
			desc = RECIPIENT_ENERGY_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			scope:recipient = {
				OR = {
					ai_greed > 0
					ai_greed < 0
				}
			}
			add = {
				value = scope:recipient.ai_greed
				multiply = 0.25
			} 
			desc = RECIPIENT_GREED_REASON
		}

		modifier = {
			scope:recipient = {
				is_independent_ruler = no
				OR = {
					opinion = {
						target = scope:recipient.liege
						value >= 25
					}
					has_dread_level_towards = {
						target = scope:recipient.liege
						level >= 1
					}
				}
				
			}
			add = {
				value = -30
				if = {
					limit = {
						scope:recipient = {
							opinion = {
								target = scope:recipient.liege
								value >= 80
							}
						}
					}
					add = -50
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						scope:recipient = {
							has_dread_level_towards = {
								target = scope:recipient.liege
								level >= 1
							}
						}
					}
					add = -20
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						scope:recipient = {
							has_dread_level_towards = {
								target = scope:recipient.liege
								level >= 2
							}
						}
					}
					add = -50
				}
			}
			desc = CONCERN_ABOUT_LIEGE_REASON
		}

		modifier = {
			scope:recipient ?= {
				ai_zeal != 0
			}
			
			add = {
				value = scope:recipient.ai_zeal
				multiply = 0.5
			}
			desc = RECIPIENT_ZEALOUSNESS_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -100
			scope:recipient = { has_trait = paranoid }
			desc = DEMAND_SUBMISSION_RECIPIENT_IS_PARANOID
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -100
			scope:recipient = { has_any_bad_relationship_with_character_trigger = { CHARACTER = scope:puppet_or_actor } }
			desc = RECIPIENT_BAD_RELATIONS
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -10
			exists = scope:target
			scope:recipient = {
				any_claim = {
					tier >= scope:target.tier
				}
			}
			desc = RECIPIENT_POWERFUL_CLAIMS
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 75
			exists = scope:target
			scope:recipient = {
				any_claim = {
					this = scope:target
				}
			}
			desc = RECIPIENT_CLAIMS_TARGET
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -50
			scope:recipient = {
				any_heir_title = {
					tier >= tier_kingdom
					place_in_line_of_succession = {
						target = scope:recipient
						value = 1
					}
				}
			}
			desc = RECIPIENT_POWERFUL_HEIR
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 200
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				has_strong_hook = scope:recipient
			}
			desc = HAS_STRONG_HOOK_RECIPIENT_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 25
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				NOT = { has_strong_hook = scope:recipient }
				has_hook = scope:recipient
			}
			desc = HAS_HOOK_RECIPIENT_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 75
			scope:recipient = {
				government_has_flag = government_is_theocracy
			}
			desc = IS_THEOCRATIC_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 100
			scope:recipient = { has_any_good_relationship_with_character_trigger = { CHARACTER = scope:puppet_or_actor } }
			desc = RECIPIENT_GOOD_RELATIONS
		}
		modifier = {
			add = {
				value = scope:recipient.intrigue
				subtract = 12
				multiply = -10
			}
			scope:recipient.intrigue > 12
			desc = RECIPIENT_RESISTS_HOOK_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 300
			puppeteer ?= scope:puppet_or_actor
			desc = IS_YOUR_PUPPET_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			add = {
				value = 50
				if = {
					limit = {
						scope:recipient.house.house_head = scope:puppet_or_actor
					}
					add = 50
				}
			}
			exists = scope:puppet_or_actor.house
			scope:recipient = {
				house ?= scope:puppet_or_actor.house
			}
			desc = OF_YOUR_HOUSE_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 20
			exists = scope:puppet_or_actor.dynasty
			scope:recipient = {
				dynasty ?= scope:puppet_or_actor.dynasty
			}
			desc = OF_YOUR_DYNASTY_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 40
			scope:recipient = {
				any_sub_realm_county = {
					any_neighboring_county = {
						holder.top_liege ?= {
							NOT = {
								faith = scope:puppet_or_actor.faith
							}
							scope:puppet_or_actor.rite = {
								rite_hostility_level = {
									target = prev.rite
									value >= faith_hostile_level
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			desc = NEIGHBORS_HOSTILE_HEATHENS_REASON
		}
		opinion_modifier = {
			opinion_target = scope:puppet_or_actor
			who = scope:recipient
			multiplier = 1
			desc = AI_OPINION_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -100
			scope:gain_weak_hook ?= yes
			desc = GAIN_WEAK_HOOK_INTERACTION_OPTION_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -250
			scope:gain_strong_hook ?= yes
			desc = GAIN_STRONG_HOOK_INTERACTION_OPTION_REASON
		}
	}

	on_accept = {
		if = {
			limit = { scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes }
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				if = {
					limit = { pam_ecclesiastical_bestow_title_treasury_cost_value > 0 }
					remove_treasury = pam_ecclesiastical_bestow_title_treasury_cost_value
				}
				if = {
					limit = { pam_ecclesiastical_bestow_title_gold_cost_value > 0 }
					remove_short_term_gold = {
						value = pam_ecclesiastical_bestow_title_gold_cost_value
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { pam_ecclesiastical_bestow_title_piety_cost_value > 0 }
					add_piety = {
						value = {
							add = pam_ecclesiastical_bestow_title_piety_cost_value
							max = piety
							multiply = -1
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		scope:puppet_or_actor = {
			save_scope_as = religious_authority
		}
		#Show opinion losses to relevant characters
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:actor = {
					is_ai = no
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:recipient = {
						is_independent_ruler = no
						liege = {
							NOT = {
								this = scope:puppet_or_actor
							}
						}
					}
				}
				scope:recipient.liege = {
					add_opinion = {
						target = scope:puppet_or_actor
						modifier = circumvented_my_authority_opinion
						opinion = {
							value = -25
							if = {
								limit = {
									exists = scope:target
									scope:target.tier = scope:recipient.liege.highest_held_title_tier_minus_one
								}
								add = -25
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			hidden_effect = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						scope:recipient = {
							liege = {
								NOT = {
									this = scope:puppet_or_actor
								}
							}
						}
					}
					scope:recipient.liege = {
						add_opinion = {
							target = scope:puppet_or_actor
							modifier = circumvented_my_authority_opinion
							opinion = {
								value = -25
								if = {
									limit = {
										exists = scope:target
										scope:target.tier = scope:recipient.liege.highest_held_title_tier_minus_one
									}
									add = -25
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:recipient = {
					is_ai = no
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:recipient = {
						is_independent_ruler = no
						liege = {
							NOT = {
								this = scope:puppet_or_actor
							}
						}
					}
				}
				scope:recipient.liege = {
					add_opinion = {
						target = scope:recipient
						modifier = circumvented_my_authority_opinion
						opinion = {
							value = -15
							if = {
								limit = {
									exists = scope:target
									scope:target.tier = scope:recipient.liege.highest_held_title_tier_minus_one
								}
								add = -15
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			hidden_effect = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						scope:recipient = {
							is_independent_ruler = no
							liege = {
								NOT = {
									this = scope:puppet_or_actor
								}
							}
						}
					}
					scope:recipient.liege = {
						add_opinion = {
							target = scope:recipient
							modifier = circumvented_my_authority_opinion
							opinion = {
								value = -10
								if = {
									limit = {
										exists = scope:target
										scope:target.tier = scope:recipient.liege.highest_held_title_tier_minus_one
									}
									add = -10
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			show_as_tooltip = { bestow_title_effect = yes }
		}
		scope:puppet_or_actor = {
			trigger_event = pam_interactions_events.0091
		}
	}
	on_decline = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:recipient = {
					is_ai = no
				}
				scope:actor = { is_ai = yes }
			}
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						exists = scope:target
					}
					add_opinion = {
						modifier = refusal_opinion
						target = scope:recipient
						opinion = {
							value = -25
							multiply = {
								value = scope:target.tier
								subtract = 2
							}
						}
					}
				}
				else = {
					add_opinion = {
						modifier = refusal_opinion
						target = scope:recipient
						opinion = -50
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:recipient = {
						is_independent_ruler = no
						liege = {
							NOT = {
								this = scope:puppet_or_actor
							}
						}
					}
				}
				scope:recipient.liege = {
					add_opinion = {
						target = scope:recipient
						modifier = loyalty_opinion
						opinion = {
							value = 10
							if = {
								limit = {
									exists = scope:target
									scope:target.tier = scope:recipient.liege.highest_held_title_tier_minus_one
								}
								add = 10
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		scope:actor = {
			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_bad
				title = ecclesiastic_title_refusal_toast_tt
				left_icon = scope:recipient
			}
		}
	}

	ai_targets = {
		ai_recipients = neighboring_rulers_including_tributary_borders
		ai_recipients = dynasty
		ai_recipients = realm_characters
		ai_recipients = subordinates
		ai_recipients = rulers_in_clerical_region
		max = 5
	}

	ai_frequency_by_tier = {
		barony = 0
		county = 60
		duchy = 120
		kingdom = 240
		empire = 0
		hegemony = 0
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		base = 20

		modifier = { # Peace of God: the Church would rather settle a title than watch it be fought over
			add = 25
			scope:puppet_or_actor.rite ?= {
				rite_has_parameter = tenet_peace_of_god_clergy_peacemaking
			}
		}

		modifier = {
			add = 10
			scope:recipient = {
				government_has_flag = government_is_theocracy
			}
		}

		modifier = {
			add = 100
			puppeteer ?= scope:puppet_or_actor
		}

		modifier = {
			exists = scope:puppet_or_actor.house
			scope:recipient = {
				house ?= scope:puppet_or_actor.house
			}
			add = 10
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 5
			exists = scope:puppet_or_actor.dynasty
			scope:recipient = {
				dynasty ?= scope:puppet_or_actor.dynasty
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 20
			scope:recipient = {
				any_sub_realm_county = {
					any_neighboring_county = {
						holder.top_liege ?= {
							NOT = {
								faith = scope:puppet_or_actor.faith
							}
							scope:puppet_or_actor.rite = {
								rite_hostility_level = {
									target = prev.rite
									value >= faith_hostile_level
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = {
				value = scope:recipient.piety_level
				multiply = 10
			}
		}
		opinion_modifier = {
			opinion_target = scope:recipient
			who = scope:actor
			multiplier = 0.5
		}
		modifier = {
			scope:recipient = {
				is_independent_ruler = no
			}
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				opinion = {
					target = scope:recipient.liege
					value >= 30
				}
			}
			add = -50
		}
		modifier = {
			scope:recipient = {
				is_independent_ruler = no
			}
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				opinion = {
					target = scope:recipient.liege
					value <= -50
				}
			}
			add = 10
		}
		modifier = {
			scope:recipient = {
				is_independent_ruler = no
			}
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				opinion = {
					target = scope:recipient.liege
					value <= -100
				}
			}
			add = 25
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -80
			exists = scope:target
			scope:target.tier >= tier_empire
			scope:recipient = {
				sub_realm_size <= 10
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -50
			exists = scope:target
			scope:target.tier >= tier_empire
			scope:recipient = {
				sub_realm_size <= 20
				sub_realm_size > 10
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -20
			exists = scope:target
			scope:target.tier >= tier_empire
			scope:recipient = {
				sub_realm_size <= 30
				sub_realm_size > 20
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -10
			exists = scope:target
			scope:target.tier >= tier_empire
			scope:recipient = {
				sub_realm_size <= 40
				sub_realm_size > 30
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -5
			exists = scope:target
			scope:target.tier >= tier_empire
			scope:recipient = {
				sub_realm_size <= 50
				sub_realm_size > 40
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -80
			exists = scope:target
			scope:target.tier >= tier_kingdom
			scope:recipient = {
				sub_realm_size <= 1
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -50
			exists = scope:target
			scope:target.tier >= tier_kingdom
			scope:recipient = {
				sub_realm_size <= 3
				sub_realm_size > 1
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -20
			exists = scope:target
			scope:target.tier >= tier_kingdom
			scope:recipient = {
				sub_realm_size <= 6
				sub_realm_size > 3
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -10
			exists = scope:target
			scope:target.tier >= tier_kingdom
			scope:recipient = {
				sub_realm_size <= 9
				sub_realm_size > 6
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -5
			exists = scope:target
			scope:target.tier >= tier_kingdom
			scope:recipient = {
				sub_realm_size <= 12
				sub_realm_size > 9
			}
		}

		modifier = {
			add = 25
			scope:puppet_or_actor = { has_any_good_relationship_with_character_trigger = { CHARACTER = scope:recipient } }
		}
		modifier = {
			save_temporary_scope_as = cleric
			add = {
				value = scope:recipient.clerical_hesitation_claim_interaction_value
				multiply = -5
			}
		}
	}
}

#Secular rulers petition the clergy to proclaim an unheld de jure title above their own and crown them with it
request_unformed_title_interaction = {
	category = interaction_category_religion
	icon = icon_clergy_claim_unformed_kingdom

	interface_priority = 40
	common_interaction = no
	target_type = title
	target_filter = dejure_titles
	target_source = actor
	show_all_targets = yes
	notification_text = REQUEST_UNFORMED_TITLE_NOTIFICATION
	greeting = positive

	use_diplomatic_range = yes
	ai_min_reply_days = 1
	ai_max_reply_days = 5
	popup_on_receive = yes

	desc = request_unformed_title_interaction_desc

	cooldown_against_recipient = { years = 10 }

	is_available = {
		has_pam_dlc_trigger = yes
	}

	is_shown = {
		scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
			highest_held_title_tier >= tier_county
			is_landed = yes
			pam_clergy_interactions_faith_trigger = yes
			NOT = { government_has_flag = government_is_theocracy }
			highest_held_title_tier < tier_empire
			is_ruler = yes
		}
		scope:puppet_or_actor != scope:recipient
		scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
			NOT = { is_at_war_with = scope:recipient }
			any_held_title = {
				de_jure_liege ?= {
					NOT = { exists = holder }
					tier < tier_hegemony
					is_landless_type_title = no
				}
			}
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			pam_request_legitimacy_priest_trigger = { CHARACTER = scope:puppet_or_actor }
			NOT = { this = scope:actor }
		}
	}

	is_valid_showing_failures_only = {
		scope:puppet_or_actor = {
			is_at_war = no
			clergy_interaction_generic_piety_level_or_puppet_req_trigger = yes
			is_available_quick = {
				incapable = no
				imprisoned = no
				hostage = no
			}
			is_landed = yes
			NOT = {
				has_trait = excommunicated
			}
			primary_title = {
				is_landless_type_title = no
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = request_unformed_title_not_clerics_vassal_tt
				NOT = {
					is_vassal_or_below_of = scope:recipient
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = request_unformed_title_liege_two_tiers_higher_tt
				OR = {
					is_independent_ruler = yes
					highest_held_title_tier < liege.highest_held_title_tier_minus_one
				}
			}
			NOT = {
				has_character_modifier = promised_pilgrimage_to_clergy_modifier
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = valid_request_unformed_title_tt
				any_held_title = {
					de_jure_liege ?= {
						target_valid_request_unformed_title_trigger = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			is_available_quick = {
				incapable = no
				imprisoned = no
				hostage = no
				ruler = yes
				adult = yes
			}
			NOT = {
				has_trait = excommunicated
			}
			piety_level >= 2
			highest_held_title_tier >= tier_duchy
			faith = scope:puppet_or_actor.faith
			trigger_if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						scope:puppet_or_actor.religious_head_or_challenger ?= this
					}
				}
				rite = scope:puppet_or_actor.rite
			}
		}
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes }
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				piety >= pam_request_unformed_title_piety_cost_value
			}
		}
	}

	can_be_picked_title = {
		scope:target = {
			target_valid_request_unformed_title_trigger = yes
		}
		# Warcraft
		# trigger_if = {
		# 	limit = {
		# 		scope:target ?= {
		# 			tier = tier_empire
		# 		}
		# 		exists = struggle:iberian_struggle
		# 	}
		# 	custom_tooltip = {
		# 		text = request_unformed_title_empire_iberian_struggle_tt
		# 		scope:puppet_or_actor = {
		# 			NOT = {
		# 				any_character_struggle = {
		# 					is_struggle_type = iberian_struggle
		# 				}
		# 			}
		# 		}
		# 	}
		# }
	}

	has_valid_target_showing_failures_only = {
		exists = scope:target
		scope:target = {
			is_landless_type_title = no
			is_head_of_faith = no
			tier > tier_county
			tier >= scope:puppet_or_actor.highest_held_title_tier
			NOT = { exists = holder }
			any_direct_de_jure_vassal_title = {
				holder ?= scope:puppet_or_actor
			}
		}
	}

	cost = {
		piety = {
			value = pam_request_unformed_title_piety_cost_value
			if = {
				limit = { scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes }
				multiply = 0
			}
		}
	}

	auto_accept = {
		custom_description = {
			text = "spending_hook"
			subject = scope:puppet_or_actor
			object = scope:recipient
			scope:use_hook = yes
			scope:puppet_or_actor = { has_strong_hook = scope:recipient }
		}
	}

	on_accept = {
		if = {
			limit = { scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes }
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				if = {
					limit = { pam_request_unformed_title_piety_cost_value > 0 }
					add_piety = {
						value = {
							add = pam_request_unformed_title_piety_cost_value
							max = piety
							multiply = -1
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			save_scope_as = cleric
		}
		#Crowning a vassal over their liege's head is an affront to the liege
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:puppet_or_actor = {
					is_independent_ruler = no
					liege = {
						NOT = {
							this = scope:recipient
						}
					}
				}
			}
			scope:puppet_or_actor.liege = {
				add_opinion = {
					target = scope:recipient
					modifier = circumvented_my_authority_opinion
					opinion = -25
				}
				add_opinion = {
					target = scope:puppet_or_actor
					modifier = circumvented_my_authority_opinion
					opinion = {
						value = -15
						if = {
							limit = {
								exists = scope:target
								scope:target.tier = scope:puppet_or_actor.liege.highest_held_title_tier_minus_one
							}
							add = -15
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:recipient = {
					is_ai = no
				}
			}
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				add_opinion = {
					target = scope:recipient
					modifier = grateful_opinion
					opinion = 30
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			hidden_effect = {
				scope:puppet_or_actor = {
					add_opinion = {
						target = scope:recipient
						modifier = grateful_opinion
						opinion = 50
					}
				}
			}
		}
		scope:puppet_or_actor = {
			show_as_tooltip = { request_unformed_title_effect = yes }
			trigger_event = pam_interactions_events.0097
		}
	}

	on_decline = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:recipient = {
					is_ai = no
				}
			}
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				add_opinion = {
					target = scope:recipient
					modifier = refusal_opinion
					opinion = -50
				}
			}
		}
		scope:puppet_or_actor = {
			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_bad
				title = request_unformed_title_refusal_toast_tt
				left_icon = scope:recipient
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes
				scope:actor != scope:recipient
			}
			scope:actor = {
				send_interface_toast = {
					type = event_toast_effect_bad
					title = request_unformed_title_refusal_toast_tt_puppeteer
					left_icon = scope:puppet_or_actor
					right_icon = scope:recipient
				}
			}
		}
	}

	send_options_exclusive = no
	send_option = {
		is_valid = {
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				has_usable_hook = scope:recipient
			}
			trigger_if = {
				limit = {
					scope:recipient = {
						is_ai = no
					}
				}
				scope:actor = { is_ai = no }
			}
		}
		flag = use_hook
		localization = EDUCATION_HOOK
	}
	send_option = {
		is_valid = {
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				short_term_gold >= major_gold_value
			}
		}
		flag = donate_faith
		localization = DONATE_TO_THE_FAITH_INTERACTION_OPTION
	}
	send_option = {
		is_valid = {
			NOT = {
				scope:recipient = {
					has_usable_hook = scope:puppet_or_actor
				}
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				can_add_hook = {
					target = scope:puppet_or_actor
					type = favor_hook
				}
			}
		}
		flag = offer_hook
		localization = OFFER_HOOK_INTERACTION_OPTION
	}
	#Swear to wrest the rest of the realm from the heathens holding it. Hidden below kingdom tier, since the free casus belli only covers kingdom-tier holy wars.
	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:target ?= {
				tier = tier_kingdom
			}
		}
		is_valid = {
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				faith = {
					NOT = { has_doctrine_parameter = unreformed }
				}
				#A personal vow of pacifism bars offensive war outright, and bonus_major_holy_war grants no exemption from it
				NOT = { has_personal_tenet_flag = tenet_pacifism_no_offensive_war }
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = valid_request_unformed_title_holy_war_option_tt
				scope:target ?= {
					any_de_jure_county = {
						percent >= 0.25
						holder ?= {
							save_temporary_scope_as = heathen_holder
							rite = {
								is_hostile_enough_for_holy_war_trigger = { RITE = scope:puppet_or_actor.rite }
							}
							#A holder you cannot lawfully attack is no use as a war target, vassals and fellow subjects included
							scope:puppet_or_actor = {
								allows_declaring_war_against_target_trigger = { TARGET = scope:heathen_holder }
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		flag = promise_holy_war
		localization = PROMISE_HOLY_WAR_INTERACTION_OPTION
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			pam_offer_pilgrimage_option_shown_trigger = { ACTOR = scope:puppet_or_actor }
		}
		is_valid = {
			pam_offer_pilgrimage_option_valid_trigger = { ACTOR = scope:puppet_or_actor }
		}
		flag = offer_pilgrimage
		localization = OFFER_PILGRIMAGE_INTERACTION_OPTION
	}

	ai_accept = {
		base = -100

		modifier = {
			desc = pam_sf_ear_of_clergy_accept_reason
			scope:puppet_or_actor = { has_character_modifier = pam_sf_ear_of_clergy_modifier }
			add = 25
		}
		modifier = {
			desc = SCHEME_WEAK_HOOK_USED
			scope:use_hook = yes
			scope:puppet_or_actor = { NOT = { has_strong_hook = scope:recipient } }
			add = 200
		}
		modifier = {
			desc = OFFER_HOOK_INTERACTION_OPTION_REASON
			scope:offer_hook = yes
			add = {
				value = scope:puppet_or_actor.highest_held_title_tier
				multiply = 20
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			desc = DONATE_FAITH_INTERACTION_OPTION_REASON
			scope:donate_faith = yes
			add = 50
		}
		modifier = {
			desc = PROMISE_HOLY_WAR_INTERACTION_OPTION_REASON
			scope:promise_holy_war = yes
			add = 75
		}
		modifier = {
			add = pam_offer_pilgrimage_acceptance_value
			scope:offer_pilgrimage = yes
			desc = OFFER_PILGRIMAGE_INTERACTION_OPTION_REASON
		}

		general_secular_to_ecclesiastic_acceptance_modifier = yes

		#Asking to be raised above your own rank is the real favour; a crown costs more than a coronet
		modifier = {
			desc = request_unformed_title_tier_above_own_reason
			exists = scope:target
			scope:target = {
				tier >= tier_kingdom
				tier > scope:puppet_or_actor.highest_held_title_tier
			}
			add = {
				value = scope:target.tier
				subtract = 3
				multiply = -50
			}
		}
		#A ruler who already rules the land is simply asking for the crown that goes with it. These bands stack.
		modifier = {
			desc = request_unformed_title_partly_controls_target_reason
			exists = scope:target
			scope:target = {
				any_de_jure_county = {
					percent >= 0.33
					holder ?= {
						target_is_same_character_or_above = scope:puppet_or_actor
					}
				}
			}
			add = 40
		}
		modifier = {
			desc = request_unformed_title_mostly_controls_target_reason
			exists = scope:target
			scope:target = {
				any_de_jure_county = {
					percent >= 0.5
					holder ?= {
						target_is_same_character_or_above = scope:puppet_or_actor
					}
				}
			}
			add = 40
		}
		modifier = {
			desc = request_unformed_title_controls_target_reason
			exists = scope:target
			scope:target = {
				any_de_jure_county = {
					count = all
					holder ?= {
						target_is_same_character_or_above = scope:puppet_or_actor
					}
				}
			}
			add = 70
		}
		#The clergy will not crown a ruler over land that is not yet theirs in spirit
		modifier = {
			desc = request_unformed_title_faithless_de_jure_reason
			exists = scope:target
			scope:target = {
				NOT = {
					any_de_jure_county = {
						percent >= 0.75
						faith = scope:puppet_or_actor.faith
					}
				}
			}
			add = -50
		}
		modifier = {
			desc = LOW_LEGITIMACY_REASON
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				has_legitimacy = yes
				legitimacy_level <= 1
			}
			add = -150
		}
		modifier = {
			desc = LOW_LEGITIMACY_REASON
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				has_legitimacy = yes
				legitimacy_level = 2
			}
			add = -25
		}
		modifier = {
			desc = HIGH_LEGITIMACY_REASON
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				has_legitimacy = yes
				legitimacy_level >= 4
			}
			add = {
				value = scope:puppet_or_actor.legitimacy_level
				subtract = 3
				multiply = 50
			}
		}
	}

	ai_targets = {
		ai_recipients = head_of_faith
		ai_recipients = realm_priest_superior
		ai_recipients = realm_priest_top_superior
		max = 3
	}

	ai_frequency_by_tier = {
		barony = 0
		county = 0
		duchy = 60
		kingdom = 90
		empire = 0
		hegemony = 0
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		base = 20
		modifier = {
			exists = scope:target
			scope:target = {
				any_de_jure_county = {
					count = all
					holder ?= {
						target_is_same_character_or_above = scope:puppet_or_actor
					}
				}
			}
			add = 50
		}
		modifier = {
			exists = scope:target
			scope:target = {
				any_de_jure_county = {
					percent >= 0.5
					holder ?= {
						target_is_same_character_or_above = scope:puppet_or_actor
					}
				}
			}
			add = 25
		}
		modifier = {
			OR = {
				ai_greed < 0
				ai_energy < 0
				trigger_if = {
					limit = {
						has_legitimacy = yes
					}
					legitimacy_level < 2
				}
			}
			factor = 0
		}
	}
}

pam_clergy_legitimize_bastard_interaction = {
	category = interaction_category_religion
	icon = legitimized_bastard
	use_diplomatic_range = no

	desc = pam_clergy_legitimize_bastard_interaction_desc

	is_available = {
		pam_is_clergy_ruler_trigger = yes
		has_pam_dlc_trigger = yes
	}

	is_shown = {
		scope:actor.rite ?= {
			rite_has_parameter = bastards_legitimize
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			this != scope:actor
			has_trait = bastard
			is_lowborn = no
			faith ?= scope:actor.faith
			rite ?= {
				rite_has_parameter = bastards_legitimize
			}
		}
	}

	is_valid_showing_failures_only = {
		scope:actor = {
			is_available_adult = yes
			NOT = {
				has_trait = excommunicated
			}
		}
		#Range: the Head of Faith reaches anyone, archbishops their clerical region, and all theocrats their own top realm
		trigger_if = {
			limit = {
				scope:actor = {
					NOT = { religious_head_or_challenger ?= this }
					any_held_title = { has_clerical_region = yes }
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = pam_clergy_legitimize_bastard_archbishop_range_tt
				OR = {
					scope:recipient.top_liege ?= scope:actor.top_liege
					scope:recipient = {
						OR = {
							capital_county ?= {
								clerical_region_title ?= { holder = scope:actor }
							}
							location.county ?= {
								clerical_region_title ?= { holder = scope:actor }
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		trigger_else_if = {
			limit = {
				scope:actor = {
					NOT = { religious_head_or_challenger ?= this }
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = pam_clergy_legitimize_bastard_realm_range_tt
				scope:recipient.top_liege ?= scope:actor.top_liege
			}
		}
		trigger_else = {
			always = yes
		}
	}

	cost = {
		piety = minor_piety_value
	}

	auto_accept = yes

	on_accept = {
		scope:actor = {
			send_interface_message = {
				type = event_religious_good
				title = pam_clergy_legitimize_bastard_interaction_notification
				right_icon = scope:recipient
				scope:recipient = {
					hidden_effect = {
						remove_trait = bastard
					}
					add_trait_force_tooltip = legitimized_bastard
					add_opinion = {
						target = scope:actor
						modifier = legitimized_me_opinion
						opinion = 35
					}
				}
				add_hook = {
					type = legitimized_by_clergy_hook
					target = scope:recipient
				}
			}
		}

		hidden_effect = {
			scope:recipient = {
				trigger_event = pam_interactions_events.0100
				every_parent = {
					limit = {
						this != scope:actor
					}
					if = { #Nudge towards friendship
						limit = {
							NOR = {
								has_relation_friend = scope:actor
								has_relation_potential_friend = scope:actor
							}
						}
						set_relation_potential_friend = scope:actor
					}
					trigger_event = pam_interactions_events.0101
				}
			}
			scope:actor = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_character_modifier = oath_of_children
						exists = scope:actor.house
						scope:recipient.house ?= scope:actor.house
					}
					remove_character_modifier = oath_of_children
					add_character_modifier = oath_of_children
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

#Secular rulers petition the clergy to recognize a neighboring title as de jure part of their primary realm
ecclesiastical_de_jure_shifting_interaction = {
	category = interaction_category_religion
	icon = ecclesiastical_de_jure_shifting_interaction

	interface_priority = 39
	common_interaction = yes
	target_type = title
	target_filter = dejure_titles
	target_source = actor
	show_all_targets = yes
	notification_text = ecclesiastical_de_jure_shifting_interaction_notification
	greeting = positive

	use_diplomatic_range = yes
	ai_min_reply_days = 1
	ai_max_reply_days = 5
	popup_on_receive = yes

	desc = ecclesiastical_de_jure_shifting_interaction_desc

	cooldown = { years = 3 }

	is_available = {
		has_pam_dlc_trigger = yes
	}

	is_shown = {
		scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
			highest_held_title_tier >= tier_kingdom
			is_landed = yes
			pam_clergy_interactions_faith_trigger = yes
			is_ruler = yes
		}
		scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
			NOR = {
				this = scope:recipient
				is_at_war_with = scope:recipient
			}
			any_sub_realm_title = {
				any_this_title_or_de_jure_above = {
					target_valid_ecclesiastical_de_jure_shifting_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			pam_request_legitimacy_priest_trigger = { CHARACTER = scope:puppet_or_actor }
			NOT = { this = scope:actor }
		}
	}

	is_valid_showing_failures_only = {
		scope:puppet_or_actor = {
			is_at_war = no
			clergy_interaction_generic_piety_level_or_puppet_req_trigger = yes
			is_available_quick = {
				incapable = no
				imprisoned = no
				hostage = no
			}
			is_landed = yes
			NOT = {
				has_trait = excommunicated
			}
			primary_title = {
				is_landless_type_title = no
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = valid_ecclesiastical_de_jure_shifting_title_tt
				any_sub_realm_title = {
						any_this_title_or_de_jure_above = {
						target_valid_ecclesiastical_de_jure_shifting_trigger = yes
						any_de_jure_county = {
							percent >= 0.5
							faith = scope:puppet_or_actor.faith
						}
						any_de_jure_county = {
							percent >= 0.5
							holder = { faith = scope:puppet_or_actor.faith }
						}
					}
				}
			}
			NOT = {
				has_character_modifier = promised_pilgrimage_to_clergy_modifier
			}
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			is_available_quick = {
				incapable = no
				imprisoned = no
				hostage = no
				ruler = yes
				adult = yes
			}
			NOT = {
				has_trait = excommunicated
			}
			highest_held_title_tier >= tier_duchy

			custom_tooltip = {
				text = relevant_theocrat_tt
				OR = {
					scope:puppet_or_actor.religious_head_or_challenger ?= scope:recipient
					scope:puppet_or_actor = {
						any_liege_or_above = {
							this = scope:recipient
						}
					}
					any_held_title = {
						has_clerical_region = yes
						any_county_in_clerical_region = {
							OR = {
								this = scope:puppet_or_actor.primary_title
								scope:puppet_or_actor.primary_title = {
									is_de_jure_liege_or_above_target = prev
								}
							}
						}
					}
					AND = {
						is_landed = yes
						any_held_title = {
							OR = {
								#they have a title beneath actor's titles
								any_this_title_or_de_jure_above = {
									holder ?= scope:puppet_or_actor
								}
								#they have a title that presides over actor's titles
								any_in_de_jure_hierarchy = {
									holder ?= scope:puppet_or_actor
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes }
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				piety >= pam_ecclesiastical_de_jure_shifting_piety_cost_value
			}
		}
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes }
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				prestige >= pam_ecclesiastical_de_jure_shifting_prestige_cost_value
			}
		}
	}

	can_be_picked_title = {
		scope:target = {
			target_valid_ecclesiastical_de_jure_shifting_trigger = yes

			#At least half of its counties belong to your faith
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = half_target_de_jure_counties_belong_to_faith_tt
				any_de_jure_county = {
					percent >= 0.5
					faith = scope:puppet_or_actor.faith
				}
			}
			
			#At least half of its counties' rulers are of your faith
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = half_target_de_jure_holders_belong_to_faith_tt
				any_de_jure_county = {
					percent >= 0.5
					holder = { faith = scope:puppet_or_actor.faith }
				}
			}
		}
		# Warcraft
		# trigger_if = {
		# 	limit = {
		# 		scope:target ?= {
		# 			tier = tier_kingdom
		# 		}
		# 		exists = struggle:iberian_struggle
		# 	}
		# 	custom_tooltip = {
		# 		text = ecclesiastical_de_jure_shifting_kingdom_iberian_struggle_tt
		# 		scope:puppet_or_actor = {
		# 			NOT = {
		# 				any_character_struggle = {
		# 					is_struggle_type = iberian_struggle
		# 				}
		# 			}
		# 		}
		# 	}
		# }
	}

	has_valid_target_showing_failures_only = {
		exists = scope:target
		scope:target = {
			is_landless_type_title = no
			is_head_of_faith = no
			tier = scope:puppet_or_actor.highest_held_title_tier_minus_one
			NOT = {
				de_jure_liege = scope:puppet_or_actor.primary_title
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = petition_de_jure_integration_target_is_connected_tt
				any_de_jure_county = {
					holder ?= {
						target_is_same_character_or_above = scope:puppet_or_actor
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	cost = {
		piety = {
			value = pam_ecclesiastical_de_jure_shifting_piety_cost_value
			if = {
				limit = { scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes }
				multiply = 0
			}
		}
		prestige = {
			value = pam_ecclesiastical_de_jure_shifting_prestige_cost_value
			if = {
				limit = { scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes }
				multiply = 0
			}
		}
	}

	auto_accept = {
		custom_description = {
			text = "spending_hook"
			subject = scope:puppet_or_actor
			object = scope:recipient
			scope:use_hook = yes
			scope:puppet_or_actor = { has_strong_hook = scope:recipient }
		}
	}

	on_accept = {
		if = {
			limit = { scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes }
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				if = {
					limit = { pam_ecclesiastical_de_jure_shifting_piety_cost_value > 0 }
					add_piety = {
						value = {
							add = pam_ecclesiastical_de_jure_shifting_piety_cost_value
							max = piety
							multiply = -1
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { pam_ecclesiastical_de_jure_shifting_prestige_cost_value > 0 }
					add_prestige = {
						value = {
							add = pam_ecclesiastical_de_jure_shifting_prestige_cost_value
							max = prestige
							multiply = -1
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		scope:target ?= {
			de_jure_liege = {
				save_scope_as = former_de_jure_liege
			}
		}
		scope:puppet_or_actor = {
			save_scope_as = de_jure_master
			faith = { save_scope_as = de_jure_faith }
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			save_scope_as = cleric
		}	
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:target.de_jure_liege.holder?= {
					NOT = {
						this = scope:puppet_or_actor
					}
				}
			}
			scope:target.de_jure_liege.holder = {
				add_opinion = {
					target = scope:puppet_or_actor
					modifier = stole_de_jure_lands_opinion
					opinion = -70
				}
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_ai = no
					}
					scope:target.de_jure_liege.holder = {
						add_opinion = {
							target = scope:puppet_or_actor
							modifier = gave_away_de_jure_lands_opinion
							opinion = -50
						}
					}
				}
				else = {
					hidden_effect = {
						scope:target.de_jure_liege.holder = {
							add_opinion = {
								target = scope:puppet_or_actor
								modifier = gave_away_de_jure_lands_opinion
								opinion = -50
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_ai = no
				}
				scope:puppet_or_actor = {
					add_opinion = {
						target = scope:recipient
						modifier = grateful_opinion
						opinion = 30
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				hidden_effect = {
					scope:puppet_or_actor = {
						add_opinion = {
							target = scope:recipient
							modifier = grateful_opinion
							opinion = 50
						}
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_ai = no
				}
				custom_tooltip = every_subordinate_in_target_de_jure_liege_opinion_loss
				custom_tooltip = every_subordinate_in_actor_title_opinion_gain
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				#Bishops based in different dioceses have feelings
				every_subordinate = {
					limit = {
						capital_province.county ?= {
							any_this_title_or_de_jure_above = {
								this = scope:former_de_jure_liege
							}
						}
						NOR = {
							this = scope:puppet_or_actor
							this = scope:recipient
						}
					}
					add_opinion = {
						target = scope:recipient
						modifier = ruled_against_my_diocese_opinion
						opinion = -25
					}
				}
				every_subordinate = {
					limit = {
						capital_province.county ?= {
							any_this_title_or_de_jure_above = {
								this = scope:puppet_or_actor.primary_title
							}
						}
						NOR = {
							this = scope:puppet_or_actor
							this = scope:recipient
						}
					}
					add_opinion = {
						target = scope:recipient
						modifier = ruled_in_favor_of_my_diocese_opinion
						opinion = 25
					}
				}
			}
		}
		scope:puppet_or_actor = {
			show_as_tooltip = {
				ecclesiastical_de_jure_shifting_effect = {
					DE_JURE_MASTER = scope:puppet_or_actor
					CLERIC = scope:recipient
				}
			}
			trigger_event = pam_interactions_events.0094
		}
	}

	on_decline = {
		scope:target ?= {
			de_jure_liege = {
				save_scope_as = former_de_jure_liege
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:recipient = {
					is_ai = no
				}
			}
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				add_opinion = {
					modifier = refusal_opinion
					target = scope:recipient
					opinion = -50
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:target.de_jure_liege.holder ?= {
						NOT = {
							this = scope:puppet_or_actor
						}
					}
				}
				scope:target.de_jure_liege.holder ?= {
					add_opinion = {
						modifier = respected_de_jure_opinion
						target = scope:recipient
						opinion = 50
					}
				}
			}

			scope:recipient = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_ai = no
					}
					custom_tooltip = every_subordinate_in_target_de_jure_liege_opinion_gain
					custom_tooltip = every_subordinate_in_actor_title_opinion_loss
				}
				hidden_effect = {
					#Bishops based in different dioceses have feelings
					every_subordinate = {
						limit = {
							capital_province.county ?= {
								any_this_title_or_de_jure_above = {
									this = scope:former_de_jure_liege
								}
							}
							NOR = {
								this = scope:puppet_or_actor
								this = scope:recipient
							}
						}
						add_opinion = {
							target = scope:recipient
							modifier = ruled_in_favor_of_my_diocese_opinion
							opinion = 25
						}
					}
					every_subordinate = {
						limit = {
							capital_province.county ?= {
								any_this_title_or_de_jure_above = {
									this = scope:puppet_or_actor.primary_title
								}
							}
							NOR = {
								this = scope:puppet_or_actor
								this = scope:recipient
							}
						}
						add_opinion = {
							target = scope:recipient
							modifier = ruled_against_my_diocese_opinion
							opinion = -25
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		
		scope:puppet_or_actor = {
			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_bad
				title = ecclesiastic_de_jure_refusal_toast_tt
				left_icon = scope:recipient
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes
				scope:actor != scope:recipient
			}
			scope:actor = {
				send_interface_toast = {
					type = event_toast_effect_bad
					title = ecclesiastic_de_jure_refusal_toast_tt_puppeteer
					left_icon = scope:puppet_or_actor
					right_icon = scope:recipient
				}
			}
		}
	}

	send_options_exclusive = no
	send_option = {
		is_valid = {
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				has_usable_hook = scope:recipient
			}
			trigger_if = {
				limit = {
					scope:recipient = {
						is_ai = no
					}
				}
				scope:actor = { is_ai = no }
			}
		}
		flag = use_hook
		localization = EDUCATION_HOOK
	}
	send_option = {
		is_valid = {
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				short_term_gold >= major_gold_value
			}
		}
		flag = donate_faith
		localization = DONATE_TO_THE_FAITH_INTERACTION_OPTION
	}
	send_option = {
		is_valid = {
			NOT = {
				scope:recipient = {
					has_usable_hook = scope:puppet_or_actor
				}
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				can_add_hook = {
					target = scope:puppet_or_actor
					type = favor_hook
				}
			}
		}
		flag = offer_hook
		localization = OFFER_HOOK_INTERACTION_OPTION
	}
	#Promise to convert every ruler of the region
	send_option = {
		is_valid = {
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = valid_domination_interaction_option_tt
				scope:target ?= {
					any_de_jure_county = {
						holder ?= {
							OR = {
								faith = {
									NOT = {
										this = scope:puppet_or_actor.faith
									}
								}
								any_liege_or_above = {
									faith = {
										NOT = {
											this = scope:puppet_or_actor.faith
										}
									}
									NOT = {
										is_liege_or_above_of = scope:puppet_or_actor
									}
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		flag = promise_domination
		localization = PROMISE_DOMINATION_INTERACTION_OPTION
	}
	#Deploy missionaries throughout the kingdom
	send_option = {
		is_valid = {
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = valid_conversion_interaction_option_tt
				scope:target ?= {
					any_de_jure_county = {
						OR = {
							NOT = {
								faith = scope:puppet_or_actor.faith
							}
							trigger_if = {
								limit = {
									scope:puppet_or_actor.rite = scope:recipient.rite
								}
								AND = {
									NOT = {
										rite = scope:puppet_or_actor.rite
									}
									rite = {
										"divergence(scope:puppet_or_actor.rite)" >= medium_rite_divergence_value
									}
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		flag = promise_conversion
		localization = PROMISE_CONVERSION_INTERACTION_OPTION
	}
	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			pam_offer_pilgrimage_option_shown_trigger = { ACTOR = scope:puppet_or_actor }
		}
		is_valid = {
			pam_offer_pilgrimage_option_valid_trigger = { ACTOR = scope:puppet_or_actor }
		}
		flag = offer_pilgrimage
		localization = OFFER_PILGRIMAGE_INTERACTION_OPTION
	}

	ai_accept = {
		base = -150

		modifier = {
			scope:puppet_or_actor = { has_character_modifier = pam_sf_ear_of_clergy_modifier }
			add = 25
			desc = pam_sf_ear_of_clergy_accept_reason
		}
		modifier = { # Peace of God: redrawing de jure borders by decree spares a war of conquest
			scope:recipient.rite ?= {
				rite_has_parameter = tenet_peace_of_god_clergy_peacemaking
			}
			add = 50
			desc = pam_peace_of_god_clergy_peacemaking_accept_reason
		}
		modifier = {
			scope:use_hook = yes
			add = 200
			scope:puppet_or_actor = { NOT = { has_strong_hook = scope:recipient } }
			desc = SCHEME_WEAK_HOOK_USED
		}
		modifier = {
			add = {
				value = 20
				multiply = scope:puppet_or_actor.highest_held_title_tier
			}
			scope:offer_hook = yes
			desc = OFFER_HOOK_INTERACTION_OPTION_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			add = {
				value = 50
				if = {
					limit = {
						pam_actor_rite_has_core_simony_trigger = yes
					}
					multiply = 5
				}
			}
			scope:donate_faith = yes
			desc = DONATE_FAITH_INTERACTION_OPTION_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			add = pam_offer_pilgrimage_acceptance_value
			scope:offer_pilgrimage = yes
			desc = OFFER_PILGRIMAGE_INTERACTION_OPTION_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			add = {
				value = 0
				scope:target ?= {
					every_de_jure_county = {
						limit = {
							holder ?= {
								OR = {
									faith = {
										NOT = {
											this = scope:puppet_or_actor.faith
										}
									}
									any_liege_or_above = {
