			add = -150
			desc = LOW_LEGITIMACY_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				has_legitimacy = yes
				legitimacy_level = 1
			}
			add = -100
			desc = LOW_LEGITIMACY_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				has_legitimacy = yes
				legitimacy_level = 2
			}
			add = -50
			desc = LOW_LEGITIMACY_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				has_legitimacy = yes
				legitimacy_level = 4
			}
			add = 25
			desc = HIGH_LEGITIMACY_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				has_legitimacy = yes
				legitimacy_level = 5
			}
			add = 100
			desc = HIGH_LEGITIMACY_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				has_legitimacy = yes
				legitimacy_level = 6
			}
			add = 150
			desc = HIGH_LEGITIMACY_REASON
		}
		modifier = {
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				has_legitimacy = yes
				legitimacy_level >= 7
			}
			add = 250
			desc = HIGH_LEGITIMACY_REASON
		}
	}

	ai_targets = {
		ai_recipients = head_of_faith
		ai_recipients = realm_priest_superior
		ai_recipients = realm_priest_top_superior
		max = 3
	}

	ai_frequency_by_tier = {
		barony = 0
		county = 0
		duchy = 0
		kingdom = 60
		empire = 60
		hegemony = 60
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		base = 10
		modifier = {
			exists = scope:target
			scope:target = {
				any_de_jure_county = {
					count = all
					holder ?= {
						OR = {
							this = scope:puppet_or_actor
							any_liege_or_above = {
								this = scope:puppet_or_actor
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			add = 50
		}
		modifier = {
			exists = scope:target
			scope:target = {
				any_de_jure_county = {
					percent >= 0.5
					holder ?= {
						OR = {
							this = scope:puppet_or_actor
							any_liege_or_above = {
								this = scope:puppet_or_actor
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			add = 50
		}
		modifier = {
			OR = {
				ai_greed < 0
				ai_energy < 0
				AND = {
					scope:target.de_jure_liege.holder ?= {
						NOR = {
							this = scope:puppet_or_actor
							this = scope:recipient
						}
					}
					has_any_good_relationship_with_character_trigger = { CHARACTER = scope:target.de_jure_liege.holder }
				}
				trigger_if = {
					limit = {
						has_legitimacy = yes
					}
					legitimacy_level < 2
				}
			}
			factor = 0
		}
	}
}

# Ecclesiastical rulers grant de jure recognition to a same-faith ruler
ecclesiastical_de_jure_shifting_grant_interaction = {
	category = interaction_category_religion
	icon = ecclesiastical_de_jure_shifting_interaction

	interface_priority = 39
	common_interaction = yes
	target_type = title
	target_filter = dejure_titles
	target_source = recipient
	show_all_targets = yes
	notification_text = ecclesiastical_de_jure_shifting_grant_interaction_notification
	greeting = positive
	cooldown_against_recipient = { years = 10 }

	use_diplomatic_range = yes
	ai_min_reply_days = 1
	ai_max_reply_days = 5
	popup_on_receive = yes

	desc = ecclesiastical_de_jure_shifting_grant_interaction_desc

	is_available = {
		has_pam_dlc_trigger = yes
	}

	cost = {
		piety = {
			value = {
				add = pam_ecclesiastical_de_jure_shifting_piety_cost_value
				multiply = 0.5
			}
			if = {
				limit = { scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes }
				multiply = 0
			}
		}
		prestige = {
			value = {
				add = pam_ecclesiastical_de_jure_shifting_prestige_cost_value
				multiply = 0.5
			}
			if = {
				limit = { scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes }
				multiply = 0
			}
		}
	}

	is_shown = {
		scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
			government_has_flag = government_is_theocracy
			is_available_quick = {
				adult = yes
				alive = yes
				imprisoned = no
				incapable = no
			}
			this != scope:recipient
		}
		exists = scope:puppet_or_actor
		scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
			OR = {
				scope:recipient.religious_head_or_challenger ?= this
				AND = {
					highest_held_title_tier >= tier_duchy
					government_has_flag = government_is_theocracy
					NOT = { exists = scope:recipient.religious_head_or_challenger }
					scope:recipient.rite.head_of_rite ?= this
				}
			}
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			is_landed = yes
			highest_held_title_tier >= tier_kingdom
			faith ?= scope:puppet_or_actor.faith
			any_held_title = {
				de_jure_liege ?= {
					tier < tier_hegemony
					NOT = { exists = holder }
					is_landless_type_title = no
				}
			}
			any_sub_realm_title = {
				any_this_title_or_de_jure_above = {
					is_landless_type_title = no
					is_head_of_faith = no
					tier = scope:recipient.highest_held_title_tier_minus_one
					NOT = {
						de_jure_liege = scope:recipient.primary_title
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	is_valid_showing_failures_only = {
		scope:puppet_or_actor = {
			is_available_quick = {
				incapable = no
				imprisoned = no
				hostage = no
				ruler = yes
				adult = yes
			}
			NOT = {
				has_trait = excommunicated
			}
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			is_available_quick = {
				incapable = no
				imprisoned = no
				hostage = no
				ruler = yes
				adult = yes
			}
			is_at_war = no
			NOT = {
				has_trait = excommunicated
			}
			primary_title = {
				is_landless_type_title = no
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = valid_ecclesiastical_de_jure_shifting_grant_title_tt
				any_sub_realm_title = {
					any_this_title_or_de_jure_above = {
						is_landless_type_title = no
						is_head_of_faith = no
						tier = scope:recipient.highest_held_title_tier_minus_one
						NOT = {
							de_jure_liege = scope:recipient.primary_title
						}
						any_de_jure_county = {
							percent >= 0.5
							faith = scope:recipient.faith
						}
						any_de_jure_county = {
							percent >= 0.5
							holder = { faith = scope:recipient.faith }
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		trigger_if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { scope:puppet_or_actor.religious_head_or_challenger ?= scope:puppet_or_actor }
			}
			scope:recipient.rite = scope:puppet_or_actor.rite
		}
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes }
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				piety >= pam_ecclesiastical_de_jure_shifting_piety_cost_value
			}
		}
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes }
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				prestige >= pam_ecclesiastical_de_jure_shifting_prestige_cost_value
			}
		}
	}

	can_be_picked_title = {
		scope:target = {
			is_landless_type_title = no
			is_head_of_faith = no
			tier = scope:recipient.highest_held_title_tier_minus_one
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = ecclesiastical_de_jure_shifting_grant_not_already_de_jure_tt
				NOT = {
					de_jure_liege = scope:recipient.primary_title
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = recipient_holds_target_de_jure_county_tt
				any_de_jure_county = {
					holder ?= {
						target_is_same_character_or_above = scope:recipient
					}
				}
			}
			trigger_if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:puppet_or_actor.religious_head_or_challenger
					NOT = {
						scope:puppet_or_actor.religious_head_or_challenger = scope:puppet_or_actor
					}
					exists = scope:target.de_jure_liege.holder
				}
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = hof_is_target_de_jure_liege_tt
					NOT = {
						scope:puppet_or_actor.religious_head_or_challenger ?= scope:target.de_jure_liege.holder
					}
				}
			}
			trigger_if = {
				limit = {
					scope:actor = {
						is_ai = no
					}
				}
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = ecclesiastical_de_jure_shifting_grant_target_must_border_de_jure_tt
					any_de_jure_county = {
						any_title_to_title_neighboring_and_across_water_county = {
							any_this_title_or_de_jure_above = {
								this = scope:recipient.primary_title
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			trigger_else = {
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = ecclesiastical_de_jure_shifting_grant_target_must_border_de_jure_land_tt
					any_de_jure_county = {
						any_title_to_title_neighboring_county = {
							any_this_title_or_de_jure_above = {
								this = scope:recipient.primary_title
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}

			# At least half of its counties belong to the beneficiary's faith
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = half_target_de_jure_counties_belong_to_faith_tt
				any_de_jure_county = {
					percent >= 0.5
					faith = scope:recipient.faith
				}
			}

			# At least half of its counties' rulers are of the beneficiary's faith
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = half_target_de_jure_holders_belong_to_faith_tt
				any_de_jure_county = {
					percent >= 0.5
					holder = { faith = scope:recipient.faith }
				}
			}
		}
		# Warcraft - Remove Struggle
	}

	has_valid_target_showing_failures_only = {
		exists = scope:target
		scope:target = {
			is_landless_type_title = no
			is_head_of_faith = no
			tier = scope:recipient.highest_held_title_tier_minus_one
			NOT = {
				de_jure_liege = scope:recipient.primary_title
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = recipient_holds_target_de_jure_county_tt
				any_de_jure_county = {
					holder ?= {
						target_is_same_character_or_above = scope:recipient
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	auto_accept = {
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = no_reason_to_refuse_tt
			OR = {
				scope:recipient = { is_ai = yes }
				scope:recipient = scope:puppet_or_actor
			}
		}
	}

	on_accept = {
		if = {
			limit = { scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes }
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				if = {
					limit = { pam_ecclesiastical_de_jure_shifting_piety_cost_value > 0 }
					add_piety = {
						value = {
							add = pam_ecclesiastical_de_jure_shifting_piety_cost_value
							max = piety
							multiply = -1
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { pam_ecclesiastical_de_jure_shifting_prestige_cost_value > 0 }
					add_prestige = {
						value = {
							add = pam_ecclesiastical_de_jure_shifting_prestige_cost_value
							max = prestige
							multiply = -1
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		# The existing effect/event expects puppet_or_actor to be the beneficiary and recipient to be the clerical authority.
		scope:recipient = {
			save_scope_as = de_jure_master
		}
		scope:puppet_or_actor = {
			save_scope_as = cleric
		}
		scope:target ?= {
			de_jure_liege = {
				save_scope_as = former_de_jure_liege
			}
		}
		scope:puppet_or_actor = {
			faith = { save_scope_as = de_jure_faith }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:target.de_jure_liege.holder ?= {
					NOR = {
						this = scope:puppet_or_actor
						this = scope:recipient
					}
				}
			}
			scope:target.de_jure_liege.holder = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						scope:actor = {
							is_ai = no
						}
					}
					add_opinion = {
						target = scope:puppet_or_actor
						modifier = gave_away_de_jure_lands_opinion
						opinion = -70
					}
				}
				else = {
					hidden_effect = {
						add_opinion = {
							target = scope:puppet_or_actor
							modifier = gave_away_de_jure_lands_opinion
							opinion = -70
						}
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						scope:recipient = scope:puppet_or_actor
					}
				}
				scope:recipient = {
					if = {
						limit = {
							is_ai = no
						}
						scope:target.de_jure_liege.holder = {
							add_opinion = {
								target = scope:recipient
								modifier = stole_de_jure_lands_opinion
								opinion = -50
							}
						}
					}
					else = {
						hidden_effect = {
							scope:target.de_jure_liege.holder = {
								add_opinion = {
									target = scope:recipient
									modifier = stole_de_jure_lands_opinion
									opinion = -50
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		
		scope:recipient = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						this = scope:puppet_or_actor
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						scope:actor = { is_ai = no }
					}
					add_opinion = {
						target = scope:puppet_or_actor
						modifier = grateful_opinion
						opinion = 30
					}
				}
				else = {
					hidden_effect = {
						scope:recipient = {
							add_opinion = {
								target = scope:puppet_or_actor
								modifier = grateful_opinion
								opinion = 30
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		scope:puppet_or_actor = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:actor = { is_ai = no }
				}
				custom_tooltip = every_subordinate_in_target_de_jure_liege_opinion_loss
				custom_tooltip = every_subordinate_in_actor_title_opinion_gain
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				# Bishops based in different dioceses have feelings
				every_subordinate = {
					limit = {
						capital_province.county ?= {
							any_this_title_or_de_jure_above = {
								this = scope:former_de_jure_liege
							}
						}
						NOR = {
							this = scope:puppet_or_actor
							this = scope:recipient
						}
					}
					add_opinion = {
						target = scope:puppet_or_actor
						modifier = ruled_against_my_diocese_opinion
						opinion = -25
					}
				}
				every_subordinate = {
					limit = {
						capital_province.county ?= {
							any_this_title_or_de_jure_above = {
								this = scope:recipient.primary_title
							}
						}
						NOR = {
							this = scope:puppet_or_actor
							this = scope:recipient
						}
					}
					add_opinion = {
						target = scope:puppet_or_actor
						modifier = ruled_in_favor_of_my_diocese_opinion
						opinion = 25
					}
				}
			}
		}
		
		scope:recipient = {
			show_as_tooltip = {
				ecclesiastical_de_jure_shifting_effect = {
					DE_JURE_MASTER = scope:recipient
					CLERIC = scope:puppet_or_actor
				}
			}
		}

		scope:puppet_or_actor = {
			trigger_event = pam_interactions_events.0096
		}
	}

	on_decline = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:recipient = {
					is_ai = no
				}
			}
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				send_interface_toast = {
					type = event_toast_effect_bad
					title = ecclesiastical_de_jure_shifting_grant_interaction_decline_toast
					left_icon = scope:recipient
				}
				add_opinion = {
					target = scope:recipient
					modifier = refusal_opinion
					opinion = -30
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes
					scope:actor != scope:recipient
				}
				scope:actor = {
					send_interface_toast = {
						type = event_toast_effect_bad
						title = ecclesiastical_de_jure_shifting_grant_interaction_decline_toast_puppeteer
						left_icon = scope:puppet_or_actor
						right_icon = scope:recipient
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:target.de_jure_liege.holder ?= {
						NOR = {
							this = scope:puppet_or_actor
							this = scope:recipient
						}
					}
				}
				scope:target.de_jure_liege.holder ?= {
					add_opinion = {
						target = scope:recipient
						modifier = respected_de_jure_opinion
						opinion = 50
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	send_options_exclusive = no

	ai_targets = {
		ai_recipients = neighboring_rulers_including_tributary_borders
		ai_recipients = dynasty
		ai_recipients = realm_characters
		ai_recipients = subordinates
		ai_recipients = rulers_in_clerical_region
		max = 5
	}

	ai_frequency_by_tier = {
		barony = 0
		county = 0
		duchy = 120
		kingdom = 60
		empire = 60
		hegemony = 60
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		base = 5
		modifier = { # Peace of God: settling de jure claims by decree forestalls the wars they would otherwise cause
			add = 25
			scope:puppet_or_actor.rite ?= {
				rite_has_parameter = tenet_peace_of_god_clergy_peacemaking
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			exists = scope:target
			scope:recipient = {
				completely_controls = scope:target
			}
			add = 20
		}
		modifier = {
			exists = scope:target
			scope:target = {
				any_de_jure_county = {
					percent >= 0.5
					holder ?= {
						target_is_same_character_or_above = scope:recipient
					}
				}
			}
			add = 10
		}
		modifier = {
			scope:recipient = {
				has_any_good_relationship_with_character_trigger = { CHARACTER = scope:puppet_or_actor }
			}
			add = 75
		}
		modifier = {
			scope:recipient = scope:puppet_or_actor
			add = 100
		}
		modifier = {
			NOT = { scope:recipient = scope:puppet_or_actor }
			scope:target ?= {
				any_de_jure_county = {
					this = scope:puppet_or_actor.capital_county
					scope:puppet_or_actor.culture = capital_county.culture
				}
			}
			factor = 0.1
		}
		modifier = {
			OR = {
				ai_energy < 0
				AND = {
					scope:target.de_jure_liege.holder ?= {
						NOR = {
							this = scope:puppet_or_actor
							this = scope:recipient
						}
					}
					has_any_good_relationship_with_character_trigger = { CHARACTER = scope:target.de_jure_liege.holder }
				}
			}
			factor = 0
		}
	}
}



# A theocrat demands that a secular ruler put aside a spouse, citing an offence against the faith (real or invented).
demand_divorce_interaction = {
	category = interaction_category_religion
	icon = icon_marriage
	interface_priority = 52
	use_diplomatic_range = no
	ai_maybe = yes
	popup_on_receive = yes

	desc = demand_divorce_interaction_desc
	send_name = SEND_DEMAND
	prompt = demand_divorce_prompt

	notification_text = {
		desc = DEMAND_DIVORCE_NOTIFICATION
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = {
					demand_divorce_marriage_incestuous_trigger = { RULER = scope:recipient SPOUSE = scope:secondary_recipient }
				}
				desc = DEMAND_DIVORCE_NOTIFICATION_INCESTUOUS
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = {
					demand_divorce_marriage_same_sex_trigger = { RULER = scope:recipient SPOUSE = scope:secondary_recipient }
				}
				desc = DEMAND_DIVORCE_NOTIFICATION_SAME_SEX
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = {
					demand_divorce_spouse_heathen_trigger = { RULER = scope:recipient SPOUSE = scope:secondary_recipient }
				}
				desc = DEMAND_DIVORCE_NOTIFICATION_HEATHEN
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = {
					demand_divorce_spouse_heretic_trigger = { RULER = scope:recipient SPOUSE = scope:secondary_recipient }
				}
				desc = DEMAND_DIVORCE_NOTIFICATION_HERETIC
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = {
					demand_divorce_spouse_infidelity_trigger = { RULER = scope:recipient SPOUSE = scope:secondary_recipient }
				}
				desc = DEMAND_DIVORCE_NOTIFICATION_INFIDELITY
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = {
					demand_divorce_marriage_polygamous_trigger = { RULER = scope:recipient SPOUSE = scope:secondary_recipient }
				}
				desc = DEMAND_DIVORCE_NOTIFICATION_POLYGAMY
			}
			desc = DEMAND_DIVORCE_NOTIFICATION_PRETEXT
		}
	}

	cooldown_against_recipient = { years = 5 }

	is_available = {
		trigger_if = {
			limit = {
				is_ai = yes
			}
			piety_level >= medium_piety_level
			NOT = { has_trait = excommunicated }
		}
		pam_is_clergy_ruler_trigger = yes
		has_pam_dlc_trigger = yes
	}

	is_shown = {
		scope:recipient = {
			this != scope:actor
			is_landed = yes
			is_married = yes
			is_valid_demand_church_succession_target_trigger = yes
			faith ?= scope:actor.faith
			primary_title ?= {
				tier >= tier_county
			}
		}
		# Rulers already served a divorce demand by any clergy are off-limits for a decade. Player actors are deliberately not bound by this.
		trigger_if = {
			limit = {
				scope:actor = { is_ai = yes }
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				NOT = { has_variable = demand_divorce_recently_demanded }
			}
		}
	}

	populate_recipient_list = {
		scope:recipient = {
			every_spouse = {
				limit = {
					this != scope:actor
					is_concubine = no
				}
				add_to_list = characters
			}
		}
	}

	has_valid_target_showing_failures_only = {
		exists = scope:secondary_recipient
		scope:secondary_recipient = {
			this != scope:actor
			is_spouse_of = scope:recipient
			is_concubine = no
			NOT = { has_strong_hook = scope:recipient }
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = demand_divorce_family_widow_tt
				NOT = {
					var:family_widow_forbidden_divorce ?= scope:secondary_recipient
				}
			}
		}
	}

	is_valid_showing_failures_only = {
		scope:actor = {
			is_available_adult = yes
			NOT = { has_trait = excommunicated }
			NOT = { is_at_war_with = scope:recipient }
		}
		#Range: the Head of Faith reaches anyone, archbishops their clerical region, and all theocrats their own top realm
		trigger_if = {
			limit = {
				scope:actor = {
					NOT = { religious_head_or_challenger ?= this }
					any_held_title = { has_clerical_region = yes }
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = demand_divorce_archbishop_range_tt
				OR = {
					scope:recipient.top_liege ?= scope:actor.top_liege
					scope:recipient = {
						OR = {
							capital_county ?= {
								clerical_region_title ?= { holder = scope:actor }
							}
							location.county ?= {
								clerical_region_title ?= { holder = scope:actor }
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		trigger_else_if = {
			limit = {
				scope:actor = {
					NOT = { religious_head_or_challenger ?= this }
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = demand_divorce_realm_range_tt
				scope:recipient.top_liege ?= scope:actor.top_liege
			}
		}
		trigger_else = {
			always = yes
		}
		# The marriage must be one the recipient could lawfully end.
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = demand_divorce_culture_forbids_tt
			NOT = {
				scope:recipient.culture = {
					has_cultural_parameter = cannot_divorce
				}
			}
		}
		NOT = {
			scope:recipient.rite = {
				rite_has_doctrine = doctrine_divorce_disallowed
			}
		}
	}

	cost = {
		piety = {
			value = minor_piety_value
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:recipient.highest_held_title_tier >= tier_kingdom
				}
				value = major_piety_value
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:actor = {
						domicile ?= { has_domicile_parameter = estate_cheaper_divorce }
					}
				}
				multiply = ecclesiastical_domicile_cheaper_divorce_mult
			}
		}
	}

	on_send = {
		# Shields the recipient from being dogpiled by every clergyman in reach; only AI actors respect it (see is_shown).
		scope:recipient = {
			set_variable = {
				name = demand_divorce_recently_demanded
				years = 10
			}
		}
	}

	on_accept = {
		scope:actor = {
			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_good
				title = demand_divorce_actor_complied_toast
				left_icon = scope:recipient
				right_icon = scope:secondary_recipient
				scope:recipient = {
					divorce_effect = {
						DIVORCER = scope:recipient
						DIVORCEE = scope:secondary_recipient
					}
					add_opinion = {
						target = scope:actor
						modifier = demanded_divorce_opinion
					}
				}
				scope:secondary_recipient = {
					add_opinion = {
						target = scope:actor
						modifier = demanded_divorce_opinion
					}
				}
			}
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			add_piety = minor_piety_value
			change_spiritual_fulfillment = minor_spiritual_fulfillment_value
			# Remarrying straight out of a Church-dissolved union makes a mockery of the ruling; the AI waits it out.
			set_variable = {
				name = pam_clerical_divorce_enforced
				years = 5
			}
		}
	}

	on_decline = {
		scope:recipient = {
			add_piety = medium_piety_loss
			change_spiritual_fulfillment = medium_spiritual_fulfillment_loss
			add_opinion = {
				target = scope:actor
				modifier = demanded_divorce_opinion
			}
		}
		scope:secondary_recipient = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = scope:actor
				modifier = demanded_divorce_opinion
			}
		}
		scope:actor = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = scope:recipient
				modifier = defied_divorce_demand_opinion
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				trigger_event = pam_interactions_events.0111
			}
		}
	}

	ai_targets = {
		ai_recipients = rulers_in_clerical_region
		ai_recipients = neighboring_rulers
		ai_recipients = scripted_relations
	}
	ai_targets = {
		ai_recipients = peer_vassals
		ai_recipients = domicile_location_top_realm_vassals
		max = 20
	}

	ai_target_quick_trigger = {
		adult = yes
	}

	ai_frequency_by_tier = {
		barony = 0
		county = 120
		duchy = 60
		kingdom = 36
		empire = 12
		hegemony = 12
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		base = 0

		# Rivalry needs no theology.
		modifier = {
			add = 100
			scope:actor = { has_relation_rival = scope:recipient }
		}
		# The pious police marriages that offend the faith.
		modifier = {
			add = 60
			scope:actor.ai_zeal >= medium_positive_ai_value
			demand_divorce_marriage_incestuous_trigger = { RULER = scope:recipient SPOUSE = scope:secondary_recipient }
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 50
			scope:actor.ai_zeal >= medium_positive_ai_value
			demand_divorce_spouse_heathen_trigger = { RULER = scope:recipient SPOUSE = scope:secondary_recipient }
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 20
			scope:actor.ai_zeal >= medium_positive_ai_value
			demand_divorce_spouse_heretic_trigger = { RULER = scope:recipient SPOUSE = scope:secondary_recipient }
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 40
			scope:actor.ai_zeal >= medium_positive_ai_value
			demand_divorce_spouse_infidelity_trigger = { RULER = scope:recipient SPOUSE = scope:secondary_recipient }
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 40
			scope:actor.ai_zeal >= medium_positive_ai_value
			demand_divorce_marriage_same_sex_trigger = { RULER = scope:recipient SPOUSE = scope:secondary_recipient }
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			scope:actor.ai_zeal >= medium_positive_ai_value
			demand_divorce_marriage_polygamous_trigger = { RULER = scope:recipient SPOUSE = scope:secondary_recipient }
		}
		# Don't shake down friends, lovers, close family, or anyone the theocrat is genuinely fond of.
		modifier = {
			factor = 0
			scope:actor = {
				OR = {
					has_relation_friend = scope:recipient
					has_relation_lover = scope:recipient
					is_close_family_of = scope:recipient
					opinion = {
						target = scope:recipient
						value >= 30
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	ai_accept = {
		base = 0

		modifier = {
			add = 50
			desc = demand_divorce_ai_accept_authority_reason
		}
		modifier = {
			add = ecclesiastical_domicile_acceptance_minor_value_1
			desc = ESTATE_DEMAND_DIVORCE_REASON
			scope:actor = {
				domicile ?= {
					has_domicile_parameter = estate_easier_demand_divorce_1
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = ecclesiastical_domicile_acceptance_minor_value_2
			desc = ESTATE_DEMAND_DIVORCE_REASON
			scope:actor = {
				domicile ?= {
					has_domicile_parameter = estate_easier_demand_divorce_2
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = ecclesiastical_domicile_acceptance_minor_value_3
			desc = ESTATE_DEMAND_DIVORCE_REASON
			scope:actor = {
				domicile ?= {
					has_domicile_parameter = estate_easier_demand_divorce_3
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = ecclesiastical_domicile_acceptance_micro_value_1
			desc = ESTATE_DECRETALS_REASON
			scope:actor = {
				domicile ?= {
					has_domicile_parameter = estate_divorce_decretals_1
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = ecclesiastical_domicile_acceptance_micro_value_2
			desc = ESTATE_DECRETALS_REASON
			scope:actor = {
				domicile ?= {
					has_domicile_parameter = estate_divorce_decretals_2
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = ecclesiastical_domicile_acceptance_micro_value_3
			desc = ESTATE_DECRETALS_REASON
			scope:actor = {
				domicile ?= {
					has_domicile_parameter = estate_divorce_decretals_3
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = ecclesiastical_domicile_acceptance_micro_value_1
			desc = ESTATE_CHANCERY_REASON
			scope:actor = {
				domicile ?= {
					has_domicile_parameter = estate_chancery_divorce_1
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = ecclesiastical_domicile_acceptance_micro_value_2
			desc = ESTATE_CHANCERY_REASON
			scope:actor = {
				domicile ?= {
					has_domicile_parameter = estate_chancery_divorce_2
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = ecclesiastical_domicile_acceptance_micro_value_3
			desc = ESTATE_CHANCERY_REASON
			scope:actor = {
				domicile ?= {
					has_domicile_parameter = estate_chancery_divorce_3
				}
			}
		}
		ecclesiastical_domicile_cathedral_awe_modifier = yes
		# A true offence is difficult to deny.
		modifier = {
			add = 25
			desc = demand_divorce_ai_accept_offence_reason
			demand_divorce_marriage_offensive_trigger = { RULER = scope:recipient SPOUSE = scope:secondary_recipient }
		}
		opinion_modifier = {
			who = scope:recipient
			opinion_target = scope:actor
			multiplier = 1
			desc = AI_OPINION_REASON
		}
		# A beloved spouse is worth defying the faith for.
		opinion_modifier = {
			who = scope:recipient
			opinion_target = scope:secondary_recipient
			multiplier = -1
			desc = AI_OPINION_REASON
		}
		# A shepherd of another rite carries less weight.
		modifier = {
			add = -30
			desc = demand_divorce_ai_accept_rite_reason
			scope:actor.rite != scope:recipient.rite
		}
		ai_value_modifier = {
			who = scope:recipient
			ai_zeal = 0.5
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -60
			desc = demand_divorce_ai_accept_rival_reason
			scope:recipient = { has_relation_rival = scope:actor }
		}
	}
}

				## Warcraft - pam_create_holy_myron_artifact_effect is an empty stub in GoA
				## hidden_effect_new_object = {
					## pam_create_holy_myron_artifact_effect = {
						## OWNER = scope:recipient
						## CREATOR = scope:actor
						## RARITY = common
					## }
				## }
			#}
			#add_opinion = {

				#modifier = grateful_opinion
				#opinion = 20
			#}
		#}
	#}

	#ai_targets = {

		#ai_recipients = peer_vassals

	#ai_frequency = 120

			#has_trait = generous
		#}

			#has_trait = zealous
		#}
		#modifier = {
			#add = {
				#value = 10
				#multiply = 2
			#}
			#opinion = {
				#target = scope:recipient
				#value >= 50
			#}
		#}
		#modifier = {
			#factor = 0
			#has_trait = greedy
		#}
		#modifier = {
			#factor = 0
			#has_trait = cynical
		#}

seek_communion_interaction = {
	category = interaction_category_religion
	icon = religious
	interface_priority = 50
	use_diplomatic_range = no

	desc = seek_communion_interaction_desc
	notification_text = seek_communion_interaction_notification

	greeting = positive
	popup_on_receive = yes
	ai_min_reply_days = 3
	ai_max_reply_days = 10

	cooldown_against_recipient = { years = 10 }
	
	is_available = {
		is_christian_trigger = yes
		faith ?= {
			trigger_if = {
				limit = { exists = religious_head }
				religious_head = root
			}
			trigger_else = {
				main_rite.head_of_rite = root
			}
			NOR = {
				has_doctrine = special_doctrine_ecumenical_christian
				always = no # Warcraft - special_doctrine_ecumenical_miaphysite does not exist in GoA
			}
		}
		has_pam_dlc_trigger = yes
	}

	is_shown = {
		scope:actor != scope:recipient
		scope:recipient = {
			OR = {
				like_catholic_faith_trigger = yes
				like_christian_faith_trigger = yes
			}
			any_held_title = { is_head_of_faith = yes }
		}
	}

	is_valid_showing_failures_only = {
		scope:actor = {
			is_adult = yes
			is_imprisoned = no
			NOT = { is_at_war_with = scope:recipient }
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			is_adult = yes
			is_imprisoned = no
			is_incapable = no
		}
	}

	send_option = {
		is_valid = {
			scope:actor = {
				has_usable_hook = scope:recipient
			}
		}
		flag = hook
		localization = SCHEME_HOOK
	}
	should_use_extra_icon = {
		scope:actor = { has_usable_hook = scope:recipient }
	}
	extra_icon = "gfx/interface/icons/character_interactions/hook_icon.dds"
	send_options_exclusive = no

	on_accept = {
		scope:actor = {
			if = {
				limit = { scope:hook = yes }
				use_hook = scope:recipient
			}
			show_as_tooltip = { pam_seek_communion_reconcile_faith_effect = yes }
			trigger_event = pam_interactions_events.0130
			faith = {
				every_faith_character = {
					limit = {
						is_ruler = yes
						this != scope:actor
					}
					trigger_event = { id = pam_interactions_events.0130 days = 1 }
				}
			}
		}
	}

	on_decline = {
		scope:actor = {
			trigger_event = pam_interactions_events.0131
		}
	}

	ai_targets = {
		ai_recipients = other_faith_heads
	}

	ai_target_quick_trigger = {
		adult = yes
	}

	ai_frequency_by_tier = {
		barony = 0
		county = 24
		duchy = 24
		kingdom = 12
		empire = 12
		hegemony = 12
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		base = 20

		# modifier = {
		# 	add = 30
		# 	situation:the_christian_church ?= { situation_current_phase = concord }
		# }
		modifier = {
			add = -20
			scope:actor = { has_trait = arrogant }
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -40
			scope:actor = { has_trait = zealous }
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 20
			scope:actor = { has_trait = content }
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 20
			scope:actor = { has_trait = humble }
		}
		modifier = { # More likely when the heresy has not drifted far from Catholic practice
			add = {
				value = "scope:actor.faith.main_rite.divergence(scope:recipient.faith.main_rite)"
				multiply = -0.5
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			scope:hook = yes
			add = 50
		}
	}

	ai_accept = {
		base = -20

		modifier = {
			add = 30
			desc = SCHEME_WEAK_HOOK_USED
			scope:hook = yes
			scope:actor = { NOT = { has_strong_hook = scope:recipient } }
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 50
			desc = SCHEME_STRONG_HOOK_USED
			scope:hook = yes
			scope:actor = { has_strong_hook = scope:recipient }
		}
		# modifier = {
		# 	add = 30
		# 	desc = CHRISTIAN_CHURCH_PHASE_REASON
		# 	situation:the_christian_church ?= { situation_current_phase = concord }
		# }
		modifier = {
			add = -40
			desc = SEEK_COMMUNION_ZEALOUS_REASON
			scope:recipient = { has_trait = zealous }
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 25
			desc = SEEK_COMMUNION_FORGIVING_REASON
			scope:recipient = { has_trait = forgiving }
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -30
			desc = SEEK_COMMUNION_EXCOMMUNICATED_REASON
			scope:actor = { has_trait = excommunicated }
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 20
			desc = SEEK_COMMUNION_PIOUS_PETITIONER_REASON
			scope:actor = { piety_level >= 3 }
		}
		modifier = { # Halved so that even a wholly divergent heresy can still be argued back in
			add = {
				value = "scope:actor.faith.main_rite.divergence(scope:recipient.faith.main_rite)"
				multiply = -0.5
			}
			desc = SEEK_COMMUNION_DIVERGENCE_REASON
		}
		opinion_modifier = { # Opinion Factor
			who = scope:recipient
			opinion_target = scope:actor
			multiplier = 0.5
		}
	}
}

pam_destroy_monastic_order_interaction = {
	category = interaction_category_religion
	icon = pam_destroy_monastic_order
	interface_priority = 39

	desc = pam_destroy_monastic_order_interaction_desc

	auto_accept = { always = yes }
	cooldown = { years = 10 }

	is_available = {
		faith.religious_head ?= this
		has_pam_dlc_trigger = yes
		is_adult = yes
	}

	is_shown = {
		scope:recipient = {
			this != scope:actor
			government_has_flag = government_is_monastic_holy_order
			holy_order ?= {
				order_faith ?= scope:actor.faith
			}
		}
	}

	is_valid_showing_failures_only = {
		scope:actor = {
			is_available_quick = {
				adult = yes
				incapable = no
				imprisoned = no
				hostage = no
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = pam_destroy_monastic_order_at_peace_tt
			scope:recipient = {
				is_at_war = no
			}
		}
	}

	cost = {
		piety = massive_piety_value
	}

	on_accept = {
		scope:recipient.holy_order ?= {
			save_scope_as = pam_dissolved_order
			title = { save_scope_as = pam_dissolved_order_title }
		}
		scope:actor = {
			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_bad
				title = pam_destroy_monastic_order_interaction_toast
				left_icon = scope:actor
				right_icon = scope:recipient
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = pam_destroy_monastic_order_dissolved_tt
					hidden_effect = {
						scope:pam_dissolved_order = {
							every_leased_title = {
								revoke_lease = yes
							}
						}
					}
				}
				scope:recipient = {
					add_opinion = {
						target = scope:actor
						modifier = revoked_order_lease_opinion
						opinion = -75
					}
				}
				scope:actor.faith ?= {
					every_faith_holy_order = {
						custom = custom.every_other_monastic_order_leader
						limit = {
							this != scope:pam_dissolved_order
							is_monastic_order_trigger = yes
						}
						leader ?= {
							add_opinion = {
								target = scope:actor
								modifier = revoked_order_lease_opinion
								opinion = -30
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	ai_targets = {
		ai_recipients = faith_holy_order_leaders
	}

	ai_target_quick_trigger = {
		adult = yes
	}

	ai_frequency_by_tier = {
		barony = 0
		county = 0
		duchy = 48
		kingdom = 24
		empire = 24
		hegemony = 24
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		base = 0

		modifier = {
			add = 100
			scope:recipient.holy_order ?= {
				order_tenet ?= {
					scope:actor.rite ?= {
						has_tenet_status = {
							tenet = prev
							status = prohibited
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 100
			scope:recipient = { has_trait = excommunicated }
		}
		modifier = { # Never against your own
			factor = 0
			scope:actor = {
				OR = {
					has_relation_friend = scope:recipient
					has_relation_best_friend = scope:recipient
					is_any_family_relation_or_spouse_trigger = { CHARACTER = scope:recipient }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
